/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — airGapBridge.ts
 * Air-Gap Bridge Engine
 *
 * Implements:
 *   - QR code chunked data transfer with Reed-Solomon error correction
 *   - Audio FSK modem (Bell 202 / custom) for acoustic data transmission
 *   - USB data diode — one-way flow enforcement at the hardware abstraction level
 *   - Printable steganographic codes (LSB pixel embedding, DCT coefficient hiding)
 *   - Optical LED pulse encoding (Manchester, NRZ, 4B5B line coding)
 *   - MIME-agnostic frame protocol for all transport channels
 *   - Chunked session management with reassembly and integrity verification
 *   - Reed-Solomon (255,223) forward error correction for noisy channels
 *   - BLAKE3 chain integrity across multi-chunk sessions
 *   - Anti-exfiltration enforcement on diode channels
 *
 * Design contract:
 *   - No network stack — zero TCP/IP dependency
 *   - All channels are unidirectional or explicitly bidirectional
 *   - Every frame carries a chain hash — undetectable truncation attacks blocked
 *   - Compatible with storeForward.ts bundle framing
 *
 * @module airGapBridge
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES & INTERFACES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type ChannelType = 'qr' | 'audio' | 'usb_diode' | 'steganographic' | 'optical_led';
export type FlowDirection = 'inbound' | 'outbound' | 'bidirectional';
export type EncodingScheme = 'manchester' | 'nrz' | '4b5b' | 'fsk' | 'qam';
export type DiodePolicy = 'allow_all' | 'whitelist' | 'blacklist' | 'challenge_response';

export interface AirGapConfig {
  channelType: ChannelType;
  flowDirection: FlowDirection;
  maxChunkBytes: number;           // Max payload per frame
  sessionTimeoutMs: number;        // Abandon partial session after this
  errorCorrectionLevel: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'max'; // RS redundancy
  enableEncryption: boolean;
  enableCompression: boolean;
  retransmitOnError: boolean;
  maxRetransmissions: number;
  // Audio FSK specific
  fskMarkFreqHz: number;           // '1' bit frequency, e.g. 1200 Hz
  fskSpaceFreqHz: number;          // '0' bit frequency, e.g. 2200 Hz
  fskBaudRate: number;             // e.g. 1200 baud
  audioSampleRateHz: number;       // e.g. 44100
  // QR specific
  qrVersion: number;               // 1-40, determines capacity
  // Optical LED specific
  ledPulseWidthMicros: number;     // microseconds per bit
  ledCarrierFreqHz: number;        // IR carrier (e.g. 38000 Hz)
  // Diode specific
  diodePolicy: DiodePolicy;
  allowedSourceIds: string[];
}

export const DEFAULT_AIRGAP_CONFIG: AirGapConfig = {
  channelType: 'qr',
  flowDirection: 'outbound',
  maxChunkBytes: 2000,
  sessionTimeoutMs: 300000,
  errorCorrectionLevel: 'high',
  enableEncryption: true,
  enableCompression: true,
  retransmitOnError: true,
  maxRetransmissions: 5,
  fskMarkFreqHz: 1200,
  fskSpaceFreqHz: 2200,
  fskBaudRate: 1200,
  audioSampleRateHz: 44100,
  qrVersion: 20,
  ledPulseWidthMicros: 833,        // 1200 bps
  ledCarrierFreqHz: 38000,
  diodePolicy: 'whitelist',
  allowedSourceIds: [],
};

export interface AirGapFrame {
  magic: 0xAE47;                   // UBE air-gap magic bytes
  version: 1;
  sessionId: string;               // 16-byte hex
  frameIndex: number;              // 0-based
  totalFrames: number;
  payloadLength: number;
  payload: Uint8Array;
  chainHash: string;               // BLAKE3-like chain: H(prev_hash || payload)
  checksum: string;                // CRC32 of frame minus checksum field
  flags: number;
  // Flags bit definitions:
  // bit 0: isFirst
  // bit 1: isLast
  // bit 2: requiresAck
  // bit 3: encrypted
  // bit 4: compressed
  // bit 5: errorCorrected
}

export const FRAME_FLAG_FIRST        = 0x01;
export const FRAME_FLAG_LAST         = 0x02;
export const FRAME_FLAG_REQUIRES_ACK = 0x04;
export const FRAME_FLAG_ENCRYPTED    = 0x08;
export const FRAME_FLAG_COMPRESSED   = 0x10;
export const FRAME_FLAG_ECC          = 0x20;

export interface AirGapSession {
  sessionId: string;
  direction: FlowDirection;
  channel: ChannelType;
  totalFrames: number;
  receivedFrames: Map<number, AirGapFrame>;
  startedAt: number;
  lastActivityAt: number;
  chainHead: string;               // running chain hash
  complete: boolean;
  sourceId?: string;
  destinationId?: string;
  metadata: Record<string, string>;
}

export interface TransmissionResult {
  sessionId: string;
  framesTransmitted: number;
  bytesTransmitted: number;
  durationMs: number;
  errorsCorrected: number;
  success: boolean;
  failureReason?: string;
}

export interface ReceptionResult {
  sessionId: string;
  data: Uint8Array | null;
  framesReceived: number;
  bytesReceived: number;
  durationMs: number;
  integrityVerified: boolean;
  errorsCorrected: number;
}

// QR code data capacity per version (approximate, high ECC)
const QR_CAPACITY_BYTES: Record<number, number> = {
  1: 7, 2: 14, 3: 24, 4: 34, 5: 44,
  6: 58, 7: 64, 8: 84, 9: 98, 10: 119,
  15: 195, 20: 283, 25: 382, 30: 461, 35: 524, 40: 562,
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// CRYPTOGRAPHIC PRIMITIVES (self-contained)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AirGapCrypto {
  // BLAKE3-inspired chained hash (simplified for zero-dependency pure TS)
  static chainHash(previousHash: string, payload: Uint8Array): string {
    const prev = AirGapCrypto.hexToBytes(previousHash.padEnd(64, '0'));
    const combined = AirGapCrypto.concat(prev, payload);
    return AirGapCrypto.toHex(AirGapCrypto.sha256(combined));
  }

  static sha256(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    const K = [
      0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,
      0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,
      0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,
      0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,
      0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,
      0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,
      0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,
      0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2,
    ];
    let h0=0x6a09e667,h1=0xbb67ae85,h2=0x3c6ef372,h3=0xa54ff53a;
    let h4=0x510e527f,h5=0x9b05688c,h6=0x1f83d9ab,h7=0x5be0cd19;
    const msgLen=data.length, bitLen=msgLen*8;
    const paddedLen=Math.ceil((msgLen+9)/64)*64;
    const padded=new Uint8Array(paddedLen);
    padded.set(data); padded[msgLen]=0x80;
    const dv=new DataView(padded.buffer);
    dv.setUint32(paddedLen-4,bitLen>>>0,false);
    dv.setUint32(paddedLen-8,Math.floor(bitLen/0x100000000),false);
    const rot=(x:number,n:number)=>(x>>>n)|(x<<(32-n));
    const add=(a:number,b:number)=>(a+b)>>>0;
    for(let i=0;i<paddedLen;i+=64){
      const w=new Uint32Array(64);
      for(let j=0;j<16;j++) w[j]=dv.getUint32(i+j*4,false);
      for(let j=16;j<64;j++){
        const s0=rot(w[j-15],7)^rot(w[j-15],18)^(w[j-15]>>>3);
        const s1=rot(w[j-2],17)^rot(w[j-2],19)^(w[j-2]>>>10);
        w[j]=add(add(add(w[j-16],s0),w[j-7]),s1);
      }
      let[a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h]=[h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7];
      for(let j=0;j<64;j++){
        const S1=rot(e,6)^rot(e,11)^rot(e,25);
        const ch=(e&f)^(~e&g);
        const t1=add(add(add(add(h,S1),ch),K[j]),w[j]);
        const S0=rot(a,2)^rot(a,13)^rot(a,22);
        const maj=(a&b)^(a&c)^(b&c);
        const t2=add(S0,maj);
        h=g;g=f;f=e;e=add(d,t1);d=c;c=b;b=a;a=add(t1,t2);
      }
      h0=add(h0,a);h1=add(h1,b);h2=add(h2,c);h3=add(h3,d);
      h4=add(h4,e);h5=add(h5,f);h6=add(h6,g);h7=add(h7,h);
    }
    const r=new Uint8Array(32);const rv=new DataView(r.buffer);
    [h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7].forEach((v,i)=>rv.setUint32(i*4,v,false));
    return r;
  }

  static crc32(data: Uint8Array): number {
    let crc = 0xFFFFFFFF;
    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      crc ^= data[i];
      for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
        crc = (crc & 1) ? (crc >>> 1) ^ 0xEDB88320 : crc >>> 1;
      }
    }
    return (crc ^ 0xFFFFFFFF) >>> 0;
  }

  static xorStream(data: Uint8Array, key: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    // ChaCha20-style keystream (simplified XOR for air-gap pre-encryption)
    const result = new Uint8Array(data.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      result[i] = data[i] ^ key[i % key.length] ^ ((i * 0x9e3779b9) & 0xFF);
    }
    return result;
  }

  // LZ77-inspired compression (simplified)
  static compress(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    // Simple RLE for runs — expand to LZ77 in production
    const out: number[] = [];
    let i = 0;
    while (i < data.length) {
      let runLen = 1;
      while (i + runLen < data.length && data[i + runLen] === data[i] && runLen < 255) runLen++;
      if (runLen > 3) {
        out.push(0xFF, runLen, data[i]);
        i += runLen;
      } else {
        if (data[i] === 0xFF) out.push(0xFF, 0, data[i]);
        else out.push(data[i]);
        i++;
      }
    }
    return new Uint8Array(out);
  }

  static decompress(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    const out: number[] = [];
    let i = 0;
    while (i < data.length) {
      if (data[i] === 0xFF) {
        const runLen = data[i + 1];
        if (runLen === 0) { out.push(data[i + 2]); i += 3; }
        else { for (let j = 0; j < runLen; j++) out.push(data[i + 2]); i += 3; }
      } else {
        out.push(data[i]); i++;
      }
    }
    return new Uint8Array(out);
  }

  static toHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
    return Array.from(bytes).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }

  static hexToBytes(hex: string): Uint8Array {
    const r = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < hex.length; i += 2) r[i/2] = parseInt(hex.slice(i,i+2),16);
    return r;
  }

  static concat(...arrays: Uint8Array[]): Uint8Array {
    const total = arrays.reduce((s,a)=>s+a.length,0);
    const r = new Uint8Array(total);
    let o = 0;
    for (const a of arrays) { r.set(a,o); o+=a.length; }
    return r;
  }

  static randomId(): string {
    const b = new Uint8Array(16);
    for (let i=0;i<16;i++) b[i]=Math.floor(Math.random()*256);
    return AirGapCrypto.toHex(b);
  }

  static randomBytes(n: number): Uint8Array {
    const b = new Uint8Array(n);
    for (let i=0;i<n;i++) b[i]=Math.floor(Math.random()*256);
    return b;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// REED-SOLOMON ERROR CORRECTION (GF(2^8))
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ReedSolomon {
  // GF(2^8) with primitive polynomial x^8 + x^4 + x^3 + x^2 + 1 (0x11D)
  private static readonly EXP: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(512);
  private static readonly LOG: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array(256);
  private static initialized = false;

  static init(): void {
    if (ReedSolomon.initialized) return;
    let x = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < 255; i++) {
      ReedSolomon.EXP[i] = x;
      ReedSolomon.LOG[x] = i;
      x <<= 1;
      if (x > 255) x ^= 0x11D;
    }
    for (let i = 255; i < 512; i++) ReedSolomon.EXP[i] = ReedSolomon.EXP[i - 255];
    ReedSolomon.initialized = true;
  }

  static gfMul(a: number, b: number): number {
    if (a === 0 || b === 0) return 0;
    return ReedSolomon.EXP[(ReedSolomon.LOG[a] + ReedSolomon.LOG[b]) % 255];
  }

  static gfDiv(a: number, b: number): number {
    if (b === 0) throw new Error('Division by zero in GF');
    if (a === 0) return 0;
    return ReedSolomon.EXP[(ReedSolomon.LOG[a] - ReedSolomon.LOG[b] + 255) % 255];
  }

  static gfPow(a: number, power: number): number {
    return ReedSolomon.EXP[(ReedSolomon.LOG[a] * power) % 255];
  }

  static gfPolyMul(p: number[], q: number[]): number[] {
    const r = new Array(p.length + q.length - 1).fill(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < p.length; i++) {
      for (let j = 0; j < q.length; j++) {
        r[i + j] ^= ReedSolomon.gfMul(p[i], q[j]);
      }
    }
    return r;
  }

  // Generate RS generator polynomial for nsym parity symbols
  static generatorPoly(nsym: number): number[] {
    ReedSolomon.init();
    let g = [1];
    for (let i = 0; i < nsym; i++) {
      g = ReedSolomon.gfPolyMul(g, [1, ReedSolomon.gfPow(2, i)]);
    }
    return g;
  }

  /**
   * Encode data with nsym parity symbols
   * Returns data + parity (total: data.length + nsym bytes)
   */
  static encode(data: Uint8Array, nsym: number): Uint8Array {
    ReedSolomon.init();
    const gen = ReedSolomon.generatorPoly(nsym);
    const msgOut = new Uint8Array(data.length + nsym);
    msgOut.set(data);

    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      const coef = msgOut[i];
      if (coef !== 0) {
        for (let j = 1; j < gen.length; j++) {
          msgOut[i + j] ^= ReedSolomon.gfMul(gen[j], coef);
        }
      }
    }

    const result = new Uint8Array(data.length + nsym);
    result.set(data);
    result.set(msgOut.slice(data.length), data.length);
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Decode and correct errors
   * Returns corrected data, or throws if too many errors
   */
  static decode(received: Uint8Array, nsym: number): { data: Uint8Array; errorsFixed: number } {
    ReedSolomon.init();
    const msg = Array.from(received);

    // Calculate syndromes
    const synd = new Array(nsym).fill(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < nsym; i++) {
      let s = 0;
      for (const b of msg) {
        s = ReedSolomon.gfMul(s, ReedSolomon.gfPow(2, i)) ^ b;
      }
      synd[i] = s;
    }

    // Check if data is valid (all syndromes = 0)
    if (synd.every(s => s === 0)) {
      return {
        data: received.slice(0, received.length - nsym),
        errorsFixed: 0,
      };
    }

    // Berlekamp-Massey algorithm for error locator polynomial
    let errLoc = [1];
    let oldLoc = [1];

    for (let i = 0; i < nsym; i++) {
      oldLoc = [...oldLoc, 0];
      let delta = synd[i];
      for (let j = 1; j < errLoc.length; j++) {
        delta ^= ReedSolomon.gfMul(errLoc[errLoc.length - 1 - j], synd[i - j]);
      }
      if (delta !== 0) {
        if (oldLoc.length > errLoc.length) {
          const newLoc = oldLoc.map(c => ReedSolomon.gfMul(c, delta));
          oldLoc = errLoc.map(c => ReedSolomon.gfMul(c, ReedSolomon.gfDiv(1, delta)));
          errLoc = newLoc;
        }
        const scale = oldLoc.map(c => ReedSolomon.gfMul(c, delta));
        for (let j = 0; j < scale.length; j++) {
          errLoc[errLoc.length - scale.length + j] ^= scale[j];
        }
      }
    }

    const errCount = errLoc.length - 1;
    if (errCount * 2 > nsym) throw new Error('Too many errors to correct');

    // Find error positions (Chien search)
    const errPos: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < received.length; i++) {
      let val = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < errLoc.length; j++) {
        val ^= ReedSolomon.gfMul(errLoc[errLoc.length - 1 - j], ReedSolomon.gfPow(2, i * j));
      }
      if (val === 0) errPos.push(received.length - 1 - i);
    }

    if (errPos.length !== errCount) throw new Error('Could not locate all errors');

    // Forney algorithm for error magnitudes
    const msgCopy = Array.from(received);
    const errEval = ReedSolomon.gfPolyMul(synd.slice(0, errCount + 1), errLoc);
    const errEvalTrimmed = errEval.slice(Math.max(0, errEval.length - errCount));

    const errLocPrime: number[] = [];
    for (let j = 0; j < errLoc.length - 1; j++) {
      if ((errLoc.length - 1 - j) % 2 !== 0) errLocPrime.push(errLoc[j]);
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < errPos.length; i++) {
      const pos = errPos[i];
      const xiInv = ReedSolomon.gfPow(2, received.length - 1 - pos);
      let ePrime = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < errLocPrime.length; j++) {
        ePrime ^= ReedSolomon.gfMul(errLocPrime[j], ReedSolomon.gfPow(xiInv, j));
      }
      if (ePrime === 0) continue;
      let eVal = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < errEvalTrimmed.length; j++) {
        eVal ^= ReedSolomon.gfMul(errEvalTrimmed[j], ReedSolomon.gfPow(xiInv, j));
      }
      msgCopy[pos] ^= ReedSolomon.gfDiv(eVal, ePrime);
    }

    return {
      data: new Uint8Array(msgCopy.slice(0, received.length - nsym)),
      errorsFixed: errPos.length,
    };
  }

  static nsymForLevel(level: AirGapConfig['errorCorrectionLevel']): number {
    switch (level) {
      case 'low':    return 8;
      case 'medium': return 16;
      case 'high':   return 32;
      case 'max':    return 64;
    }
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// FRAME CODEC — Serialise / deserialise AirGapFrame
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class FrameCodec {
  private static readonly HEADER_SIZE = 2+1+16+4+4+2+64+8+1; // 102 bytes fixed header

  static encode(frame: AirGapFrame, eccNsym: number): Uint8Array {
    const sessionIdBytes = AirGapCrypto.hexToBytes(frame.sessionId);
    const chainHashBytes = AirGapCrypto.hexToBytes(frame.chainHash.padEnd(64,'0').slice(0,64));

    const headerBuf = new Uint8Array(FrameCodec.HEADER_SIZE);
    const hv = new DataView(headerBuf.buffer);
    let off = 0;

    hv.setUint16(off, frame.magic, false); off += 2;
    headerBuf[off] = frame.version; off++;
    headerBuf.set(sessionIdBytes, off); off += 16;
    hv.setUint32(off, frame.frameIndex, false); off += 4;
    hv.setUint32(off, frame.totalFrames, false); off += 4;
    hv.setUint16(off, frame.payloadLength, false); off += 2;
    headerBuf.set(chainHashBytes, off); off += 64;
    // 8 bytes reserved for checksum (filled after)
    off += 8;
    headerBuf[off] = frame.flags; off++;

    // Combine header + payload
    const combined = AirGapCrypto.concat(headerBuf, frame.payload);

    // Compute CRC32 over all bytes except checksum slot
    const crc = AirGapCrypto.crc32(combined);
    const cv = new DataView(combined.buffer);
    cv.setUint32(FrameCodec.HEADER_SIZE - 8, crc, false);
    cv.setUint32(FrameCodec.HEADER_SIZE - 4, crc ^ 0xDEADBEEF, false);

    // Apply Reed-Solomon ECC
    if (eccNsym > 0) {
      return ReedSolomon.encode(combined, eccNsym);
    }
    return combined;
  }

  static decode(
    raw: Uint8Array,
    eccNsym: number
  ): { frame: AirGapFrame; errorsFixed: number } {
    let data = raw;
    let errorsFixed = 0;

    if (eccNsym > 0) {
      const decoded = ReedSolomon.decode(raw, eccNsym);
      data = decoded.data;
      errorsFixed = decoded.errorsFixed;
    }

    const dv = new DataView(data.buffer, data.byteOffset, data.byteLength);
    let off = 0;

    const magic = dv.getUint16(off, false) as 0xAE47; off += 2;
    const version = data[off] as 1; off++;
    const sessionIdBytes = data.slice(off, off + 16); off += 16;
    const frameIndex = dv.getUint32(off, false); off += 4;
    const totalFrames = dv.getUint32(off, false); off += 4;
    const payloadLength = dv.getUint16(off, false); off += 2;
    const chainHashBytes = data.slice(off, off + 64); off += 64;
    const storedCrc = dv.getUint32(off, false); off += 8; // skip both crc slots
    const flags = data[off]; off++;

    const payload = data.slice(off, off + payloadLength);

    // Verify CRC
    const withoutCrc = new Uint8Array(data);
    withoutCrc.fill(0, FrameCodec.HEADER_SIZE - 8, FrameCodec.HEADER_SIZE - 4);
    const computedCrc = AirGapCrypto.crc32(withoutCrc);
    if (computedCrc !== storedCrc) {
      throw new Error(`CRC mismatch: expected ${storedCrc}, got ${computedCrc}`);
    }

    return {
      frame: {
        magic,
        version,
        sessionId: AirGapCrypto.toHex(sessionIdBytes),
        frameIndex,
        totalFrames,
        payloadLength,
        payload,
        chainHash: AirGapCrypto.toHex(chainHashBytes),
        checksum: storedCrc.toString(16),
        flags,
      },
      errorsFixed,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// QR CODE CHANNEL
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class QRChannel {
  private readonly config: AirGapConfig;
  private readonly eccNsym: number;

  constructor(config: AirGapConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.eccNsym = ReedSolomon.nsymForLevel(config.errorCorrectionLevel);
  }

  /**
   * Encode binary data into sequence of QR payloads
   * Each QR payload is a base64-encoded, ECC-wrapped frame
   */
  encodeToQRPayloads(data: Uint8Array, sessionId?: string): string[] {
    const sid = sessionId ?? AirGapCrypto.randomId();
    const processed = this.preprocess(data);
    const chunks = this.chunkData(processed);
    const qrPayloads: string[] = [];
    let chainHead = '0'.repeat(64);

    for (let i = 0; i < chunks.length; i++) {
      const chunk = chunks[i];
      chainHead = AirGapCrypto.chainHash(chainHead, chunk);

      let flags = 0;
      if (i === 0) flags |= FRAME_FLAG_FIRST;
      if (i === chunks.length - 1) flags |= FRAME_FLAG_LAST;
      if (this.config.enableEncryption) flags |= FRAME_FLAG_ENCRYPTED;
      if (this.config.enableCompression) flags |= FRAME_FLAG_COMPRESSED;
      flags |= FRAME_FLAG_ECC;

      const frame: AirGapFrame = {
        magic: 0xAE47,
        version: 1,
        sessionId: sid,
        frameIndex: i,
        totalFrames: chunks.length,
        payloadLength: chunk.length,
        payload: chunk,
        chainHash: chainHead,
        checksum: '',
        flags,
      };

      const encoded = FrameCodec.encode(frame, this.eccNsym);
      // Base64url encode for QR data (alphanumeric mode)
      qrPayloads.push(this.toBase64Url(encoded));
    }

    return qrPayloads;
  }

  /**
   * Decode a sequence of scanned QR payloads back to binary data
   */
  decodeFromQRPayloads(payloads: string[]): ReceptionResult {
    const startedAt = Date.now();
    const frames = new Map<number, AirGapFrame>();
    let totalErrorsFixed = 0;
    let sessionId = '';

    for (const payload of payloads) {
      try {
        const raw = this.fromBase64Url(payload);
        const { frame, errorsFixed } = FrameCodec.decode(raw, this.eccNsym);
        frames.set(frame.frameIndex, frame);
        totalErrorsFixed += errorsFixed;
        sessionId = frame.sessionId;
      } catch (e) {
        // Frame corrupted beyond ECC capacity — mark as missing
      }
    }

    if (frames.size === 0) {
      return { sessionId, data: null, framesReceived: 0, bytesReceived: 0,
               durationMs: Date.now() - startedAt, integrityVerified: false, errorsCorrected: 0 };
    }

    // Verify chain integrity
    const maxFrame = Math.max(...frames.keys());
    const totalFrames = frames.get(maxFrame)?.totalFrames ?? maxFrame + 1;

    let chainHead = '0'.repeat(64);
    let integrityOk = true;

    const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < totalFrames; i++) {
      const frame = frames.get(i);
      if (!frame) { integrityOk = false; continue; }

      const expectedChain = AirGapCrypto.chainHash(chainHead, frame.payload);
      if (expectedChain !== frame.chainHash) { integrityOk = false; }
      chainHead = frame.chainHash;
      chunks.push(frame.payload);
    }

    const combined = AirGapCrypto.concat(...chunks);
    const data = this.postprocess(combined);

    return {
      sessionId,
      data,
      framesReceived: frames.size,
      bytesReceived: combined.length,
      durationMs: Date.now() - startedAt,
      integrityVerified: integrityOk,
      errorsCorrected: totalErrorsFixed,
    };
  }

  private preprocess(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    let d = data;
    if (this.config.enableCompression) d = AirGapCrypto.compress(d);
    if (this.config.enableEncryption) {
      const key = AirGapCrypto.randomBytes(32);
      d = AirGapCrypto.concat(key, AirGapCrypto.xorStream(d, key));
    }
    return d;
  }

  private postprocess(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    let d = data;
    if (this.config.enableEncryption) {
      const key = d.slice(0, 32);
      d = AirGapCrypto.xorStream(d.slice(32), key);
    }
    if (this.config.enableCompression) d = AirGapCrypto.decompress(d);
    return d;
  }

  private chunkData(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array[] {
    const maxPayload = QR_CAPACITY_BYTES[this.config.qrVersion] ??
      this.config.maxChunkBytes - this.eccNsym - 102;
    const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
    let offset = 0;
    while (offset < data.length) {
      chunks.push(data.slice(offset, offset + maxPayload));
      offset += maxPayload;
    }
    return chunks;
  }

  private toBase64Url(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
    const chars = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_';
    let result = '';
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 3) {
      const b0 = bytes[i], b1 = bytes[i+1] ?? 0, b2 = bytes[i+2] ?? 0;
      result += chars[b0 >> 2];
      result += chars[((b0 & 3) << 4) | (b1 >> 4)];
      if (i+1 < bytes.length) result += chars[((b1 & 0xF) << 2) | (b2 >> 6)];
      if (i+2 < bytes.length) result += chars[b2 & 0x3F];
    }
    return result;
  }

  private fromBase64Url(str: string): Uint8Array {
    const chars = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789-_';
    const lookup = new Map(Array.from(chars).map((c, i) => [c, i]));
    const bytes: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i += 4) {
      const c0 = lookup.get(str[i]) ?? 0;
      const c1 = lookup.get(str[i+1]) ?? 0;
      const c2 = lookup.get(str[i+2]) ?? 0;
      const c3 = lookup.get(str[i+3]) ?? 0;
      bytes.push((c0 << 2) | (c1 >> 4));
      if (str[i+2]) bytes.push(((c1 & 0xF) << 4) | (c2 >> 2));
      if (str[i+3]) bytes.push(((c2 & 3) << 6) | c3);
    }
    return new Uint8Array(bytes);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// AUDIO FSK MODEM — Bell 202 + custom high-speed variant
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class AudioFSKModem {
  private readonly config: AirGapConfig;
  private readonly samplesPerBit: number;
  private readonly markSamples: Float64Array;
  private readonly spaceSamples: Float64Array;
  private readonly eccNsym: number;

  constructor(config: AirGapConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.eccNsym = ReedSolomon.nsymForLevel(config.errorCorrectionLevel);
    this.samplesPerBit = Math.floor(config.audioSampleRateHz / config.fskBaudRate);

    // Pre-compute mark and space tone waveforms for one bit period
    this.markSamples = this.generateTone(config.fskMarkFreqHz, this.samplesPerBit);
    this.spaceSamples = this.generateTone(config.fskSpaceFreqHz, this.samplesPerBit);
  }

  private generateTone(freqHz: number, numSamples: number): Float64Array {
    const samples = new Float64Array(numSamples);
    const phaseIncrement = 2 * Math.PI * freqHz / this.config.audioSampleRateHz;
    for (let i = 0; i < numSamples; i++) {
      samples[i] = Math.sin(phaseIncrement * i);
    }
    return samples;
  }

  /**
   * Modulate binary data into PCM audio samples (Float64, -1.0 to +1.0)
   * Includes: preamble, framing, HDLC-like bit stuffing, postamble
   */
  modulate(data: Uint8Array): Float64Array {
    const processed = this.applyECC(data);
    const bits = this.bytesToBits(processed);

    // Bit-stuffed bits with HDLC framing
    const framed = this.hdlcFrame(bits);

    // Preamble: 300ms of mark tone (carrier detect)
    const preambleBits = Math.floor(this.config.fskBaudRate * 0.3);
    const preamble = new Array(preambleBits).fill(1);

    // Postamble: 100ms
    const postambleBits = Math.floor(this.config.fskBaudRate * 0.1);
    const postamble = new Array(postambleBits).fill(1);

    const allBits = [...preamble, ...framed, ...postamble];
    const totalSamples = allBits.length * this.samplesPerBit;
    const pcm = new Float64Array(totalSamples);

    let sampleOffset = 0;
    for (const bit of allBits) {
      const tone = bit === 1 ? this.markSamples : this.spaceSamples;
      pcm.set(tone, sampleOffset);
      sampleOffset += this.samplesPerBit;
    }

    return pcm;
  }

  /**
   * Demodulate PCM audio samples back to binary data
   * Uses Goertzel algorithm for efficient single-frequency detection
   */
  demodulate(pcm: Float64Array): Uint8Array | null {
    const bits: number[] = [];
    const numBits = Math.floor(pcm.length / this.samplesPerBit);

    let inPreamble = true;
    let preambleMarkCount = 0;
    const preambleThreshold = Math.floor(this.config.fskBaudRate * 0.2);

    for (let i = 0; i < numBits; i++) {
      const sampleStart = i * this.samplesPerBit;
      const slice = pcm.slice(sampleStart, sampleStart + this.samplesPerBit);

      const markPower = this.goertzel(slice, this.config.fskMarkFreqHz);
      const spacePower = this.goertzel(slice, this.config.fskSpaceFreqHz);
      const bit = markPower > spacePower ? 1 : 0;

      if (inPreamble) {
        if (bit === 1) preambleMarkCount++;
        if (preambleMarkCount >= preambleThreshold) {
          inPreamble = false;
        }
      } else {
        bits.push(bit);
      }
    }

    if (bits.length === 0) return null;

    // HDLC unframe
    const unframed = this.hdlcUnframe(bits);
    if (!unframed) return null;

    // Remove ECC and correct errors
    try {
      const { data } = ReedSolomon.decode(new Uint8Array(unframed), this.eccNsym);
      return data;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Goertzel algorithm — efficient single-frequency DFT bin
   * More efficient than full FFT for detecting specific tones
   */
  private goertzel(samples: Float64Array, targetFreqHz: number): number {
    const k = Math.round(samples.length * targetFreqHz / this.config.audioSampleRateHz);
    const omega = 2 * Math.PI * k / samples.length;
    const coeff = 2 * Math.cos(omega);
    let s0 = 0, s1 = 0, s2 = 0;

    for (const sample of samples) {
      s0 = sample + coeff * s1 - s2;
      s2 = s1;
      s1 = s0;
    }

    return s1 * s1 + s2 * s2 - coeff * s1 * s2;
  }

  /**
   * HDLC-like framing with bit stuffing
   * Flag: 01111110 (0x7E)
   */
  private hdlcFrame(bits: number[]): number[] {
    const FLAG = [0,1,1,1,1,1,1,0];
    const stuffed: number[] = [];
    let consecutiveOnes = 0;

    for (const bit of bits) {
      stuffed.push(bit);
      if (bit === 1) {
        consecutiveOnes++;
        if (consecutiveOnes === 5) {
          stuffed.push(0); // Bit stuff
          consecutiveOnes = 0;
        }
      } else {
        consecutiveOnes = 0;
      }
    }

    return [...FLAG, ...stuffed, ...FLAG];
  }

  private hdlcUnframe(bits: number[]): number[] | null {
    const FLAG = [0,1,1,1,1,1,1,0];
    const flagStr = FLAG.join('');

    const bitStr = bits.join('');
    const firstFlag = bitStr.indexOf(flagStr);
    if (firstFlag === -1) return null;

    const afterFirst = firstFlag + FLAG.length;
    const secondFlag = bitStr.indexOf(flagStr, afterFirst);
    if (secondFlag === -1) return null;

    const payload = bits.slice(afterFirst, secondFlag);

    // Remove bit stuffing
    const unstuffed: number[] = [];
    let consecutiveOnes = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < payload.length; i++) {
      const bit = payload[i];
      if (bit === 1) {
        consecutiveOnes++;
        unstuffed.push(bit);
        if (consecutiveOnes === 5) {
          i++; // Skip stuffed zero
          consecutiveOnes = 0;
        }
      } else {
        consecutiveOnes = 0;
        unstuffed.push(bit);
      }
    }

    return unstuffed;
  }

  private bytesToBits(bytes: Uint8Array): number[] {
    const bits: number[] = [];
    for (const byte of bytes) {
      for (let i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
        bits.push((byte >> i) & 1);
      }
    }
    return bits;
  }

  private bitsToBytes(bits: number[]): Uint8Array {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(Math.floor(bits.length / 8));
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
      let byte = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
        byte = (byte << 1) | (bits[i * 8 + j] ?? 0);
      }
      bytes[i] = byte;
    }
    return bytes;
  }

  private applyECC(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    return ReedSolomon.encode(data, this.eccNsym);
  }

  /**
   * Generate dual-tone multi-frequency (DTMF) control signals
   * Used for out-of-band session negotiation
   */
  generateDTMF(digit: string): Float64Array {
    const dtmfFreqs: Record<string, [number, number]> = {
      '1': [697, 1209], '2': [697, 1336], '3': [697, 1477],
      '4': [770, 1209], '5': [770, 1336], '6': [770, 1477],
      '7': [852, 1209], '8': [852, 1336], '9': [852, 1477],
      '*': [941, 1209], '0': [941, 1336], '#': [941, 1477],
      'A': [697, 1633], 'B': [770, 1633], 'C': [852, 1633], 'D': [941, 1633],
    };

    const freqs = dtmfFreqs[digit] ?? [697, 1209];
    const durationMs = 100;
    const numSamples = Math.floor(this.config.audioSampleRateHz * durationMs / 1000);
    const samples = new Float64Array(numSamples);

    for (let i = 0; i < numSamples; i++) {
      const t = i / this.config.audioSampleRateHz;
      samples[i] = 0.5 * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * freqs[0] * t) +
                   0.5 * Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * freqs[1] * t);
    }

    return samples;
  }

  /**
   * Estimate channel capacity based on SNR measurement
   * Shannon-Hartley theorem: C = B * log2(1 + S/N)
   */
  estimateChannelCapacity(noisePower: number, signalPower: number): number {
    const snr = signalPower / Math.max(noisePower, 1e-10);
    const bandwidth = Math.abs(this.config.fskMarkFreqHz - this.config.fskSpaceFreqHz) * 2;
    return bandwidth * Math.log2(1 + snr); // bits per second
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// USB DATA DIODE — One-way flow enforcement
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class USBDataDiode {
  private readonly config: AirGapConfig;
  private readonly auditLog: Array<{
    timestamp: number;
    direction: 'sent' | 'blocked';
    sourceId: string;
    bytes: number;
    reason?: string;
  }>;
  private readonly eccNsym: number;
  private bytesTransferred: number;
  private bytesBlocked: number;

  constructor(config: AirGapConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.auditLog = [];
    this.eccNsym = ReedSolomon.nsymForLevel(config.errorCorrectionLevel);
    this.bytesTransferred = 0;
    this.bytesBlocked = 0;
  }

  /**
   * Enforce one-way flow: only outbound data passes
   * Any attempt to receive data on an outbound diode is blocked and logged
   */
  enforcePolicy(
    data: Uint8Array,
    direction: 'send' | 'receive',
    sourceId: string
  ): { allowed: boolean; data?: Uint8Array; reason?: string } {
    const timestamp = Date.now();

    // Hardware-level enforcement: outbound diode cannot receive
    if (this.config.flowDirection === 'outbound' && direction === 'receive') {
      this.auditLog.push({ timestamp, direction: 'blocked', sourceId, bytes: data.length,
        reason: 'DIODE_VIOLATION: receive attempted on outbound-only diode' });
      this.bytesBlocked += data.length;
      return { allowed: false, reason: 'Diode violation: outbound-only channel' };
    }

    if (this.config.flowDirection === 'inbound' && direction === 'send') {
      this.auditLog.push({ timestamp, direction: 'blocked', sourceId, bytes: data.length,
        reason: 'DIODE_VIOLATION: send attempted on inbound-only diode' });
      this.bytesBlocked += data.length;
      return { allowed: false, reason: 'Diode violation: inbound-only channel' };
    }

    // Policy enforcement
    if (!this.checkPolicy(sourceId)) {
      this.auditLog.push({ timestamp, direction: 'blocked', sourceId, bytes: data.length,
        reason: 'POLICY_VIOLATION: source not permitted' });
      this.bytesBlocked += data.length;
      return { allowed: false, reason: `Source ${sourceId} not permitted by diode policy` };
    }

    // Sanitize data — remove any hidden channel indicators
    const sanitized = this.sanitize(data);

    // Apply ECC for reliable one-way transmission
    const protected_ = ReedSolomon.encode(sanitized, this.eccNsym);

    this.auditLog.push({ timestamp, direction: 'sent', sourceId, bytes: protected_.length });
    this.bytesTransferred += protected_.length;

    return { allowed: true, data: protected_ };
  }

  /**
   * Receive and verify data from diode input
   */
  receive(data: Uint8Array): { data: Uint8Array | null; errorsFixed: number } {
    try {
      const { data: decoded, errorsFixed } = ReedSolomon.decode(data, this.eccNsym);
      return { data: decoded, errorsFixed };
    } catch {
      return { data: null, errorsFixed: 0 };
    }
  }

  private checkPolicy(sourceId: string): boolean {
    switch (this.config.diodePolicy) {
      case 'allow_all':
        return true;
      case 'whitelist':
        return this.config.allowedSourceIds.includes(sourceId);
      case 'blacklist':
        return !this.config.allowedSourceIds.includes(sourceId);
      case 'challenge_response':
        // In production: implement TOTP or HMAC challenge
        return this.config.allowedSourceIds.includes(sourceId);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Sanitize data to remove covert channel artifacts
   * Strips timing metadata, normalizes whitespace, removes steganographic markers
   */
  private sanitize(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    // Strip trailing zero bytes that could encode information
    let end = data.length;
    while (end > 0 && data[end - 1] === 0) end--;

    // Normalize: ensure no reserved UBE magic bytes in payload
    const sanitized = data.slice(0, end);
    for (let i = 0; i < sanitized.length - 1; i++) {
      // Replace any 0xAE47 magic that could be mistaken for frames
      if (sanitized[i] === 0xAE && sanitized[i+1] === 0x47) {
        sanitized[i] = 0xAE;
        sanitized[i+1] = 0x46; // Change to non-magic value
      }
    }

    return sanitized;
  }

  getAuditLog() { return [...this.auditLog]; }

  getStats() {
    return {
      bytesTransferred: this.bytesTransferred,
      bytesBlocked: this.bytesBlocked,
      totalEvents: this.auditLog.length,
      violations: this.auditLog.filter(e => e.reason?.includes('VIOLATION')).length,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// STEGANOGRAPHIC CHANNEL — LSB pixel embedding + DCT coefficient hiding
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class SteganographicChannel {
  private readonly config: AirGapConfig;
  private readonly eccNsym: number;

  constructor(config: AirGapConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.eccNsym = ReedSolomon.nsymForLevel(config.errorCorrectionLevel);
  }

  /**
   * Embed data into a carrier image using LSB steganography
   * Carrier is treated as raw RGBA pixel data (width * height * 4 bytes)
   *
   * Capacity: (width * height * 3 channels * 1 bit) / 8 bytes
   * For 1920x1080: ~777,600 bytes capacity
   */
  embedLSB(
    carrier: Uint8Array,
    secret: Uint8Array,
    imageWidth: number,
    imageHeight: number
  ): Uint8Array {
    const secretWithECC = ReedSolomon.encode(secret, this.eccNsym);

    // Header: 4 bytes for payload length
    const header = new Uint8Array(4);
    const hv = new DataView(header.buffer);
    hv.setUint32(0, secretWithECC.length, false);

    const payload = AirGapCrypto.concat(header, secretWithECC);
    const bits = this.bytesToBits(payload);

    const maxBits = imageWidth * imageHeight * 3; // R, G, B channels (not alpha)
    if (bits.length > maxBits) {
      throw new Error(`Payload too large: ${bits.length} bits, capacity ${maxBits} bits`);
    }

    const result = new Uint8Array(carrier);
    let bitIndex = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < result.length && bitIndex < bits.length; i++) {
      // Skip alpha channel (every 4th byte)
      if (i % 4 === 3) continue;

      // Replace LSB of pixel with message bit
      // Use pseudo-random permutation of pixel order for security
      const permutedIndex = this.permutedPixelIndex(i, imageWidth, imageHeight);
      result[permutedIndex] = (result[permutedIndex] & 0xFE) | bits[bitIndex];
      bitIndex++;
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Extract embedded data from steganographic image
   */
  extractLSB(
    stego: Uint8Array,
    imageWidth: number,
    imageHeight: number
  ): Uint8Array | null {
    const bits: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < stego.length; i++) {
      if (i % 4 === 3) continue; // Skip alpha
      const permutedIndex = this.permutedPixelIndex(i, imageWidth, imageHeight);
      bits.push(stego[permutedIndex] & 1);
    }

    if (bits.length < 32) return null;

    // Read header: payload length
    const headerBytes = this.bitsToBytes(bits.slice(0, 32));
    const hv = new DataView(headerBytes.buffer);
    const payloadLength = hv.getUint32(0, false);

    if (payloadLength === 0 || payloadLength > bits.length / 8) return null;

    const payloadBits = bits.slice(32, 32 + payloadLength * 8);
    const payload = this.bitsToBytes(payloadBits);

    try {
      const { data } = ReedSolomon.decode(payload, this.eccNsym);
      return data;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * DCT-domain steganography (JPEG-compatible)
   * Embeds data in mid-frequency DCT coefficients of 8x8 blocks
   * More robust to JPEG re-compression than LSB
   */
  embedDCT(
    dctCoefficients: Float64Array,
    secret: Uint8Array
  ): Float64Array {
    const secretWithECC = ReedSolomon.encode(secret, this.eccNsym);
    const bits = this.bytesToBits(secretWithECC);

    // Mid-frequency DCT coefficient indices (avoid DC and high-freq)
    const midFreqCoeffs = [5, 6, 9, 10, 14, 15, 20, 21]; // zigzag positions 5-21

    const result = new Float64Array(dctCoefficients);
    let bitIndex = 0;

    for (let block = 0; block < result.length / 64 && bitIndex < bits.length; block++) {
      const blockOffset = block * 64;
      for (const coeffIdx of midFreqCoeffs) {
        if (bitIndex >= bits.length) break;

        const coeff = result[blockOffset + coeffIdx];
        // Quantize to nearest even/odd based on bit value
        const quantized = Math.round(coeff);
        const parity = quantized % 2;
        const targetParity = bits[bitIndex];

        if (parity !== targetParity) {
          result[blockOffset + coeffIdx] = coeff + (targetParity === 1 ? 1 : -1) * 0.5;
        }
        bitIndex++;
      }
    }

    return result;
  }

  extractDCT(dctCoefficients: Float64Array): Uint8Array | null {
    const midFreqCoeffs = [5, 6, 9, 10, 14, 15, 20, 21];
    const bits: number[] = [];

    for (let block = 0; block < dctCoefficients.length / 64; block++) {
      const blockOffset = block * 64;
      for (const coeffIdx of midFreqCoeffs) {
        const coeff = Math.round(dctCoefficients[blockOffset + coeffIdx]);
        bits.push(Math.abs(coeff) % 2);
      }
    }

    if (bits.length < 8) return null;
    const data = this.bitsToBytes(bits);

    try {
      const { data: decoded } = ReedSolomon.decode(data, this.eccNsym);
      return decoded;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Generate a printable steganographic code
   * Encodes data as variations in character spacing / micro-typography
   * Readable by humans, decodable by software
   */
  generatePrintableCode(
    secret: Uint8Array,
    coverText: string
  ): { text: string; metadata: string } {
    const secretWithECC = ReedSolomon.encode(secret, this.eccNsym);
    const bits = this.bytesToBits(secretWithECC);

    // Unicode variation selectors for zero-width embedding
    // VS1 (U+FE00) = bit 0, VS2 (U+FE01) = bit 1
    const VS0 = '\uFE00';
    const VS1 = '\uFE01';

    const words = coverText.split(' ');
    let result = '';
    let bitIndex = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < words.length; i++) {
      result += words[i];
      if (bitIndex < bits.length) {
        // Embed bit as variation selector after each word
        result += bits[bitIndex] === 1 ? VS1 : VS0;
        bitIndex++;
      }
      if (i < words.length - 1) result += ' ';
    }

    const metadata = AirGapCrypto.toHex(AirGapCrypto.sha256(
      new TextEncoder().encode(coverText)
    )).slice(0, 16);

    return { text: result, metadata };
  }

  extractPrintableCode(stegoText: string): Uint8Array | null {
    const VS0 = '\uFE00';
    const VS1 = '\uFE01';
    const bits: number[] = [];

    for (const char of stegoText) {
      if (char === VS0) bits.push(0);
      else if (char === VS1) bits.push(1);
    }

    if (bits.length === 0) return null;
    const data = this.bitsToBytes(bits);

    try {
      const { data: decoded } = ReedSolomon.decode(data, this.eccNsym);
      return decoded;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private permutedPixelIndex(linearIndex: number, width: number, _height: number): number {
    // Pseudo-random permutation using LCG — in production use keyed shuffle
    const a = 1664525, c = 1013904223, m = 4294967296;
    return ((linearIndex * a + c) % m) % (width * _height * 4);
  }

  private bytesToBits(bytes: Uint8Array): number[] {
    const bits: number[] = [];
    for (const byte of bytes) {
      for (let i = 7; i >= 0; i--) bits.push((byte >> i) & 1);
    }
    return bits;
  }

  private bitsToBytes(bits: number[]): Uint8Array {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(Math.floor(bits.length / 8));
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
      let byte = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) byte = (byte << 1) | (bits[i*8+j] ?? 0);
      bytes[i] = byte;
    }
    return bytes;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// OPTICAL LED CHANNEL — IR LED / Laser pulse encoding
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class OpticalLEDChannel {
  private readonly config: AirGapConfig;
  private readonly eccNsym: number;
  private readonly bitsPerSecond: number;

  constructor(config: AirGapConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.eccNsym = ReedSolomon.nsymForLevel(config.errorCorrectionLevel);
    this.bitsPerSecond = Math.floor(1_000_000 / config.ledPulseWidthMicros);
  }

  /**
   * Encode data as Manchester-coded LED pulse sequence
   * Manchester: 0 = HIGH→LOW transition, 1 = LOW→HIGH transition
   * Self-clocking — no separate clock line required
   */
  encodeManchesterPulses(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    const withECC = ReedSolomon.encode(data, this.eccNsym);

    // Add preamble: 8 alternating transitions for clock recovery
    const preambleBits = [1,0,1,0,1,0,1,0];
    // Start delimiter: 11000000
    const startDelimiter = [1,1,0,0,0,0,0,0];

    const rawBits = this.bytesToBits(withECC);
    const allBits = [...preambleBits, ...startDelimiter, ...rawBits];

    // Manchester encoding: each bit becomes 2 half-bit periods
    const manchesterBits: number[] = [];
    for (const bit of allBits) {
      if (bit === 1) {
        manchesterBits.push(0, 1); // LOW then HIGH
      } else {
        manchesterBits.push(1, 0); // HIGH then LOW
      }
    }

    // Pack into bytes (each byte = 8 LED states)
    const result = new Uint8Array(Math.ceil(manchesterBits.length / 8));
    for (let i = 0; i < manchesterBits.length; i++) {
      if (manchesterBits[i]) {
        result[Math.floor(i / 8)] |= (1 << (7 - (i % 8)));
      }
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Decode Manchester-coded pulse sequence
   */
  decodeManchesterPulses(pulses: Uint8Array): Uint8Array | null {
    const bits: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < pulses.length * 8; i++) {
      const byteIdx = Math.floor(i / 8);
      const bitIdx = 7 - (i % 8);
      bits.push((pulses[byteIdx] >> bitIdx) & 1);
    }

    // Decode Manchester pairs
    const decoded: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < bits.length - 1; i += 2) {
      const first = bits[i];
      const second = bits[i + 1];
      if (first === 0 && second === 1) decoded.push(1);
      else if (first === 1 && second === 0) decoded.push(0);
      // else: violation — could be delimiter
    }

    // Find start delimiter and extract payload
    const delimStr = '11000000';
    const bitStr = decoded.join('');
    const delimPos = bitStr.indexOf(delimStr);
    if (delimPos === -1) return null;

    const payloadBits = decoded.slice(delimPos + 8);
    const payload = this.bitsToBytes(payloadBits);

    try {
      const { data } = ReedSolomon.decode(payload, this.eccNsym);
      return data;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * 4B5B line coding — 4 bits encoded as 5 bits, no more than 3 consecutive zeros
   * Used for DC balance and clock synchronization
   */
  encode4B5B(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    const TABLE_4B5B: Record<number, number> = {
      0x0: 0b11110, 0x1: 0b01001, 0x2: 0b10100, 0x3: 0b10101,
      0x4: 0b01010, 0x5: 0b01011, 0x6: 0b01110, 0x7: 0b01111,
      0x8: 0b10010, 0x9: 0b10011, 0xA: 0b10110, 0xB: 0b10111,
      0xC: 0b11010, 0xD: 0b11011, 0xE: 0b11100, 0xF: 0b11101,
    };

    const withECC = ReedSolomon.encode(data, this.eccNsym);
    const encoded5Bits: number[] = [];

    for (const byte of withECC) {
      const hi = (byte >> 4) & 0xF;
      const lo = byte & 0xF;
      encoded5Bits.push(TABLE_4B5B[hi], TABLE_4B5B[lo]);
    }

    // Pack 5-bit symbols into bytes
    const totalBits = encoded5Bits.length * 5;
    const result = new Uint8Array(Math.ceil(totalBits / 8));
    let bitPos = 0;
    for (const sym of encoded5Bits) {
      for (let i = 4; i >= 0; i--) {
        const bit = (sym >> i) & 1;
        if (bit) result[Math.floor(bitPos / 8)] |= (1 << (7 - (bitPos % 8)));
        bitPos++;
      }
    }

    return result;
  }

  decode4B5B(encoded: Uint8Array): Uint8Array | null {
    const TABLE_5B4B: Record<number, number> = {
      0b11110: 0x0, 0b01001: 0x1, 0b10100: 0x2, 0b10101: 0x3,
      0b01010: 0x4, 0b01011: 0x5, 0b01110: 0x6, 0b01111: 0x7,
      0b10010: 0x8, 0b10011: 0x9, 0b10110: 0xA, 0b10111: 0xB,
      0b11010: 0xC, 0b11011: 0xD, 0b11100: 0xE, 0b11101: 0xF,
    };

    // Unpack bits
    const bits: number[] = [];
    for (const byte of encoded) {
      for (let i = 7; i >= 0; i--) bits.push((byte >> i) & 1);
    }

    // Extract 5-bit symbols
    const nibbles: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i + 4 < bits.length; i += 5) {
      const sym = (bits[i] << 4) | (bits[i+1] << 3) | (bits[i+2] << 2) | (bits[i+3] << 1) | bits[i+4];
      const nibble = TABLE_5B4B[sym];
      if (nibble === undefined) continue;
      nibbles.push(nibble);
    }

    // Pair nibbles into bytes
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(Math.floor(nibbles.length / 2));
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
      bytes[i] = (nibbles[i*2] << 4) | nibbles[i*2+1];
    }

    try {
      const { data } = ReedSolomon.decode(bytes, this.eccNsym);
      return data;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Generate IR carrier-modulated pulse sequence
   * Carrier frequency modulation: 38kHz carrier burst = '1', silence = '0'
   * Compatible with common IR receiver modules (TSOP series)
   */
  generateIRCarrier(data: Uint8Array): Float64Array {
    const manchester = this.encodeManchesterPulses(data);
    const bits = this.bytesToBits(manchester);

    const samplesPerBit = Math.floor(this.config.audioSampleRateHz / this.bitsPerSecond);
    const carrierSamplesPerCycle = Math.floor(
      this.config.audioSampleRateHz / this.config.ledCarrierFreqHz
    );

    const totalSamples = bits.length * samplesPerBit;
    const pcm = new Float64Array(totalSamples);

    for (let bitIdx = 0; bitIdx < bits.length; bitIdx++) {
      const sampleStart = bitIdx * samplesPerBit;
      if (bits[bitIdx] === 1) {
        // Burst at carrier frequency
        for (let s = 0; s < samplesPerBit; s++) {
          const phase = (s % carrierSamplesPerCycle) / carrierSamplesPerCycle;
          pcm[sampleStart + s] = Math.sin(2 * Math.PI * phase);
        }
      }
      // else: silence (zeros already)
    }

    return pcm;
  }

  /**
   * Atmospheric turbulence compensation for long-range FSO links
   * Uses adaptive modulation: reduce data rate when bit error rate is high
   */
  adaptModulation(currentBER: number): {
    recommendedBaudRate: number;
    recommendedCodingRate: number;
    recommendedModulation: string;
  } {
    if (currentBER < 1e-6) {
      return { recommendedBaudRate: this.bitsPerSecond, recommendedCodingRate: 1.0, recommendedModulation: 'OOK-4B5B' };
    } else if (currentBER < 1e-4) {
      return { recommendedBaudRate: Math.floor(this.bitsPerSecond * 0.75), recommendedCodingRate: 0.8, recommendedModulation: 'Manchester' };
    } else if (currentBER < 1e-2) {
      return { recommendedBaudRate: Math.floor(this.bitsPerSecond * 0.5), recommendedCodingRate: 0.5, recommendedModulation: 'Manchester-RS' };
    } else {
      return { recommendedBaudRate: Math.floor(this.bitsPerSecond * 0.25), recommendedCodingRate: 0.25, recommendedModulation: 'Slow-Manchester-RS-Max' };
    }
  }

  get nominalBitsPerSecond(): number { return this.bitsPerSecond; }

  private bytesToBits(bytes: Uint8Array): number[] {
    const bits: number[] = [];
    for (const byte of bytes) {
      for (let i = 7; i >= 0; i--) bits.push((byte >> i) & 1);
    }
    return bits;
  }

  private bitsToBytes(bits: number[]): Uint8Array {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(Math.floor(bits.length / 8));
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
      let byte = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) byte = (byte << 1) | (bits[i*8+j] ?? 0);
      bytes[i] = byte;
    }
    return bytes;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// SESSION MANAGER — Multi-channel coordination
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class AirGapSessionManager {
  private readonly sessions: Map<string, AirGapSession>;
  private readonly config: AirGapConfig;
  private readonly sessionTimeoutMs: number;

  constructor(config: AirGapConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.sessions = new Map();
    this.sessionTimeoutMs = config.sessionTimeoutMs;
  }

  createSession(
    channel: ChannelType,
    direction: FlowDirection,
    totalFrames: number,
    metadata: Record<string, string> = {}
  ): AirGapSession {
    const session: AirGapSession = {
      sessionId: AirGapCrypto.randomId(),
      direction,
      channel,
      totalFrames,
      receivedFrames: new Map(),
      startedAt: Date.now(),
      lastActivityAt: Date.now(),
      chainHead: '0'.repeat(64),
      complete: false,
      metadata,
    };
    this.sessions.set(session.sessionId, session);
    return session;
  }

  receiveFrame(frame: AirGapFrame): {
    session: AirGapSession;
    complete: boolean;
    data?: Uint8Array;
  } {
    let session = this.sessions.get(frame.sessionId);
    if (!session) {
      session = this.createSession(
        this.config.channelType,
        this.config.flowDirection,
        frame.totalFrames
      );
      session.sessionId = frame.sessionId;
      this.sessions.set(frame.sessionId, session);
    }

    session.receivedFrames.set(frame.frameIndex, frame);
    session.lastActivityAt = Date.now();

    if (session.receivedFrames.size === session.totalFrames) {
      session.complete = true;

      // Reassemble
      const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < session.totalFrames; i++) {
        const f = session.receivedFrames.get(i);
        if (f) chunks.push(f.payload);
      }

      const data = AirGapCrypto.concat(...chunks);
      return { session, complete: true, data };
    }

    return { session, complete: false };
  }

  cleanupExpiredSessions(): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    let cleaned = 0;
    for (const [id, session] of this.sessions) {
      if (now - session.lastActivityAt > this.sessionTimeoutMs) {
        this.sessions.delete(id);
        cleaned++;
      }
    }
    return cleaned;
  }

  getSession(sessionId: string): AirGapSession | null {
    return this.sessions.get(sessionId) ?? null;
  }

  getMissingFrames(sessionId: string): number[] {
    const session = this.sessions.get(sessionId);
    if (!session) return [];
    const missing: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < session.totalFrames; i++) {
      if (!session.receivedFrames.has(i)) missing.push(i);
    }
    return missing;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// AIR-GAP BRIDGE — Unified multi-channel orchestrator
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class AirGapBridge {
  private readonly config: AirGapConfig;
  private readonly qrChannel: QRChannel;
  private readonly audioModem: AudioFSKModem;
  private readonly usbDiode: USBDataDiode;
  private readonly stegoChannel: SteganographicChannel;
  private readonly opticalChannel: OpticalLEDChannel;
  private readonly sessionManager: AirGapSessionManager;

  // Statistics
  private stats = {
    transmissions: 0,
    receptions: 0,
    bytesTransmitted: 0,
    bytesReceived: 0,
    eccCorrections: 0,
    integrityFailures: 0,
    diodeViolations: 0,
  };

  constructor(config: Partial<AirGapConfig> = {}) {
    this.config = { ...DEFAULT_AIRGAP_CONFIG, ...config };
    this.qrChannel = new QRChannel(this.config);
    this.audioModem = new AudioFSKModem(this.config);
    this.usbDiode = new USBDataDiode(this.config);
    this.stegoChannel = new SteganographicChannel(this.config);
    this.opticalChannel = new OpticalLEDChannel(this.config);
    this.sessionManager = new AirGapSessionManager(this.config);
  }

  // ─── QR CHANNEL ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  encodeQR(data: Uint8Array): string[] {
    this.stats.transmissions++;
    this.stats.bytesTransmitted += data.length;
    return this.qrChannel.encodeToQRPayloads(data);
  }

  decodeQR(payloads: string[]): ReceptionResult {
    const result = this.qrChannel.decodeFromQRPayloads(payloads);
    this.stats.receptions++;
    if (result.data) this.stats.bytesReceived += result.data.length;
    this.stats.eccCorrections += result.errorsCorrected;
    if (!result.integrityVerified) this.stats.integrityFailures++;
    return result;
  }

  // ─── AUDIO FSK CHANNEL ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  modulateAudio(data: Uint8Array): Float64Array {
    this.stats.transmissions++;
    this.stats.bytesTransmitted += data.length;
    return this.audioModem.modulate(data);
  }

  demodulateAudio(pcm: Float64Array): Uint8Array | null {
    this.stats.receptions++;
    const result = this.audioModem.demodulate(pcm);
    if (result) this.stats.bytesReceived += result.length;
    return result;
  }

  // ─── USB DIODE ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  sendViaDiode(data: Uint8Array, sourceId: string): {
    allowed: boolean;
    data?: Uint8Array;
    reason?: string;
  } {
    const result = this.usbDiode.enforcePolicy(data, 'send', sourceId);
    if (!result.allowed) this.stats.diodeViolations++;
    else {
      this.stats.transmissions++;
      this.stats.bytesTransmitted += data.length;
    }
    return result;
  }

  receiveViaDiode(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array | null {
    const { data: decoded, errorsFixed } = this.usbDiode.receive(data);
    if (errorsFixed > 0) this.stats.eccCorrections += errorsFixed;
    if (decoded) {
      this.stats.receptions++;
      this.stats.bytesReceived += decoded.length;
    }
    return decoded;
  }

  // ─── STEGANOGRAPHIC CHANNEL ───────────────────────────────────────────────

  embedInImage(secret: Uint8Array, carrier: Uint8Array, w: number, h: number): Uint8Array {
    this.stats.transmissions++;
    this.stats.bytesTransmitted += secret.length;
    return this.stegoChannel.embedLSB(carrier, secret, w, h);
  }

  extractFromImage(stego: Uint8Array, w: number, h: number): Uint8Array | null {
    const result = this.stegoChannel.extractLSB(stego, w, h);
    if (result) { this.stats.receptions++; this.stats.bytesReceived += result.length; }
    return result;
  }

  embedInText(secret: Uint8Array, coverText: string): { text: string; metadata: string } {
    this.stats.transmissions++;
    return this.stegoChannel.generatePrintableCode(secret, coverText);
  }

  extractFromText(stegoText: string): Uint8Array | null {
    return this.stegoChannel.extractPrintableCode(stegoText);
  }

  // ─── OPTICAL LED CHANNEL ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  encodeOptical(data: Uint8Array, encoding: 'manchester' | '4b5b' | 'ir_carrier' = 'manchester'): Uint8Array | Float64Array {
    this.stats.transmissions++;
    this.stats.bytesTransmitted += data.length;
    switch (encoding) {
      case 'manchester': return this.opticalChannel.encodeManchesterPulses(data);
      case '4b5b':       return this.opticalChannel.encode4B5B(data);
      case 'ir_carrier': return this.opticalChannel.generateIRCarrier(data);
    }
  }

  decodeOptical(data: Uint8Array, encoding: 'manchester' | '4b5b' = 'manchester'): Uint8Array | null {
    this.stats.receptions++;
    let result: Uint8Array | null;
    switch (encoding) {
      case 'manchester': result = this.opticalChannel.decodeManchesterPulses(data); break;
      case '4b5b':       result = this.opticalChannel.decode4B5B(data); break;
    }
    if (result) this.stats.bytesReceived += result.length;
    return result;
  }

  // ─── ADAPTIVE CHANNEL SELECTION ───────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Auto-select best available air-gap channel given constraints
   */
  selectChannel(constraints: {
    rfForbidden?: boolean;
    audioForbidden?: boolean;
    visualForbidden?: boolean;
    maxLatencyMs?: number;
    payloadBytes: number;
  }): ChannelType {
    if (!constraints.rfForbidden && !constraints.audioForbidden && !constraints.visualForbidden) {
      // Prefer QR: highest throughput, zero emissions
      return 'qr';
    }
    if (!constraints.visualForbidden) {
      return constraints.payloadBytes > 50000 ? 'optical_led' : 'qr';
    }
    if (!constraints.audioForbidden) {
      return 'audio';
    }
    if (!constraints.rfForbidden) {
      // No RF on air-gap channels but allow USB diode
      return 'usb_diode';
    }
    // Last resort: steganographic
    return 'steganographic';
  }

  // ─── STATUS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getStats() { return { ...this.stats }; }

  getDiodeAuditLog() { return this.usbDiode.getAuditLog(); }

  getChannelCapacities(): Record<ChannelType, string> {
    return {
      qr: `~${QR_CAPACITY_BYTES[this.config.qrVersion] ?? 200} bytes/frame`,
      audio: `${this.audioModem.estimateChannelCapacity(1, 100).toFixed(0)} bps (est)`,
      usb_diode: `~480 Mbps raw (USB 2.0 limited)`,
      steganographic: `~${Math.floor(1920 * 1080 * 3 / 8)} bytes/1080p frame (LSB)`,
      optical_led: `${this.opticalChannel.nominalBitsPerSecond} bps nominal`,
    };
  }

  cleanupSessions(): number {
    return this.sessionManager.cleanupExpiredSessions();
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// FACTORY & EXPORT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createAirGapBridge(config: Partial<AirGapConfig> = {}): AirGapBridge {
  return new AirGapBridge(config);
}

export {
  AirGapCrypto,
  ReedSolomon,
  FrameCodec,
};

export default AirGapBridge;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — File 15: cognitiveRadio.ts
 * Cognitive Radio Engine — Dynamic Spectrum Sensing & Adaptive Access
 *
 * The mesh uses whatever spectrum is available at any given moment, adapting
 * in real time to interference, jammers, incumbent users, and regulatory
 * environment — making UBE nodes impossible to permanently silence through
 * any single-frequency attack.
 *
 * Capabilities:
 *  - Wideband energy detection scan: 10 MHz – 6 GHz
 *  - Primary user detection and protection (licensed spectrum avoidance)
 *  - Cooperative spectrum sensing across multiple mesh nodes (fusion center)
 *  - Optimal channel selection: multi-armed bandit (UCB1) over spectrum bands
 *  - Adaptive modulation and coding (MCS) based on channel conditions
 *  - Graceful spectrum handoff — seamless migration when channel unavailable
 *  - Offline regulatory spectrum database (by region/country)
 *  - Cyclostationary feature detection (identifies modulation type of incumbents)
 *  - Distributed channel allocation across mesh
 *  - Integration with jammingDefense.ts for jammer-aware channel selection
 *
 * @module @kingube/sdk/phase11/cognitiveRadio
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'crypto';

// ─── Constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const MIN_FREQ_HZ = 10e6;          // 10 MHz
const MAX_FREQ_HZ = 6e9;           // 6 GHz
const NOISE_FLOOR_DBM = -174;      // Thermal noise floor dBm/Hz (kTB at 290K)
const SPEED_OF_LIGHT = 2.998e8;
const UCB1_EXPLORATION_CONST = Math.sqrt(2);
const SENSING_FRAME_MS = 10;       // Energy detection integration time
const CYCLO_FFT_SIZE = 4096;
const MAX_CHANNELS_PER_BAND = 200;
const HANDOFF_HYSTERESIS_DB = 3;   // dB margin before triggering handoff
const COOPERATIVE_THRESHOLD = 0.6; // Fraction of nodes that must agree on occupancy
const PATHLOSS_EXPONENT = 3.5;     // Urban environment
const CSMA_BACKOFF_SLOTS = 16;
const CHANNEL_HISTORY_DEPTH = 1000;

// ─── Enumerations ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export enum ChannelState {
  AVAILABLE = 'AVAILABLE',
  OCCUPIED_PRIMARY = 'OCCUPIED_PRIMARY',
  OCCUPIED_SECONDARY = 'OCCUPIED_SECONDARY',
  JAMMED = 'JAMMED',
  UNKNOWN = 'UNKNOWN',
  RESERVED = 'RESERVED',
  HANDOFF = 'HANDOFF',
}

export enum ModulationScheme {
  BPSK = 'BPSK',
  QPSK = 'QPSK',
  QAM16 = 'QAM16',
  QAM64 = 'QAM64',
  QAM256 = 'QAM256',
  OFDM_BPSK = 'OFDM_BPSK',
  OFDM_QPSK = 'OFDM_QPSK',
  OFDM_QAM16 = 'OFDM_QAM16',
  OFDM_QAM64 = 'OFDM_QAM64',
  DSSS_BPSK = 'DSSS_BPSK',
  FHSS_GFSK = 'FHSS_GFSK',
}

export enum PrimaryUserType {
  CELLULAR = 'CELLULAR',
  RADAR = 'RADAR',
  BROADCAST_TV = 'BROADCAST_TV',
  GNSS = 'GNSS',
  MILITARY = 'MILITARY',
  AVIATION = 'AVIATION',
  MARITIME = 'MARITIME',
  NAVY_RADAR = 'NAVY_RADAR',
  UNKNOWN = 'UNKNOWN',
}

export enum SensingMethod {
  ENERGY_DETECTION = 'ENERGY_DETECTION',
  CYCLOSTATIONARY = 'CYCLOSTATIONARY',
  MATCHED_FILTER = 'MATCHED_FILTER',
  COOPERATIVE = 'COOPERATIVE',
  WAVEFORM_BASED = 'WAVEFORM_BASED',
}

export enum HandoffTrigger {
  PRIMARY_USER_DETECTED = 'PRIMARY_USER_DETECTED',
  JAMMER_DETECTED = 'JAMMER_DETECTED',
  QUALITY_DEGRADED = 'QUALITY_DEGRADED',
  BETTER_CHANNEL_FOUND = 'BETTER_CHANNEL_FOUND',
  REGULATORY_VIOLATION = 'REGULATORY_VIOLATION',
  SCHEDULED = 'SCHEDULED',
}

// ─── Interfaces ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface SpectrumBand {
  name: string;
  startHz: number;
  endHz: number;
  maxTxDbm: number;
  licensed: boolean;
  primaryUser: string | null;
  region: string;
  duplexMode: 'TDD' | 'FDD' | 'SIMPLEX' | 'AM';
  channelBwHz: number;
}

export interface Channel {
  id: string;
  bandName: string;
  centerFreqHz: number;
  bandwidthHz: number;
  state: ChannelState;
  rssiDbm: number;
  noiseFloorDbm: number;
  snrDb: number;
  occupancyRatio: number;      // 0.0 – 1.0
  lastSensedAt: number;
  primaryUserDetected: boolean;
  primaryUserType: PrimaryUserType | null;
  jammerPresent: boolean;
  cycloFeatures: CyclostationaryFeatures | null;
  usable: boolean;
  maxDataRateMbps: number;
  propagationLossDb: number;
}

export interface CyclostationaryFeatures {
  // Spectral correlation function peak
  scfPeakDb: number;
  // Carrier frequency estimate (from SCF analysis)
  estimatedCarrierHz: number;
  // Modulation classification
  detectedModulation: ModulationScheme | null;
  // Cyclic frequencies detected (alpha values)
  cyclicFrequencies: number[];
  // Confidence of modulation detection (0–1)
  confidence: number;
  // Primary user identification
  likelyUser: PrimaryUserType;
}

export interface SensingReport {
  nodeId: string;
  channelId: string;
  method: SensingMethod;
  rssiDbm: number;
  occupied: boolean;
  confidence: number;
  timestamp: number;
  cycloFeatures?: CyclostationaryFeatures;
}

export interface MCSEntry {
  scheme: ModulationScheme;
  codeRate: number;          // e.g. 3/4
  minSnrDb: number;          // Minimum required SNR
  bitsPerSymbol: number;
  spectralEfficiencyBpHz: number;
  dataRateMbps: number;      // At reference bandwidth of 10 MHz
}

export interface ChannelAllocation {
  nodeId: string;
  channelId: string;
  centerFreqHz: number;
  mcs: MCSEntry;
  txPowerDbm: number;
  startTime: number;
  durationMs: number;
  priority: number;          // 0–100, higher = more critical
}

export interface BanditArm {
  channelId: string;
  pulls: number;
  totalReward: number;
  meanReward: number;
  ucb1Value: number;
  lastUpdated: number;
}

export interface SpectrumMap {
  timestamp: number;
  region: string;
  channels: Map<string, Channel>;
  occupancyByBand: Map<string, number>;  // band name -> avg occupancy
  availableChannels: Channel[];
  bestChannel: Channel | null;
}

export interface HandoffPlan {
  currentChannelId: string;
  targetChannelId: string;
  trigger: HandoffTrigger;
  estimatedDowntimeMs: number;
  fallbackChannelId: string | null;
  mcs: MCSEntry;
  txPowerDbm: number;
}

export interface CooperativeSensingResult {
  channelId: string;
  participatingNodes: string[];
  reports: SensingReport[];
  fusedDecision: boolean;       // true = occupied
  confidence: number;
  detectionMethod: 'OR_RULE' | 'AND_RULE' | 'MAJORITY_VOTE' | 'WEIGHTED';
  primaryUserType: PrimaryUserType | null;
}

export interface CognitiveRadioConfig {
  nodeId: string;
  region: string;
  minFreqHz: number;
  maxFreqHz: number;
  sensingBandwidthHz: number;
  sensingDwellMs: number;
  energyDetectionThresholdDbm: number;
  primaryUserProtectionDb: number;  // Extra margin above noise floor
  cooperativeSensingEnabled: boolean;
  cooperativeMinNodes: number;
  cyclostationaryEnabled: boolean;
  banditExplorationConst: number;
  maxTxPowerDbm: number;
  handoffHysteresisDb: number;
  scanIntervalMs: number;
  channelHistoryDepth: number;
  jammingDefenseIntegration: boolean;
  enableDfs: boolean;           // Dynamic Frequency Selection
  enableTpc: boolean;           // Transmit Power Control
  spectralMaskCompliance: boolean;
}
