// ─── Energy Detector ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class EnergyDetector {
  private readonly thresholdDbm: number;
  private readonly dwellMs: number;
  private readonly noiseFloorDbm: number;

  constructor(thresholdDbm: number, dwellMs: number, noiseFigureDb: number = 6) {
    this.thresholdDbm = thresholdDbm;
    this.dwellMs = dwellMs;
    // Noise floor = kTB + noise figure + 10*log10(BW)
    // Reference BW = 1 Hz, actual added by caller
    this.noiseFloorDbm = NOISE_FLOOR_DBM + noiseFigureDb;
  }

  /**
   * Radiometer decision: compare averaged power to threshold
   * Uses Neyman-Pearson criterion for fixed false-alarm rate
   */
  detect(
    rssiDbm: number,
    bandwidthHz: number,
    falseAlarmProb: number = 0.01
  ): { occupied: boolean; confidence: number; snrDb: number } {
    // Noise power in band: N = kTB = -174 dBm/Hz + 10*log10(BW)
    const noisePowerDbm = this.noiseFloorDbm + 10 * Math.log10(bandwidthHz);

    // Threshold from Neyman-Pearson: λ = N * (1 + Q^-1(P_fa) / sqrt(N * T * BW))
    const timeBandwidthProduct = (this.dwellMs / 1000) * bandwidthHz;
    const qInvPfa = Math.sqrt(2) * this.erfcInv(2 * falseAlarmProb);
    const thresholdLinear = 1 + qInvPfa / Math.sqrt(timeBandwidthProduct);
    const thresholdDbm = noisePowerDbm + 10 * Math.log10(thresholdLinear);

    const snrDb = rssiDbm - noisePowerDbm;
    const occupied = rssiDbm > thresholdDbm;

    // Confidence based on excess above threshold
    const margin = rssiDbm - thresholdDbm;
    const confidence = occupied
      ? Math.min(0.5 + 0.5 * (1 - Math.exp(-margin / 5)), 0.99)
      : Math.min(0.5 + 0.5 * Math.exp(margin / 5), 0.99);

    return { occupied, confidence, snrDb };
  }

  /**
   * Approximate inverse erfc for threshold calculation
   */
  private erfcInv(p: number): number {
    if (p <= 0 || p >= 2) return p <= 0 ? Infinity : -Infinity;
    const q = p < 1 ? p : 2 - p;
    const t = Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(q / 2));
    const x = t - (2.515517 + 0.802853 * t + 0.010328 * t * t) /
      (1 + 1.432788 * t + 0.189269 * t * t + 0.001308 * t * t * t);
    return p < 1 ? x : -x;
  }

  noiseFloor(bandwidthHz: number): number {
    return this.noiseFloorDbm + 10 * Math.log10(bandwidthHz);
  }
}

// ─── Cyclostationary Feature Detector ────────────────────────────────────────

class CyclostationaryDetector {
  private readonly fftSize: number;

  constructor(fftSize: number = CYCLO_FFT_SIZE) {
    this.fftSize = fftSize;
  }

  /**
   * Simplified Spectral Correlation Function (SCF) analysis
   * Full implementation: conjugate product of frequency-shifted signal copies
   *
   * SCF(f, α) = E[X(f + α/2) · X*(f - α/2)]
   * Peak at cyclic frequency α = symbol rate or carrier offset
   */
  analyze(
    signal: Float32Array,
    sampleRateHz: number,
    centerFreqHz: number
  ): CyclostationaryFeatures {
    // Compute magnitude spectrum via DFT
    const spectrum = this.computeMagnitudeSpectrum(signal);

    // Search for spectral correlation peaks at different lag offsets
    const cyclicFrequencies: number[] = [];
    let maxScfPeak = 0;
    let dominantAlpha = 0;

    // Sweep alpha (cyclic frequency) over symbol rate candidates
    for (let alphaIdx = 1; alphaIdx < Math.min(spectrum.length / 2, 100); alphaIdx++) {
      const alpha = alphaIdx * sampleRateHz / this.fftSize;
      let scfPower = 0;

      for (let f = 0; f < spectrum.length / 2; f++) {
        const fPlus = f + alphaIdx;
        const fMinus = f - alphaIdx;
        if (fPlus < spectrum.length && fMinus >= 0) {
          scfPower += spectrum[fPlus] * spectrum[fMinus];
        }
      }

      scfPower /= spectrum.length;

      if (scfPower > maxScfPeak * 0.5) {
        cyclicFrequencies.push(alpha);
      }
      if (scfPower > maxScfPeak) {
        maxScfPeak = scfPower;
        dominantAlpha = alpha;
      }
    }

    // Estimate carrier from dominant spectral peak
    let maxIdx = 0;
    for (let i = 1; i < spectrum.length; i++) {
      if (spectrum[i] > spectrum[maxIdx]) maxIdx = i;
    }
    const estimatedCarrierHz = centerFreqHz + (maxIdx - spectrum.length / 2) * sampleRateHz / this.fftSize;

    // Classify modulation from cyclic features
    const { modulation, confidence, user } = this.classifyModulation(
      cyclicFrequencies, maxScfPeak, sampleRateHz
    );

    const scfPeakDb = maxScfPeak > 0 ? 10 * Math.log10(maxScfPeak) : -Infinity;

    return {
      scfPeakDb,
      estimatedCarrierHz,
      detectedModulation: modulation,
      cyclicFrequencies: cyclicFrequencies.slice(0, 10),
      confidence,
      likelyUser: user,
    };
  }

  private computeMagnitudeSpectrum(signal: Float32Array): Float32Array {
    const n = Math.min(signal.length, this.fftSize);
    const spectrum = new Float32Array(n);

    // DFT (for production: use FFT library)
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      let re = 0, im = 0;
      for (let t = 0; t < n; t++) {
        const angle = -2 * Math.PI * k * t / n;
        re += signal[t] * Math.cos(angle);
        im += signal[t] * Math.sin(angle);
      }
      spectrum[k] = Math.sqrt(re * re + im * im) / n;
    }
    return spectrum;
  }

  private classifyModulation(
    cyclicFreqs: number[],
    scfPeak: number,
    sampleRateHz: number
  ): { modulation: ModulationScheme | null; confidence: number; user: PrimaryUserType } {
    // Heuristics based on cyclic frequency patterns
    const numCyclicFreqs = cyclicFreqs.length;

    if (numCyclicFreqs === 0 || scfPeak < 0.001) {
      return { modulation: null, confidence: 0.3, user: PrimaryUserType.UNKNOWN };
    }

    // GSM: characteristic symbol rate 270.833 kHz
    const hasGsmRate = cyclicFreqs.some(f => Math.abs(f - 270833) < 5000);
    if (hasGsmRate) {
      return { modulation: ModulationScheme.OFDM_QPSK, confidence: 0.8, user: PrimaryUserType.CELLULAR };
    }

    // OFDM: regular spacing of cyclic frequencies
    if (numCyclicFreqs > 5 && this.isRegularlySpaced(cyclicFreqs)) {
      return { modulation: ModulationScheme.OFDM_QAM64, confidence: 0.75, user: PrimaryUserType.CELLULAR };
    }

    // DSSS: wide bandwidth, low peak SCF
    if (scfPeak < 0.01 && numCyclicFreqs < 3) {
      return { modulation: ModulationScheme.DSSS_BPSK, confidence: 0.6, user: PrimaryUserType.MILITARY };
    }

    // Broadcast TV (ATSC/DVB): specific rates
    const hasBroadcastRate = cyclicFreqs.some(f => Math.abs(f - 6e6) < 100e3 || Math.abs(f - 8e6) < 100e3);
    if (hasBroadcastRate) {
      return { modulation: ModulationScheme.OFDM_QAM64, confidence: 0.85, user: PrimaryUserType.BROADCAST_TV };
    }

    // Radar: typically no stable cyclic features, impulsive
    if (numCyclicFreqs > 20) {
      return { modulation: null, confidence: 0.65, user: PrimaryUserType.RADAR };
    }

    return { modulation: ModulationScheme.QPSK, confidence: 0.4, user: PrimaryUserType.UNKNOWN };
  }

  private isRegularlySpaced(freqs: number[], tolerancePct: number = 0.05): boolean {
    if (freqs.length < 3) return false;
    const sorted = [...freqs].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const gaps = sorted.slice(1).map((f, i) => f - sorted[i]);
    const meanGap = gaps.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / gaps.length;
    return gaps.every(g => Math.abs(g - meanGap) / meanGap < tolerancePct);
  }
}

// ─── UCB1 Bandit ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SpectrumBandit {
  private arms: Map<string, BanditArm> = new Map();
  private totalPulls: number = 0;
  private readonly explorationConst: number;

  constructor(explorationConst: number = UCB1_EXPLORATION_CONST) {
    this.explorationConst = explorationConst;
  }

  addArm(channelId: string): void {
    this.arms.set(channelId, {
      channelId,
      pulls: 0,
      totalReward: 0,
      meanReward: 0,
      ucb1Value: Infinity, // Unexplored arms have infinite UCB1
      lastUpdated: Date.now(),
    });
  }

  removeArm(channelId: string): void {
    this.arms.delete(channelId);
  }

  /**
   * UCB1 selection: argmax(μ_i + c * sqrt(ln(n) / n_i))
   */
  selectBestArm(excludeIds: Set<string> = new Set()): string | null {
    let bestId: string | null = null;
    let bestValue = -Infinity;

    for (const [id, arm] of this.arms.entries()) {
      if (excludeIds.has(id)) continue;

      const ucb = arm.pulls === 0
        ? Infinity
        : arm.meanReward + this.explorationConst * Math.sqrt(Math.log(this.totalPulls + 1) / arm.pulls);

      if (ucb > bestValue) {
        bestValue = ucb;
        bestId = id;
      }
    }

    return bestId;
  }

  /**
   * Update arm reward after using channel
   * Reward = normalized throughput * (1 - BER) * (1 - occupancy)
   */
  update(channelId: string, reward: number): void {
    const arm = this.arms.get(channelId);
    if (!arm) return;

    arm.pulls++;
    this.totalPulls++;
    arm.totalReward += reward;
    arm.meanReward = arm.totalReward / arm.pulls;
    arm.ucb1Value = arm.meanReward + this.explorationConst * Math.sqrt(Math.log(this.totalPulls) / arm.pulls);
    arm.lastUpdated = Date.now();
  }

  /**
   * Penalize channel (jammer detected, PU appeared)
   */
  penalize(channelId: string, penaltyFactor: number = 0.5): void {
    const arm = this.arms.get(channelId);
    if (!arm) return;
    arm.totalReward *= penaltyFactor;
    arm.meanReward = arm.pulls > 0 ? arm.totalReward / arm.pulls : 0;
  }

  getArmStats(): BanditArm[] {
    return Array.from(this.arms.values()).sort((a, b) => b.meanReward - a.meanReward);
  }

  reset(channelId: string): void {
    const arm = this.arms.get(channelId);
    if (arm) {
      arm.pulls = 0;
      arm.totalReward = 0;
      arm.meanReward = 0;
      arm.ucb1Value = Infinity;
    }
  }
}

// ─── Cooperative Fusion Center ────────────────────────────────────────────────

class CooperativeFusionCenter {
  private reports: Map<string, SensingReport[]> = new Map(); // channelId -> reports
  private readonly minNodes: number;

  constructor(minNodes: number) {
    this.minNodes = minNodes;
  }

  addReport(report: SensingReport): void {
    if (!this.reports.has(report.channelId)) {
      this.reports.set(report.channelId, []);
    }
    const reports = this.reports.get(report.channelId)!;

    // Replace old report from same node
    const idx = reports.findIndex(r => r.nodeId === report.nodeId);
    if (idx >= 0) reports.splice(idx, 1);
    reports.push(report);

    // Age out reports older than 10 seconds
    const cutoff = Date.now() - 10_000;
    this.reports.set(report.channelId, reports.filter(r => r.timestamp > cutoff));
  }

  /**
   * Fuse reports using weighted majority voting
   * Weight = confidence of each report
   */
  fuse(channelId: string): CooperativeSensingResult | null {
    const reports = this.reports.get(channelId) || [];
    if (reports.length < this.minNodes) return null;

    const participatingNodes = reports.map(r => r.nodeId);

    // Weighted sum: Σ(w_i * d_i) where d_i ∈ {0,1} and w_i = confidence
    const totalWeight = reports.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.confidence, 0);
    const occupiedWeight = reports.reduce((acc, r) => acc + (r.occupied ? r.confidence : 0), 0);

    const occupiedFraction = totalWeight > 0 ? occupiedWeight / totalWeight : 0;
    const fusedDecision = occupiedFraction >= COOPERATIVE_THRESHOLD;
    const fusedConfidence = Math.abs(occupiedFraction - 0.5) * 2;

    // Primary user type: consensus from detectors with cyclo features
    const puReports = reports.filter(r => r.cycloFeatures);
    let puType: PrimaryUserType | null = null;
    if (puReports.length > 0) {
      const types = puReports.map(r => r.cycloFeatures!.likelyUser);
      puType = this.mostCommon(types);
    }

    return {
      channelId,
      participatingNodes,
      reports,
      fusedDecision,
      confidence: fusedConfidence,
      detectionMethod: 'WEIGHTED',
      primaryUserType: puType,
    };
  }

  private mostCommon<T>(arr: T[]): T {
    const counts = new Map<T, number>();
    for (const item of arr) counts.set(item, (counts.get(item) || 0) + 1);
    let max = 0, result = arr[0];
    for (const [item, count] of counts.entries()) {
      if (count > max) { max = count; result = item; }
    }
    return result;
  }

  clearChannel(channelId: string): void {
    this.reports.delete(channelId);
  }
}

// ─── Distributed Channel Allocator ───────────────────────────────────────────

class DistributedChannelAllocator {
  private allocations: Map<string, ChannelAllocation[]> = new Map(); // channelId -> allocations
  private nodeAllocations: Map<string, ChannelAllocation> = new Map(); // nodeId -> current alloc

  /**
   * CSMA/CA style distributed allocation with collision avoidance
   * Uses random backoff + priority-based contention resolution
   */
  async requestAllocation(
    nodeId: string,
    candidates: Channel[],
    mcs: MCSEntry,
    txPowerDbm: number,
    priorityLevel: number,
    durationMs: number
  ): Promise<ChannelAllocation | null> {
    // Sort candidates by usability and SNR
    const ranked = candidates
      .filter(c => c.usable && c.state === ChannelState.AVAILABLE)
      .sort((a, b) => b.snrDb - a.snrDb);

    for (const channel of ranked) {
      // Check for existing conflicting allocations
      const existing = this.allocations.get(channel.id) || [];
      const now = Date.now();
      const active = existing.filter(a => a.startTime + a.durationMs > now);

      // High-priority nodes can preempt low-priority ones
      const conflict = active.find(a => a.priority >= priorityLevel);
      if (conflict) continue;

      // Random backoff (CSMA/CA)
      const backoffSlots = Math.floor(Math.random() * CSMA_BACKOFF_SLOTS);
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, backoffSlots * 1));

      // Re-check after backoff
      const postBackoff = (this.allocations.get(channel.id) || [])
        .filter(a => a.startTime + a.durationMs > Date.now());
      const stillConflict = postBackoff.find(a => a.priority >= priorityLevel);
      if (stillConflict) continue;

      const allocation: ChannelAllocation = {
        nodeId,
        channelId: channel.id,
        centerFreqHz: channel.centerFreqHz,
        mcs,
        txPowerDbm,
        startTime: Date.now(),
        durationMs,
        priority: priorityLevel,
      };

      if (!this.allocations.has(channel.id)) this.allocations.set(channel.id, []);
      this.allocations.get(channel.id)!.push(allocation);
      this.nodeAllocations.set(nodeId, allocation);
      return allocation;
    }

    return null;
  }

  releaseAllocation(nodeId: string): void {
    const alloc = this.nodeAllocations.get(nodeId);
    if (!alloc) return;
    const chAllocs = this.allocations.get(alloc.channelId);
    if (chAllocs) {
      const idx = chAllocs.findIndex(a => a.nodeId === nodeId);
      if (idx >= 0) chAllocs.splice(idx, 1);
    }
    this.nodeAllocations.delete(nodeId);
  }

  getAllocation(nodeId: string): ChannelAllocation | null {
    return this.nodeAllocations.get(nodeId) || null;
  }

  getChannelLoad(channelId: string): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    const active = (this.allocations.get(channelId) || [])
      .filter(a => a.startTime + a.durationMs > now);
    return active.length;
  }
}

// ─── Spectrum Scanner ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SpectrumScanner extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly config: CognitiveRadioConfig;
  private readonly energyDetector: EnergyDetector;
  private readonly cycloDetector: CyclostationaryDetector;
  private scanTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
  private scanning: boolean = false;
  private channelHistory: Map<string, number[]> = new Map(); // channelId -> rssi history

  constructor(config: CognitiveRadioConfig) {
    super();
    this.config = config;
    this.energyDetector = new EnergyDetector(
      config.energyDetectionThresholdDbm,
      config.sensingDwellMs
    );
    this.cycloDetector = new CyclostationaryDetector(CYCLO_FFT_SIZE);
  }

  startContinuousScan(channels: Channel[]): void {
    if (this.scanning) return;
    this.scanning = true;
    this.scheduleScan(channels);
  }

  stopScan(): void {
    this.scanning = false;
    if (this.scanTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.scanTimer);
      this.scanTimer = null;
    }
  }

  private scheduleScan(channels: Channel[]): void {
    if (!this.scanning) return;
    this.scanTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
      await this.runScanCycle(channels);
      this.scheduleScan(channels);
    }, this.config.scanIntervalMs);
  }

  async scanChannel(channel: Channel, signal?: Float32Array): Promise<SensingReport> {
    // Simulate RSSI measurement
    const simulatedRssi = this.simulateRssi(channel);

    const { occupied, confidence, snrDb } = this.energyDetector.detect(
      simulatedRssi,
      channel.bandwidthHz
    );

    let cycloFeatures: CyclostationaryFeatures | undefined;
    if (this.config.cyclostationaryEnabled && occupied && signal) {
      cycloFeatures = this.cycloDetector.analyze(signal, channel.bandwidthHz, channel.centerFreqHz);
    }

    // Update channel state
    channel.rssiDbm = simulatedRssi;
    channel.noiseFloorDbm = this.energyDetector.noiseFloor(channel.bandwidthHz);
    channel.snrDb = snrDb;
    channel.primaryUserDetected = occupied && (cycloFeatures?.likelyUser !== PrimaryUserType.UNKNOWN ?? false);
    channel.primaryUserType = cycloFeatures?.likelyUser ?? null;
    channel.cycloFeatures = cycloFeatures ?? null;
    channel.lastSensedAt = Date.now();

    // Update occupancy history
    if (!this.channelHistory.has(channel.id)) this.channelHistory.set(channel.id, []);
    const history = this.channelHistory.get(channel.id)!;
    history.push(occupied ? 1 : 0);
    if (history.length > this.config.channelHistoryDepth) history.shift();
    channel.occupancyRatio = history.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / history.length;

    // Determine channel state
    if (channel.jammerPresent) {
      channel.state = ChannelState.JAMMED;
    } else if (occupied && channel.primaryUserDetected) {
      channel.state = ChannelState.OCCUPIED_PRIMARY;
    } else if (occupied) {
      channel.state = ChannelState.OCCUPIED_SECONDARY;
    } else {
      channel.state = ChannelState.AVAILABLE;
    }

    channel.usable = channel.state === ChannelState.AVAILABLE &&
      channel.snrDb > MCS_TABLE[0].minSnrDb;

    const report: SensingReport = {
      nodeId: this.config.nodeId,
      channelId: channel.id,
      method: this.config.cyclostationaryEnabled ? SensingMethod.CYCLOSTATIONARY : SensingMethod.ENERGY_DETECTION,
      rssiDbm: simulatedRssi,
      occupied,
      confidence,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      cycloFeatures,
    };

    this.emit('scanner:report', report);
    return report;
  }

  private async runScanCycle(channels: Channel[]): Promise<void> {
    const reports: SensingReport[] = [];
    for (const channel of channels) {
      const report = await this.scanChannel(channel);
      reports.push(report);
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, this.config.sensingDwellMs));
    }
    this.emit('scanner:cycle_complete', { reports, timestamp: Date.now() });
  }

  private simulateRssi(channel: Channel): number {
    // Realistic RSSI simulation based on channel state and band
    const noiseFloor = this.energyDetector.noiseFloor(channel.bandwidthHz);
    if (channel.state === ChannelState.OCCUPIED_PRIMARY) {
      return noiseFloor + 15 + (Math.random() * 10 - 5);
    } else if (channel.state === ChannelState.JAMMED) {
      return noiseFloor + 25 + (Math.random() * 5);
    } else {
      return noiseFloor + (Math.random() * 6 - 3); // Noise only
    }
  }
}

// ─── MCS Selector ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class MCSSelector {
  /**
   * Select highest-rate MCS that satisfies SNR requirement with margin
   */
  select(snrDb: number, marginDb: number = 2): MCSEntry {
    const candidates = MCS_TABLE.filter(m => m.minSnrDb + marginDb <= snrDb);
    if (candidates.length === 0) return MCS_TABLE[0]; // Fallback to most robust

    // Return highest spectral efficiency
    return candidates.reduce((best, m) =>
      m.spectralEfficiencyBpHz > best.spectralEfficiencyBpHz ? m : best
    );
  }

  /**
   * Estimate data rate for given channel and MCS
   */
  estimateDataRate(channel: Channel, mcs: MCSEntry): number {
    return mcs.spectralEfficiencyBpHz * channel.bandwidthHz / 1e6; // Mbps
  }

  /**
   * Transmit power control: adjust power for target SNR with minimal interference
   */
  computeTxPower(
    targetSnrDb: number,
    propagationLossDb: number,
    noisePowerDbm: number,
    maxTxPowerDbm: number
  ): number {
    const requiredTxPower = noisePowerDbm + targetSnrDb + propagationLossDb;
    return Math.min(requiredTxPower, maxTxPowerDbm);
  }
}

// ─── Handoff Controller ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class HandoffController extends EventEmitter {
  private handoffInProgress: boolean = false;
  private readonly hysteresisDb: number;

  constructor(hysteresisDb: number) {
    super();
    this.hysteresisDb = hysteresisDb;
  }

  shouldHandoff(
    currentChannel: Channel,
    candidateChannel: Channel,
    trigger?: HandoffTrigger
  ): boolean {
    if (trigger === HandoffTrigger.PRIMARY_USER_DETECTED ||
        trigger === HandoffTrigger.JAMMER_DETECTED ||
        trigger === HandoffTrigger.REGULATORY_VIOLATION) {
      return true;
    }

    // Hysteresis: only handoff if candidate is significantly better
    const snrImprovement = candidateChannel.snrDb - currentChannel.snrDb;
    return snrImprovement > this.hysteresisDb && candidateChannel.usable;
  }

  async executeHandoff(
    plan: HandoffPlan,
    currentAlloc: ChannelAllocation | null,
    allocator: DistributedChannelAllocator
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (this.handoffInProgress) return false;

    this.handoffInProgress = true;
    this.emit('handoff:started', { plan });

    try {
      // Make-before-break: establish new channel before releasing old
      // In real radio: prepare transceiver for new frequency
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, plan.estimatedDowntimeMs));

      if (currentAlloc) {
        allocator.releaseAllocation(currentAlloc.nodeId);
      }

      this.emit('handoff:completed', { plan });
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      this.emit('handoff:failed', { plan, error: err });
      return false;
    } finally {
      this.handoffInProgress = false;
    }
  }

  get busy(): boolean { return this.handoffInProgress; }
}

// ─── Main Cognitive Radio Engine ──────────────────────────────────────────────

export class CognitiveRadio extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly config: CognitiveRadioConfig;
  private readonly channels: Map<string, Channel> = new Map();
  private readonly bandit: SpectrumBandit;
  private readonly fusionCenter: CooperativeFusionCenter;
  private readonly allocator: DistributedChannelAllocator;
  private readonly scanner: SpectrumScanner;
  private readonly mcsSelector: MCSSelector;
  private readonly handoffController: HandoffController;
  private currentChannel: Channel | null = null;
  private currentAllocation: ChannelAllocation | null = null;
  private jammingReport: Set<string> = new Set(); // Channel IDs reported jammed

  constructor(config: CognitiveRadioConfig) {
    super();
    this.config = config;
    this.bandit = new SpectrumBandit(config.banditExplorationConst);
    this.fusionCenter = new CooperativeFusionCenter(config.cooperativeMinNodes);
    this.allocator = new DistributedChannelAllocator();
    this.scanner = new SpectrumScanner(config);
    this.mcsSelector = new MCSSelector();
    this.handoffController = new HandoffController(config.handoffHysteresisDb);

    this.wireEvents();
    this.initializeChannels();
  }

  // ── Initialization ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private initializeChannels(): void {
    const globalBands = REGULATORY_DB['GLOBAL'] || [];
    const regionBands = REGULATORY_DB[this.config.region] || [];
    const allBands = [...globalBands, ...regionBands];

    for (const band of allBands) {
      if (band.licensed) continue; // Skip licensed bands
      if (band.startHz < this.config.minFreqHz || band.endHz > this.config.maxFreqHz) continue;

      // Generate channels within band
      const numChannels = Math.min(
        Math.floor((band.endHz - band.startHz) / band.channelBwHz),
        MAX_CHANNELS_PER_BAND
      );

      for (let i = 0; i < numChannels; i++) {
        const centerFreq = band.startHz + (i + 0.5) * band.channelBwHz;
        const channelId = `${band.name}_ch${i}`;

        const channel: Channel = {
          id: channelId,
          bandName: band.name,
          centerFreqHz: centerFreq,
          bandwidthHz: band.channelBwHz,
          state: ChannelState.UNKNOWN,
          rssiDbm: -100,
          noiseFloorDbm: -120,
          snrDb: 0,
          occupancyRatio: 0,
          lastSensedAt: 0,
          primaryUserDetected: false,
          primaryUserType: null,
          jammerPresent: false,
          cycloFeatures: null,
          usable: false,
          maxDataRateMbps: 0,
          propagationLossDb: 0,
        };

        this.channels.set(channelId, channel);
        this.bandit.addArm(channelId);
      }
    }

    this.emit('cr:initialized', { totalChannels: this.channels.size, region: this.config.region });
  }

  private wireEvents(): void {
    this.scanner.on('scanner:report', (report: SensingReport) => {
      this.handleScanReport(report);
    });

    this.scanner.on('scanner:cycle_complete', ({ reports }) => {
      this.emit('cr:scan_complete', {
        channels: reports.length,
        available: Array.from(this.channels.values()).filter(c => c.usable).length,
      });
    });

    this.handoffController.on('handoff:completed', ({ plan }) => {
      this.emit('cr:handoff_complete', plan);
    });

    this.handoffController.on('handoff:failed', ({ plan, error }) => {
      this.emit('cr:handoff_failed', { plan, error });
    });
  }

  // ── Spectrum Sensing ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  startSensing(): void {
    const channelList = Array.from(this.channels.values());
    this.scanner.startContinuousScan(channelList);
    this.emit('cr:sensing_started', { channels: channelList.length });
  }

  stopSensing(): void {
    this.scanner.stopScan();
  }

  async senseChannel(channelId: string, signal?: Float32Array): Promise<SensingReport | null> {
    const channel = this.channels.get(channelId);
    if (!channel) return null;
    return this.scanner.scanChannel(channel, signal);
  }

  // ── Cooperative Sensing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  receiveSensingReport(report: SensingReport): void {
    if (!this.config.cooperativeSensingEnabled) return;
    this.fusionCenter.addReport(report);

    // Try to fuse immediately
    const result = this.fusionCenter.fuse(report.channelId);
    if (result) {
      const channel = this.channels.get(report.channelId);
      if (channel) {
        if (result.fusedDecision) {
          channel.state = result.primaryUserType
            ? ChannelState.OCCUPIED_PRIMARY
            : ChannelState.OCCUPIED_SECONDARY;
          channel.primaryUserDetected = result.primaryUserType !== null;
          channel.primaryUserType = result.primaryUserType;
          channel.usable = false;
        }
      }
      this.emit('cr:cooperative_result', result);
    }
  }

  // ── Channel Selection ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  selectBestChannel(
    excludeJammed: boolean = true,
    excludePrimary: boolean = true
  ): Channel | null {
    const excluded = new Set<string>();

    if (excludeJammed) {
      for (const [id, ch] of this.channels.entries()) {
        if (ch.state === ChannelState.JAMMED || this.jammingReport.has(id)) excluded.add(id);
      }
    }

    if (excludePrimary) {
      for (const [id, ch] of this.channels.entries()) {
        if (ch.state === ChannelState.OCCUPIED_PRIMARY) excluded.add(id);
      }
    }

    const bestId = this.bandit.selectBestArm(excluded);
    return bestId ? this.channels.get(bestId) ?? null : null;
  }

  getTopChannels(n: number = 5): Channel[] {
    const stats = this.bandit.getArmStats();
    return stats
      .slice(0, n)
      .map(arm => this.channels.get(arm.channelId))
      .filter((c): c is Channel => c !== undefined && c.usable);
  }

  buildSpectrumMap(): SpectrumMap {
    const available: Channel[] = [];
    const occupancyByBand = new Map<string, number[]>();

    for (const channel of this.channels.values()) {
      if (channel.usable) available.push(channel);

      if (!occupancyByBand.has(channel.bandName)) occupancyByBand.set(channel.bandName, []);
      occupancyByBand.get(channel.bandName)!.push(channel.occupancyRatio);
    }

    const avgOccupancy = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const [band, ratios] of occupancyByBand.entries()) {
      avgOccupancy.set(band, ratios.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / ratios.length);
    }

    const best = available.sort((a, b) => b.snrDb - a.snrDb)[0] ?? null;

    return {
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      region: this.config.region,
      channels: this.channels,
      occupancyByBand: avgOccupancy,
      availableChannels: available,
      bestChannel: best,
    };
  }

  // ── MCS Adaptation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  selectMCS(channel: Channel, marginDb: number = 2): MCSEntry {
    return this.mcsSelector.select(channel.snrDb, marginDb);
  }

  computeTxPower(channel: Channel, targetSnrDb: number = 15): number {
    if (!this.config.enableTpc) return this.config.maxTxPowerDbm;
    return this.mcsSelector.computeTxPower(
      targetSnrDb,
      channel.propagationLossDb,
      channel.noiseFloorDbm,
      this.config.maxTxPowerDbm
    );
  }

  // ── Channel Allocation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async acquireChannel(
    priority: number = 50,
    durationMs: number = 60_000
  ): Promise<ChannelAllocation | null> {
    const candidates = this.getTopChannels(10);
    if (candidates.length === 0) {
      this.emit('cr:no_channels_available');
      return null;
    }

    const best = candidates[0];
    const mcs = this.selectMCS(best);
    const txPower = this.computeTxPower(best);

    const alloc = await this.allocator.requestAllocation(
      this.config.nodeId,
      candidates,
      mcs,
      txPower,
      priority,
      durationMs
    );

    if (alloc) {
      this.currentChannel = this.channels.get(alloc.channelId) ?? null;
      this.currentAllocation = alloc;
      this.emit('cr:channel_acquired', { allocation: alloc, mcs });
    }

    return alloc;
  }

  releaseChannel(): void {
    this.allocator.releaseAllocation(this.config.nodeId);
    this.currentChannel = null;
    this.currentAllocation = null;
    this.emit('cr:channel_released');
  }

  // ── Spectrum Handoff ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async triggerHandoff(trigger: HandoffTrigger): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!this.currentChannel || this.handoffController.busy) return false;

    // Find next best channel (excluding current)
    const excludedIds = new Set([this.currentChannel.id]);
    if (trigger === HandoffTrigger.JAMMER_DETECTED) {
      this.jammingReport.add(this.currentChannel.id);
      this.bandit.penalize(this.currentChannel.id, 0.1);
    } else if (trigger === HandoffTrigger.PRIMARY_USER_DETECTED) {
      this.currentChannel.state = ChannelState.OCCUPIED_PRIMARY;
      this.bandit.penalize(this.currentChannel.id, 0.3);
    }

    const targetId = this.bandit.selectBestArm(excludedIds);
    if (!targetId) {
      this.emit('cr:handoff_no_target', { trigger });
      return false;
    }

    const target = this.channels.get(targetId)!;
    const mcs = this.selectMCS(target);

    const plan: HandoffPlan = {
      currentChannelId: this.currentChannel.id,
      targetChannelId: targetId,
      trigger,
      estimatedDowntimeMs: 50, // 50ms make-before-break
      fallbackChannelId: this.bandit.selectBestArm(new Set([this.currentChannel.id, targetId])),
      mcs,
      txPowerDbm: this.computeTxPower(target),
    };

    this.emit('cr:handoff_initiated', plan);
    return this.handoffController.executeHandoff(plan, this.currentAllocation, this.allocator);
  }

  // ── Jammer Integration ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  reportJammer(channelId: string): void {
    const channel = this.channels.get(channelId);
    if (channel) {
      channel.jammerPresent = true;
      channel.state = ChannelState.JAMMED;
      channel.usable = false;
      this.jammingReport.add(channelId);
      this.bandit.penalize(channelId, 0.05);

      this.emit('cr:jammer_reported', { channelId, centerFreqHz: channel.centerFreqHz });

      // Trigger handoff if this is our current channel
      if (this.currentChannel?.id === channelId) {
        this.triggerHandoff(HandoffTrigger.JAMMER_DETECTED).catch(() => {});
      }
    }
  }

  clearJammer(channelId: string): void {
    const channel = this.channels.get(channelId);
    if (channel) {
      channel.jammerPresent = false;
      channel.state = ChannelState.UNKNOWN;
      this.jammingReport.delete(channelId);
      this.bandit.reset(channelId);
      this.emit('cr:jammer_cleared', { channelId });
    }
  }

  // ── Primary User Protection ───────────────────────────────────────────────

  private handleScanReport(report: SensingReport): void {
    const channel = this.channels.get(report.channelId);
    if (!channel) return;

    // Update bandit reward
    const reward = channel.usable
      ? (channel.snrDb / 30) * (1 - channel.occupancyRatio)
      : 0;
    this.bandit.update(report.channelId, Math.max(0, Math.min(1, reward)));

    // DFS: if primary user detected on current channel, evacuate immediately
    if (this.config.enableDfs &&
        report.occupied &&
        report.cycloFeatures?.likelyUser === PrimaryUserType.RADAR) {
      if (this.currentChannel?.id === report.channelId) {
        this.emit('cr:dfs_evacuation', { channelId: report.channelId });
        this.triggerHandoff(HandoffTrigger.PRIMARY_USER_DETECTED).catch(() => {});
      }
    }

    // Share local report for cooperative sensing
    if (this.config.cooperativeSensingEnabled) {
      this.emit('cr:share_report', report);
    }
  }

  // ── Distributed Mesh Coordination ────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Receive channel state from peer node for distributed allocation
   */
  receivePeerChannelState(
    peerId: string,
    channelId: string,
    state: ChannelState,
    snrDb: number
  ): void {
    const channel = this.channels.get(channelId);
    if (!channel) return;

    // Conservative: if any peer says primary/jammed, we trust it
    if (state === ChannelState.OCCUPIED_PRIMARY || state === ChannelState.JAMMED) {
      channel.state = state;
      channel.usable = false;
      this.bandit.penalize(channelId, 0.2);
    }
  }

  broadcastChannelState(): Record<string, { state: ChannelState; snrDb: number; occupancy: number }> {
    const out: Record<string, { state: ChannelState; snrDb: number; occupancy: number }> = {};
    for (const [id, ch] of this.channels.entries()) {
      if (ch.lastSensedAt > Date.now() - 30_000) { // Only fresh readings
        out[id] = { state: ch.state, snrDb: ch.snrDb, occupancy: ch.occupancyRatio };
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  // ── Status & Diagnostics ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getChannelById(id: string): Channel | null {
    return this.channels.get(id) ?? null;
  }

  getAllChannels(): Channel[] {
    return Array.from(this.channels.values());
  }

  getAvailableChannels(): Channel[] {
    return Array.from(this.channels.values()).filter(c => c.usable);
  }

  getCurrentChannel(): Channel | null {
    return this.currentChannel;
  }

  getCurrentAllocation(): ChannelAllocation | null {
    return this.currentAllocation;
  }

  diagnostics(): Record<string, unknown> {
    const all = Array.from(this.channels.values());
    const available = all.filter(c => c.usable);
    const jammed = all.filter(c => c.jammerPresent);
    const primaryOccupied = all.filter(c => c.state === ChannelState.OCCUPIED_PRIMARY);
    const banditStats = this.bandit.getArmStats();

    const bandSummary: Record<string, { total: number; available: number; avgSnr: number }> = {};
    for (const ch of all) {
      if (!bandSummary[ch.bandName]) bandSummary[ch.bandName] = { total: 0, available: 0, avgSnr: 0 };
      bandSummary[ch.bandName].total++;
      if (ch.usable) bandSummary[ch.bandName].available++;
      bandSummary[ch.bandName].avgSnr += ch.snrDb / all.length;
    }

    return {
      nodeId: this.config.nodeId,
      region: this.config.region,
      scanRange: `${this.config.minFreqHz / 1e6} MHz – ${this.config.maxFreqHz / 1e6} MHz`,
      totalChannels: all.length,
      availableChannels: available.length,
      jammedChannels: jammed.length,
      primaryOccupiedChannels: primaryOccupied.length,
      currentChannel: this.currentChannel
        ? { id: this.currentChannel.id, freqMhz: this.currentChannel.centerFreqHz / 1e6, snrDb: this.currentChannel.snrDb }
        : null,
      currentMcs: this.currentAllocation?.mcs ?? null,
      topBanditArms: banditStats.slice(0, 5).map(a => ({
        channelId: a.channelId,
        pulls: a.pulls,
        meanReward: a.meanReward.toFixed(3),
        ucb1: a.ucb1Value.toFixed(3),
      })),
      bandSummary,
      cooperativeSensing: this.config.cooperativeSensingEnabled,
      cyclostationaryDetection: this.config.cyclostationaryEnabled,
      dfsScan: this.config.enableDfs,
      tpc: this.config.enableTpc,
      jammingReports: this.jammingReport.size,
    };
  }

  shutdown(): void {
    this.stopSensing();
    this.releaseChannel();
    this.emit('cr:shutdown');
  }
}

// ─── Factory ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createCognitiveRadio(overrides?: Partial<CognitiveRadioConfig>): CognitiveRadio {
  const defaults: CognitiveRadioConfig = {
    nodeId: `cr_${randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}`,
    region: 'GLOBAL',
    minFreqHz: MIN_FREQ_HZ,
    maxFreqHz: MAX_FREQ_HZ,
    sensingBandwidthHz: 1e6,
    sensingDwellMs: SENSING_FRAME_MS,
    energyDetectionThresholdDbm: -95,
    primaryUserProtectionDb: 10,
    cooperativeSensingEnabled: true,
    cooperativeMinNodes: 3,
    cyclostationaryEnabled: true,
    banditExplorationConst: UCB1_EXPLORATION_CONST,
    maxTxPowerDbm: 30,
    handoffHysteresisDb: HANDOFF_HYSTERESIS_DB,
    scanIntervalMs: 1000,
    channelHistoryDepth: CHANNEL_HISTORY_DEPTH,
    jammingDefenseIntegration: true,
    enableDfs: true,
    enableTpc: true,
    spectralMaskCompliance: true,
  };

  return new CognitiveRadio({ ...defaults, ...overrides });
}

  EnergyDetector,
  CyclostationaryDetector,
  SpectrumBandit,
  CooperativeFusionCenter,
  DistributedChannelAllocator,
  SpectrumScanner,
  MCSSelector,
  HandoffController,
  MCS_TABLE,
};

export default CognitiveRadio;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — jammingDefense.ts
 * RF Jamming Defense Engine
 *
 * Implements:
 *   - Energy detection based RF jamming classification (broadband, spot, sweep, pulse, reactive)
 *   - Reactive frequency hopping when channel is jammed (FHSS with dynamic table)
 *   - Spread spectrum switching under attack (DSSS, FHSS, THSS, hybrid)
 *   - Multi-channel redundant simultaneous transmission
 *   - Interference heat map construction across spectrum (10 MHz — 6 GHz)
 *   - Jamming fingerprinting to identify adversary's jamming pattern
 *   - Anti-reactive-jammer protocol (transmit decoys, delay real transmission)
 *   - Signal-to-Interference-plus-Noise Ratio (SINR) estimation
 *   - Probabilistic jamming detection using CUSUM change detection
 *   - Adaptive spreading gain and channel coding rate adjustment
 *
 * Design contract:
 *   - Zero dependency on network stack — pure signal processing
 *   - Decision loop runs in < 10ms to outmaneuver reactive jammers
 *   - Integrates with loraMesh, cognitiveRadio transports
 *   - All decisions logged to tamper-evident audit trail
 *
 * @module jammingDefense
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES & INTERFACES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type JammingType =
  | 'none'
  | 'broadband'          // Wideband noise across entire band
  | 'spot'               // Continuous on a single frequency
  | 'sweep'              // Sweeping across frequencies
  | 'pulse'              // Periodic high-power bursts
  | 'reactive'           // Responds to detected transmissions
  | 'smart'              // Adaptive — learns and targets protocol
  | 'partial_band';      // Targets a subset of available spectrum

export type SpreadSpectrumMode =
  | 'none'
  | 'fhss'               // Frequency Hopping Spread Spectrum
  | 'dsss'               // Direct Sequence Spread Spectrum
  | 'thss'               // Time Hopping Spread Spectrum
  | 'hybrid_fhss_dsss';  // Combined

export type ChannelState = 'clear' | 'busy' | 'jammed' | 'unknown';

export interface JammingDefenseConfig {
  nodeId: string;
  // Spectrum bounds
  minFreqHz: number;                   // e.g. 433e6 (433 MHz)
  maxFreqHz: number;                   // e.g. 928e6 (928 MHz)
  channelBandwidthHz: number;          // e.g. 125e3 (125 kHz LoRa)
  // Jamming detection thresholds
  noiseFloorDbm: number;               // e.g. -120 dBm
  jammingThresholdDbm: number;         // e.g. -85 dBm (above = jammed)
  sinrMinDb: number;                   // Minimum acceptable SINR
  // CUSUM parameters
  cusumSlack: number;                  // k parameter, typically 0.5σ
  cusumThreshold: number;              // Decision threshold h
  // FHSS parameters
  hopIntervalMs: number;               // Time per channel before hopping
  hopSequenceLength: number;           // Pseudo-random sequence length
  hopSeedKey: string;                  // 32-byte hex key for sequence
  // Spreading
  defaultSpreadMode: SpreadSpectrumMode;
  dsssChippingRate: number;            // chips per bit
  maxSpreadingGain: number;            // dB
  // Multi-channel
  simultaneousChannels: number;        // How many channels to TX on simultaneously
  redundancyFactor: number;            // Copies of each packet
  // Anti-reactive jammer
  decoyTransmitProbability: number;    // 0-1, probability to send decoy
  decoyDelayMaxMs: number;             // Max delay before real transmission
  // Adaptation
  adaptationIntervalMs: number;        // How often to re-evaluate strategy
  blacklistDurationMs: number;         // How long to avoid a jammed channel
}

  nodeId: 'node-0',

};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PSEUDO-RANDOM HOP SEQUENCE GENERATOR (AES-CTR based PRNG)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class HopSequenceGenerator {
  private readonly key: Uint8Array;
  private counter: number;
  private readonly sequenceLength: number;
  private cachedSequence: number[];

  constructor(seedKey: string, sequenceLength: number) {
    this.key = this.hexToBytes(seedKey.padEnd(64, '0').slice(0, 64));
    this.counter = 0;
    this.sequenceLength = sequenceLength;
    this.cachedSequence = [];
    this.generateSequence();
  }

  private generateSequence(): void {
    // RC4-based PRNG keyed with seed (simplified — production uses AES-CTR)
    const S = new Uint8Array(256);
    for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) S[i] = i;

    let j = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
      j = (j + S[i] + this.key[i % this.key.length]) & 0xFF;
      [S[i], S[j]] = [S[j], S[i]];
    }

    this.cachedSequence = [];
    let si = 0, sj = 0;
    for (let n = 0; n < this.sequenceLength; n++) {
      si = (si + 1) & 0xFF;
      sj = (sj + S[si]) & 0xFF;
      [S[si], S[sj]] = [S[sj], S[si]];
      this.cachedSequence.push(S[(S[si] + S[sj]) & 0xFF]);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Get next channel index from hop sequence
   */
  next(numChannels: number): number {
    const raw = this.cachedSequence[this.counter % this.sequenceLength];
    this.counter++;
    return raw % numChannels;
  }

  /**
   * Get N steps ahead without advancing the sequence
   */
  peek(steps: number, numChannels: number): number[] {
    const result: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
      const raw = this.cachedSequence[(this.counter + i) % this.sequenceLength];
      result.push(raw % numChannels);
    }
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Re-key the sequence (use after detecting smart jammer has learned the pattern)
   */
  rekey(newSeed: Uint8Array): void {
    for (let i = 0; i < this.key.length; i++) {
      this.key[i] = newSeed[i % newSeed.length];
    }
    this.counter = 0;
    this.generateSequence();
  }

  private hexToBytes(hex: string): Uint8Array {
    const r = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < hex.length; i += 2) r[i/2] = parseInt(hex.slice(i,i+2), 16);
    return r;
  }

  get position(): number { return this.counter; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// CUSUM CHANGE DETECTION — Statistical jamming onset detection
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class CUSUMDetector {
  private readonly slack: number;        // k — slack parameter (half the minimum shift to detect)
  private readonly threshold: number;   // h — decision threshold
  private cusumPos: number;             // S+ statistic (detects positive shifts)
  private cusumNeg: number;             // S- statistic (detects negative shifts)
  private readonly baseline: number;    // μ₀ baseline mean
  private readonly baselineStd: number; // σ₀ baseline standard deviation
  private alarmCount: number;
  private lastAlarmTime: number;

  constructor(
    baseline: number,
    baselineStd: number,
    slack: number,
    threshold: number
  ) {
    this.baseline = baseline;
    this.baselineStd = baselineStd;
    this.slack = slack * baselineStd; // absolute slack
    this.threshold = threshold * baselineStd;
    this.cusumPos = 0;
    this.cusumNeg = 0;
    this.alarmCount = 0;
    this.lastAlarmTime = 0;
  }

  /**
   * Update CUSUM with new observation
   * Returns true if change (jamming onset) detected
   */
  update(observation: number): { alarm: boolean; statistic: number; direction: 'up' | 'down' | 'none' } {
    const normalized = (observation - this.baseline) / this.baselineStd;

    // Page's CUSUM
    this.cusumPos = Math.max(0, this.cusumPos + normalized - this.slack / this.baselineStd);
    this.cusumNeg = Math.max(0, this.cusumNeg - normalized - this.slack / this.baselineStd);

    const statistic = Math.max(this.cusumPos, this.cusumNeg);
    const alarmThreshold = this.threshold / this.baselineStd;

    if (statistic > alarmThreshold) {
      this.alarmCount++;
      this.lastAlarmTime = Date.now();
      const direction = this.cusumPos > this.cusumNeg ? 'up' : 'down';
      // Reset after alarm
      this.cusumPos = 0;
      this.cusumNeg = 0;
      return { alarm: true, statistic, direction };
    }

    return { alarm: false, statistic, direction: 'none' };
  }

  reset(): void {
    this.cusumPos = 0;
    this.cusumNeg = 0;
  }

  get statistics(): { pos: number; neg: number; alarmCount: number } {
    return { pos: this.cusumPos, neg: this.cusumNeg, alarmCount: this.alarmCount };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// SPECTRUM HEAT MAP BUILDER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class SpectrumHeatMapBuilder {
  private readonly minFreqHz: number;
  private readonly maxFreqHz: number;
  private readonly resolutionHz: number;
  private readonly numBins: number;
  private bins: Float64Array;
  private binCount: Float64Array;  // samples per bin for averaging
  private readonly noiseFloorDbm: number;
  private readonly jammingThresholdDbm: number;
  private history: SpectrumHeatMap[];
  private readonly maxHistory = 20;

  constructor(
    minFreqHz: number,
    maxFreqHz: number,
    resolutionHz: number,
    noiseFloorDbm: number,
    jammingThresholdDbm: number
  ) {
    this.minFreqHz = minFreqHz;
    this.maxFreqHz = maxFreqHz;
    this.resolutionHz = resolutionHz;
    this.numBins = Math.ceil((maxFreqHz - minFreqHz) / resolutionHz);
    this.bins = new Float64Array(this.numBins).fill(noiseFloorDbm);
    this.binCount = new Float64Array(this.numBins);
    this.noiseFloorDbm = noiseFloorDbm;
    this.jammingThresholdDbm = jammingThresholdDbm;
    this.history = [];
  }

  /**
   * Update a frequency bin with a new measurement (running average)
   */
  updateBin(freqHz: number, powerDbm: number): void {
    const binIdx = Math.floor((freqHz - this.minFreqHz) / this.resolutionHz);
    if (binIdx < 0 || binIdx >= this.numBins) return;

    // Exponential moving average with α = 0.2
    const alpha = 0.2;
    this.bins[binIdx] = alpha * powerDbm + (1 - alpha) * this.bins[binIdx];
    this.binCount[binIdx]++;
  }

  /**
   * Update multiple bins from a channel measurement
   */
  updateFromMeasurement(measurement: ChannelMeasurement, channelBandwidthHz: number): void {
    const halfBw = channelBandwidthHz / 2;
    const startFreq = measurement.centerFreqHz - halfBw;
    const endFreq = measurement.centerFreqHz + halfBw;

    for (let f = startFreq; f <= endFreq; f += this.resolutionHz) {
      this.updateBin(f, measurement.rssiDbm);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Build heat map snapshot with jamming region analysis
   */
  buildSnapshot(): SpectrumHeatMap {
    const jammingRegions: SpectrumHeatMap['jammingRegions'] = [];
    const clearRegions: SpectrumHeatMap['clearRegions'] = [];

    let inJammedRegion = false;
    let regionStart = 0;
    let regionPowerSum = 0;
    let regionBins = 0;

    let inClearRegion = false;
    let clearStart = 0;
    let clearPowerSum = 0;
    let clearBins = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < this.numBins; i++) {
      const freqHz = this.minFreqHz + i * this.resolutionHz;
      const power = this.bins[i];
      const isJammed = power > this.jammingThresholdDbm;

      if (isJammed) {
        if (!inJammedRegion) {
          inJammedRegion = true;
          regionStart = freqHz;
          regionPowerSum = 0;
          regionBins = 0;
        }
        regionPowerSum += power;
        regionBins++;

        if (inClearRegion) {
          inClearRegion = false;
          clearRegions.push({
            startFreqHz: clearStart,
            endFreqHz: freqHz,
            avgNoiseFloorDbm: clearPowerSum / clearBins,
          });
        }
      } else {
        if (inJammedRegion) {
          inJammedRegion = false;
          jammingRegions.push({
            startFreqHz: regionStart,
            endFreqHz: freqHz,
            avgPowerDbm: regionPowerSum / regionBins,
            jammingType: this.classifyRegion(regionPowerSum / regionBins, freqHz - regionStart),
          });
        }

        if (!inClearRegion) {
          inClearRegion = true;
          clearStart = freqHz;
          clearPowerSum = 0;
          clearBins = 0;
        }
        clearPowerSum += power;
        clearBins++;
      }
    }

    // Close open regions
    if (inJammedRegion) {
      jammingRegions.push({
        startFreqHz: regionStart,
        endFreqHz: this.maxFreqHz,
        avgPowerDbm: regionPowerSum / regionBins,
        jammingType: this.classifyRegion(regionPowerSum / regionBins, this.maxFreqHz - regionStart),
      });
    }
    if (inClearRegion) {
      clearRegions.push({
        startFreqHz: clearStart,
        endFreqHz: this.maxFreqHz,
        avgNoiseFloorDbm: clearPowerSum / clearBins,
      });
    }

    const snapshot: SpectrumHeatMap = {
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      minFreqHz: this.minFreqHz,
      maxFreqHz: this.maxFreqHz,
      resolutionHz: this.resolutionHz,
      bins: new Float64Array(this.bins),
      jammingRegions,
      clearRegions,
    };

    this.history.push(snapshot);
    if (this.history.length > this.maxHistory) this.history.shift();

    return snapshot;
  }

  private classifyRegion(avgPowerDbm: number, bandwidthHz: number): JammingType {
    const totalBandwidth = this.maxFreqHz - this.minFreqHz;
    if (bandwidthHz > totalBandwidth * 0.8) return 'broadband';
    if (bandwidthHz < 500e3) return 'spot';
    return 'partial_band';
  }

  /**
   * Find best N clear channels given required bandwidth
   */
  findClearChannels(requiredBandwidthHz: number, count: number): number[] {
    const channelScores: Array<{ freqHz: number; score: number }> = [];
    const halfBw = requiredBandwidthHz / 2;

    for (let i = 0; i < this.numBins; i++) {
      const freqHz = this.minFreqHz + i * this.resolutionHz;
      if (freqHz - halfBw < this.minFreqHz || freqHz + halfBw > this.maxFreqHz) continue;

      const startBin = Math.floor((freqHz - halfBw - this.minFreqHz) / this.resolutionHz);
      const endBin = Math.ceil((freqHz + halfBw - this.minFreqHz) / this.resolutionHz);

      let maxPower = -Infinity;
      for (let b = startBin; b <= endBin && b < this.numBins; b++) {
        if (this.bins[b] > maxPower) maxPower = this.bins[b];
      }

      // Score: lower power = better
      if (maxPower < this.jammingThresholdDbm) {
        channelScores.push({ freqHz, score: -maxPower });
      }
    }

    channelScores.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
    return channelScores.slice(0, count).map(c => c.freqHz);
  }

  getHistory(): SpectrumHeatMap[] { return [...this.history]; }
  getLatestSnapshot(): SpectrumHeatMap | null { return this.history[this.history.length - 1] ?? null; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// DSSS SPREADING ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class DSSSEngine {
  private readonly chipSequence: Int8Array; // PN (pseudo-noise) sequence, ±1
  private readonly chippingRate: number;
  private readonly spreadingGain: number;

  constructor(chippingRate: number) {
    this.chippingRate = chippingRate;
    this.spreadingGain = 10 * Math.log10(chippingRate); // dB
    this.chipSequence = this.generateMSequence(chippingRate);
  }

  /**
   * Maximum-length sequence (m-sequence) generator — optimal PN code
   * Uses 11-stage LFSR with polynomial x^11 + x^2 + 1
   */
  private generateMSequence(length: number): Int8Array {
    const chips = new Int8Array(length);
    let state = 0b11111111111; // All ones initial state

    for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) {
      // Tap at positions 11 and 2 (Barker-like)
      const bit = ((state >> 10) ^ (state >> 1)) & 1;
      chips[i] = (state & 1) === 1 ? 1 : -1;
      state = ((state >> 1) | (bit << 10)) & 0x7FF;
    }

    return chips;
  }

  /**
   * Spread a bit stream using DSSS
   * Each bit becomes chippingRate chips
   */
  spread(bits: number[]): Int8Array {
    const output = new Int8Array(bits.length * this.chippingRate);
    for (let i = 0; i < bits.length; i++) {
      const bit = bits[i] === 1 ? 1 : -1;
      for (let c = 0; c < this.chippingRate; c++) {
        output[i * this.chippingRate + c] = bit * this.chipSequence[c % this.chipSequence.length];
      }
    }
    return output;
  }

  /**
   * Despread received chip stream using correlation
   * Returns bit decisions with confidence scores
   */
  despread(chips: Float64Array): Array<{ bit: number; confidence: number }> {
    const numBits = Math.floor(chips.length / this.chippingRate);
    const results: Array<{ bit: number; confidence: number }> = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < numBits; i++) {
      let correlation = 0;
      for (let c = 0; c < this.chippingRate; c++) {
        const chipIdx = i * this.chippingRate + c;
        correlation += chips[chipIdx] * this.chipSequence[c % this.chipSequence.length];
      }

      // Normalize correlation
      const normalized = correlation / this.chippingRate;
      results.push({
        bit: normalized >= 0 ? 1 : 0,
        confidence: Math.min(1, Math.abs(normalized)),
      });
    }

    return results;
  }

  /**
   * Compute processing gain (jamming margin improvement)
   */
  get processingGainDb(): number { return this.spreadingGain; }

  /**
   * Estimate SINR improvement from spreading
   * J/S ratio that can be tolerated = processing gain - required Eb/N0
   */
  computeJammingMarginDb(requiredEbN0Db: number): number {
    return this.processingGainDb - requiredEbN0Db;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// JAMMER FINGERPRINTER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class JammerFingerprinter {
  private fingerprints: Map<string, JammerFingerprint>;
  private detectionHistory: JammingDetectionResult[];
  private readonly maxHistory = 200;

  constructor() {
    this.fingerprints = new Map();
    this.detectionHistory = [];
  }

  /**
   * Record a jamming detection event and build/update fingerprint
   */
  record(detection: JammingDetectionResult): JammerFingerprint | null {
    this.detectionHistory.push(detection);
    if (this.detectionHistory.length > this.maxHistory) this.detectionHistory.shift();

    if (detection.jammingType === 'none') return null;

    // Try to match to existing fingerprint
    const matchedId = this.matchToExisting(detection);
    if (matchedId) {
      const fp = this.fingerprints.get(matchedId)!;
      fp.lastSeenAt = detection.detectedAt;
      fp.occurrenceCount++;
      fp.averagePowerDbm = (fp.averagePowerDbm * (fp.occurrenceCount - 1) + detection.estimatedJammerPowerDbm) / fp.occurrenceCount;
      return fp;
    }

    // Create new fingerprint
    const fp: JammerFingerprint = {
      fingerprintId: this.generateId(),
      firstSeenAt: detection.detectedAt,
      lastSeenAt: detection.detectedAt,
      jammingType: detection.jammingType,
      centerFreqHz: detection.affectedFreqRanges.length > 0
        ? (detection.affectedFreqRanges[0].minHz + detection.affectedFreqRanges[0].maxHz) / 2
        : null,
      bandwidth: detection.affectedFreqRanges.length > 0
        ? detection.affectedFreqRanges[0].maxHz - detection.affectedFreqRanges[0].minHz
        : null,
      sweepRateHzPerMs: this.estimateSweepRate(),
      pulseRepetitionRateHz: this.estimatePulseRate(),
      reactionTimeMs: this.estimateReactionTime(detection),
      averagePowerDbm: detection.estimatedJammerPowerDbm,
      occurrenceCount: 1,
    };

    this.fingerprints.set(fp.fingerprintId, fp);
    return fp;
  }

  private matchToExisting(detection: JammingDetectionResult): string | null {
    for (const [id, fp] of this.fingerprints) {
      if (fp.jammingType !== detection.jammingType) continue;
      if (Date.now() - fp.lastSeenAt > 300000) continue; // 5 min stale

      // For spot jammers: check frequency proximity
      if (fp.jammingType === 'spot' && detection.affectedFreqRanges.length > 0) {
        const detFreq = (detection.affectedFreqRanges[0].minHz + detection.affectedFreqRanges[0].maxHz) / 2;
        if (fp.centerFreqHz && Math.abs(detFreq - fp.centerFreqHz) < 1e6) return id; // within 1 MHz
      }

      // For broadband/pulse: match by power level
      if (['broadband', 'pulse'].includes(fp.jammingType)) {
        const powerDiff = Math.abs(fp.averagePowerDbm - detection.estimatedJammerPowerDbm);
        if (powerDiff < 5) return id; // within 5 dB
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  private estimateSweepRate(): number | null {
    const sweepEvents = this.detectionHistory.filter(d => d.jammingType === 'sweep');
    if (sweepEvents.length < 3) return null;

    // Look at frequency progression between events
    const rates: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < sweepEvents.length; i++) {
      const dt = sweepEvents[i].detectedAt - sweepEvents[i-1].detectedAt;
      if (dt === 0) continue;
      const df = sweepEvents[i].affectedFreqRanges[0]?.minHz - sweepEvents[i-1].affectedFreqRanges[0]?.minHz;
      if (df !== undefined) rates.push(Math.abs(df / dt));
    }

    return rates.length > 0 ? rates.reduce((s, r) => s + r, 0) / rates.length : null;
  }

  private estimatePulseRate(): number | null {
    const pulseEvents = this.detectionHistory.filter(d => d.jammingType === 'pulse');
    if (pulseEvents.length < 4) return null;

    const intervals: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < pulseEvents.length; i++) {
      intervals.push(pulseEvents[i].detectedAt - pulseEvents[i-1].detectedAt);
    }

    const avgIntervalMs = intervals.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / intervals.length;
    return avgIntervalMs > 0 ? 1000 / avgIntervalMs : null;
  }

  private estimateReactionTime(detection: JammingDetectionResult): number | null {
    if (detection.jammingType !== 'reactive') return null;

    // In a reactive jammer, the jam appears some ms after our transmission
    // Without knowing our TX time precisely, return null for now
    // In production: timestamp TX start and measure onset delta
    return null;
  }

  private generateId(): string {
    const b = new Uint8Array(8);
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) b[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
    return Array.from(b).map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }

  getFingerprints(): JammerFingerprint[] {
    return Array.from(this.fingerprints.values());
  }

  getActiveFingerprintCount(): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    return Array.from(this.fingerprints.values()).filter(fp => now - fp.lastSeenAt < 60000).length;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// JAMMING DEFENSE ENGINE — Main orchestrator
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class JammingDefenseEngine {
  private readonly config: JammingDefenseConfig;
  private readonly channels: number[];       // Available center frequencies
  private readonly hopGen: HopSequenceGenerator;
  private readonly cusumDetectors: Map<number, CUSUMDetector>; // per channel
  private readonly heatMap: SpectrumHeatMapBuilder;
  private readonly dsss: DSSSEngine;
  private readonly fingerprinter: JammerFingerprinter;

  private state: DefenseState;
  private adaptationTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;

  // Callbacks
  private onJammingDetected?: (result: JammingDetectionResult) => void;
  private onChannelHopped?: (fromFreq: number, toFreq: number) => void;
  private onModeChanged?: (mode: SpreadSpectrumMode) => void;
  private onTransmit?: (data: Uint8Array, freqHz: number, spreadMode: SpreadSpectrumMode) => Promise<boolean>;

  // Statistics
  private stats = {
    jammingDetections: 0,
    totalHops: 0,
    successfulTransmissions: 0,
    failedTransmissions: 0,
    decoysSent: 0,
    channelsBlacklisted: 0,
    rekeysPerformed: 0,
    totalMeasurements: 0,
  };

  constructor(config: Partial<JammingDefenseConfig> = {}) {
    this.config = { ...DEFAULT_JAMMING_CONFIG, ...config };

    // Build channel list
    this.channels = [];
    for (let f = this.config.minFreqHz; f <= this.config.maxFreqHz; f += this.config.channelBandwidthHz) {
      this.channels.push(f);
    }

    this.hopGen = new HopSequenceGenerator(
      this.config.hopSeedKey,
      this.config.hopSequenceLength
    );

    // CUSUM detector per channel — baseline at noise floor
    this.cusumDetectors = new Map();
    for (let i = 0; i < this.channels.length; i++) {
      this.cusumDetectors.set(i, new CUSUMDetector(
        this.config.noiseFloorDbm,
        3.0,   // σ = 3 dBm typical noise variation
        this.config.cusumSlack,
        this.config.cusumThreshold
      ));
    }

    this.heatMap = new SpectrumHeatMapBuilder(
      this.config.minFreqHz,
      this.config.maxFreqHz,
      this.config.channelBandwidthHz,
      this.config.noiseFloorDbm,
      this.config.jammingThresholdDbm
    );

    this.dsss = new DSSSEngine(this.config.dsssChippingRate);
    this.fingerprinter = new JammerFingerprinter();

    const initialChannel = 0;
    this.state = {
      currentMode: this.config.defaultSpreadMode,
      currentChannelIndex: initialChannel,
      currentFreqHz: this.channels[initialChannel],
      hopSequencePosition: 0,
      activeChannels: [initialChannel],
      spreadingGain: 10,
      jammingActive: false,
      jammingType: 'none',
      blacklistedChannels: new Map(),
      lastHopTime: Date.now(),
      consecutiveJammedHops: 0,
      totalHops: 0,
      totalJammedChannels: 0,
      decoysSent: 0,
    };

    this.adaptationTimer = null;
  }

  // ─── STARTUP / SHUTDOWN ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  start(): void {
    this.adaptationTimer = setInterval(() => {
      this.runAdaptationCycle();
    }, this.config.adaptationIntervalMs);
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.adaptationTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.adaptationTimer);
      this.adaptationTimer = null;
    }
  }

  // ─── CHANNEL MEASUREMENT ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Process a channel energy measurement
   * This is the primary input from the radio layer
   */
  processMeasurement(measurement: ChannelMeasurement): JammingDetectionResult | null {
    this.stats.totalMeasurements++;

    // Update heat map
    this.heatMap.updateFromMeasurement(measurement, this.config.channelBandwidthHz);

    // Run CUSUM detection
    const detector = this.cusumDetectors.get(measurement.channelIndex);
    if (!detector) return null;

    const { alarm, statistic } = detector.update(measurement.rssiDbm);

    if (!alarm && measurement.rssiDbm < this.config.jammingThresholdDbm) {
      return null; // No jamming detected
    }

    // Jamming detected — classify
    const jammingType = this.classifyJamming(measurement, statistic);
    if (jammingType === 'none') return null;

    const result: JammingDetectionResult = {
      detectedAt: measurement.timestamp,
      jammingType,
      confidence: Math.min(1, statistic / (this.config.cusumThreshold * 2)),
      affectedChannels: [measurement.channelIndex],
      affectedFreqRanges: [{
        minHz: measurement.centerFreqHz - this.config.channelBandwidthHz / 2,
        maxHz: measurement.centerFreqHz + this.config.channelBandwidthHz / 2,
      }],
      estimatedJammerPowerDbm: measurement.rssiDbm,
      recommendedAction: this.selectDefenseAction(jammingType, measurement),
      cusumStatistic: statistic,
    };

    this.stats.jammingDetections++;
    this.state.jammingActive = true;
    this.state.jammingType = jammingType;

    // Blacklist this channel
    this.blacklistChannel(measurement.channelIndex);

    // Fingerprint the jammer
    this.fingerprinter.record(result);

    this.onJammingDetected?.(result);

    // Execute defense action
    this.executeDefenseAction(result.recommendedAction, result);

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Classify jamming type from measurement characteristics
   */
  private classifyJamming(measurement: ChannelMeasurement, cusumStatistic: number): JammingType {
    const power = measurement.rssiDbm;
    if (power < this.config.jammingThresholdDbm) return 'none';

    const heatMapSnapshot = this.heatMap.getLatestSnapshot();
    const jammingBandwidth = heatMapSnapshot
      ? heatMapSnapshot.jammingRegions.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.maxHz - r.minHz), 0)
      : 0;
    const totalBandwidth = this.config.maxFreqHz - this.config.minFreqHz;

    // Broadband: jams >80% of available spectrum
    if (jammingBandwidth > totalBandwidth * 0.8) return 'broadband';

    // Analyze temporal pattern from CUSUM history
    const recentDetections = this.fingerprinter.getFingerprints();

    // Pulse jammer: periodic high-power bursts
    const pulseFP = recentDetections.find(fp => fp.jammingType === 'pulse' && fp.pulseRepetitionRateHz !== null);
    if (pulseFP && pulseFP.occurrenceCount > 3) return 'pulse';

    // Sweep jammer: moving center frequency
    const sweepFP = recentDetections.find(fp => fp.jammingType === 'sweep' && fp.sweepRateHzPerMs !== null);
    if (sweepFP) return 'sweep';

    // Reactive jammer: only appears when we transmit (hard to detect without TX correlation)
    // Heuristic: short-duration interference bursts
    if (measurement.measurementDurationUs < 1000 && cusumStatistic > this.config.cusumThreshold * 3) {
      return 'reactive';
    }

    // Default: spot jammer
    return 'spot';
  }

  // ─── DEFENSE ACTION SELECTION ─────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Select best defense action based on jamming type and current state
   */
  private selectDefenseAction(
    jammingType: JammingType,
    measurement: ChannelMeasurement
  ): DefenseAction {
    switch (jammingType) {
      case 'broadband':
        // Full band jammed — DSSS spreading is best (operates below noise floor)
        return 'switch_to_dsss';

      case 'spot':
        // Single frequency — hop away
        return 'hop_channel';

      case 'sweep':
        // Sweeping — FHSS with fast hopping beats the sweep
        return 'switch_to_fhss';

      case 'pulse':
        // Periodic bursts — time-hop to transmit between pulses
        return 'switch_to_fhss';

      case 'reactive':
        // Reacts to our TX — use decoys to burn jammer capacity, then TX real data
        return 'deploy_decoys';

      case 'smart':
        // Has learned our pattern — re-key hop sequence
        return 'hop_channel';

      case 'partial_band':
        // Part of band jammed — move to clear region
        return 'hop_channel';

      default:
        return 'none';
    }
  }

  // ─── DEFENSE EXECUTION ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private executeDefenseAction(action: DefenseAction, context: JammingDetectionResult): void {
    switch (action) {
      case 'hop_channel':
        this.performHop();
        break;

      case 'switch_to_dsss':
        this.switchSpreadMode('dsss');
        break;

      case 'switch_to_fhss':
        this.switchSpreadMode('fhss');
        break;

      case 'increase_spreading':
        this.increaseSpreadingGain();
        break;

      case 'multi_channel_redundancy':
        this.activateMultiChannelRedundancy();
        break;

      case 'deploy_decoys':
        this.deployDecoys();
        break;

      case 'emergency_silence':
        // Stop all transmissions — use alternative channel (optical, acoustic)
        break;

      case 'switch_band':
        this.switchBand();
        break;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Perform frequency hop to next non-jammed channel in sequence
   */
  private performHop(): void {
    const maxAttempts = this.channels.length;
    let attempts = 0;
    let nextChannel = this.state.currentChannelIndex;

    while (attempts < maxAttempts) {
      nextChannel = this.hopGen.next(this.channels.length);

      // Skip blacklisted channels
      if (this.isChannelBlacklisted(nextChannel)) {
        attempts++;
        continue;
      }

      break;
    }

    if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {
      // All channels blacklisted — clear old blacklists and try again
      this.clearExpiredBlacklists();
      nextChannel = this.hopGen.next(this.channels.length);
    }

    const fromFreq = this.state.currentFreqHz;
    const toFreq = this.channels[nextChannel];

    this.state.currentChannelIndex = nextChannel;
    this.state.currentFreqHz = toFreq;
    this.state.lastHopTime = Date.now();
    this.state.totalHops++;
    this.stats.totalHops++;

    this.onChannelHopped?.(fromFreq, toFreq);
  }

  /**
   * Switch spread spectrum mode
   */
  private switchSpreadMode(mode: SpreadSpectrumMode): void {
    if (this.state.currentMode === mode) return;

    this.state.currentMode = mode;

    if (mode === 'dsss') {
      this.state.spreadingGain = this.dsss.processingGainDb;
    } else if (mode === 'fhss') {
      this.state.spreadingGain = 10;
    }

    this.onModeChanged?.(mode);
  }

  /**
   * Increase spreading gain for better jamming resistance
   */
  private increaseSpreadingGain(): void {
    this.state.spreadingGain = Math.min(
      this.config.maxSpreadingGain,
      this.state.spreadingGain + 3
    );
  }

  /**
   * Activate multi-channel redundant transmission
   */
  private activateMultiChannelRedundancy(): void {
    const clearFreqs = this.heatMap.findClearChannels(
      this.config.channelBandwidthHz,
      this.config.simultaneousChannels
    );

    this.state.activeChannels = clearFreqs.map(f => {
      return this.channels.findIndex(ch => Math.abs(ch - f) < this.config.channelBandwidthHz / 2);
    }).filter(i => i >= 0);
  }

  /**
   * Deploy decoy transmissions to exhaust reactive jammer capacity
   * Anti-reactive-jammer protocol:
   * 1. Send N decoy packets (noise-like, no useful data)
   * 2. While jammer is busy responding to decoys, send real data
   */
  private async deployDecoys(): Promise<void> {
    const numDecoys = Math.floor(1 / this.config.decoyTransmitProbability);

    for (let i = 0; i < numDecoys; i++) {
      // Decoy: random data on a sacrificial channel
      const decoyChannel = this.hopGen.next(this.channels.length);
      const decoyData = new Uint8Array(64);
      for (let j = 0; j < decoyData.length; j++) decoyData[j] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);

      if (this.onTransmit) {
        await this.onTransmit(decoyData, this.channels[decoyChannel], 'fhss');
      }

      this.state.decoysSent++;
      this.stats.decoysSent++;

      // Small delay between decoys
      await this.delay(Math.random() * this.config.decoyDelayMaxMs);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Switch to alternate frequency band (if multi-band radio available)
   */
  private switchBand(): void {
    // In production: instruct radio to switch to 2.4 GHz or 5.8 GHz
    // Here we hop to end of current band as proxy
    this.performHop();
  }

  // ─── CHANNEL MANAGEMENT ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private blacklistChannel(channelIndex: number): void {
    const expiry = Date.now() + this.config.blacklistDurationMs;
    this.state.blacklistedChannels.set(channelIndex, expiry);
    this.state.totalJammedChannels++;
    this.stats.channelsBlacklisted++;
  }

  private isChannelBlacklisted(channelIndex: number): boolean {
    const expiry = this.state.blacklistedChannels.get(channelIndex);
    if (!expiry) return false;
    if (Date.now() > expiry) {
      this.state.blacklistedChannels.delete(channelIndex);
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  private clearExpiredBlacklists(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [ch, expiry] of this.state.blacklistedChannels) {
      if (now > expiry) this.state.blacklistedChannels.delete(ch);
    }
  }

  // ─── TRANSMISSION WITH JAMMING PROTECTION ─────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Transmit data with full jamming defense applied
   */
  async transmitSecure(request: TransmissionRequest): Promise<TransmissionResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    let attempts = 0;
    let success = false;
    let jammingEncountered = false;
    let hopsPerformed = 0;
    const channelsUsed: number[] = [];

    while (attempts < request.maxRetries && Date.now() < request.deadlineMs) {
      attempts++;

      // Deploy decoys if reactive jammer detected
      if (this.state.jammingType === 'reactive' &&
          Math.random() < this.config.decoyTransmitProbability) {
        await this.deployDecoys();
        jammingEncountered = true;
      }

      // Apply spread spectrum encoding
      let transmitData: Uint8Array;
      if (this.state.currentMode === 'dsss') {
        const bits = Array.from(request.payload).flatMap(byte =>
          Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => (byte >> (7 - i)) & 1)
        );
        const chips = this.dsss.spread(bits);
        transmitData = new Uint8Array(chips.buffer);
      } else {
        transmitData = request.payload;
      }

      // Multi-channel redundancy
      if (this.state.activeChannels.length > 1) {
        const txResults = await Promise.all(
          this.state.activeChannels.map(async chIdx => {
            channelsUsed.push(chIdx);
            return this.onTransmit?.(transmitData, this.channels[chIdx], this.state.currentMode) ?? false;
          })
        );
        success = txResults.some(r => r === true);
      } else {
        channelsUsed.push(this.state.currentChannelIndex);
        success = await this.onTransmit?.(transmitData, this.state.currentFreqHz, this.state.currentMode) ?? false;
      }

      if (success) break;

      // TX failed — assume jamming, hop and retry
      jammingEncountered = true;
      this.performHop();
      hopsPerformed++;

      // If consecutive failures suggest smart jammer learned our sequence
      if (hopsPerformed > 5 && hopsPerformed % 5 === 0) {
        this.rekeyHopSequence();
      }
    }

    if (success) this.stats.successfulTransmissions++;
    else this.stats.failedTransmissions++;

    return {
      requestId: request.requestId,
      success,
      channelsUsed: [...new Set(channelsUsed)],
      attemptCount: attempts,
      totalDurationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      jammingEncountered,
      hopsPerformed,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Re-key the hop sequence when smart jammer has learned it
   */
  private rekeyHopSequence(): void {
    const newSeed = new Uint8Array(32);
    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) newSeed[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
    this.hopGen.rekey(newSeed);
    this.stats.rekeysPerformed++;
  }

  // ─── ADAPTATION CYCLE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private runAdaptationCycle(): void {
    // Clear expired blacklists
    this.clearExpiredBlacklists();

    // If no jamming recently, relax defense posture
    if (this.state.jammingActive) {
      const recentActive = this.fingerprinter.getFingerprints()
        .some(fp => Date.now() - fp.lastSeenAt < this.config.adaptationIntervalMs * 3);

      if (!recentActive) {
        this.state.jammingActive = false;
        this.state.jammingType = 'none';
        this.state.consecutiveJammedHops = 0;
        // Return to default mode
        this.switchSpreadMode(this.config.defaultSpreadMode);
        this.state.activeChannels = [this.state.currentChannelIndex];
      }
    }

    // Schedule next hop if FHSS active
    if (this.state.currentMode === 'fhss' || this.state.currentMode === 'hybrid_fhss_dsss') {
      const timeSinceHop = Date.now() - this.state.lastHopTime;
      if (timeSinceHop > this.config.hopIntervalMs) {
        this.performHop();
      }
    }

    // Rebuild heat map snapshot periodically
    this.heatMap.buildSnapshot();
  }

  // ─── CALLBACKS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  onJammingDetectedCallback(cb: (result: JammingDetectionResult) => void): void {
    this.onJammingDetected = cb;
  }

  onChannelHoppedCallback(cb: (from: number, to: number) => void): void {
    this.onChannelHopped = cb;
  }

  onModeChangedCallback(cb: (mode: SpreadSpectrumMode) => void): void {
    this.onModeChanged = cb;
  }

  setTransmitCallback(cb: (data: Uint8Array, freqHz: number, mode: SpreadSpectrumMode) => Promise<boolean>): void {
    this.onTransmit = cb;
  }

  // ─── STATUS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getState(): DefenseState {
    return {
      ...this.state,
      blacklistedChannels: new Map(this.state.blacklistedChannels),
    };
  }

  getStats() { return { ...this.stats }; }

  getHeatMap(): SpectrumHeatMap | null {
    return this.heatMap.buildSnapshot();
  }

  getJammerFingerprints(): JammerFingerprint[] {
    return this.fingerprinter.getFingerprints();
  }

  getChannelCount(): number { return this.channels.length; }

  getAvailableChannels(): number[] {
    return this.channels.filter((_, idx) => !this.isChannelBlacklisted(idx));
  }
