  getClearChannelCount(): number {
    return this.channels.filter((_, idx) => !this.isChannelBlacklisted(idx)).length;
  }

  getDSSSProcessingGain(): number { return this.dsss.processingGainDb; }

  private delay(ms: number): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// FACTORY & EXPORT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createJammingDefenseEngine(
  config: Partial<JammingDefenseConfig> = {}
): JammingDefenseEngine {
  return new JammingDefenseEngine(config);
}

export {
  HopSequenceGenerator,
  CUSUMDetector,
  DSSSEngine,
  JammerFingerprinter,
};

export default JammingDefenseEngine;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — loraMesh.ts
 * LoRa Radio Mesh Networking — Internet-Optional Operation
 *
 * LoRa (Long Range) operates at 433/868/915 MHz with 2–15 km range per hop.
 * No internet. No cellular. No WiFi. Just radio.
 *
 * Capabilities implemented:
 *   LoRaRadioInterface   — Hardware abstraction over serial/GPIO (SX1276/SX1278/SX1262)
 *   FrequencyHopper      — FHSS: 50-channel frequency hopping spread spectrum
 *   PacketFragmenter     — Fragment/reassemble for LoRa's 255-byte payload limit
 *   LoRaMeshRouter       — Multi-hop routing over LoRa with flooding + gradient routing
 *   ARQEngine            — Stop-and-wait + selective-repeat ARQ for reliable delivery
 *   ChannelScanner       — Scan for active nodes and interference before transmission
 *   DutyCycleManager     — Enforce EU 1% / US FCC duty cycle regulations
 *   LoRaMeshNode         — Full mesh node for off-grid UBE operation
 *
 * Hardware target: Raspberry Pi + RAK811/SX1276 module via serial UART
 * or direct SPI via pigpio. Works with any AT-command LoRa module.
 *
 * Physical layer specs used:
 *   Bandwidth:      125 kHz (configurable: 62.5, 125, 250, 500)
 *   Spreading Factor: SF7–SF12 (SF12 = max range, min throughput ~250 bps)
 *   Coding Rate:    4/5 – 4/8
 *   Preamble:       8 symbols
 *   Max payload:    255 bytes (hardware), 222 bytes (practical with overhead)
 */

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import * as crypto from 'crypto';
import { Writable, Readable } from 'stream';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

const LORA_MAX_PAYLOAD = 222;          // Bytes after headers
const LORA_HEADER_SIZE = 12;           // Mesh header overhead
const LORA_FRAGMENT_DATA_SIZE = LORA_MAX_PAYLOAD - LORA_HEADER_SIZE - 4; // 4B frag header
const LORA_DEFAULT_FREQ_HZ = 915_000_000;  // 915 MHz (North America)
const LORA_EU_FREQ_HZ = 868_100_000;       // 868.1 MHz (Europe)
const LORA_ASIA_FREQ_HZ = 433_175_000;     // 433.175 MHz (Asia)
const LORA_FHSS_CHANNELS = 50;
const LORA_FHSS_DWELL_MS = 400;
const LORA_EU_DUTY_CYCLE = 0.01;       // 1% duty cycle
const LORA_US_DWELL_LIMIT_MS = 400;    // 400ms max dwell per channel
const LORA_ARQ_MAX_RETRIES = 5;
const LORA_ARQ_TIMEOUT_BASE_MS = 2_000;
const LORA_MESH_TTL = 7;
const LORA_NODE_ID_BYTES = 4;          // 4-byte node IDs (bandwidth constrained)
const LORA_BEACON_INTERVAL_MS = 60_000;
const LORA_ROUTE_TIMEOUT_MS = 300_000;
const LORA_MAX_FRAGMENT_REASSEMBLY_MS = 30_000;
const LORA_CAD_TIMEOUT_MS = 100;       // Channel Activity Detection

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export type LoRaRegion = 'EU868' | 'US915' | 'AU915' | 'AS923' | 'CN470' | 'IN865';
export type LoRaSF = 7 | 8 | 9 | 10 | 11 | 12;
export type LoRaBW = 62.5 | 125 | 250 | 500;
export type LoRaCR = '4/5' | '4/6' | '4/7' | '4/8';

export type LoRaPacketType =
  | 'DATA'
  | 'DATA_ACK'
  | 'FRAGMENT'
  | 'FRAGMENT_ACK'
  | 'BEACON'
  | 'ROUTE_REQ'
  | 'ROUTE_REPLY'
  | 'ROUTE_ERROR'
  | 'BROADCAST'
  | 'PING'
  | 'PONG';

export interface LoRaConfig {
  region: LoRaRegion;
  spreadingFactor: LoRaSF;
  bandwidth: LoRaBW;
  codingRate: LoRaCR;
  txPower: number;          // dBm, max 20 dBm (14 dBm EU limit)
  syncWord: number;         // Network identifier (0x12 = public, 0x34+ = private)
  enableFHSS: boolean;
  enableCRC: boolean;
  serialPort?: string;      // e.g., '/dev/ttyS0', 'COM3'
  spiDevice?: string;       // e.g., '/dev/spidev0.0'
}

export interface LoRaPacket {
  type: LoRaPacketType;
  srcId: Buffer;            // 4-byte source node ID
  dstId: Buffer;            // 4-byte destination ID (0xFFFFFFFF = broadcast)
  msgId: number;            // 16-bit sequence number
  ttl: number;              // Hop limit
  flags: number;            // Bit flags (ACK_REQ, FRAG, etc.)
  payload: Buffer;
  rssi?: number;            // Received Signal Strength (dBm)
  snr?: number;             // Signal-to-Noise Ratio (dB)
  hopCount: number;
}

export interface LoRaRadioStats {
  txPackets: number;
  rxPackets: number;
  txBytes: number;
  rxBytes: number;
  txErrors: number;
  rxCRCErrors: number;
  collisions: number;
  channelBusy: number;
  dutyCycleUsed: number;    // 0.0–1.0
  currentRSSI: number;
  currentSNR: number;
  temperature?: number;     // Module temperature if available
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LORA SHORT NODE ID
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export function deriveLoRaId(fullNodeId: Buffer): Buffer {
  // XOR-fold the full 20-byte node ID into 4 bytes
  const id = Buffer.alloc(4, 0);
  for (let i = 0; i < fullNodeId.length; i++) {
    id[i % 4] ^= fullNodeId[i];
  }
  return id;
}

export const BROADCAST_ID = Buffer.from([0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF]);

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// FREQUENCY HOPPER — FHSS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class FrequencyHopper {
  private readonly channels: number[];
  private currentIndex: number = 0;
  private lastHop: number = 0;
  private readonly hopKey: Buffer;   // Shared secret for synchronized hopping
  private hopping: boolean = false;
  private hopTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  constructor(region: LoRaRegion, hopKey: Buffer) {
    this.channels = FrequencyHopper.generateChannelPlan(region, LORA_FHSS_CHANNELS);
    this.hopKey = hopKey;
  }

  static generateChannelPlan(region: LoRaRegion, count: number): number[] {
    const plans: Record<LoRaRegion, { base: number; step: number }> = {
      EU868: { base: 867_100_000, step: 200_000 },
      US915: { base: 902_300_000, step: 200_000 },
      AU915: { base: 915_200_000, step: 200_000 },
      AS923: { base: 923_200_000, step: 200_000 },
      CN470: { base: 470_300_000, step: 200_000 },
      IN865: { base: 865_062_500, step: 200_000 },
    };
    const plan = plans[region];
    const channels: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      channels.push(plan.base + i * plan.step);
    }
    // Pseudorandom shuffle with hop key for network isolation
    const seed = crypto.createHash('sha256').update(Buffer.from(count.toString())).digest();
    return FrequencyHopper.shuffleWithSeed(channels, seed);
  }

  private static shuffleWithSeed(arr: number[], seed: Buffer): number[] {
    const result = [...arr];
    for (let i = result.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = seed[i % seed.length] % (i + 1);
      [result[i], result[j]] = [result[j], result[i]];
    }
    return result;
  }

  currentFrequency(): number {
    return this.channels[this.currentIndex];
  }

  nextFrequency(): number {
    this.currentIndex = (this.currentIndex + 1) % this.channels.length;
    this.lastHop = Date.now();
    return this.channels[this.currentIndex];
  }

  // Deterministic channel for a given time window — enables receiver sync
  channelForTime(timestamp: number): number {
    const window = Math.floor(timestamp / LORA_FHSS_DWELL_MS);
    const hmac = crypto.createHmac('sha256', this.hopKey)
      .update(Buffer.from(window.toString()))
      .digest();
    return this.channels[hmac.readUInt32BE(0) % this.channels.length];
  }

  startHopping(onHop: (freq: number) => void): void {
    this.hopping = true;
    this.hopTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (this.hopping) onHop(this.nextFrequency());
    }, LORA_FHSS_DWELL_MS);
  }

  stopHopping(): void {
    this.hopping = false;
    if (this.hopTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.hopTimer);
      this.hopTimer = null;
    }
  }

  allChannels(): number[] {
    return [...this.channels];
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// DUTY CYCLE MANAGER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class DutyCycleManager {
  private txHistory: Array<{ startMs: number; durationMs: number }> = [];
  private readonly windowMs: number = 3_600_000; // 1 hour rolling window
  private readonly maxDutyCycle: number;
  private readonly region: LoRaRegion;

  constructor(region: LoRaRegion) {
    this.region = region;
    this.maxDutyCycle = region === 'EU868' ? LORA_EU_DUTY_CYCLE : 1.0;
  }

  canTransmit(estimatedDurationMs: number): boolean {
    if (this.maxDutyCycle >= 1.0) return true;

    const used = this.currentUsage();
    const window = this.windowMs;
    const maxMs = window * this.maxDutyCycle;
    return (used + estimatedDurationMs) <= maxMs;
  }

  recordTransmission(durationMs: number): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    this.txHistory.push({ startMs: now, durationMs });
    this.sweep(now);
  }

  currentUsage(): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    this.sweep(now);
    return this.txHistory.reduce((sum, tx) => sum + tx.durationMs, 0);
  }

  dutyCyclePercent(): number {
    return (this.currentUsage() / this.windowMs) * 100;
  }

  remainingBudgetMs(): number {
    const maxMs = this.windowMs * this.maxDutyCycle;
    return Math.max(0, maxMs - this.currentUsage());
  }

  estimateAirtime(payloadBytes: number, sf: LoRaSF, bw: LoRaBW, cr: LoRaCR): number {
    // Semtech airtime formula
    const tSym = (Math.pow(2, sf) / (bw * 1000)) * 1000; // ms per symbol
    const preamble = 8;
    const header = 1;
    const crNum = parseInt(cr.split('/')[1]);

    // Payload symbol count
    const payloadSymbols = 8 + Math.max(
      Math.ceil((8 * payloadBytes - 4 * sf + 28 + 16) / (4 * sf)) * crNum,
      0,
    );

    const nPreamble = preamble + 4.25;
    return Math.ceil((nPreamble + payloadSymbols) * tSym);
  }

  private sweep(now: number): void {
    const cutoff = now - this.windowMs;
    this.txHistory = this.txHistory.filter(tx => tx.startMs > cutoff);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// PACKET FRAGMENTER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface FragmentHeader {
  msgId: number;        // 16-bit message ID
  fragIndex: number;    // 8-bit fragment index (0-based)
  fragTotal: number;    // 8-bit total fragments
  checksum: number;     // 16-bit CRC of fragment data
}

export interface ReassemblyBuffer {
  msgId: number;
  srcId: string;
  fragments: Map<number, Buffer>;
  totalFragments: number;
  receivedAt: number;
  lastFragment: number;
}

export class PacketFragmenter {
  private reassemblyBuffers: Map<string, ReassemblyBuffer> = new Map();
  private msgIdCounter: number = Math.floor(Math.random() * 65536);

  fragment(data: Buffer): Array<{ header: FragmentHeader; payload: Buffer }> {
    const dataSize = LORA_FRAGMENT_DATA_SIZE;
    const totalFragments = Math.ceil(data.length / dataSize);
    if (totalFragments > 255) throw new Error(`Data too large: ${data.length} bytes requires ${totalFragments} fragments (max 255)`);

    const msgId = this.msgIdCounter = (this.msgIdCounter + 1) & 0xFFFF;
    const fragments: Array<{ header: FragmentHeader; payload: Buffer }> = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < totalFragments; i++) {
      const start = i * dataSize;
      const payload = data.slice(start, start + dataSize);
      const checksum = PacketFragmenter.crc16(payload);
      fragments.push({
        header: { msgId, fragIndex: i, fragTotal: totalFragments, checksum },
        payload,
      });
    }
    return fragments;
  }

  serializeFragment(header: FragmentHeader, payload: Buffer): Buffer {
    const buf = Buffer.allocUnsafe(4 + payload.length);
    buf.writeUInt16BE(header.msgId, 0);
    buf[2] = header.fragIndex;
    buf[3] = header.fragTotal;
    // Note: checksum embedded in header but not in wire format for brevity — payload CRC in packet CRC
    payload.copy(buf, 4);
    return buf;
  }

  deserializeFragment(buf: Buffer): { header: FragmentHeader; payload: Buffer } | null {
    if (buf.length < 5) return null;
    return {
      header: {
        msgId: buf.readUInt16BE(0),
        fragIndex: buf[2],
        fragTotal: buf[3],
        checksum: 0,
      },
      payload: buf.slice(4),
    };
  }

  receive(srcId: string, header: FragmentHeader, payload: Buffer): Buffer | null {
    const key = `${srcId}:${header.msgId}`;
    const now = Date.now();

    if (!this.reassemblyBuffers.has(key)) {
      this.reassemblyBuffers.set(key, {
        msgId: header.msgId,
        srcId,
        fragments: new Map(),
        totalFragments: header.fragTotal,
        receivedAt: now,
        lastFragment: now,
      });
    }

    const buf = this.reassemblyBuffers.get(key)!;
    buf.fragments.set(header.fragIndex, payload);
    buf.lastFragment = now;

    if (buf.fragments.size === buf.totalFragments) {
      // Reassemble
      const parts: Buffer[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < buf.totalFragments; i++) {
        const part = buf.fragments.get(i);
        if (!part) return null; // Missing fragment
        parts.push(part);
      }
      this.reassemblyBuffers.delete(key);
      return Buffer.concat(parts);
    }

    return null; // Still waiting
  }

  getMissingFragments(srcId: string, msgId: number): number[] {
    const key = `${srcId}:${msgId}`;
    const buf = this.reassemblyBuffers.get(key);
    if (!buf) return [];

    const missing: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < buf.totalFragments; i++) {
      if (!buf.fragments.has(i)) missing.push(i);
    }
    return missing;
  }

  sweep(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [key, buf] of this.reassemblyBuffers) {
      if (now - buf.receivedAt > LORA_MAX_FRAGMENT_REASSEMBLY_MS) {
        this.reassemblyBuffers.delete(key);
      }
    }
  }

  static crc16(data: Buffer): number {
    let crc = 0xFFFF;
    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      crc ^= data[i] << 8;
      for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) {
        if (crc & 0x8000) crc = (crc << 1) ^ 0x1021;
        else crc <<= 1;
        crc &= 0xFFFF;
      }
    }
    return crc;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// ARQ ENGINE — Reliable delivery over lossy LoRa
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ARQFrame {
  msgId: number;
  data: Buffer;
  dstId: Buffer;
  attempts: number;
  sentAt: number;
  nextRetry: number;
  resolve: (success: boolean) => void;
  reject: (err: Error) => void;
}

export class ARQEngine {
  private pending: Map<number, ARQFrame> = new Map();
  private msgIdCounter: number = 0;
  private readonly maxRetries: number;
  private retryTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  constructor(maxRetries: number = LORA_ARQ_MAX_RETRIES) {
    this.maxRetries = maxRetries;
    this.retryTimer = setInterval(() => this.processRetries(), 500);
  }

  sendReliable(
    dstId: Buffer,
    data: Buffer,
    transmit: (dstId: Buffer, data: Buffer, msgId: number) => Promise<void>,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const msgId = this.msgIdCounter = (this.msgIdCounter + 1) & 0xFFFF;

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const frame: ARQFrame = {
        msgId,
        data,
        dstId,
        attempts: 0,
        sentAt: Date.now(),
        nextRetry: Date.now(),
        resolve,
        reject,
      };

      this.pending.set(msgId, frame);

      transmit(dstId, data, msgId).catch(() => {
        frame.attempts++;
        frame.nextRetry = Date.now() + LORA_ARQ_TIMEOUT_BASE_MS * Math.pow(2, frame.attempts);
      });
    });
  }

  acknowledge(msgId: number): void {
    const frame = this.pending.get(msgId);
    if (frame) {
      this.pending.delete(msgId);
      frame.resolve(true);
    }
  }

  private processRetries(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [msgId, frame] of this.pending) {
      if (now < frame.nextRetry) continue;

      if (frame.attempts >= this.maxRetries) {
        this.pending.delete(msgId);
        frame.resolve(false);
        continue;
      }

      frame.attempts++;
      frame.nextRetry = now + LORA_ARQ_TIMEOUT_BASE_MS * Math.pow(2, frame.attempts);
      // Signal retry needed
      this.emit?.('retry', { msgId, attempt: frame.attempts, dstId: frame.dstId, data: frame.data });
    }
  }

  private emit?: (event: string, ...args: any[]) => void;

  setEmitter(emitter: (event: string, ...args: any[]) => void): void {
    this.emit = emitter;
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.retryTimer) clearInterval(this.retryTimer);
    for (const frame of this.pending.values()) {
      frame.reject(new Error('ARQ engine stopped'));
    }
    this.pending.clear();
  }

  pendingCount(): number {
    return this.pending.size;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LORA ROUTING TABLE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface LoRaRoute {
  dstId: string;        // hex 4-byte dst
  nextHop: string;      // hex 4-byte next hop
  hopCount: number;
  rssi: number;         // Signal quality of route
  snr: number;
  lastUsed: number;
  discoveredAt: number;
}

export class LoRaRoutingTable {
  private routes: Map<string, LoRaRoute> = new Map();

  set(route: LoRaRoute): void {
    const existing = this.routes.get(route.dstId);
    if (!existing || route.hopCount < existing.hopCount ||
        (route.hopCount === existing.hopCount && route.rssi > existing.rssi)) {
      this.routes.set(route.dstId, route);
    }
  }

  get(dstId: string): LoRaRoute | undefined {
    const route = this.routes.get(dstId);
    if (route && Date.now() - route.discoveredAt > LORA_ROUTE_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      this.routes.delete(dstId);
      return undefined;
    }
    return route;
  }

  invalidate(dstId: string): void {
    this.routes.delete(dstId);
  }

  invalidateVia(nextHop: string): number {
    let count = 0;
    for (const [dst, route] of this.routes) {
      if (route.nextHop === nextHop) {
        this.routes.delete(dst);
        count++;
      }
    }
    return count;
  }

  allRoutes(): LoRaRoute[] {
    return Array.from(this.routes.values());
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LORA RADIO HARDWARE INTERFACE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ILoRaHardware extends EventEmitter {
  initialize(config: LoRaConfig): Promise<void>;
  setFrequency(hz: number): Promise<void>;
  setTxPower(dBm: number): Promise<void>;
  setSF(sf: LoRaSF): Promise<void>;
  setBW(bw: LoRaBW): Promise<void>;
  transmit(data: Buffer): Promise<number>;  // Returns airtime ms
  startReceive(): Promise<void>;
  stopReceive(): Promise<void>;
  channelActivityDetect(): Promise<boolean>; // True if channel busy
  getRSSI(): number;
  getSNR(): number;
  getTemperature(): number | null;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// SIMULATED LORA HARDWARE (for testing without physical hardware)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class SimulatedLoRaHardware extends EventEmitter implements ILoRaHardware {
  private currentFreq: number = LORA_DEFAULT_FREQ_HZ;
  private currentSF: LoRaSF = 7;
  private currentBW: LoRaBW = 125;
  private currentPower: number = 14;
  private receiving: boolean = false;
  private rssi: number = -85;
  private snr: number = 9;
  private readonly deviceId: string;

  // Shared simulation bus — all simulated nodes share this
  private static bus: Array<(srcId: string, data: Buffer, freq: number) => void> = [];
  private static listeners: Map<string, SimulatedLoRaHardware> = new Map();

  constructor(deviceId: string) {
    super();
    this.deviceId = deviceId;
    SimulatedLoRaHardware.listeners.set(deviceId, this);
  }

  async initialize(config: LoRaConfig): Promise<void> {
    this.currentFreq = config.region === 'EU868' ? LORA_EU_FREQ_HZ : LORA_DEFAULT_FREQ_HZ;
    this.currentSF = config.spreadingFactor;
    this.currentBW = config.bandwidth;
    this.currentPower = config.txPower;
  }

  async setFrequency(hz: number): Promise<void> { this.currentFreq = hz; }
  async setTxPower(dBm: number): Promise<void> { this.currentPower = dBm; }
  async setSF(sf: LoRaSF): Promise<void> { this.currentSF = sf; }
  async setBW(bw: LoRaBW): Promise<void> { this.currentBW = bw; }

  async transmit(data: Buffer): Promise<number> {
    const airtime = this.estimateAirtime(data.length);
    // Simulate transmission on shared bus
    for (const [id, hw] of SimulatedLoRaHardware.listeners) {
      if (id !== this.deviceId && hw.receiving && hw.currentFreq === this.currentFreq) {
        setTimeout(() => {
          hw.rssi = -80 - Math.random() * 40;
          hw.snr = 12 - Math.random() * 15;
          if (hw.rssi > -130) { // Signal strength sufficient
            hw.emit('data', data, { rssi: hw.rssi, snr: hw.snr });
          }
        }, Math.random() * 50);
      }
    }
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, airtime));
    return airtime;
  }

  async startReceive(): Promise<void> { this.receiving = true; }
  async stopReceive(): Promise<void> { this.receiving = false; }

  async channelActivityDetect(): Promise<boolean> {
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, LORA_CAD_TIMEOUT_MS));
    return Math.random() < 0.05; // 5% busy probability in simulation
  }

  getRSSI(): number { return this.rssi; }
  getSNR(): number { return this.snr; }
  getTemperature(): number | null { return 35 + Math.random() * 10; }

  async close(): Promise<void> {
    this.receiving = false;
    SimulatedLoRaHardware.listeners.delete(this.deviceId);
  }

  private estimateAirtime(bytes: number): number {
    // Rough estimate in ms
    const tSym = (Math.pow(2, this.currentSF) / (this.currentBW * 1000)) * 1000;
    return Math.ceil((bytes * 8 / 250) * tSym);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CHANNEL SCANNER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ChannelScan {
  frequency: number;
  rssi: number;
  busy: boolean;
  scannedAt: number;
}

export class ChannelScanner {
  private scanResults: Map<number, ChannelScan> = new Map();

  async scan(
    hardware: ILoRaHardware,
    channels: number[],
    dwellMs: number = 100,
  ): Promise<ChannelScan[]> {
    const results: ChannelScan[] = [];

    for (const freq of channels) {
      await hardware.setFrequency(freq);
      await hardware.startReceive();
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, dwellMs));
      const busy = await hardware.channelActivityDetect();
      const rssi = hardware.getRSSI();
      await hardware.stopReceive();

      const scan: ChannelScan = { frequency: freq, rssi, busy, scannedAt: Date.now() };
      this.scanResults.set(freq, scan);
      results.push(scan);
    }

    return results;
  }

  bestChannel(channels: number[]): number | null {
    let bestFreq: number | null = null;
    let bestRSSI = -Infinity;

    for (const freq of channels) {
      const scan = this.scanResults.get(freq);
      if (scan && !scan.busy && scan.rssi > bestRSSI) {
        bestRSSI = scan.rssi;
        bestFreq = freq;
      }
    }
    return bestFreq;
  }

  getCachedScan(frequency: number): ChannelScan | undefined {
    return this.scanResults.get(frequency);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// NEIGHBOR TABLE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface LoRaNeighbor {
  loraId: Buffer;         // 4-byte LoRa ID
  fullNodeId?: string;    // Full 20-byte hex node ID (from beacon)
  rssi: number;           // Latest RSSI
  snr: number;
  lastBeacon: number;
  txCount: number;
  rxCount: number;
  reachable: boolean;
}

export class LoRaNeighborTable {
  private neighbors: Map<string, LoRaNeighbor> = new Map();
  private readonly timeoutMs: number;

  constructor(timeoutMs: number = 3 * LORA_BEACON_INTERVAL_MS) {
    this.timeoutMs = timeoutMs;
  }

  update(loraId: Buffer, rssi: number, snr: number, fullNodeId?: string): void {
    const key = loraId.toString('hex');
    const existing = this.neighbors.get(key);
    if (existing) {
      existing.rssi = rssi;
      existing.snr = snr;
      existing.lastBeacon = Date.now();
      existing.reachable = true;
      if (fullNodeId) existing.fullNodeId = fullNodeId;
    } else {
      this.neighbors.set(key, {
        loraId,
        fullNodeId,
        rssi,
        snr,
        lastBeacon: Date.now(),
        txCount: 0,
        rxCount: 0,
        reachable: true,
      });
    }
  }

  get(loraId: Buffer): LoRaNeighbor | undefined {
    return this.neighbors.get(loraId.toString('hex'));
  }

  all(): LoRaNeighbor[] {
    const now = Date.now();
    return Array.from(this.neighbors.values()).filter(n => now - n.lastBeacon < this.timeoutMs);
  }

  reachable(): LoRaNeighbor[] {
    return this.all().filter(n => n.reachable);
  }

  bestNext(dstId: Buffer): LoRaNeighbor | null {
    const neighbors = this.reachable();
    if (neighbors.length === 0) return null;
    // Return neighbor with best RSSI that's not the destination
    return neighbors
      .filter(n => !n.loraId.equals(dstId))
      .sort((a, b) => b.rssi - a.rssi)[0] ?? null;
  }

  sweep(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [key, n] of this.neighbors) {
      if (now - n.lastBeacon > this.timeoutMs) this.neighbors.delete(key);
    }
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LORA MESH NODE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface LoRaMeshConfig {
  nodeId: Buffer;         // Full 20-byte node ID
  config: LoRaConfig;
  hardware?: ILoRaHardware;
  enableARQ?: boolean;
  enableFHSS?: boolean;
  hopKey?: Buffer;
}

export class LoRaMeshNode extends EventEmitter {
  public readonly loraId: Buffer;
  private readonly nodeId: Buffer;
  private readonly hardware: ILoRaHardware;
  private readonly loraConfig: LoRaConfig;
  private readonly fragmenter: PacketFragmenter;
  private readonly arq: ARQEngine;
  private readonly routingTable: LoRaRoutingTable;
  private readonly neighborTable: LoRaNeighborTable;
  private readonly dutyCycle: DutyCycleManager;
  private readonly channelScanner: ChannelScanner;
  private readonly frequencyHopper: FrequencyHopper | null;

  private running: boolean = false;
  private seenMessages: Map<string, number> = new Map();
  private beaconTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private sweepTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private msgSeq: number = 0;
  private stats: LoRaRadioStats;

  constructor(meshConfig: LoRaMeshConfig) {
    super();
    this.nodeId = meshConfig.nodeId;
    this.loraId = deriveLoRaId(meshConfig.nodeId);
    this.loraConfig = meshConfig.config;
    this.hardware = meshConfig.hardware ?? new SimulatedLoRaHardware(meshConfig.nodeId.toString('hex'));
    this.fragmenter = new PacketFragmenter();
    this.arq = new ARQEngine();
    this.routingTable = new LoRaRoutingTable();
    this.neighborTable = new LoRaNeighborTable();
    this.dutyCycle = new DutyCycleManager(meshConfig.config.region);
    this.channelScanner = new ChannelScanner();
    this.frequencyHopper = meshConfig.enableFHSS
      ? new FrequencyHopper(meshConfig.config.region, meshConfig.hopKey ?? crypto.randomBytes(32))
      : null;

    this.stats = {
      txPackets: 0, rxPackets: 0, txBytes: 0, rxBytes: 0,
      txErrors: 0, rxCRCErrors: 0, collisions: 0, channelBusy: 0,
      dutyCycleUsed: 0, currentRSSI: 0, currentSNR: 0,
    };

    this.arq.setEmitter((event, data) => {
      if (event === 'retry') {
        this.transmitPacket(data.dstId, 'DATA', data.data, data.msgId);
      }
    });
  }

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    await this.hardware.initialize(this.loraConfig);
    await this.hardware.startReceive();

    this.hardware.on('data', (rawData: Buffer, meta: { rssi: number; snr: number }) => {
      this.stats.rxPackets++;
      this.stats.rxBytes += rawData.length;
      this.stats.currentRSSI = meta.rssi;
      this.stats.currentSNR = meta.snr;
      this.handleIncoming(rawData, meta.rssi, meta.snr);
    });

    if (this.frequencyHopper) {
      this.frequencyHopper.startHopping(async (freq) => {
        await this.hardware.setFrequency(freq);
      });
    }

    this.beaconTimer = setInterval(() => this.sendBeacon(), LORA_BEACON_INTERVAL_MS);
    this.sweepTimer = setInterval(() => this.sweep(), 60_000);

    this.running = true;
    await this.sendBeacon();
    this.emit('started', { loraId: this.loraId.toString('hex') });
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    this.running = false;
    if (this.beaconTimer) clearInterval(this.beaconTimer);
    if (this.sweepTimer) clearInterval(this.sweepTimer);
    if (this.frequencyHopper) this.frequencyHopper.stopHopping();
    this.arq.stop();
    await this.hardware.stopReceive();
    await this.hardware.close();
    this.emit('stopped');
  }

  // ── SEND ───────────────────────────────────

  async send(dstId: Buffer, data: Buffer, reliable: boolean = true): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!this.running) return false;

    if (data.length <= LORA_FRAGMENT_DATA_SIZE) {
      // Single packet
      if (reliable) {
        return this.arq.sendReliable(dstId, data,
          (dst, d, msgId) => this.transmitPacket(dst, 'DATA', d, msgId));
      } else {
        return this.transmitPacket(dstId, 'DATA', data);
      }
    } else {
      // Fragment
      const fragments = this.fragmenter.fragment(data);
      let success = true;
      for (const frag of fragments) {
        const payload = this.fragmenter.serializeFragment(frag.header, frag.payload);
        const ok = reliable
          ? await this.arq.sendReliable(dstId, payload,
              (dst, d, msgId) => this.transmitPacket(dst, 'FRAGMENT', d, msgId))
          : await this.transmitPacket(dstId, 'FRAGMENT', payload);
        if (!ok) { success = false; break; }
      }
      return success;
    }
  }

  async broadcast(data: Buffer, type: LoRaPacketType = 'BROADCAST'): Promise<boolean> {
    return this.transmitPacket(BROADCAST_ID, type, data);
  }

  private async transmitPacket(
    dstId: Buffer,
    type: LoRaPacketType,
    payload: Buffer,
    msgId?: number,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const id = msgId ?? (this.msgSeq = (this.msgSeq + 1) & 0xFFFF);

    // Check channel busy
    const busy = await this.hardware.channelActivityDetect();
    if (busy) {
      this.stats.channelBusy++;
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100 + Math.random() * 400)); // Random backoff
    }

    const packet = this.serializePacket({
      type, srcId: this.loraId, dstId, msgId: id, ttl: LORA_MESH_TTL,
      flags: 0, payload, hopCount: 0,
    });

    const estimatedAirtime = this.dutyCycle.estimateAirtime(
      packet.length, this.loraConfig.spreadingFactor,
      this.loraConfig.bandwidth, this.loraConfig.codingRate,
    );

    if (!this.dutyCycle.canTransmit(estimatedAirtime)) {
      this.emit('duty_cycle:exceeded', {
        remaining: this.dutyCycle.remainingBudgetMs(),
        requested: estimatedAirtime,
      });
      return false;
    }

    try {
      const airtime = await this.hardware.transmit(packet);
      this.dutyCycle.recordTransmission(airtime);
      this.stats.txPackets++;
      this.stats.txBytes += packet.length;
      this.stats.dutyCycleUsed = this.dutyCycle.dutyCyclePercent() / 100;
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      this.stats.txErrors++;
      return false;
    }
  }

  // ── RECEIVE ────────────────────────────────

  private handleIncoming(raw: Buffer, rssi: number, snr: number): void {
    const packet = this.deserializePacket(raw);
    if (!packet) { this.stats.rxCRCErrors++; return; }

    packet.rssi = rssi;
    packet.snr = snr;

    // Deduplication
    const key = `${packet.srcId.toString('hex')}:${packet.msgId}`;
    const now = Date.now();
    if (this.seenMessages.has(key)) return;
    this.seenMessages.set(key, now + 60_000);

    // Update neighbor table
    this.neighborTable.update(packet.srcId, rssi, snr);

    switch (packet.type) {
      case 'BEACON': this.handleBeacon(packet); break;
      case 'DATA': this.handleData(packet); break;
      case 'FRAGMENT': this.handleFragment(packet); break;
      case 'DATA_ACK': this.arq.acknowledge(packet.msgId); break;
      case 'FRAGMENT_ACK': this.arq.acknowledge(packet.msgId); break;
      case 'BROADCAST': this.handleBroadcast(packet); break;
      case 'ROUTE_REQ': this.handleRouteRequest(packet); break;
      case 'ROUTE_REPLY': this.handleRouteReply(packet); break;
      case 'ROUTE_ERROR': this.handleRouteError(packet); break;
      case 'PING': this.sendPong(packet); break;
    }
  }

  private handleBeacon(packet: LoRaPacket): void {
    try {
      const info = JSON.parse(packet.payload.toString('utf8'));
      this.neighborTable.update(packet.srcId, packet.rssi ?? -100, packet.snr ?? 0, info.fullNodeId);

      // Update routing table (direct neighbor = 1 hop)
      this.routingTable.set({
        dstId: packet.srcId.toString('hex'),
        nextHop: packet.srcId.toString('hex'),
        hopCount: 1,
        rssi: packet.rssi ?? -100,
        snr: packet.snr ?? 0,
        lastUsed: Date.now(),
        discoveredAt: Date.now(),
      });

      this.emit('neighbor:discovered', {
        loraId: packet.srcId.toString('hex'),
        fullNodeId: info.fullNodeId,
        rssi: packet.rssi,
        snr: packet.snr,
      });
    } catch {}
  }

  private handleData(packet: LoRaPacket): void {
    if (packet.dstId.equals(this.loraId)) {
      // For us — send ACK and deliver
      this.transmitPacket(packet.srcId, 'DATA_ACK', Buffer.alloc(2), packet.msgId);
      this.emit('data', { srcId: packet.srcId, data: packet.payload, rssi: packet.rssi, snr: packet.snr });
    } else if (packet.dstId.equals(BROADCAST_ID)) {
      this.emit('data', { srcId: packet.srcId, data: packet.payload, broadcast: true });
    } else if (packet.ttl > 1) {
      // Route forward
      this.forwardPacket(packet);
    }
  }

  private handleFragment(packet: LoRaPacket): void {
    const fragData = this.fragmenter.deserializeFragment(packet.payload);
    if (!fragData) return;

    const reassembled = this.fragmenter.receive(
      packet.srcId.toString('hex'),
      fragData.header,
      fragData.payload,
    );

    this.transmitPacket(packet.srcId, 'FRAGMENT_ACK', Buffer.alloc(2), packet.msgId);

    if (reassembled) {
      this.emit('data', { srcId: packet.srcId, data: reassembled, rssi: packet.rssi, snr: packet.snr });
    }
  }

  private handleBroadcast(packet: LoRaPacket): void {
    this.emit('broadcast', { srcId: packet.srcId, data: packet.payload, rssi: packet.rssi });

    // Rebroadcast if TTL permits (with delay to avoid collisions)
    if (packet.ttl > 1) {
      const delay = Math.random() * 2000; // 0–2s random delay
      setTimeout(() => {
        const forward: LoRaPacket = { ...packet, ttl: packet.ttl - 1, hopCount: packet.hopCount + 1 };
        this.transmitPacket(BROADCAST_ID, 'BROADCAST', forward.payload);
      }, delay);
    }
  }

  private handleRouteRequest(packet: LoRaPacket): void {
    try {
      const req = JSON.parse(packet.payload.toString('utf8'));
      const targetLoraId = Buffer.from(req.targetId, 'hex');

      // Do we have a route to target?
      const route = this.routingTable.get(req.targetId);
      if (route || targetLoraId.equals(this.loraId)) {
        // Send ROUTE_REPLY back to requester
        const reply = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
          requestId: req.requestId,
          path: [...(req.path ?? []), this.loraId.toString('hex')],
          hopCount: (req.hopCount ?? 0) + 1,
        }));
        this.transmitPacket(packet.srcId, 'ROUTE_REPLY', reply);
      } else if (packet.ttl > 1) {
        // Forward RREQ with path appended
        const forward = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
          ...req,
          path: [...(req.path ?? []), this.loraId.toString('hex')],
          hopCount: (req.hopCount ?? 0) + 1,
        }));
        this.transmitPacket(BROADCAST_ID, 'ROUTE_REQ', forward);
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  private handleRouteReply(packet: LoRaPacket): void {
    try {
      const reply = JSON.parse(packet.payload.toString('utf8'));
      const path: string[] = reply.path ?? [];

      if (path.length >= 2) {
        // Learn route: we're path[i], next hop is path[i+1]
        const myIdx = path.indexOf(this.loraId.toString('hex'));
        if (myIdx >= 0 && myIdx < path.length - 1) {
          this.routingTable.set({
            dstId: path[path.length - 1],
            nextHop: path[myIdx + 1],
            hopCount: path.length - myIdx - 1,
            rssi: packet.rssi ?? -100,
            snr: packet.snr ?? 0,
            lastUsed: Date.now(),
            discoveredAt: Date.now(),
          });
        }
      }

      this.emit('route:discovered', { path, hopCount: reply.hopCount });
    } catch {}
  }

  private handleRouteError(packet: LoRaPacket): void {
    try {
      const err = JSON.parse(packet.payload.toString('utf8'));
      const affected = this.routingTable.invalidateVia(err.brokenLink);
      this.emit('route:error', { brokenLink: err.brokenLink, routesInvalidated: affected });
    } catch {}
  }

  private forwardPacket(packet: LoRaPacket): void {
    const dstHex = packet.dstId.toString('hex');
    const route = this.routingTable.get(dstHex);

    if (route) {
      const nextHop = Buffer.from(route.nextHop, 'hex');
      const forward: LoRaPacket = { ...packet, ttl: packet.ttl - 1, hopCount: packet.hopCount + 1 };
      this.transmitPacket(nextHop, forward.type, forward.payload, forward.msgId);
    } else {
      // Route not known — initiate discovery
      this.initiateRouteDiscovery(packet.dstId);
    }
  }

  private async initiateRouteDiscovery(targetId: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
      requestId: crypto.randomBytes(4).toString('hex'),
      targetId: targetId.toString('hex'),
      initiator: this.loraId.toString('hex'),
      path: [this.loraId.toString('hex')],
      hopCount: 0,
    }));
    await this.transmitPacket(BROADCAST_ID, 'ROUTE_REQ', payload);
  }

  private sendPong(pingPacket: LoRaPacket): void {
    this.transmitPacket(pingPacket.srcId, 'PONG', Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
      pingId: pingPacket.msgId,
      respondedAt: Date.now(),
    })));
  }

  private async sendBeacon(): Promise<void> {
    const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
      fullNodeId: this.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      loraId: this.loraId.toString('hex'),
      sf: this.loraConfig.spreadingFactor,
      bw: this.loraConfig.bandwidth,
      neighbors: this.neighborTable.reachable().length,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    }));
    await this.broadcast(payload, 'BEACON');
  }

  private sweep(): void {
    this.neighborTable.sweep();
    this.fragmenter.sweep();

    // Sweep seen messages
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [key, expiry] of this.seenMessages) {
      if (now > expiry) this.seenMessages.delete(key);
    }

    this.stats.currentRSSI = this.hardware.getRSSI();
    this.stats.currentSNR = this.hardware.getSNR();
    const temp = this.hardware.getTemperature();
    if (temp !== null) this.stats.temperature = temp;
  }

  // ── SERIALIZATION ──────────────────────────

  private serializePacket(packet: LoRaPacket): Buffer {
    // Header: type(1) + src(4) + dst(4) + msgId(2) + ttl(1) + flags(1) + hopCount(1) = 14 bytes
    const headerSize = 14;
    const buf = Buffer.allocUnsafe(headerSize + packet.payload.length);

    buf[0] = this.packetTypeByte(packet.type);
    packet.srcId.copy(buf, 1);
    packet.dstId.copy(buf, 5);
    buf.writeUInt16BE(packet.msgId, 9);
    buf[11] = packet.ttl;
    buf[12] = packet.flags;
    buf[13] = packet.hopCount;
    packet.payload.copy(buf, 14);
    return buf;
  }

  private deserializePacket(buf: Buffer): LoRaPacket | null {
    if (buf.length < 14) return null;
    try {
      return {
        type: this.byteToPacketType(buf[0]),
        srcId: buf.slice(1, 5),
        dstId: buf.slice(5, 9),
        msgId: buf.readUInt16BE(9),
        ttl: buf[11],
        flags: buf[12],
        hopCount: buf[13],
        payload: buf.slice(14),
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private packetTypeByte(type: LoRaPacketType): number {
    const map: Record<LoRaPacketType, number> = {
      DATA: 0x01, DATA_ACK: 0x02, FRAGMENT: 0x03, FRAGMENT_ACK: 0x04,
      BEACON: 0x05, ROUTE_REQ: 0x06, ROUTE_REPLY: 0x07, ROUTE_ERROR: 0x08,
      BROADCAST: 0x09, PING: 0x0A, PONG: 0x0B,
    };
    return map[type] ?? 0x00;
  }

  private byteToPacketType(byte: number): LoRaPacketType {
    const map: Record<number, LoRaPacketType> = {
      0x01: 'DATA', 0x02: 'DATA_ACK', 0x03: 'FRAGMENT', 0x04: 'FRAGMENT_ACK',
      0x05: 'BEACON', 0x06: 'ROUTE_REQ', 0x07: 'ROUTE_REPLY', 0x08: 'ROUTE_ERROR',
      0x09: 'BROADCAST', 0x0A: 'PING', 0x0B: 'PONG',
    };
    return map[byte] ?? 'DATA';
  }

  // ── PUBLIC API ─────────────────────────────

  async ping(targetId: Buffer): Promise<{ success: boolean; latencyMs: number }> {
    const start = Date.now();
    const pingPayload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ pingAt: start }));

    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => resolve({ success: false, latencyMs: -1 }), 10_000);

      this.once('pong', (data: any) => {
        if (data.srcId?.equals?.(targetId)) {
          clearTimeout(timeout);
          resolve({ success: true, latencyMs: Date.now() - start });
        }
      });

      this.transmitPacket(targetId, 'PING', pingPayload);
    });
  }

  getStats(): LoRaRadioStats {
    return { ...this.stats };
  }

  getNeighbors(): LoRaNeighbor[] {
    return this.neighborTable.reachable();
  }

  getRoutes(): LoRaRoute[] {
    return this.routingTable.allRoutes();
  }

  getDutyCycle(): { used: number; budgetRemainingMs: number } {
    return {
      used: this.dutyCycle.dutyCyclePercent(),
      budgetRemainingMs: this.dutyCycle.remainingBudgetMs(),
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LORA BRIDGE — Connects LoRa mesh to TCP mesh
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class LoRaBridge extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly loraMesh: LoRaMeshNode;
  private loraToTcp: Map<string, string> = new Map(); // loraId -> full nodeId
  private tcpToLora: Map<string, Buffer> = new Map(); // full nodeId -> loraId

  constructor(loraMesh: LoRaMeshNode) {
    super();
    this.loraMesh = loraMesh;

    loraMesh.on('data', (event: any) => {
      const fullNodeId = this.loraToTcp.get(event.srcId?.toString('hex') ?? '');
      this.emit('bridge:data', { ...event, fullNodeId });
    });

    loraMesh.on('neighbor:discovered', (event: any) => {
      if (event.fullNodeId) {
        this.registerMapping(Buffer.from(event.loraId, 'hex'), event.fullNodeId);
      }
    });
  }

  registerMapping(loraId: Buffer, fullNodeId: string): void {
    this.loraToTcp.set(loraId.toString('hex'), fullNodeId);
    this.tcpToLora.set(fullNodeId, loraId);
    this.emit('mapping:registered', { loraId: loraId.toString('hex'), fullNodeId });
  }

  async sendToFullNode(fullNodeId: string, data: Buffer): Promise<boolean> {
    const loraId = this.tcpToLora.get(fullNodeId);
    if (!loraId) return false;
    return this.loraMesh.send(loraId, data);
  }

  getFullNodeId(loraId: string): string | undefined {
    return this.loraToTcp.get(loraId);
  }

  getLoRaId(fullNodeId: string): Buffer | undefined {
    return this.tcpToLora.get(fullNodeId);
  }
}

export { LoRaMeshNode as LoRaMesh };
export default LoRaMeshNode;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — meshConsensus.ts
 * Byzantine Fault Tolerant Consensus — Tendermint Protocol
 *
 * Consensus is what separates a mesh of nodes from a sovereign distributed system.
 * Without consensus, any node can lie, fork the state, or replay old decisions.
 * With BFT consensus, up to f < n/3 malicious nodes cannot corrupt the system.
 *
 * Protocol: Tendermint (used by Cosmos, Celestia, Penumbra)
 * Safety: No two honest nodes commit different blocks at the same height (ever)
 * Liveness: Progress guaranteed when >2/3 of validators are honest and online
 *
 * Phases per round:
 *   PROPOSE   → Leader broadcasts a Block proposal
 *   PREVOTE   → Each validator votes on the proposal (or nil)
 *   PRECOMMIT → Each validator precommits if 2/3+ prevotes for same block
 *   COMMIT    → Block committed when 2/3+ precommits for same block
 *
 * View change (round change): automatic timeout escalation if no progress
 *
 * Components:
 *   Block              — Unit of consensus (batch of transactions)
 *   Vote               — Signed message: prevote or precommit
 *   ValidatorSet       — Dynamic set of voting nodes with weights
 *   RoundState         — State machine for one consensus round
 *   ProposerSelector   — Round-robin weighted proposer election
 *   CommitLog          — Append-only log of committed blocks
 *   ConsensusEngine    — Orchestrates the full protocol
 */

import * as crypto from 'crypto';
import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import { NodeIdentity } from './meshNode';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

const CONSENSUS_PROPOSE_TIMEOUT_MS = 3_000;
const CONSENSUS_PREVOTE_TIMEOUT_MS = 2_000;
const CONSENSUS_PRECOMMIT_TIMEOUT_MS = 2_000;
const CONSENSUS_COMMIT_TIMEOUT_MS = 1_000;
const CONSENSUS_TIMEOUT_DELTA_MS = 500;   // Add per round for adaptive timeouts
const MAX_BLOCK_TRANSACTIONS = 1000;
const MAX_BLOCK_BYTES = 1_048_576;        // 1 MB
const COMMIT_LOG_MAX = 100_000;
const BFT_THRESHOLD_NUMERATOR = 2;
const BFT_THRESHOLD_DENOMINATOR = 3;
const ROUND_TIMEOUT_MAX_MS = 60_000;
const VALIDATOR_MAX_MISSED_ROUNDS = 10;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export type VoteType = 'PREVOTE' | 'PRECOMMIT';
export type RoundStep = 'PROPOSE' | 'PREVOTE' | 'PRECOMMIT' | 'COMMIT' | 'NEW_HEIGHT';
export type ConsensusMessageType = 'PROPOSAL' | 'VOTE' | 'ROUND_CHANGE' | 'COMMIT_CERT' | 'SYNC_REQUEST' | 'SYNC_RESPONSE';

export interface Transaction {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  data: Buffer;
  sender: string;
  timestamp: number;
  signature: string;
  fee: number;
}

export interface BlockHeader {
  height: number;
  round: number;
  timestamp: number;
  prevBlockHash: string;
  txRoot: string;           // Merkle root of transactions
  stateRoot: string;        // Merkle root of state
  validatorsHash: string;   // Hash of current validator set
  proposer: string;         // hex nodeId of proposer
  chainId: string;
}

export interface Block {
  header: BlockHeader;
  transactions: Transaction[];
  hash: string;
  commitCertificate?: CommitCertificate;
}

export interface Vote {
  type: VoteType;
  height: number;
  round: number;
  blockHash: string | null;   // null = nil vote
  validatorId: string;
  validatorPublicKey: string;
  timestamp: number;
  signature: string;
}

export interface Proposal {
  height: number;
  round: number;
  polRound: number;           // Proof-of-Lock round (-1 if none)
  block: Block;
  proposerId: string;
  timestamp: number;
  signature: string;
}

export interface CommitCertificate {
  height: number;
  round: number;
  blockHash: string;
  signatures: Array<{ validatorId: string; signature: string }>;
}

export interface ValidatorInfo {
  nodeId: string;
  publicKey: string;
  votingPower: number;
  accumulatedPriority: number;   // For proposer selection
  missedRounds: number;
  jailed: boolean;
  joinedAt: number;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// BLOCK UTILITIES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export function computeBlockHash(header: BlockHeader): string {
  const canonical = JSON.stringify({
    height: header.height, round: header.round, timestamp: header.timestamp,
    prevBlockHash: header.prevBlockHash, txRoot: header.txRoot,
    stateRoot: header.stateRoot, validatorsHash: header.validatorsHash,
    proposer: header.proposer, chainId: header.chainId,
  });
  return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(canonical).digest('hex');
}

export function computeTxMerkleRoot(transactions: Transaction[]): string {
  if (transactions.length === 0) return '0'.repeat(64);
  const hashes = transactions.map(tx =>
    crypto.createHash('sha256').update(tx.id + tx.data.toString('hex')).digest('hex')
  );
  return merkleRoot(hashes);
}

function merkleRoot(hashes: string[]): string {
  if (hashes.length === 1) return hashes[0];
  const next: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < hashes.length; i += 2) {
    const left = hashes[i];
    const right = hashes[i + 1] ?? hashes[i];
    next.push(crypto.createHash('sha256').update(left + right).digest('hex'));
  }
  return merkleRoot(next);
}

export function buildBlock(
  height: number,
  round: number,
  transactions: Transaction[],
  prevHash: string,
  stateRoot: string,
  validatorsHash: string,
  proposerId: string,
  chainId: string,
): Block {
  const txRoot = computeTxMerkleRoot(transactions);
  const header: BlockHeader = {
    height, round, timestamp: Date.now(),
    prevBlockHash: prevHash, txRoot, stateRoot,
    validatorsHash, proposer: proposerId, chainId,
  };
  const hash = computeBlockHash(header);
  return { header, transactions, hash };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// VOTE UTILITIES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

function serializeVoteForSigning(vote: Omit<Vote, 'signature'>): Buffer {
  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
    type: vote.type, height: vote.height, round: vote.round,
    blockHash: vote.blockHash, validatorId: vote.validatorId,
    validatorPublicKey: vote.validatorPublicKey, timestamp: vote.timestamp,
  }));
}

export function createVote(
  type: VoteType, height: number, round: number,
  blockHash: string | null, identity: NodeIdentity,
): Vote {
  const partial = {
    type, height, round, blockHash,
    validatorId: identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
    validatorPublicKey: identity.keypair.publicKey.toString('hex'),
    timestamp: Date.now(),
  };
  const sig = identity.sign(serializeVoteForSigning(partial));
  return { ...partial, signature: sig.toString('hex') };
}

export function verifyVote(vote: Vote, publicKey: Buffer): boolean {
  try {
    const data = serializeVoteForSigning(vote);
    const sig = Buffer.from(vote.signature, 'hex');
    const key = crypto.createPublicKey({
      key: Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('302a300506032b6570032100', 'hex'), publicKey]),
      format: 'der', type: 'spki',
    });
    return crypto.verify(null, data, key, sig);
  } catch { return false; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// VOTE SET — Collect and tally votes
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface VoteSummary {
  totalPower: number;
  nilPower: number;
  blockPowers: Map<string, number>;  // blockHash -> voting power
  majority: string | null;           // blockHash with 2/3+, or 'nil', or null
  hasTwoThirds: boolean;
  hasNilTwoThirds: boolean;
}

export class VoteSet {
  private votes: Map<string, Vote> = new Map(); // validatorId -> vote
  private readonly height: number;
  private readonly round: number;
  private readonly type: VoteType;
  private readonly validators: ValidatorSet;

  constructor(height: number, round: number, type: VoteType, validators: ValidatorSet) {
    this.height = height;
    this.round = round;
    this.type = type;
    this.validators = validators;
  }

  addVote(vote: Vote): 'added' | 'duplicate' | 'invalid' | 'unknown_validator' {
    if (vote.height !== this.height || vote.round !== this.round || vote.type !== this.type) {
      return 'invalid';
    }
    const validator = this.validators.get(vote.validatorId);
    if (!validator) return 'unknown_validator';
    if (this.votes.has(vote.validatorId)) return 'duplicate';

    const pubKey = Buffer.from(validator.publicKey, 'hex');
    if (!verifyVote(vote, pubKey)) return 'invalid';

    this.votes.set(vote.validatorId, vote);
    return 'added';
  }

  summarize(): VoteSummary {
    const totalPower = this.validators.totalVotingPower();
    const threshold = Math.ceil((totalPower * BFT_THRESHOLD_NUMERATOR) / BFT_THRESHOLD_DENOMINATOR) + 1;

    let nilPower = 0;
    const blockPowers = new Map<string, number>();

    for (const vote of this.votes.values()) {
      const validator = this.validators.get(vote.validatorId);
      const power = validator?.votingPower ?? 0;
      if (vote.blockHash === null) {
        nilPower += power;
      } else {
        blockPowers.set(vote.blockHash, (blockPowers.get(vote.blockHash) ?? 0) + power);
      }
    }

    let majority: string | null = null;
    for (const [hash, power] of blockPowers) {
      if (power >= threshold) { majority = hash; break; }
    }

    return {
      totalPower, nilPower, blockPowers,
      majority,
      hasTwoThirds: majority !== null,
      hasNilTwoThirds: nilPower >= threshold,
    };
  }

  getVote(validatorId: string): Vote | undefined {
    return this.votes.get(validatorId);
  }

  allVotes(): Vote[] {
    return Array.from(this.votes.values());
  }

  count(): number { return this.votes.size; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// VALIDATOR SET
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class ValidatorSet {
  private validators: Map<string, ValidatorInfo> = new Map();
  private version: number = 0;

  addValidator(info: ValidatorInfo): void {
    this.validators.set(info.nodeId, info);
    this.version++;
  }

  removeValidator(nodeId: string): void {
    this.validators.delete(nodeId);
    this.version++;
  }

  get(nodeId: string): ValidatorInfo | undefined {
    return this.validators.get(nodeId);
  }

  has(nodeId: string): boolean {
    return this.validators.has(nodeId);
  }

  all(): ValidatorInfo[] {
    return Array.from(this.validators.values()).filter(v => !v.jailed);
  }

  jailValidator(nodeId: string): void {
    const v = this.validators.get(nodeId);
    if (v) { v.jailed = true; this.version++; }
  }

  unjailValidator(nodeId: string): void {
    const v = this.validators.get(nodeId);
    if (v) { v.jailed = false; v.missedRounds = 0; this.version++; }
  }

  totalVotingPower(): number {
    return this.all().reduce((s, v) => s + v.votingPower, 0);
  }

  computeHash(): string {
    const sorted = this.all().sort((a, b) => a.nodeId.localeCompare(b.nodeId));
    return crypto.createHash('sha256')
      .update(JSON.stringify(sorted.map(v => ({ id: v.nodeId, power: v.votingPower }))))
      .digest('hex');
  }

  size(): number { return this.validators.size; }
  getVersion(): number { return this.version; }

  isByzantineTolerant(): boolean {
    const active = this.all();
    const totalPower = active.reduce((s, v) => s + v.votingPower, 0);
    const minHonestPower = Math.ceil((totalPower * 2) / 3) + 1;
    return active.length >= 4; // Need at least 4 for f=1
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// PROPOSER SELECTOR — Weighted round-robin
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class ProposerSelector {
  private priorities: Map<string, number> = new Map();

  // Tendermint proposer selection algorithm
  select(validators: ValidatorSet, height: number, round: number): string | null {
    const active = validators.all();
    if (active.length === 0) return null;

    // Initialize priorities
    for (const v of active) {
      if (!this.priorities.has(v.nodeId)) {
        this.priorities.set(v.nodeId, 0);
      }
    }

    // Increment by voting power
    for (const v of active) {
      const current = this.priorities.get(v.nodeId) ?? 0;
      this.priorities.set(v.nodeId, current + v.votingPower);
    }

    // Proposer is validator with highest priority
    let maxPriority = -Infinity;
    let proposerId = '';
    for (const v of active) {
      const p = this.priorities.get(v.nodeId) ?? 0;
      if (p > maxPriority) { maxPriority = p; proposerId = v.nodeId; }
    }

    // Decrement proposer by total power
    const total = validators.totalVotingPower();
    const propPriority = this.priorities.get(proposerId) ?? 0;
    this.priorities.set(proposerId, propPriority - total);

    return proposerId || null;
  }

  reset(): void { this.priorities.clear(); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// COMMIT LOG
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class CommitLog {
  private commits: Block[] = [];
  private readonly maxEntries: number;
  private heightIndex: Map<number, Block> = new Map();

  constructor(maxEntries: number = COMMIT_LOG_MAX) {
    this.maxEntries = maxEntries;
  }

  append(block: Block): void {
    this.commits.push(block);
    this.heightIndex.set(block.header.height, block);
    if (this.commits.length > this.maxEntries) {
      const evicted = this.commits.shift()!;
      this.heightIndex.delete(evicted.header.height);
    }
  }

  getByHeight(height: number): Block | undefined {
    return this.heightIndex.get(height);
  }

  latest(): Block | null {
    return this.commits[this.commits.length - 1] ?? null;
  }

  latestHeight(): number {
    return this.latest()?.header.height ?? 0;
  }

  latestHash(): string {
    return this.latest()?.hash ?? '0'.repeat(64);
  }

  range(fromHeight: number, toHeight: number): Block[] {
    return this.commits.filter(b => b.header.height >= fromHeight && b.header.height <= toHeight);
  }

  size(): number { return this.commits.length; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// MEMPOOL
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class Mempool {
  private txs: Map<string, Transaction> = new Map();
  private readonly maxSize: number;

  constructor(maxSize: number = 10_000) {
    this.maxSize = maxSize;
  }

  add(tx: Transaction): boolean {
    if (this.txs.size >= this.maxSize) return false;
    if (this.txs.has(tx.id)) return false;
    this.txs.set(tx.id, tx);
    return true;
  }

  remove(txIds: string[]): void {
    for (const id of txIds) this.txs.delete(id);
  }

  selectForBlock(maxCount: number = MAX_BLOCK_TRANSACTIONS): Transaction[] {
    return Array.from(this.txs.values())
      .sort((a, b) => b.fee - a.fee || a.timestamp - b.timestamp)
      .slice(0, maxCount);
  }

  has(txId: string): boolean { return this.txs.has(txId); }
  size(): number { return this.txs.size; }
  flush(): void { this.txs.clear(); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LOCK — Proof of Lock for Tendermint safety
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface Lock {
  blockHash: string;
  round: number;
  votes: Vote[];
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// ROUND STATE MACHINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RoundState {
  height: number;
  round: number;
  step: RoundStep;
  proposal: Proposal | null;
  prevotes: VoteSet;
  precommits: VoteSet;
  lockedBlock: string | null;  // blockHash if locked
  lockedRound: number;
  validBlock: string | null;
  validRound: number;
  stepStart: number;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONSENSUS ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ConsensusConfig {
  identity: NodeIdentity;
  chainId: string;
  validators: ValidatorSet;
  sendMessage: (targetId: string | null, type: ConsensusMessageType, payload: Buffer) => Promise<void>;
  executeBlock?: (block: Block) => Promise<string>; // Returns new state root
}

export interface ConsensusStats {
  height: number;
  round: number;
  step: RoundStep;
  isProposer: boolean;
  validators: number;
  mempoolSize: number;
  committedBlocks: number;
  roundChanges: number;
  totalVotes: number;
  byzantineFaults: number;
  startedAt: number;
}

export class ConsensusEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly identity: NodeIdentity;
  private readonly chainId: string;
  private readonly validators: ValidatorSet;
  private readonly proposerSelector: ProposerSelector;
  private readonly commitLog: CommitLog;
  public readonly mempool: Mempool;
  private readonly sendMessage: ConsensusConfig['sendMessage'];
  private readonly executeBlock: ConsensusConfig['executeBlock'];

  private rs: RoundState;
  private stepTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  private running: boolean = false;

  private stats: ConsensusStats;
  private roundChanges: number = 0;
  private totalVotes: number = 0;
  private byzantineFaults: number = 0;

  constructor(config: ConsensusConfig) {
    super();
    this.identity = config.identity;
    this.chainId = config.chainId;
    this.validators = config.validators;
    this.proposerSelector = new ProposerSelector();
    this.commitLog = new CommitLog();
    this.mempool = new Mempool();
    this.sendMessage = config.sendMessage;
    this.executeBlock = config.executeBlock;

    this.rs = this.newRoundState(1, 0);
    this.stats = {
      height: 1, round: 0, step: 'NEW_HEIGHT', isProposer: false,
      validators: this.validators.size(), mempoolSize: 0,
      committedBlocks: 0, roundChanges: 0, totalVotes: 0,
      byzantineFaults: 0, startedAt: Date.now(),
    };
  }

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.running) return;
    this.running = true;
    this.emit('started', { chainId: this.chainId, height: this.rs.height });
    await this.enterNewHeight(this.rs.height);
  }

  stop(): void {
    this.running = false;
    if (this.stepTimer) clearTimeout(this.stepTimer);
    this.emit('stopped');
  }

  // Called by network layer when a message is received
  async handleMessage(fromId: string, type: ConsensusMessageType, payload: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.running) return;
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(payload.toString('utf8'));
      switch (type) {
        case 'PROPOSAL': await this.handleProposal(data as Proposal); break;
        case 'VOTE': await this.handleVote(fromId, data as Vote); break;
        case 'ROUND_CHANGE': await this.handleRoundChange(data); break;
        case 'SYNC_REQUEST': await this.handleSyncRequest(fromId, data); break;
        case 'COMMIT_CERT': await this.handleCommitCert(data as CommitCertificate); break;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      this.emit('error', { type: 'message_parse', err });
    }
  }

  addTransaction(tx: Transaction): boolean {
    return this.mempool.add(tx);
  }

  getStatus(): ConsensusStats {
    return {
      ...this.stats,
      height: this.rs.height,
      round: this.rs.round,
      step: this.rs.step,
      isProposer: this.isProposer(),
      validators: this.validators.size(),
      mempoolSize: this.mempool.size(),
    };
  }

  getCommitLog(): CommitLog { return this.commitLog; }

  // ── PROTOCOL PHASES ────────────────────────

  private async enterNewHeight(height: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.running) return;
    this.rs = this.newRoundState(height, 0);
    this.emit('height:new', { height });
    await this.enterPropose(height, 0);
  }

  private async enterPropose(height: number, round: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.running || !this.atStep(height, round, 'PROPOSE')) return;
    this.rs.step = 'PROPOSE';
    this.rs.stepStart = Date.now();
    this.emit('step:propose', { height, round });

    const proposer = this.proposerSelector.select(this.validators, height, round);

    if (proposer === this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex')) {
      await this.broadcastProposal(height, round);
    }

    const timeout = this.proposeTimeout(round);
    this.scheduleTimeout(timeout, () => this.onProposeTimeout(height, round));
  }

  private async broadcastProposal(height: number, round: number): Promise<void> {
    const txs = this.mempool.selectForBlock();
    const stateRoot = await this.executeBlock?.({ header: {} as any, transactions: txs, hash: '' }) ?? '0'.repeat(64);

    const block = buildBlock(
      height, round, txs,
      this.commitLog.latestHash(),
      stateRoot,
      this.validators.computeHash(),
      this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      this.chainId,
    );

    const proposalBody = {
      height, round,
      polRound: this.rs.validRound,
      block,
      proposerId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    };
    const sig = this.identity.sign(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(proposalBody)));
    const proposal: Proposal = { ...proposalBody, signature: sig.toString('hex') };

    this.rs.proposal = proposal;
    await this.broadcast('PROPOSAL', Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(proposal)));
    this.emit('proposal:broadcast', { height, round, blockHash: block.hash, txCount: txs.length });
  }

  private async enterPrevote(height: number, round: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.running || !this.atStep(height, round, 'PREVOTE')) return;
    this.rs.step = 'PREVOTE';
    this.rs.stepStart = Date.now();
    this.emit('step:prevote', { height, round });

    let voteBlockHash: string | null = null;

    if (this.rs.lockedBlock !== null && this.rs.lockedRound > this.rs.validRound) {
      // Vote for locked block
      voteBlockHash = this.rs.lockedBlock;
    } else if (this.rs.proposal !== null) {
      // Vote for proposal if valid
      if (this.validateBlock(this.rs.proposal.block)) {
        voteBlockHash = this.rs.proposal.block.hash;
      }
    }

    const vote = createVote('PREVOTE', height, round, voteBlockHash, this.identity);
    await this.addAndBroadcastVote(vote);

    const timeout = this.prevoteTimeout(round);
    this.scheduleTimeout(timeout, () => this.onPrevoteTimeout(height, round));
  }

  private async enterPrecommit(height: number, round: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.running || !this.atStep(height, round, 'PRECOMMIT')) return;
    this.rs.step = 'PRECOMMIT';
    this.rs.stepStart = Date.now();
    this.emit('step:precommit', { height, round });

    const summary = this.rs.prevotes.summarize();
    let voteBlockHash: string | null = null;

    if (summary.hasTwoThirds && summary.majority) {
      // Lock on the block with 2/3+ prevotes
      this.rs.lockedBlock = summary.majority;
      this.rs.lockedRound = round;
      this.rs.validBlock = summary.majority;
      this.rs.validRound = round;
      voteBlockHash = summary.majority;
    } else if (summary.hasNilTwoThirds) {
      // Unlock
      this.rs.lockedBlock = null;
      this.rs.lockedRound = -1;
    }

    const vote = createVote('PRECOMMIT', height, round, voteBlockHash, this.identity);
    await this.addAndBroadcastVote(vote);

    const timeout = this.precommitTimeout(round);
    this.scheduleTimeout(timeout, () => this.onPrecommitTimeout(height, round));
  }

  private async enterCommit(height: number, round: number, blockHash: string): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.running) return;
    this.rs.step = 'COMMIT';
    this.emit('step:commit', { height, round, blockHash });

    const block = this.rs.proposal?.block;
    if (!block || block.hash !== blockHash) {
      // We don't have the block — need to sync
      this.emit('sync:needed', { height, blockHash });
      return;
    }

    // Build commit certificate from precommit votes
    const precommitVotes = this.rs.precommits.allVotes().filter(v => v.blockHash === blockHash);
    const cert: CommitCertificate = {
      height, round, blockHash,
      signatures: precommitVotes.map(v => ({ validatorId: v.validatorId, signature: v.signature })),
    };
    block.commitCertificate = cert;

    // Execute block
    if (this.executeBlock) {
      await this.executeBlock(block);
    }

    // Remove committed transactions from mempool
    this.mempool.remove(block.transactions.map(tx => tx.id));

    // Append to commit log
    this.commitLog.append(block);

    this.emit('block:committed', {
      height, round, blockHash,
      txCount: block.transactions.length,
      proposer: block.header.proposer,
    });

    this.stats.committedBlocks++;

    // Update validator stats
    for (const v of this.validators.all()) {
      const voted = precommitVotes.some(pv => pv.validatorId === v.nodeId);
      if (!voted) {
        v.missedRounds++;
        if (v.missedRounds >= VALIDATOR_MAX_MISSED_ROUNDS) {
          this.validators.jailValidator(v.nodeId);
          this.emit('validator:jailed', { nodeId: v.nodeId, missedRounds: v.missedRounds });
        }
      } else {
        v.missedRounds = 0;
      }
    }

    // Advance height
    setTimeout(() => this.enterNewHeight(height + 1), CONSENSUS_COMMIT_TIMEOUT_MS);
  }

  private async enterNewRound(height: number, round: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.running) return;
    this.rs.round = round;
    this.rs.step = 'PROPOSE';
    this.rs.proposal = null;
    this.rs.prevotes = new VoteSet(height, round, 'PREVOTE', this.validators);
    this.rs.precommits = new VoteSet(height, round, 'PRECOMMIT', this.validators);
    this.roundChanges++;
    this.stats.roundChanges = this.roundChanges;
    this.emit('round:new', { height, round });

    // Broadcast round change
    await this.broadcast('ROUND_CHANGE', Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ height, round, validatorId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex') })));
    await this.enterPropose(height, round);
  }

  // ── VOTE HANDLING ──────────────────────────

  private async handleProposal(proposal: Proposal): Promise<void> {
    if (proposal.height !== this.rs.height || proposal.round !== this.rs.round) return;
    if (this.rs.proposal !== null) return;
    if (!this.validateProposal(proposal)) {
      this.emit('security:invalid_proposal', { proposerId: proposal.proposerId });
      return;
    }
    this.rs.proposal = proposal;
    this.emit('proposal:received', { height: proposal.height, round: proposal.round, blockHash: proposal.block.hash });

    if (this.rs.step === 'PROPOSE') {
      await this.enterPrevote(this.rs.height, this.rs.round);
    }
  }

  private async handleVote(fromId: string, vote: Vote): Promise<void> {
    if (vote.height !== this.rs.height) {
      if (vote.height < this.rs.height) return; // Old vote
      // Future height — may need to sync
      this.emit('sync:needed', { height: vote.height });
      return;
    }

    const voteSet = vote.type === 'PREVOTE' ? this.rs.prevotes : this.rs.precommits;
    const result = voteSet.addVote(vote);

    if (result === 'invalid') {
      this.byzantineFaults++;
      this.stats.byzantineFaults = this.byzantineFaults;
      this.emit('security:invalid_vote', { fromId, vote });
      return;
    }

    if (result !== 'added') return;
    this.totalVotes++;
    this.stats.totalVotes = this.totalVotes;

    const summary = voteSet.summarize();

    if (vote.type === 'PREVOTE') {
      if (summary.hasTwoThirds && this.rs.step === 'PREVOTE') {
        await this.enterPrecommit(this.rs.height, this.rs.round);
      }
      if (summary.hasNilTwoThirds && this.rs.step === 'PREVOTE') {
        // Nil prevote 2/3+: precommit nil
        await this.enterPrecommit(this.rs.height, this.rs.round);
      }
    } else if (vote.type === 'PRECOMMIT') {
      if (summary.hasTwoThirds && summary.majority) {
        await this.enterCommit(this.rs.height, this.rs.round, summary.majority);
      }
      if (summary.hasNilTwoThirds && this.rs.step === 'PRECOMMIT') {
        await this.enterNewRound(this.rs.height, this.rs.round + 1);
      }
    }
  }

  private async handleRoundChange(data: any): Promise<void> {
    const { height, round } = data;
    if (height !== this.rs.height) return;
    if (round > this.rs.round + 1) {
      // Catch up to majority round
      await this.enterNewRound(height, round);
    }
  }

  private async handleCommitCert(cert: CommitCertificate): Promise<void> {
    if (cert.height <= this.commitLog.latestHeight()) return;
    this.emit('sync:commit_cert', cert);
  }

  private async handleSyncRequest(fromId: string, data: any): Promise<void> {
    const { fromHeight, toHeight } = data;
    const blocks = this.commitLog.range(fromHeight, Math.min(toHeight, fromHeight + 100));
    await this.sendMessage(fromId, 'SYNC_RESPONSE', Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ blocks })));
  }

  // ── TIMEOUTS ───────────────────────────────

  private async onProposeTimeout(height: number, round: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.atStep(height, round, 'PROPOSE')) return;
    this.emit('timeout:propose', { height, round });
    await this.enterPrevote(height, round);
  }

  private async onPrevoteTimeout(height: number, round: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.atStep(height, round, 'PREVOTE')) return;
    this.emit('timeout:prevote', { height, round });
    await this.enterPrecommit(height, round);
  }

  private async onPrecommitTimeout(height: number, round: number): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.atStep(height, round, 'PRECOMMIT')) return;
    this.emit('timeout:precommit', { height, round });
    await this.enterNewRound(height, round + 1);
  }

  // ── VALIDATION ─────────────────────────────

  private validateProposal(proposal: Proposal): boolean {
    const proposer = this.proposerSelector.select(this.validators, proposal.height, proposal.round);
    if (proposer !== proposal.proposerId) return false;

    const validator = this.validators.get(proposal.proposerId);
    if (!validator) return false;

    const body = {
      height: proposal.height, round: proposal.round, polRound: proposal.polRound,
      block: proposal.block, proposerId: proposal.proposerId, timestamp: proposal.timestamp,
    };
    const sig = Buffer.from(proposal.signature, 'hex');
    const pubKey = Buffer.from(validator.publicKey, 'hex');
    const data = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body));
    try {
      const key = crypto.createPublicKey({
        key: Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('302a300506032b6570032100', 'hex'), pubKey]),
        format: 'der', type: 'spki',
      });
      return crypto.verify(null, data, key, sig);
    } catch { return false; }
  }

  private validateBlock(block: Block): boolean {
    const computedHash = computeBlockHash(block.header);
    if (computedHash !== block.hash) return false;
    const computedTxRoot = computeTxMerkleRoot(block.transactions);
    if (computedTxRoot !== block.header.txRoot) return false;
    if (block.header.chainId !== this.chainId) return false;
    if (block.header.height !== this.rs.height) return false;
    if (block.header.prevBlockHash !== this.commitLog.latestHash()) return false;
    return true;
  }

  // ── HELPERS ────────────────────────────────

  private async addAndBroadcastVote(vote: Vote): Promise<void> {
    const voteSet = vote.type === 'PREVOTE' ? this.rs.prevotes : this.rs.precommits;
    voteSet.addVote(vote);
    await this.broadcast('VOTE', Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(vote)));
    this.emit('vote:cast', { type: vote.type, height: vote.height, round: vote.round, blockHash: vote.blockHash });
  }

  private async broadcast(type: ConsensusMessageType, payload: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    await this.sendMessage(null, type, payload);
  }

  private isProposer(): boolean {
    const proposer = this.proposerSelector.select(this.validators, this.rs.height, this.rs.round);
    return proposer === this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex');
  }

  private atStep(height: number, round: number, step: RoundStep): boolean {
    return this.rs.height === height && this.rs.round === round && this.rs.step === step;
  }

  private scheduleTimeout(ms: number, fn: () => void): void {
    if (this.stepTimer) clearTimeout(this.stepTimer);
    this.stepTimer = setTimeout(fn, Math.min(ms, ROUND_TIMEOUT_MAX_MS));
  }

  private proposeTimeout(round: number): number {
    return CONSENSUS_PROPOSE_TIMEOUT_MS + round * CONSENSUS_TIMEOUT_DELTA_MS;
  }

  private prevoteTimeout(round: number): number {
    return CONSENSUS_PREVOTE_TIMEOUT_MS + round * CONSENSUS_TIMEOUT_DELTA_MS;
  }

  private precommitTimeout(round: number): number {
    return CONSENSUS_PRECOMMIT_TIMEOUT_MS + round * CONSENSUS_TIMEOUT_DELTA_MS;
  }

  private newRoundState(height: number, round: number): RoundState {
    return {
      height, round, step: 'NEW_HEIGHT', proposal: null,
      prevotes: new VoteSet(height, round, 'PREVOTE', this.validators),
      precommits: new VoteSet(height, round, 'PRECOMMIT', this.validators),
      lockedBlock: null, lockedRound: -1,
      validBlock: null, validRound: -1,
      stepStart: Date.now(),
    };
  }
}

export default ConsensusEngine;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — File 14: meshIntegrityVerifier.ts
 * Continuous Mesh Integrity Verification & Anti-Infiltration Engine
 *
 * Continuously verifies that every mesh node is running genuine UBE code and
 * has not been replaced, compromised, or silently backdoored.
 *
 * Capabilities:
 *  - Remote attestation via TEE-based code measurement (SGX/TDX quote verification)
 *  - Behavioral fingerprinting: traffic pattern, timing, response distribution
 *  - Merkle proof of code hash at runtime (TPM-style PCR chain)
 *  - Isolation Forest anomaly detection on node behavior vectors
 *  - Consensus-based ejection (f+1 nodes must agree before ejection)
 *  - Sybil attack detection via stake-weighted identity verification
 *  - Dead-node vs compromised-node disambiguation
 *  - Rolling randomized attestation schedule
 *  - Tamper-evident evidence ledger
 *  - Clean node replacement procedure
 *
 * @module @kingube/sdk/phase11/meshIntegrityVerifier
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import { createHash, randomBytes, createHmac } from 'crypto';

// ─── Constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const ATTESTATION_GRACE_PERIOD_MS = 30_000;  // 30s to respond to challenge
const PCR_COUNT = 24;                         // TPM-style platform config registers
const ISOLATION_FOREST_TREES = 100;
const ISOLATION_FOREST_SUBSAMPLE = 256;
const EJECTION_THRESHOLD_F = 1;              // f+1 Byzantine fault tolerance
const SYBIL_STAKE_MINIMUM = 0.01;            // Min stake ratio to be considered valid
const BEHAVIOR_VECTOR_DIM = 16;              // Dimensions of behavior fingerprint
const EVIDENCE_LEDGER_MAX = 100_000;         // Max ledger entries
const ROLLING_ATTESTATION_INTERVAL_MS = 300_000; // 5 min avg, randomized ±50%
