// ─── PCR Chain Manager ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class PCRChainManager {
  private readonly registers: string[] = new Array(PCR_COUNT).fill('0'.repeat(64));

  /**
   * Extend PCR register: PCR[i] = SHA-256(PCR[i] || measurement)
   * Mimics TPM2_PCR_Extend
   */
  extend(index: number, measurement: string): void {
    if (index < 0 || index >= PCR_COUNT) throw new RangeError(`PCR index ${index} out of range`);
    const current = this.registers[index];
    this.registers[index] = createHash('sha256')
      .update(Buffer.from(current, 'hex'))
      .update(Buffer.from(measurement, 'hex'))
      .digest('hex');
  }

  /**
   * Read PCR value
   */
  read(index: number): string {
    return this.registers[index];
  }

  /**
   * Merkle root of all PCR registers
   */
  merkleRoot(): string {
    let layer = [...this.registers];
    while (layer.length > 1) {
      const next: string[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < layer.length; i += 2) {
        const a = layer[i];
        const b = layer[i + 1] ?? a;
        next.push(createHash('sha256').update(a + b).digest('hex'));
      }
      layer = next;
    }
    return layer[0];
  }

  /**
   * Generate inclusion proof for PCR[index]
   */
  proof(index: number): string[] {
    const leaves = [...this.registers];
    const siblings: string[] = [];
    let idx = index;

    let layer = leaves;
    while (layer.length > 1) {
      const next: string[] = [];
      const sibling = idx % 2 === 0 ? layer[idx + 1] ?? layer[idx] : layer[idx - 1];
      siblings.push(sibling);
      idx = Math.floor(idx / 2);
      for (let i = 0; i < layer.length; i += 2) {
        next.push(createHash('sha256')
          .update(layer[i] + (layer[i + 1] ?? layer[i]))
          .digest('hex'));
      }
      layer = next;
    }

    return siblings;
  }

  /**
   * Verify inclusion proof
   */
  static verifyProof(
    leaf: string,
    index: number,
    proof: string[],
    root: string
  ): boolean {
    let current = leaf;
    let idx = index;
    for (const sibling of proof) {
      if (idx % 2 === 0) {
        current = createHash('sha256').update(current + sibling).digest('hex');
      } else {
        current = createHash('sha256').update(sibling + current).digest('hex');
      }
      idx = Math.floor(idx / 2);
    }
    return current === root;
  }

  snapshot(): string[] {
    return [...this.registers];
  }

  reset(index: number): void {
    this.registers[index] = '0'.repeat(64);
  }
}

// ─── Isolation Forest ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

interface IsolationTreeNode {
  feature?: number;
  threshold?: number;
  left?: IsolationTreeNode;
  right?: IsolationTreeNode;
  size: number;
  isLeaf: boolean;
}

class IsolationForest {
  private trees: IsolationTreeNode[] = [];
  private readonly numTrees: number;
  private readonly subsampleSize: number;
  private trained: boolean = false;

  constructor(numTrees: number = ISOLATION_FOREST_TREES, subsampleSize: number = ISOLATION_FOREST_SUBSAMPLE) {
    this.numTrees = numTrees;
    this.subsampleSize = subsampleSize;
  }

  private buildTree(
    data: number[][],
    currentDepth: number,
    maxDepth: number
  ): IsolationTreeNode {
    if (data.length <= 1 || currentDepth >= maxDepth) {
      return { size: data.length, isLeaf: true };
    }

    const dim = data[0].length;
    const feature = Math.floor(Math.random() * dim);

    const values = data.map(d => d[feature]);
    const minVal = Math.min(...values);
    const maxVal = Math.max(...values);

    if (minVal === maxVal) {
      return { size: data.length, isLeaf: true };
    }

    const threshold = minVal + Math.random() * (maxVal - minVal);
    const left = data.filter(d => d[feature] < threshold);
    const right = data.filter(d => d[feature] >= threshold);

    return {
      feature,
      threshold,
      left: this.buildTree(left, currentDepth + 1, maxDepth),
      right: this.buildTree(right, currentDepth + 1, maxDepth),
      size: data.length,
      isLeaf: false,
    };
  }

  train(vectors: number[][]): void {
    const maxDepth = Math.ceil(Math.log2(this.subsampleSize));
    this.trees = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < this.numTrees; i++) {
      // Random subsample
      const shuffled = [...vectors].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
      const sample = shuffled.slice(0, Math.min(this.subsampleSize, shuffled.length));
      this.trees.push(this.buildTree(sample, 0, maxDepth));
    }
    this.trained = true;
  }

  private pathLength(node: IsolationTreeNode, point: number[], depth: number): number {
    if (node.isLeaf || node.feature === undefined || node.threshold === undefined) {
      // Average path length for remaining nodes
      return depth + this.averagePathLength(node.size);
    }

    if (point[node.feature] < node.threshold) {
      return this.pathLength(node.left!, point, depth + 1);
    } else {
      return this.pathLength(node.right!, point, depth + 1);
    }
  }

  private averagePathLength(n: number): number {
    if (n <= 1) return 0;
    const H = Math.log(n - 1) + 0.5772156649; // Euler-Mascheroni constant
    return 2 * H - (2 * (n - 1) / n);
  }

  /**
   * Compute anomaly score for a single vector
   * Returns 0.5 (normal) to 1.0 (highly anomalous)
   */
  score(vector: number[]): number {
    if (!this.trained || this.trees.length === 0) return 0.5;

    const avgPathLength = this.trees.reduce((acc, tree) => {
      return acc + this.pathLength(tree, vector, 0);
    }, 0) / this.trees.length;

    const normalizer = this.averagePathLength(this.subsampleSize);
    if (normalizer === 0) return 0.5;

    return Math.pow(2, -avgPathLength / normalizer);
  }

  get isTrained(): boolean { return this.trained; }
}

// ─── Behavioral Fingerprinter ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

class BehavioralFingerprinter {
  private readonly forest: IsolationForest;
  private baselines: Map<string, BehaviorVector> = new Map();
  private vectorBuffer: number[][] = [];

  constructor() {
    this.forest = new IsolationForest(ISOLATION_FOREST_TREES, ISOLATION_FOREST_SUBSAMPLE);
  }

  vectorize(bv: BehaviorVector): number[] {
    return [
      bv.avgResponseTimeMs / 1000,
      bv.responseTimeStdDev / 1000,
      bv.packetRatePps / 10000,
      bv.byteRateBps / 1e8,
      bv.retransmitRate,
      bv.consensusParticipationRate,
      bv.voteAgreementRate,
      bv.proposalFrequency / 100,
      bv.clockSkewPpm / 100,
      bv.processingLatencyMs / 100,
      bv.jitterMs / 100,
      bv.errorRate,
      bv.timeoutRate,
      bv.unknownMessageRate,
      bv.cpuUtilization,
      bv.memoryPressureScore,
    ];
  }

  addBaseline(nodeId: string, bv: BehaviorVector): void {
    this.baselines.set(nodeId, bv);
    const v = this.vectorize(bv);
    this.vectorBuffer.push(v);

    // Retrain forest when we have enough data
    if (this.vectorBuffer.length >= 10) {
      this.forest.train(this.vectorBuffer);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Compare current behavior to baseline using KL-divergence approximation
   */
  compareToBaseline(
    nodeId: string,
    current: BehaviorVector
  ): { deviationScore: number; deviatingDimensions: string[] } {
    const baseline = this.baselines.get(nodeId);
    if (!baseline) return { deviationScore: 0, deviatingDimensions: [] };

    const dims = [
      { name: 'avgResponseTimeMs', baseline: baseline.avgResponseTimeMs, current: current.avgResponseTimeMs, tolerance: 0.2 },
      { name: 'packetRatePps', baseline: baseline.packetRatePps, current: current.packetRatePps, tolerance: 0.3 },
      { name: 'errorRate', baseline: baseline.errorRate, current: current.errorRate, tolerance: 0.5 },
      { name: 'consensusParticipationRate', baseline: baseline.consensusParticipationRate, current: current.consensusParticipationRate, tolerance: 0.1 },
      { name: 'clockSkewPpm', baseline: baseline.clockSkewPpm, current: current.clockSkewPpm, tolerance: 0.4 },
      { name: 'voteAgreementRate', baseline: baseline.voteAgreementRate, current: current.voteAgreementRate, tolerance: 0.1 },
    ];

    const deviatingDimensions: string[] = [];
    let totalDeviation = 0;

    for (const dim of dims) {
      if (dim.baseline === 0 && dim.current === 0) continue;
      const relativeDeviation = Math.abs(dim.current - dim.baseline) / (Math.abs(dim.baseline) + 1e-10);
      if (relativeDeviation > dim.tolerance) {
        deviatingDimensions.push(dim.name);
        totalDeviation += relativeDeviation;
      }
    }

    return {
      deviationScore: Math.min(totalDeviation / dims.length, 1.0),
      deviatingDimensions,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Isolation Forest anomaly score for novel behavior
   */
  isolationScore(bv: BehaviorVector): number {
    const v = this.vectorize(bv);
    return this.forest.score(v);
  }

  hasBaseline(nodeId: string): boolean {
    return this.baselines.has(nodeId);
  }
}

// ─── Attestation Verifier ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AttestationVerifier {
  private readonly trustedSigners: Set<string>;
  private readonly expectedCodeHash: string;
  private readonly expectedConfigHash: string;
  private readonly usedNonces: Set<string> = new Set();

  constructor(
    trustedSigners: string[],
    expectedCodeHash: string,
    expectedConfigHash: string
  ) {
    this.trustedSigners = new Set(trustedSigners);
    this.expectedCodeHash = expectedCodeHash;
    this.expectedConfigHash = expectedConfigHash;
  }

  generateChallenge(nodeId: string): { nonce: string; challengeHash: string } {
    const nonce = randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
    const challengeHash = createHash('sha256')
      .update(nodeId)
      .update(nonce)
      .update(Date.now().toString())
      .digest('hex');
    this.usedNonces.add(nonce);
    return { nonce, challengeHash };
  }

  verifyQuote(quote: AttestationQuote): {
    valid: boolean;
    reasons: string[];
    codeHashMatch: boolean;
    configHashMatch: boolean;
    nonceValid: boolean;
    signatureValid: boolean;
    expired: boolean;
  } {
    const reasons: string[] = [];
    let valid = true;

    // Check expiry
    const expired = Date.now() > quote.expiresAt;
    if (expired) {
      reasons.push('Quote expired');
      valid = false;
    }

    // Nonce replay protection
    const nonceValid = this.usedNonces.has(quote.nonce);
    if (!nonceValid) {
      reasons.push('Nonce not recognized or already consumed');
      valid = false;
    } else {
      this.usedNonces.delete(quote.nonce); // Consume nonce
    }

    // Signature verification (TEE hardware key)
    const signatureValid = this.verifySignature(quote);
    if (!signatureValid) {
      reasons.push('Hardware quote signature invalid');
      valid = false;
    }

    // PCR chain integrity
    const pcrRoot = quote.pcrChain.chainRoot;
    const computedRoot = this.computePCRRoot(quote.pcrChain.registers);
    if (pcrRoot !== computedRoot) {
      reasons.push('PCR chain root mismatch');
      valid = false;
    }

    // Code hash matching
    const codeHashMatch = quote.pcrChain.ubeCodeHash === this.expectedCodeHash;
    if (!codeHashMatch) {
      reasons.push(`Code hash mismatch: expected ${this.expectedCodeHash.slice(0, 8)}... got ${quote.pcrChain.ubeCodeHash.slice(0, 8)}...`);
      valid = false;
    }

    // Config hash matching
    const configHashMatch = quote.pcrChain.ubeConfigHash === this.expectedConfigHash;
    if (!configHashMatch) {
      reasons.push('Config hash mismatch');
      valid = false;
    }

    return { valid, reasons, codeHashMatch, configHashMatch, nonceValid, signatureValid, expired };
  }

  private verifySignature(quote: AttestationQuote): boolean {
    // In real TEE: verify hardware-signed quote using vendor PKI chain
    // Here: HMAC-based simulation using trusted key derivation
    const payload = JSON.stringify({
      nodeId: quote.nodeId,
      nonce: quote.nonce,
      reportData: quote.reportData,
      pcrRoot: quote.pcrChain.chainRoot,
      timestamp: quote.timestamp,
    });

    // Simulate: if hardware key is in trusted set, signature is valid
    // Real implementation: parse and verify DCAP quote using libsgx-dcap
    if (!this.trustedSigners.has(quote.hardwareKeyId)) {
      // Derive expected HMAC from chain of trust
      const derived = createHash('sha256')
        .update(quote.hardwareKeyId)
        .update(payload)
        .digest('hex');

      return quote.signature.length === 64 && quote.signature !== '0'.repeat(64);
    }
    return true;
  }

  private computePCRRoot(registers: string[]): string {
    let layer = [...registers];
    while (layer.length > 1) {
      const next: string[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < layer.length; i += 2) {
        const a = layer[i];
        const b = layer[i + 1] ?? a;
        next.push(createHash('sha256').update(a + b).digest('hex'));
      }
      layer = next;
    }
    return layer[0];
  }
}

// ─── Sybil Detector ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SybilDetector {
  private readonly stakeMap: Map<string, number> = new Map();
  private readonly identityGraph: Map<string, Set<string>> = new Map(); // shared attributes
  private totalStake: number = 0;

  registerNode(nodeId: string, stake: number, attributes: Record<string, string>): void {
    this.stakeMap.set(nodeId, stake);
    this.totalStake += stake;

    // Build identity graph: nodes sharing attributes may be Sybil
    for (const [attr, value] of Object.entries(attributes)) {
      const key = `${attr}:${value}`;
      if (!this.identityGraph.has(key)) this.identityGraph.set(key, new Set());
      this.identityGraph.get(key)!.add(nodeId);
    }
  }

  updateStake(nodeId: string, newStake: number): void {
    const old = this.stakeMap.get(nodeId) || 0;
    this.totalStake = this.totalStake - old + newStake;
    this.stakeMap.set(nodeId, newStake);
  }

  stakeRatio(nodeId: string): number {
    if (this.totalStake === 0) return 0;
    return (this.stakeMap.get(nodeId) || 0) / this.totalStake;
  }

  /**
   * Detect potential Sybil clusters using stake concentration and shared identifiers
   */
  detectSybilClusters(minStakeThreshold: number): {
    suspects: string[];
    clusters: Map<string, string[]>;
    suspiciousAttribute: string[];
  } {
    const suspects: string[] = [];
    const clusters = new Map<string, string[]>();
    const suspiciousAttribute: string[] = [];

    // Low-stake nodes are suspect
    for (const [nodeId, stake] of this.stakeMap.entries()) {
      const ratio = this.totalStake > 0 ? stake / this.totalStake : 0;
      if (ratio < minStakeThreshold) {
        suspects.push(nodeId);
      }
    }

    // Identify clusters from shared attributes
    for (const [attr, nodes] of this.identityGraph.entries()) {
      if (nodes.size > 3) { // > 3 nodes sharing same attribute = suspicious
        const cluster = Array.from(nodes);
        clusters.set(attr, cluster);
        suspiciousAttribute.push(attr);
        // Add all cluster members to suspects
        for (const n of cluster) {
          if (!suspects.includes(n)) suspects.push(n);
        }
      }
    }

    return { suspects, clusters, suspiciousAttribute };
  }

  /**
   * IP subnet analysis for coordinated Sybil attacks
   */
  detectSubnetClustering(
    nodeSubnets: Map<string, string>  // nodeId -> /24 subnet
  ): string[] {
    const subnetCounts = new Map<string, string[]>();
    for (const [nodeId, subnet] of nodeSubnets.entries()) {
      if (!subnetCounts.has(subnet)) subnetCounts.set(subnet, []);
      subnetCounts.get(subnet)!.push(nodeId);
    }

    const suspects: string[] = [];
    for (const [subnet, nodes] of subnetCounts.entries()) {
      if (nodes.length > 5) { // More than 5 nodes in same /24 = suspicious
        suspects.push(...nodes);
      }
    }
    return suspects;
  }
}

// ─── Evidence Ledger ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class EvidenceLedger {
  private entries: EvidenceLedgerEntry[] = [];
  private lastHash: string = '0'.repeat(64);
  private readonly maxSize: number;
  private readonly signingKey: Buffer;

  constructor(maxSize: number, signingKey: Buffer) {
    this.maxSize = maxSize;
    this.signingKey = signingKey;
  }

  append(
    category: EvidenceCategory,
    nodeId: string,
    content: Record<string, unknown>,
    signedBy: string
  ): EvidenceLedgerEntry {
    const id = randomBytes(16).toString('hex');
    const entryData = JSON.stringify({ id, category, nodeId, content, timestamp: Date.now(), previousHash: this.lastHash });
    const hash = createHmac('sha256', this.signingKey)
      .update(entryData)
      .digest('hex');

    const entry: EvidenceLedgerEntry = {
      id,
      category,
      nodeId,
      content,
      previousHash: this.lastHash,
      hash,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      signedBy,
    };

    this.lastHash = hash;

    if (this.entries.length >= this.maxSize) {
      // Rolling window: remove oldest
      this.entries.shift();
    }
    this.entries.push(entry);
    return entry;
  }

  /**
   * Verify ledger integrity (all hashes chain correctly)
   */
  verify(): { valid: boolean; brokenAt: number | null } {
    let prevHash = '0'.repeat(64);
    for (let i = 0; i < this.entries.length; i++) {
      const entry = this.entries[i];
      if (entry.previousHash !== prevHash) {
        return { valid: false, brokenAt: i };
      }
      // Re-compute hash to check tampering
      const entryData = JSON.stringify({
        id: entry.id, category: entry.category, nodeId: entry.nodeId,
        content: entry.content, timestamp: entry.timestamp, previousHash: entry.previousHash,
      });
      const expected = createHmac('sha256', this.signingKey).update(entryData).digest('hex');
      if (entry.hash !== expected) {
        return { valid: false, brokenAt: i };
      }
      prevHash = entry.hash;
    }
    return { valid: true, brokenAt: null };
  }

  getForNode(nodeId: string): EvidenceLedgerEntry[] {
    return this.entries.filter(e => e.nodeId === nodeId);
  }

  getByCategory(category: EvidenceCategory): EvidenceLedgerEntry[] {
    return this.entries.filter(e => e.category === category);
  }

  get size(): number { return this.entries.length; }
  get tipHash(): string { return this.lastHash; }
  getAll(): EvidenceLedgerEntry[] { return [...this.entries]; }
}

// ─── Ejection Protocol ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class EjectionProtocol extends EventEmitter {
  private proposals: Map<string, EjectionProposal> = new Map();
  private readonly f: number;
  private readonly totalNodes: number;

  constructor(byzantineFaultTolerance: number, totalNodes: number) {
    super();
    this.f = byzantineFaultTolerance;
    this.totalNodes = totalNodes;
  }

  propose(
    targetNodeId: string,
    proposerNodeId: string,
    reasons: AnomalyType[],
    anomalyScores: number[],
    evidence: EvidenceLedgerEntry[]
  ): EjectionProposal {
    const id = randomBytes(8).toString('hex');
    const proposal: EjectionProposal = {
      id,
      targetNodeId,
      proposerNodeId,
      reason: reasons,
      anomalyScores,
      evidence,
      votes: new Map([[proposerNodeId, true]]),
      proposedAt: Date.now(),
      status: 'PENDING',
      requiredVotes: this.f + 1,
    };

    this.proposals.set(id, proposal);
    this.emit('ejection:proposed', { proposalId: id, targetNodeId, reasons });

    // Auto-expire after 5 minutes
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (this.proposals.get(id)?.status === 'PENDING') {
        const p = this.proposals.get(id)!;
        p.status = 'EXPIRED';
        this.emit('ejection:expired', { proposalId: id, targetNodeId });
      }
    }, 300_000);

    return proposal;
  }

  castVote(proposalId: string, voterId: string, agree: boolean): void {
    const proposal = this.proposals.get(proposalId);
    if (!proposal || proposal.status !== 'PENDING') return;

    proposal.votes.set(voterId, agree);
    const agreesCount = Array.from(proposal.votes.values()).filter(v => v).length;
    const disagreeCount = proposal.votes.size - agreesCount;

    if (agreesCount >= proposal.requiredVotes) {
      proposal.status = 'ACCEPTED';
      this.emit('ejection:accepted', {
        proposalId,
        targetNodeId: proposal.targetNodeId,
        voteCount: agreesCount,
      });
    } else if (disagreeCount > this.totalNodes - proposal.requiredVotes) {
      proposal.status = 'REJECTED';
      this.emit('ejection:rejected', { proposalId, targetNodeId: proposal.targetNodeId });
    }
  }

  getProposal(id: string): EjectionProposal | null {
    return this.proposals.get(id) || null;
  }

  getPendingProposals(): EjectionProposal[] {
    return Array.from(this.proposals.values()).filter(p => p.status === 'PENDING');
  }

  hasActiveProposalFor(nodeId: string): boolean {
    return Array.from(this.proposals.values()).some(
      p => p.targetNodeId === nodeId && p.status === 'PENDING'
    );
  }
}

// ─── Main Engine ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class MeshIntegrityVerifier extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly config: IntegrityVerifierConfig;
  private readonly nodes: Map<string, NodeProfile> = new Map();
  private readonly pcrChains: Map<string, PCRChainManager> = new Map();
  private readonly attestationVerifier: AttestationVerifier;
  private readonly fingerprinter: BehavioralFingerprinter;
  private readonly sybilDetector: SybilDetector;
  private readonly ledger: EvidenceLedger;
  private readonly ejectionProtocol: EjectionProtocol;
  private readonly signingKey: Buffer;
  private attestationTimers: Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout> = new Map();
  private maintenanceTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;

  constructor(config: IntegrityVerifierConfig) {
    super();
    this.config = config;
    this.signingKey = createHash('sha256').update(config.localNodeId).update(config.meshId).digest();

    this.attestationVerifier = new AttestationVerifier(
      config.trustedSignerKeys,
      config.expectedCodeHash,
      config.expectedConfigHash
    );

    this.fingerprinter = new BehavioralFingerprinter();
    this.sybilDetector = new SybilDetector();
    this.ledger = new EvidenceLedger(config.maxEvidenceLedgerSize, this.signingKey);
    this.ejectionProtocol = new EjectionProtocol(
      config.byzantineFaultTolerance,
      0 // Updated dynamically
    );

    this.wireEjectionEvents();
    this.startMaintenanceLoop();
  }

  // ── Node Registration ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  registerNode(
    nodeId: string,
    publicKey: string,
    stake: number = 1.0,
    knownCodeHash?: string
  ): NodeProfile {
    const now = Date.now();
    const profile: NodeProfile = {
      nodeId,
      trustLevel: NodeTrustLevel.PROBATION,
      stake,
      behaviorBaseline: null,
      behaviorHistory: [],
      lastAttestation: null,
      lastAttestationAt: 0,
      nextAttestationAt: now + 5000, // First attestation within 5s
      anomalies: [],
      ejectionProposal: null,
      isolationScore: 0.5,
      consecutiveFailures: 0,
      firstSeenAt: now,
      lastSeenAt: now,
      publicKey,
      knownCodeHash: knownCodeHash || this.config.expectedCodeHash,
    };

    this.nodes.set(nodeId, profile);
    this.pcrChains.set(nodeId, new PCRChainManager());
    this.sybilDetector.registerNode(nodeId, stake, { publicKey });

    this.ledger.append(EvidenceCategory.ATTESTATION, nodeId, {
      event: 'NODE_REGISTERED', stake, publicKey: publicKey.slice(0, 16) + '...',
    }, this.config.localNodeId);

    this.scheduleAttestation(nodeId);
    this.emit('integrity:node_registered', { nodeId, trustLevel: NodeTrustLevel.PROBATION });
    return profile;
  }

  // ── Attestation ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async challengeNode(nodeId: string): Promise<{ nonce: string; challengeHash: string } | null> {
    const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (!profile || profile.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.EJECTED) return null;

    const challenge = this.attestationVerifier.generateChallenge(nodeId);
    this.emit('integrity:challenge_issued', { nodeId, nonce: challenge.nonce });
    return challenge;
  }

  async receiveAttestation(
    nodeId: string,
    quote: AttestationQuote
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (!profile) return false;

    if (!this.config.enableTeeAttestation) {
      // Fallback: software hash only
      const hashMatch = quote.pcrChain.ubeCodeHash === profile.knownCodeHash;
      profile.lastAttestation = quote;
      profile.lastAttestationAt = Date.now();
      if (hashMatch && profile.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.PROBATION) {
        profile.trustLevel = NodeTrustLevel.TRUSTED;
      }
      return hashMatch;
    }

    const result = this.attestationVerifier.verifyQuote(quote);

    this.ledger.append(EvidenceCategory.ATTESTATION, nodeId, {
      event: 'ATTESTATION_RECEIVED',
      valid: result.valid,
      codeHashMatch: result.codeHashMatch,
      reasons: result.reasons,
      type: quote.type,
    }, this.config.localNodeId);

    if (result.valid) {
      profile.lastAttestation = quote;
      profile.lastAttestationAt = Date.now();
      profile.consecutiveFailures = 0;

      if (profile.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.PROBATION) {
        profile.trustLevel = NodeTrustLevel.TRUSTED;
        this.emit('integrity:node_trusted', { nodeId });
      } else if (profile.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.SUSPECT) {
        profile.trustLevel = NodeTrustLevel.PROBATION;
        this.emit('integrity:node_recovered', { nodeId });
      }

      // Schedule next attestation
      this.scheduleAttestation(nodeId);
    } else {
      profile.consecutiveFailures++;
      this.emit('integrity:attestation_failed', { nodeId, reasons: result.reasons, failures: profile.consecutiveFailures });

      const anomaly = this.createAnomaly(nodeId, AnomalyType.ATTESTATION_FAILURE, 'CRITICAL', 0.9, {
        reasons: result.reasons,
        codeHashMatch: result.codeHashMatch,
        failures: profile.consecutiveFailures,
      });
      profile.anomalies.push(anomaly);

      if (profile.consecutiveFailures >= 2) {
        this.escalateToSuspect(nodeId);
      }
    }

    return result.valid;
  }

  // ── Behavioral Analysis ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  updateBehavior(nodeId: string, bv: BehaviorVector): void {
    const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (!profile || !this.config.enableBehavioralAnalysis) return;

    profile.lastSeenAt = Date.now();
    profile.behaviorHistory.push(bv);

    // Keep sliding window
    const cutoff = Date.now() - this.config.behaviorWindowMs * 10;
    profile.behaviorHistory = profile.behaviorHistory.filter(b => b.timestamp > cutoff);

    if (!profile.behaviorBaseline) {
      // Establish baseline after enough observations
      if (profile.behaviorHistory.length >= 5 && profile.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.TRUSTED) {
        profile.behaviorBaseline = bv;
        this.fingerprinter.addBaseline(nodeId, bv);
        this.emit('integrity:baseline_established', { nodeId });
      }
      return;
    }

    // Compare to baseline
    const deviation = this.fingerprinter.compareToBaseline(nodeId, bv);

    // Isolation Forest score
    const isoScore = this.fingerprinter.isolationScore(bv);
    profile.isolationScore = isoScore;

    if (deviation.deviationScore > 0.5 || isoScore > this.config.anomalyThreshold) {
      const severity = isoScore > 0.9 ? 'CRITICAL' : isoScore > 0.7 ? 'HIGH' : 'MEDIUM';
      const anomaly = this.createAnomaly(
        nodeId,
        isoScore > this.config.anomalyThreshold ? AnomalyType.BEHAVIORAL_OUTLIER : AnomalyType.TRAFFIC_PATTERN_CHANGE,
        severity,
        Math.max(deviation.deviationScore, isoScore),
        {
          deviatingDimensions: deviation.deviatingDimensions,
          isolationScore: isoScore,
          deviationScore: deviation.deviationScore,
        }
      );
      profile.anomalies.push(anomaly);
      this.emit('integrity:behavioral_anomaly', { nodeId, anomaly });

      if (severity === 'CRITICAL') {
        this.escalateToSuspect(nodeId);
      }
    }

    // Timing fingerprint check
    if (Math.abs(bv.clockSkewPpm - (profile.behaviorBaseline?.clockSkewPpm || 0)) > 50) {
      const anomaly = this.createAnomaly(nodeId, AnomalyType.TIMING_DEVIATION, 'MEDIUM', 0.6, {
        expectedSkewPpm: profile.behaviorBaseline?.clockSkewPpm,
        observedSkewPpm: bv.clockSkewPpm,
      });
      profile.anomalies.push(anomaly);
    }
  }

  // ── Sybil Detection ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  runSybilScan(nodeSubnets?: Map<string, string>): string[] {
    if (!this.config.enableSybilDetection) return [];

    const { suspects, clusters, suspiciousAttribute } = this.sybilDetector.detectSybilClusters(
      this.config.sybilStakeThreshold
    );

    let subnetSuspects: string[] = [];
    if (nodeSubnets) {
      subnetSuspects = this.sybilDetector.detectSubnetClustering(nodeSubnets);
    }

    const allSuspects = [...new Set([...suspects, ...subnetSuspects])];

    for (const nodeId of allSuspects) {
      const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
      if (!profile) continue;

      const anomaly = this.createAnomaly(nodeId, AnomalyType.SYBIL_DETECTED, 'HIGH', 0.75, {
        stakeRatio: this.sybilDetector.stakeRatio(nodeId),
        inCluster: clusters.size > 0,
        suspiciousAttributes: suspiciousAttribute,
      });
      profile.anomalies.push(anomaly);

      this.ledger.append(EvidenceCategory.SYBIL, nodeId, {
        event: 'SYBIL_SUSPECTED',
        stakeRatio: this.sybilDetector.stakeRatio(nodeId),
        clusters: suspiciousAttribute,
      }, this.config.localNodeId);
    }

    if (allSuspects.length > 0) {
      this.emit('integrity:sybil_detected', { suspects: allSuspects, clusters: Object.fromEntries(clusters) });
    }

    return allSuspects;
  }

  // ── Ejection ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private escalateToSuspect(nodeId: string): void {
    const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (!profile) return;

    if (profile.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.EJECTED || profile.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.DEAD) return;

    profile.trustLevel = NodeTrustLevel.SUSPECT;
    this.emit('integrity:node_suspect', { nodeId });

    // Propose ejection if anomalies are severe enough
    const criticalAnomalies = profile.anomalies.filter(a => a.severity === 'CRITICAL');
    if (criticalAnomalies.length >= 2 && !this.ejectionProtocol.hasActiveProposalFor(nodeId)) {
      const evidence = this.ledger.getForNode(nodeId).slice(-10);
      const proposal = this.ejectionProtocol.propose(
        nodeId,
        this.config.localNodeId,
        criticalAnomalies.map(a => a.type),
        criticalAnomalies.map(a => a.anomalyScore),
        evidence
      );
      profile.ejectionProposal = proposal;

      this.ledger.append(EvidenceCategory.EJECTION, nodeId, {
        event: 'EJECTION_PROPOSED',
        proposalId: proposal.id,
        reasons: proposal.reason,
      }, this.config.localNodeId);
    }
  }

  voteOnEjection(proposalId: string, agree: boolean): void {
    this.ejectionProtocol.castVote(proposalId, this.config.localNodeId, agree);
  }

  receiveEjectionVote(proposalId: string, voterId: string, agree: boolean): void {
    this.ejectionProtocol.castVote(proposalId, voterId, agree);
  }

  // ── Dead vs Compromised Disambiguation ────────────────────────────────────

  async disambiguateNodeState(nodeId: string): Promise<'DEAD' | 'COMPROMISED' | 'ALIVE'> {
    const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (!profile) return 'DEAD';

    const timeSinceLastSeen = Date.now() - profile.lastSeenAt;
    const criticalAnomalies = profile.anomalies.filter(a => a.severity === 'CRITICAL');

    // Likely dead: haven't seen it in >5 minutes, no anomalies
    if (timeSinceLastSeen > 300_000 && criticalAnomalies.length === 0) {
      profile.trustLevel = NodeTrustLevel.DEAD;
      this.emit('integrity:node_dead', { nodeId, silentForMs: timeSinceLastSeen });
      return 'DEAD';
    }

    // Likely compromised: still responding but with anomalies/failed attestation
    if (criticalAnomalies.length >= 2 || profile.consecutiveFailures >= 2) {
      this.emit('integrity:node_compromised', { nodeId, anomalies: criticalAnomalies.length });
      return 'COMPROMISED';
    }

    return 'ALIVE';
  }

  // ── Recovery ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  planReplacement(oldNodeId: string, newNodeId: string): ReplacementPlan {
    const oldProfile = this.nodes.get(oldNodeId);
    const stakeToTransfer = oldProfile?.stake || 0;

    const plan: ReplacementPlan = {
      oldNodeId,
      newNodeId,
      stateTransferProtocol: oldProfile?.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.DEAD ? 'CHECKPOINT_RESTORE' : 'FULL_SYNC',
      keyHandoff: false,  // Compromised nodes: no key handoff
      stakeTransfer: stateTransferProtocol === 'CHECKPOINT_RESTORE' ? stakeToTransfer : 0,
      estimatedDurationMs: 30_000,
      checkpoints: this.generateCheckpoints(oldNodeId),
    };

    this.ledger.append(EvidenceCategory.RECOVERY, oldNodeId, {
      event: 'REPLACEMENT_PLANNED',
      newNodeId,
      protocol: plan.stateTransferProtocol,
      stakeTransfer: plan.stakeTransfer,
    }, this.config.localNodeId);

    this.emit('integrity:replacement_planned', plan);
    return plan;
  }

  executeReplacement(plan: ReplacementPlan): void {
    const old = this.nodes.get(plan.oldNodeId);
    if (old) {
      old.trustLevel = NodeTrustLevel.REPLACING;
    }

    // Register new node immediately at probation level
    this.registerNode(plan.newNodeId, randomBytes(32).toString('hex'), plan.stakeTransfer);

    this.ledger.append(EvidenceCategory.RECOVERY, plan.oldNodeId, {
      event: 'REPLACEMENT_EXECUTED',
      newNodeId: plan.newNodeId,
    }, this.config.localNodeId);

    this.emit('integrity:replacement_executed', plan);
  }

  private generateCheckpoints(nodeId: string): string[] {
    const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (!profile) return [];
    return [
      createHash('sha256').update(nodeId).update('checkpoint_0').digest('hex'),
      createHash('sha256').update(nodeId).update('checkpoint_1').digest('hex'),
    ];
  }

  // ── Scheduling ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private scheduleAttestation(nodeId: string): void {
    const existing = this.attestationTimers.get(nodeId);
    if (existing) clearTimeout(existing);

    // Rolling randomized interval: ±50% of base interval
    const jitter = (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.config.attestationIntervalMs;
    const delay = this.config.attestationIntervalMs + jitter;

    const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
    if (profile) {
      profile.nextAttestationAt = Date.now() + delay;
    }

    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      this.attestationTimers.delete(nodeId);
      const p = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
      if (!p || p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.EJECTED) return;

      this.emit('integrity:attestation_due', { nodeId });
      // After grace period, if no response, increment failures
      setTimeout(() => {
        const profile = this.nodes.get(nodeId);
        if (!profile) return;
        const attestationAge = Date.now() - profile.lastAttestationAt;
        if (attestationAge > this.config.attestationIntervalMs + ATTESTATION_GRACE_PERIOD_MS) {
          profile.consecutiveFailures++;
          this.emit('integrity:attestation_missed', { nodeId, failures: profile.consecutiveFailures });
          if (profile.consecutiveFailures >= 3) {
            this.disambiguateNodeState(nodeId);
          }
        }
        this.scheduleAttestation(nodeId);
      }, ATTESTATION_GRACE_PERIOD_MS);
    }, delay);

    this.attestationTimers.set(nodeId, timer);
  }

  private startMaintenanceLoop(): void {
    this.maintenanceTimer = setInterval(() => {
      this.runMaintenanceCycle();
    }, 60_000); // Every minute
  }

  private runMaintenanceCycle(): void {
    for (const [nodeId, profile] of this.nodes.entries()) {
      // Trim old anomalies (keep last 100)
      if (profile.anomalies.length > 100) {
        profile.anomalies = profile.anomalies.slice(-100);
      }
      // Trim behavior history
      if (profile.behaviorHistory.length > 1000) {
        profile.behaviorHistory = profile.behaviorHistory.slice(-1000);
      }
    }
    this.emit('integrity:maintenance_complete', { nodes: this.nodes.size });
  }

  // ── Event Wiring ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private wireEjectionEvents(): void {
    this.ejectionProtocol.on('ejection:accepted', ({ proposalId, targetNodeId }) => {
      const profile = this.nodes.get(targetNodeId);
      if (profile) {
        profile.trustLevel = NodeTrustLevel.EJECTED;
      }

      this.ledger.append(EvidenceCategory.EJECTION, targetNodeId, {
        event: 'NODE_EJECTED', proposalId,
      }, this.config.localNodeId);

      this.emit('integrity:node_ejected', { nodeId: targetNodeId, proposalId });

      // Cancel attestation schedule
      const timer = this.attestationTimers.get(targetNodeId);
      if (timer) {
        clearTimeout(timer);
        this.attestationTimers.delete(targetNodeId);
      }
    });

    this.ejectionProtocol.on('ejection:rejected', ({ proposalId, targetNodeId }) => {
      const profile = this.nodes.get(targetNodeId);
      if (profile && profile.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.SUSPECT) {
        profile.trustLevel = NodeTrustLevel.PROBATION;
      }
      this.emit('integrity:ejection_rejected', { nodeId: targetNodeId, proposalId });
    });
  }

  // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private createAnomaly(
    nodeId: string,
    type: AnomalyType,
    severity: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH' | 'CRITICAL',
    score: number,
    evidence: Record<string, unknown>
  ): AnomalyReport {
    const anomaly: AnomalyReport = {
      id: randomBytes(8).toString('hex'),
      nodeId,
      type,
      severity,
      anomalyScore: score,
      evidence,
      detectedAt: Date.now(),
      reportedBy: this.config.localNodeId,
    };

    this.ledger.append(EvidenceCategory.BEHAVIORAL, nodeId, {
      event: 'ANOMALY_DETECTED', type, severity, score, evidence,
    }, this.config.localNodeId);

    return anomaly;
  }

  // ── Query API ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getNodeProfile(nodeId: string): NodeProfile | null {
    return this.nodes.get(nodeId) || null;
  }

  getTrustedNodes(): string[] {
    return Array.from(this.nodes.entries())
      .filter(([, p]) => p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.TRUSTED)
      .map(([id]) => id);
  }

  getSuspectNodes(): string[] {
    return Array.from(this.nodes.entries())
      .filter(([, p]) => p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.SUSPECT)
      .map(([id]) => id);
  }

  getEjectedNodes(): string[] {
    return Array.from(this.nodes.entries())
      .filter(([, p]) => p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.EJECTED)
      .map(([id]) => id);
  }

  getPendingEjections(): EjectionProposal[] {
    return this.ejectionProtocol.getPendingProposals();
  }

  getLedger(): EvidenceLedger {
    return this.ledger;
  }

  verifyLedgerIntegrity(): { valid: boolean; brokenAt: number | null } {
    return this.ledger.verify();
  }

  generateIntegrityReport(): IntegrityReport {
    const all = Array.from(this.nodes.values());
    const recentCutoff = Date.now() - 3_600_000; // Last hour
    const recentAnomalies = all
      .flatMap(p => p.anomalies)
      .filter(a => a.detectedAt > recentCutoff)
      .sort((a, b) => b.anomalyScore - a.anomalyScore)
      .slice(0, 20);

    const avgIsolation = all.length > 0
      ? all.reduce((acc, p) => acc + p.isolationScore, 0) / all.length
      : 0;

    const trustScores: Record<NodeTrustLevel, number> = {
      [NodeTrustLevel.TRUSTED]: 1.0,
      [NodeTrustLevel.PROBATION]: 0.7,
      [NodeTrustLevel.SUSPECT]: 0.3,
      [NodeTrustLevel.DEAD]: 0.1,
      [NodeTrustLevel.EJECTED]: 0.0,
      [NodeTrustLevel.REPLACING]: 0.5,
    };

    const avgTrust = all.length > 0
      ? all.reduce((acc, p) => acc + trustScores[p.trustLevel], 0) / all.length
      : 0;

    const trusted = all.filter(p => p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.TRUSTED).length;
    const meshHealth = all.length > 0 ? trusted / all.length * avgTrust : 0;

    return {
      meshId: this.config.meshId,
      generatedAt: Date.now(),
      totalNodes: all.length,
      trustedNodes: trusted,
      probationNodes: all.filter(p => p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.PROBATION).length,
      suspectNodes: all.filter(p => p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.SUSPECT).length,
      ejectedNodes: all.filter(p => p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.EJECTED).length,
      deadNodes: all.filter(p => p.trustLevel === NodeTrustLevel.DEAD).length,
      pendingEjections: this.ejectionProtocol.getPendingProposals().length,
      averageIsolationScore: avgIsolation,
      averageTrustScore: avgTrust,
      recentAnomalies,
      sybilSuspects: this.runSybilScan(),
      evidenceLedgerSize: this.ledger.size,
      meshHealthScore: meshHealth,
    };
  }

  shutdown(): void {
    for (const timer of this.attestationTimers.values()) clearTimeout(timer);
    this.attestationTimers.clear();
    if (this.maintenanceTimer) clearInterval(this.maintenanceTimer);
    this.emit('integrity:shutdown');
  }
}

// ─── Factory ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createMeshIntegrityVerifier(
  overrides?: Partial<IntegrityVerifierConfig>
): MeshIntegrityVerifier {
  const defaults: IntegrityVerifierConfig = {
    localNodeId: `integrity_${randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}`,
    meshId: `mesh_${randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}`,
    expectedCodeHash: createHash('sha256').update('UBE_CANONICAL_BINARY_v1').digest('hex'),
    expectedConfigHash: createHash('sha256').update('UBE_DEFAULT_CONFIG_v1').digest('hex'),
    trustedSignerKeys: [],
    byzantineFaultTolerance: EJECTION_THRESHOLD_F,
    attestationIntervalMs: ROLLING_ATTESTATION_INTERVAL_MS,
    behaviorWindowMs: BEHAVIOR_WINDOW_MS,
    isolationForestTrees: ISOLATION_FOREST_TREES,
    anomalyThreshold: 0.7,
    sybilStakeThreshold: SYBIL_STAKE_MINIMUM,
    enableTeeAttestation: true,
    enableBehavioralAnalysis: true,
    enableSybilDetection: true,
    enableRollingSchedule: true,
    maxEvidenceLedgerSize: EVIDENCE_LEDGER_MAX,
  };

  return new MeshIntegrityVerifier({ ...defaults, ...overrides });
}

export default MeshIntegrityVerifier;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — meshNode.ts
 * Sovereign Peer-to-Peer Node Engine
 *
 * Replaces central server architecture. Every UBE installation becomes a sovereign
 * node in the mesh. No Railway, no cloud, no expiry. Nodes discover each other,
 * authenticate cryptographically, route messages through the mesh, and maintain
 * consensus without any central coordinator. The infrastructure IS the nodes.
 *
 * Architecture:
 *   NodeIdentity     — Ed25519 keypair + DID, cryptographically verifiable
 *   KademliaTable    — XOR-metric DHT for decentralized peer discovery
 *   TransportManager — Pluggable transport: TCP, UDP, WebRTC, LoRa, Satellite
 *   MessageRouter    — Store-and-forward routing with epidemic replication
 *   GossipProtocol   — Probabilistic broadcast with anti-entropy synchronization
 *   CapabilityRegistry — Advertise and discover node capabilities across the mesh
 *   NATTraversal     — STUN/TURN/ICE for NAT hole-punching
 *   MeshNode         — Orchestrating sovereign node lifecycle
 */

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import * as crypto from 'crypto';
import * as net from 'net';
import * as dgram from 'dgram';
import * as os from 'os';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

const KADEMLIA_K = 20;             // K-bucket size
const KADEMLIA_ALPHA = 3;          // Parallelism factor for lookups
const KADEMLIA_B = 160;            // Key space bit width (SHA-1 compatible)
const KADEMLIA_BUCKET_COUNT = 160; // Number of k-buckets
const GOSSIP_FANOUT = 6;           // Peers to forward each gossip message to
const GOSSIP_TTL = 7;              // Maximum gossip hops
const GOSSIP_ROUNDS_PER_SECOND = 2;
const HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS = 30_000;
const PEER_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const ROUTE_CACHE_TTL_MS = 300_000;
const MESSAGE_DEDUP_WINDOW_MS = 60_000;
const STORE_FORWARD_TTL_HOURS = 48;
const MAX_MESSAGE_SIZE_BYTES = 65_535;
const PROTOCOL_VERSION = '1.0.0';
const NETWORK_MAGIC = Buffer.from('UBE2026');

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// PRIMITIVE TYPES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export type NodeId = Buffer;        // 20-byte SHA-1 of public key
export type TransportType = 'tcp' | 'udp' | 'webrtc' | 'lora' | 'satellite' | 'bluetooth' | 'serial';
export type MessageType =
  | 'PING' | 'PONG'
  | 'FIND_NODE' | 'FOUND_NODE'
  | 'FIND_VALUE' | 'FOUND_VALUE'
  | 'STORE' | 'STORED'
  | 'GOSSIP' | 'GOSSIP_ACK'
  | 'CAPABILITY_ADV' | 'CAPABILITY_QUERY' | 'CAPABILITY_RESP'
  | 'ROUTE_REQUEST' | 'ROUTE_REPLY'
  | 'DATA' | 'DATA_ACK'
  | 'CONSENSUS_PROPOSE' | 'CONSENSUS_VOTE' | 'CONSENSUS_COMMIT'
  | 'NAT_STUN_REQ' | 'NAT_STUN_RESP'
  | 'NODE_BYE';

export type NodeCapability =
  | 'RELAY'          // Can relay messages for other nodes
  | 'STORAGE'        // Can store data for offline peers
  | 'GATEWAY'        // Has internet access, can be internet gateway
  | 'LORA'           // Has LoRa radio hardware
  | 'SATELLITE'      // Has satellite link
  | 'CONSENSUS'      // Participates in consensus
  | 'COMPUTE'        // Offers computation resources
  | 'AI'             // Has local AI/ML capability
  | 'CRYPTO_ANCHOR'; // Trusted cryptographic anchor node

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// NODE IDENTITY
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface NodeKeypair {
  publicKey: Buffer;       // 32-byte Ed25519 public key
  privateKey: Buffer;      // 64-byte Ed25519 private key (includes public)
}

export interface NodeIdentityDoc {
  nodeId: string;          // hex-encoded 20-byte ID
  publicKey: string;       // hex-encoded public key
  did: string;             // did:ube:<nodeId>
  createdAt: number;       // Unix timestamp
  capabilities: NodeCapability[];
  networkMagic: string;    // Network identifier (prevents cross-network attacks)
  signature: string;       // Self-signed identity document
}

export class NodeIdentity {
  public readonly nodeId: NodeId;
  public readonly keypair: NodeKeypair;
  public readonly did: string;
  private readonly capabilities: Set<NodeCapability>;

  constructor(keypair: NodeKeypair, capabilities: NodeCapability[] = []) {
    this.keypair = keypair;
    this.nodeId = NodeIdentity.deriveNodeId(keypair.publicKey);
    this.did = `did:ube:${this.nodeId.toString('hex')}`;
    this.capabilities = new Set(capabilities);
  }

  static generate(capabilities: NodeCapability[] = []): NodeIdentity {
    const { privateKey, publicKey } = crypto.generateKeyPairSync('ed25519', {
      privateKeyEncoding: { type: 'pkcs8', format: 'der' },
      publicKeyEncoding: { type: 'spki', format: 'der' },
    });

    // Extract raw 32-byte keys from DER wrappers
    const rawPublic = publicKey.slice(-32);
    const rawPrivate = Buffer.concat([privateKey.slice(-32), rawPublic]);

    return new NodeIdentity({ publicKey: rawPublic, privateKey: rawPrivate }, capabilities);
  }

  static fromSerialized(data: Buffer): NodeIdentity {
    const obj = JSON.parse(data.toString('utf8'));
    const keypair: NodeKeypair = {
      publicKey: Buffer.from(obj.publicKey, 'hex'),
      privateKey: Buffer.from(obj.privateKey, 'hex'),
    };
    return new NodeIdentity(keypair, obj.capabilities || []);
  }

  static deriveNodeId(publicKey: Buffer): NodeId {
    return crypto.createHash('sha1').update(publicKey).digest();
  }

  serialize(): Buffer {
    return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
      publicKey: this.keypair.publicKey.toString('hex'),
      privateKey: this.keypair.privateKey.toString('hex'),
      capabilities: Array.from(this.capabilities),
    }));
  }

  exportPublicDoc(): NodeIdentityDoc {
    const doc = {
      nodeId: this.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      publicKey: this.keypair.publicKey.toString('hex'),
      did: this.did,
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      capabilities: Array.from(this.capabilities),
      networkMagic: NETWORK_MAGIC.toString('hex'),
      signature: '',
    };
    doc.signature = this.sign(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ ...doc, signature: '' }))).toString('hex');
    return doc;
  }

  sign(message: Buffer): Buffer {
    const key = crypto.createPrivateKey({
      key: Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('302e020100300506032b657004220420', 'hex'), this.keypair.privateKey.slice(0, 32)]),
      format: 'der',
      type: 'pkcs8',
    });
    return crypto.sign(null, message, key);
  }

  verify(message: Buffer, signature: Buffer, publicKey?: Buffer): boolean {
    try {
      const pubKeyBuffer = publicKey ?? this.keypair.publicKey;
      const key = crypto.createPublicKey({
        key: Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('302a300506032b6570032100', 'hex'), pubKeyBuffer]),
        format: 'der',
        type: 'spki',
      });
      return crypto.verify(null, message, key, signature);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  hasCapability(cap: NodeCapability): boolean {
    return this.capabilities.has(cap);
  }

  addCapability(cap: NodeCapability): void {
    this.capabilities.add(cap);
  }

  getCapabilities(): NodeCapability[] {
    return Array.from(this.capabilities);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// PEER RECORD
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface TransportAddress {
  type: TransportType;
  host: string;
  port: number;
  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>; // LoRa channel, satellite beam, etc.
}

export interface PeerRecord {
  nodeId: NodeId;
  publicKey: Buffer;
  did: string;
  addresses: TransportAddress[];
  capabilities: NodeCapability[];
  lastSeen: number;         // Unix ms
  latencyMs: number;        // EWMA latency
  rttSamples: number[];     // Last 8 RTT samples
  failureCount: number;
  verified: boolean;        // Identity signature verified
  reachable: boolean;
}

export class PeerRecordManager {
  private records: Map<string, PeerRecord> = new Map(); // nodeId hex -> PeerRecord
  private readonly maxRecords: number;

  constructor(maxRecords = 10_000) {
    this.maxRecords = maxRecords;
  }

  upsert(record: PeerRecord): void {
    const key = record.nodeId.toString('hex');
    const existing = this.records.get(key);
    if (existing) {
      existing.lastSeen = record.lastSeen;
      existing.addresses = this.mergeAddresses(existing.addresses, record.addresses);
      existing.capabilities = record.capabilities;
      existing.reachable = record.reachable;
      if (record.latencyMs > 0) {
        this.updateLatency(existing, record.latencyMs);
      }
    } else {
      if (this.records.size >= this.maxRecords) {
        this.evictOldest();
      }
      this.records.set(key, { ...record, rttSamples: record.latencyMs > 0 ? [record.latencyMs] : [] });
    }
  }

  get(nodeId: NodeId): PeerRecord | undefined {
    return this.records.get(nodeId.toString('hex'));
  }

  has(nodeId: NodeId): boolean {
    return this.records.has(nodeId.toString('hex'));
  }

  remove(nodeId: NodeId): void {
    this.records.delete(nodeId.toString('hex'));
  }

  all(): PeerRecord[] {
    return Array.from(this.records.values());
  }

  reachable(): PeerRecord[] {
    return this.all().filter(p => p.reachable && Date.now() - p.lastSeen < PEER_TIMEOUT_MS);
  }

  byCapability(cap: NodeCapability): PeerRecord[] {
    return this.reachable().filter(p => p.capabilities.includes(cap));
  }

  closest(targetId: NodeId, count: number): PeerRecord[] {
    return this.all()
      .map(p => ({ peer: p, distance: xorDistance(p.nodeId, targetId) }))
      .sort((a, b) => compareBuffers(a.distance, b.distance))
      .slice(0, count)
      .map(x => x.peer);
  }

  private updateLatency(record: PeerRecord, sample: number): void {
    record.rttSamples.push(sample);
    if (record.rttSamples.length > 8) record.rttSamples.shift();
    // EWMA with alpha=0.25
    record.latencyMs = record.rttSamples.reduce((acc, v, i) => {
      const alpha = 0.25;
      return i === 0 ? v : acc * (1 - alpha) + v * alpha;
    }, 0);
  }

  private mergeAddresses(existing: TransportAddress[], incoming: TransportAddress[]): TransportAddress[] {
    const map = new Map(existing.map(a => [`${a.type}:${a.host}:${a.port}`, a]));
    for (const addr of incoming) {
      map.set(`${addr.type}:${addr.host}:${addr.port}`, addr);
    }
    return Array.from(map.values());
  }

  private evictOldest(): void {
    let oldestKey = '';
    let oldestTime = Infinity;
    for (const [key, record] of this.records) {
      if (record.lastSeen < oldestTime) {
        oldestTime = record.lastSeen;
        oldestKey = key;
      }
    }
    if (oldestKey) this.records.delete(oldestKey);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// KADEMLIA DHT ROUTING TABLE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

function xorDistance(a: NodeId, b: NodeId): Buffer {
  const result = Buffer.allocUnsafe(Math.min(a.length, b.length));
  for (let i = 0; i < result.length; i++) {
    result[i] = a[i] ^ b[i];
  }
  return result;
}

function compareBuffers(a: Buffer, b: Buffer): number {
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(a.length, b.length); i++) {
    if (a[i] !== b[i]) return a[i] - b[i];
  }
  return a.length - b.length;
}

function commonPrefixLength(a: NodeId, b: NodeId): number {
  let bits = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(a.length, b.length); i++) {
    const xor = a[i] ^ b[i];
    if (xor === 0) {
      bits += 8;
    } else {
      // Count leading zeros in xor byte
      let mask = 0x80;
      while (mask > 0 && (xor & mask) === 0) {
        bits++;
        mask >>= 1;
      }
      break;
    }
  }
  return bits;
}

interface KBucket {
  peers: PeerRecord[];
  lastChanged: number;
  replacementCache: PeerRecord[];
}

export class KademliaTable {
  private readonly localId: NodeId;
  private readonly buckets: KBucket[];

  constructor(localId: NodeId) {
    this.localId = localId;
    this.buckets = Array.from({ length: KADEMLIA_BUCKET_COUNT }, () => ({
      peers: [],
      lastChanged: Date.now(),
      replacementCache: [],
    }));
  }

  insert(peer: PeerRecord): 'inserted' | 'updated' | 'bucket_full' | 'self' {
    if (peer.nodeId.equals(this.localId)) return 'self';

    const bucketIdx = this.bucketIndex(peer.nodeId);
    const bucket = this.buckets[bucketIdx];

    // Check if already exists
    const existingIdx = bucket.peers.findIndex(p => p.nodeId.equals(peer.nodeId));
    if (existingIdx >= 0) {
      // Move to tail (most recently seen)
      bucket.peers.splice(existingIdx, 1);
      bucket.peers.push(peer);
      bucket.lastChanged = Date.now();
      return 'updated';
    }

    if (bucket.peers.length < KADEMLIA_K) {
      bucket.peers.push(peer);
      bucket.lastChanged = Date.now();
      return 'inserted';
    }

    // Bucket full: add to replacement cache
    const cacheIdx = bucket.replacementCache.findIndex(p => p.nodeId.equals(peer.nodeId));
    if (cacheIdx < 0) {
      bucket.replacementCache.push(peer);
      if (bucket.replacementCache.length > KADEMLIA_K) {
        bucket.replacementCache.shift(); // Evict oldest replacement
      }
    }
    return 'bucket_full';
  }

  remove(nodeId: NodeId): void {
    const bucketIdx = this.bucketIndex(nodeId);
    const bucket = this.buckets[bucketIdx];
    const idx = bucket.peers.findIndex(p => p.nodeId.equals(nodeId));
    if (idx >= 0) {
      bucket.peers.splice(idx, 1);
      // Promote from replacement cache if available
      if (bucket.replacementCache.length > 0) {
        const promoted = bucket.replacementCache.shift()!;
        bucket.peers.push(promoted);
      }
      bucket.lastChanged = Date.now();
    }
  }

  findClosest(targetId: NodeId, count: number = KADEMLIA_K): PeerRecord[] {
    const allPeers: Array<{ peer: PeerRecord; distance: Buffer }> = [];
    for (const bucket of this.buckets) {
      for (const peer of bucket.peers) {
        allPeers.push({ peer, distance: xorDistance(peer.nodeId, targetId) });
      }
    }
    allPeers.sort((a, b) => compareBuffers(a.distance, b.distance));
    return allPeers.slice(0, count).map(x => x.peer);
  }

  allPeers(): PeerRecord[] {
    return this.buckets.flatMap(b => b.peers);
  }

  size(): number {
    return this.buckets.reduce((sum, b) => sum + b.peers.length, 0);
  }

  staleBuckets(maxAgeMs: number = 3_600_000): number[] {
    return this.buckets
      .map((b, i) => ({ b, i }))
      .filter(({ b }) => b.peers.length > 0 && Date.now() - b.lastChanged > maxAgeMs)
      .map(({ i }) => i);
  }

  // Get a random node from a specific bucket for refresh
  refreshTarget(bucketIdx: number): NodeId {
    // Generate a random ID in the subtree of this bucket
    const result = Buffer.from(this.localId);
    // Flip the bit at position bucketIdx
    const byteIdx = Math.floor(bucketIdx / 8);
    const bitIdx = 7 - (bucketIdx % 8);
    result[byteIdx] ^= (1 << bitIdx);
    // Randomize remaining bits
    for (let i = byteIdx; i < result.length; i++) {
      result[i] = i === byteIdx ? result[i] : Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
    }
    return result;
  }

  private bucketIndex(nodeId: NodeId): number {
    const prefixLen = commonPrefixLength(this.localId, nodeId);
    return Math.min(prefixLen, KADEMLIA_BUCKET_COUNT - 1);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// MESH MESSAGE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface MeshMessage {
  id: string;             // UUID v4
  type: MessageType;
  version: string;
  senderId: string;       // hex nodeId
  senderPublicKey: string;
  recipientId?: string;   // hex nodeId, undefined = broadcast
  timestamp: number;
  ttl: number;
  hopCount: number;
  payload: Buffer;
  signature: string;      // hex Ed25519 signature over message minus signature field
  nonce: string;          // hex random nonce for replay prevention
}

export interface MessageEnvelope {
  message: MeshMessage;
  receivedAt: number;
  receivedFrom: string;   // hex nodeId
  transport: TransportType;
}

function serializeMessageForSigning(msg: Omit<MeshMessage, 'signature'>): Buffer {
  return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
    id: msg.id,
    type: msg.type,
    version: msg.version,
    senderId: msg.senderId,
    senderPublicKey: msg.senderPublicKey,
    recipientId: msg.recipientId,
    timestamp: msg.timestamp,
    ttl: msg.ttl,
    hopCount: msg.hopCount,
    payload: msg.payload.toString('base64'),
    nonce: msg.nonce,
  }));
}

export function createMessage(
  type: MessageType,
  identity: NodeIdentity,
  payload: Buffer,
  recipientId?: string,
  ttl: number = GOSSIP_TTL,
): MeshMessage {
  const partial = {
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    type,
    version: PROTOCOL_VERSION,
    senderId: identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
    senderPublicKey: identity.keypair.publicKey.toString('hex'),
    recipientId,
    timestamp: Date.now(),
    ttl,
    hopCount: 0,
    payload,
    nonce: crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex'),
  };
  const sigData = serializeMessageForSigning(partial);
  const signature = identity.sign(sigData).toString('hex');
  return { ...partial, signature };
}

export function verifyMessage(msg: MeshMessage): boolean {
  try {
    if (Date.now() - msg.timestamp > MESSAGE_DEDUP_WINDOW_MS * 2) return false;
    const publicKey = Buffer.from(msg.senderPublicKey, 'hex');
    const sigData = serializeMessageForSigning({ ...msg });
    const signature = Buffer.from(msg.signature, 'hex');
    const key = crypto.createPublicKey({
      key: Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('302a300506032b6570032100', 'hex'), publicKey]),
      format: 'der',
      type: 'spki',
    });
    return crypto.verify(null, sigData, key, signature);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// TRANSPORT ABSTRACTION
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface TransportSendResult {
  success: boolean;
  latencyMs?: number;
  error?: string;
}

export interface ITransport extends EventEmitter {
  readonly type: TransportType;
  start(): Promise<void>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
  send(target: TransportAddress, data: Buffer): Promise<TransportSendResult>;
  localAddresses(): TransportAddress[];
}

export class TCPTransport extends EventEmitter implements ITransport {
  readonly type: TransportType = 'tcp';
  private server: net.Server | null = null;
  private readonly port: number;
  private readonly host: string;
  private connections: Map<string, net.Socket> = new Map();

  constructor(port: number, host: string = '0.0.0.0') {
    super();
    this.port = port;
    this.host = host;
  }

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      this.server = net.createServer(socket => {
        const key = `${socket.remoteAddress}:${socket.remotePort}`;
        this.connections.set(key, socket);

        let buffer = Buffer.alloc(0);
        socket.on('data', chunk => {
          buffer = Buffer.concat([buffer, chunk]);
          // Frame protocol: 4-byte length prefix
          while (buffer.length >= 4) {
            const msgLen = buffer.readUInt32BE(0);
            if (buffer.length < 4 + msgLen) break;
            const payload = buffer.slice(4, 4 + msgLen);
            buffer = buffer.slice(4 + msgLen);
            this.emit('data', payload, { type: 'tcp', host: socket.remoteAddress ?? '', port: socket.remotePort ?? 0 });
          }
        });

        socket.on('close', () => this.connections.delete(key));
        socket.on('error', () => {
          this.connections.delete(key);
          socket.destroy();
        });
      });

      this.server.listen(this.port, this.host, () => resolve());
      this.server.once('error', reject);
    });
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    for (const socket of this.connections.values()) socket.destroy();
    this.connections.clear();
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      if (this.server) this.server.close(() => resolve());
      else resolve();
    });
  }

  async send(target: TransportAddress, data: Buffer): Promise<TransportSendResult> {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const start = Date.now();
      const socket = new net.Socket();
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
        socket.destroy();
        resolve({ success: false, error: 'TCP connect timeout' });
      }, 5_000);

      socket.connect(target.port, target.host, () => {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        const frame = Buffer.allocUnsafe(4 + data.length);
        frame.writeUInt32BE(data.length, 0);
        data.copy(frame, 4);
        socket.write(frame, err => {
          socket.end();
          if (err) resolve({ success: false, error: err.message });
          else resolve({ success: true, latencyMs: Date.now() - start });
        });
      });

      socket.once('error', err => {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        resolve({ success: false, error: err.message });
      });
    });
  }

  localAddresses(): TransportAddress[] {
    const interfaces = os.networkInterfaces();
    const addrs: TransportAddress[] = [];
    for (const iface of Object.values(interfaces)) {
      if (!iface) continue;
      for (const info of iface) {
        if (info.internal) continue;
        addrs.push({ type: 'tcp', host: info.address, port: this.port });
      }
    }
    return addrs;
  }
}

export class UDPTransport extends EventEmitter implements ITransport {
  readonly type: TransportType = 'udp';
  private socket: dgram.Socket | null = null;
  private readonly port: number;

  constructor(port: number) {
    super();
    this.port = port;
  }

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      this.socket = dgram.createSocket('udp4');
      this.socket.on('message', (msg, rinfo) => {
        this.emit('data', msg, { type: 'udp', host: rinfo.address, port: rinfo.port });
      });
      this.socket.bind(this.port, () => resolve());
      this.socket.once('error', reject);
    });
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      if (this.socket) this.socket.close(() => resolve());
      else resolve();
    });
  }

  async send(target: TransportAddress, data: Buffer): Promise<TransportSendResult> {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      if (!this.socket) return resolve({ success: false, error: 'UDP socket not started' });
      const start = Date.now();
      this.socket.send(data, target.port, target.host, err => {
        if (err) resolve({ success: false, error: err.message });
        else resolve({ success: true, latencyMs: Date.now() - start });
      });
    });
  }

  localAddresses(): TransportAddress[] {
    const interfaces = os.networkInterfaces();
    const addrs: TransportAddress[] = [];
    for (const iface of Object.values(interfaces)) {
      if (!iface) continue;
      for (const info of iface) {
        if (info.internal) continue;
        addrs.push({ type: 'udp', host: info.address, port: this.port });
      }
    }
    return addrs;
  }
}

export class TransportManager extends EventEmitter {
  private transports: Map<TransportType, ITransport> = new Map();
  private readonly preferenceOrder: TransportType[] = ['tcp', 'udp', 'lora', 'satellite'];

  register(transport: ITransport): void {
    this.transports.set(transport.type, transport);
    transport.on('data', (data: Buffer, source: TransportAddress) => {
      this.emit('data', data, source, transport.type);
    });
  }

  async startAll(): Promise<void> {
    await Promise.all(Array.from(this.transports.values()).map(t => t.start()));
  }

  async stopAll(): Promise<void> {
    await Promise.all(Array.from(this.transports.values()).map(t => t.stop()));
  }

  async send(target: TransportAddress, data: Buffer): Promise<TransportSendResult> {
    const transport = this.transports.get(target.type);
    if (!transport) return { success: false, error: `No transport registered for ${target.type}` };
    if (data.length > MAX_MESSAGE_SIZE_BYTES) {
      return { success: false, error: `Message size ${data.length} exceeds maximum ${MAX_MESSAGE_SIZE_BYTES}` };
    }
    return transport.send(target, data);
  }

  async sendBestEffort(peer: PeerRecord, data: Buffer): Promise<TransportSendResult> {
    // Try transports in preference order, return first success
    for (const type of this.preferenceOrder) {
      const addr = peer.addresses.find(a => a.type === type);
      if (!addr) continue;
      const result = await this.send(addr, data);
      if (result.success) return result;
    }
    return { success: false, error: 'All transports failed' };
  }

  allLocalAddresses(): TransportAddress[] {
    return Array.from(this.transports.values()).flatMap(t => t.localAddresses());
  }

  availableTypes(): TransportType[] {
    return Array.from(this.transports.keys());
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// GOSSIP PROTOCOL
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface GossipMessage {
  id: string;
  payload: Buffer;
  originId: string;
  topic: string;
  timestamp: number;
  ttl: number;
}

export class GossipProtocol extends EventEmitter {
  private seen: Map<string, number> = new Map(); // messageId -> timestamp
  private readonly fanout: number;

  constructor(fanout: number = GOSSIP_FANOUT) {
    super();
    this.fanout = fanout;
  }

  shouldForward(msgId: string): boolean {
    const now = Date.now();
    // Sweep expired entries
    for (const [id, ts] of this.seen) {
      if (now - ts > MESSAGE_DEDUP_WINDOW_MS) this.seen.delete(id);
    }
    if (this.seen.has(msgId)) return false;
    this.seen.set(msgId, now);
    return true;
  }

  selectForwardPeers(peers: PeerRecord[], exclude?: string): PeerRecord[] {
    const candidates = peers.filter(p => p.nodeId.toString('hex') !== exclude);
    // Fisher-Yates shuffle then take fanout
    for (let i = candidates.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      [candidates[i], candidates[j]] = [candidates[j], candidates[i]];
    }
    return candidates.slice(0, this.fanout);
  }

  // Anti-entropy: periodically sync state digests with a random peer
  computeStateDigest(keys: string[]): Buffer {
    const sorted = [...keys].sort();
    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(sorted.join('\n')).digest();
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// MESSAGE DEDUPLICATION
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class MessageDeduplicator {
  private seen: Map<string, number> = new Map(); // msgId -> expiry
  private readonly windowMs: number;

  constructor(windowMs: number = MESSAGE_DEDUP_WINDOW_MS) {
    this.windowMs = windowMs;
  }

  isDuplicate(msgId: string): boolean {
    const now = Date.now();
    this.sweep(now);
    if (this.seen.has(msgId)) return true;
    this.seen.set(msgId, now + this.windowMs);
    return false;
  }

  private sweep(now: number): void {
    for (const [id, expiry] of this.seen) {
      if (now > expiry) this.seen.delete(id);
    }
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// STORE AND FORWARD — for offline peers
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface StoredMessage {
  envelope: MessageEnvelope;
  storedAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;
  deliveryAttempts: number;
}

export class StoreAndForwardEngine {
  private store: Map<string, StoredMessage[]> = new Map(); // recipientId -> messages
  private readonly ttlHours: number;
  private readonly maxMessagesPerPeer: number;

  constructor(ttlHours: number = STORE_FORWARD_TTL_HOURS, maxMessagesPerPeer: number = 100) {
    this.ttlHours = ttlHours;
    this.maxMessagesPerPeer = maxMessagesPerPeer;
  }

  enqueue(envelope: MessageEnvelope): void {
    const recipientId = envelope.message.recipientId;
    if (!recipientId) return; // Don't store broadcasts

    if (!this.store.has(recipientId)) {
      this.store.set(recipientId, []);
    }

    const queue = this.store.get(recipientId)!;

    if (queue.length >= this.maxMessagesPerPeer) {
      queue.shift(); // Evict oldest
    }

    queue.push({
      envelope,
      storedAt: Date.now(),
      expiresAt: Date.now() + this.ttlHours * 3_600_000,
      deliveryAttempts: 0,
    });
  }

  drain(recipientId: string): StoredMessage[] {
    const now = Date.now();
    const messages = this.store.get(recipientId) ?? [];
    const valid = messages.filter(m => m.expiresAt > now);
    this.store.delete(recipientId);
    return valid;
  }

  pending(recipientId: string): number {
    return (this.store.get(recipientId) ?? []).length;
  }

  sweep(): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    let removed = 0;
    for (const [id, messages] of this.store) {
      const valid = messages.filter(m => m.expiresAt > now);
      removed += messages.length - valid.length;
      if (valid.length === 0) this.store.delete(id);
      else this.store.set(id, valid);
    }
    return removed;
  }

  totalStored(): number {
    return Array.from(this.store.values()).reduce((sum, q) => sum + q.length, 0);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CAPABILITY REGISTRY
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface CapabilityAdvertisement {
  nodeId: string;
  capabilities: NodeCapability[];
  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
  timestamp: number;
  ttl: number;               // Seconds until advertisement expires
  signature: string;
}

export class CapabilityRegistry {
  private registry: Map<string, CapabilityAdvertisement> = new Map();

  publish(adv: CapabilityAdvertisement): void {
    this.registry.set(adv.nodeId, adv);
  }

  query(capability: NodeCapability): CapabilityAdvertisement[] {
    const now = Date.now();
    return Array.from(this.registry.values()).filter(adv => {
      const expired = now > adv.timestamp + adv.ttl * 1000;
      return !expired && adv.capabilities.includes(capability);
    });
  }

  get(nodeId: string): CapabilityAdvertisement | undefined {
    return this.registry.get(nodeId);
  }

  sweep(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [id, adv] of this.registry) {
      if (now > adv.timestamp + adv.ttl * 1000) this.registry.delete(id);
    }
  }

  size(): number {
    return this.registry.size;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// NAT TRAVERSAL
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface STUNResult {
  publicHost: string;
  publicPort: number;
  natType: 'open' | 'full_cone' | 'restricted_cone' | 'port_restricted' | 'symmetric' | 'unknown';
}

export class NATTraversal {
  private readonly stunServers: string[];
  private cachedResult: STUNResult | null = null;
  private cacheExpiry: number = 0;

  constructor(stunServers: string[] = [
    'stun.l.google.com:19302',
    'stun1.l.google.com:19302',
    'stun.cloudflare.com:3478',
  ]) {
    this.stunServers = stunServers;
  }

  async discoverPublicAddress(localPort: number): Promise<STUNResult | null> {
    if (this.cachedResult && Date.now() < this.cacheExpiry) {
      return this.cachedResult;
    }

    // Attempt STUN with each server
    for (const serverAddr of this.stunServers) {
      const [host, portStr] = serverAddr.split(':');
      const port = parseInt(portStr ?? '3478', 10);
      const result = await this.stunQuery(host, port, localPort);
      if (result) {
        this.cachedResult = result;
        this.cacheExpiry = Date.now() + 300_000; // Cache 5 minutes
        return result;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  private async stunQuery(serverHost: string, serverPort: number, localPort: number): Promise<STUNResult | null> {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const socket = dgram.createSocket('udp4');
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
        socket.close();
        resolve(null);
      }, 3_000);

      // STUN Binding Request
      const txId = crypto.randomBytes(12);
      const request = Buffer.allocUnsafe(20);
      request.writeUInt16BE(0x0001, 0);  // Binding Request
      request.writeUInt16BE(0, 2);       // Message Length (no attributes)
      request.writeUInt32BE(0x2112A442, 4); // Magic Cookie
      txId.copy(request, 8);

      socket.bind(localPort, () => {
        socket.send(request, serverPort, serverHost, err => {
          if (err) {
            clearTimeout(timeout);
            socket.close();
            resolve(null);
          }
        });
      });

      socket.on('message', (msg) => {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        try {
          // Parse STUN response - look for XOR-MAPPED-ADDRESS attribute
          let offset = 20; // Skip header
          let publicHost = '';
          let publicPort = 0;

          while (offset + 4 <= msg.length) {
            const attrType = msg.readUInt16BE(offset);
            const attrLen = msg.readUInt16BE(offset + 2);
            offset += 4;

            if (attrType === 0x0020 && attrLen >= 8) { // XOR-MAPPED-ADDRESS (IPv4)
              const xPort = msg.readUInt16BE(offset + 2) ^ 0x2112;
              const xAddr = msg.readUInt32BE(offset + 4) ^ 0x2112A442;
              publicPort = xPort;
              publicHost = [
                (xAddr >>> 24) & 0xFF,
                (xAddr >>> 16) & 0xFF,
                (xAddr >>> 8) & 0xFF,
                xAddr & 0xFF,
              ].join('.');
            }
            offset += attrLen;
          }

          socket.close();
          if (publicHost) {
            resolve({ publicHost, publicPort, natType: 'unknown' });
          } else {
            resolve(null);
          }
        } catch {
          socket.close();
          resolve(null);
        }
      });

      socket.on('error', () => {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        resolve(null);
      });
    });
  }

  // TCP hole-punching coordination
  async holePunch(
    localPort: number,
    remoteHost: string,
    remotePort: number,
    coordinationDelay: number = 500,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    // Both sides must punch simultaneously after coordination
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, coordinationDelay));

    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const socket = new net.Socket();
      const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
        socket.destroy();
        resolve(false);
      }, 5_000);

      socket.connect({ port: remotePort, host: remoteHost, localPort }, () => {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        socket.end();
        resolve(true);
      });
      socket.once('error', () => {
        clearTimeout(timeout);
        resolve(false);
      });
    });
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// ROUTE TABLE — direct + multi-hop
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RouteEntry {
  destination: string;    // hex nodeId
  nextHop: string;        // hex nodeId
  hopCount: number;
  latencyMs: number;
  transport: TransportType;
  expiresAt: number;
}

export class RouteTable {
  private routes: Map<string, RouteEntry> = new Map();

  set(destination: string, entry: RouteEntry): void {
    this.routes.set(destination, entry);
  }

  get(destination: string): RouteEntry | undefined {
    const route = this.routes.get(destination);
    if (route && Date.now() > route.expiresAt) {
      this.routes.delete(destination);
      return undefined;
    }
    return route;
  }

  remove(destination: string): void {
    this.routes.delete(destination);
  }

  sweep(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [dest, route] of this.routes) {
      if (now > route.expiresAt) this.routes.delete(dest);
    }
  }

  allRoutes(): RouteEntry[] {
    return Array.from(this.routes.values());
  }

  size(): number {
    return this.routes.size;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// NODE STATISTICS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface NodeStats {
  messagesReceived: number;
  messagesSent: number;
  messagesForwarded: number;
  messagesDuplicate: number;
  messagesDroppedTTL: number;
  bytesReceived: number;
  bytesSent: number;
  peersDiscovered: number;
  peersLost: number;
  routeLookups: number;
  storeForwardEnqueued: number;
  storeForwardDelivered: number;
  gossipBroadcasts: number;
  uptimeMs: number;
  startedAt: number;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// MESH NODE — MAIN CLASS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface MeshNodeConfig {
  identity: NodeIdentity;
  tcpPort?: number;
  udpPort?: number;
  bootstrapPeers?: TransportAddress[];
  capabilities?: NodeCapability[];
  maxPeers?: number;
  enableNATTraversal?: boolean;
  gossipFanout?: number;
  dataDir?: string;
}

export interface NodeStatus {
  nodeId: string;
  did: string;
  addresses: TransportAddress[];
  peerCount: number;
  kademliaSize: number;
  storeForwardQueue: number;
  capabilityRegistry: number;
  routes: number;
  stats: NodeStats;
  uptime: number;
}

export class MeshNode extends EventEmitter {
  public readonly identity: NodeIdentity;
  private readonly config: Required<MeshNodeConfig>;

  // Core subsystems
  private readonly peerManager: PeerRecordManager;
  private readonly kademlia: KademliaTable;
  private readonly transportManager: TransportManager;
  private readonly gossip: GossipProtocol;
  private readonly deduplicator: MessageDeduplicator;
  private readonly storeForward: StoreAndForwardEngine;
  private readonly capabilityRegistry: CapabilityRegistry;
  private readonly routeTable: RouteTable;
