  private readonly natTraversal: NATTraversal;

  // Timers
  private heartbeatTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private refreshTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private sweepTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private gossipTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  // State
  private running: boolean = false;
  private stats: NodeStats;

  // Pending RPC callbacks
  private pendingRPCs: Map<string, {
    resolve: (msg: MeshMessage) => void;
    reject: (err: Error) => void;
    timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;
  }> = new Map();

  constructor(config: MeshNodeConfig) {
    super();
    this.identity = config.identity;
    this.config = {
      tcpPort: config.tcpPort ?? 7770,
      udpPort: config.udpPort ?? 7771,
      bootstrapPeers: config.bootstrapPeers ?? [],
      capabilities: config.capabilities ?? [],
      maxPeers: config.maxPeers ?? 1000,
      enableNATTraversal: config.enableNATTraversal ?? true,
      gossipFanout: config.gossipFanout ?? GOSSIP_FANOUT,
      dataDir: config.dataDir ?? './ube-node-data',
      identity: this.identity,
    };

    // Apply capabilities to identity
    for (const cap of this.config.capabilities) {
      this.identity.addCapability(cap);
    }

    this.peerManager = new PeerRecordManager(this.config.maxPeers);
    this.kademlia = new KademliaTable(this.identity.nodeId);
    this.transportManager = new TransportManager();
    this.gossip = new GossipProtocol(this.config.gossipFanout);
    this.deduplicator = new MessageDeduplicator();
    this.storeForward = new StoreAndForwardEngine();
    this.capabilityRegistry = new CapabilityRegistry();
    this.routeTable = new RouteTable();
    this.natTraversal = new NATTraversal();

    this.stats = {
      messagesReceived: 0,
      messagesSent: 0,
      messagesForwarded: 0,
      messagesDuplicate: 0,
      messagesDroppedTTL: 0,
      bytesReceived: 0,
      bytesSent: 0,
      peersDiscovered: 0,
      peersLost: 0,
      routeLookups: 0,
      storeForwardEnqueued: 0,
      storeForwardDelivered: 0,
      gossipBroadcasts: 0,
      uptimeMs: 0,
      startedAt: 0,
    };

    this.setupTransports();
  }

  // ── LIFECYCLE ──────────────────────────────

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.running) throw new Error('MeshNode already running');
    this.running = true;
    this.stats.startedAt = Date.now();

    await this.transportManager.startAll();

    if (this.config.enableNATTraversal) {
      const stun = await this.natTraversal.discoverPublicAddress(this.config.udpPort);
      if (stun) {
        this.emit('nat:discovered', stun);
      }
    }

    this.transportManager.on('data', (data: Buffer, source: TransportAddress, transport: TransportType) => {
      this.handleRawData(data, source, transport);
    });

    // Start background tasks
    this.heartbeatTimer = setInterval(() => this.heartbeat(), HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS);
    this.refreshTimer = setInterval(() => this.refreshKademlia(), 3_600_000);
    this.sweepTimer = setInterval(() => this.sweep(), 60_000);
    this.gossipTimer = setInterval(() => this.gossipTick(), 1000 / GOSSIP_ROUNDS_PER_SECOND);

    // Bootstrap
    await this.bootstrap();

    // Publish own capabilities
    this.publishCapabilities();

    this.emit('started', { nodeId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex') });
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.running) return;
    this.running = false;

    // Send BYE to all peers
    const bye = createMessage('NODE_BYE', this.identity, Buffer.alloc(0), undefined, 1);
    await this.broadcast(bye);

    if (this.heartbeatTimer) clearInterval(this.heartbeatTimer);
    if (this.refreshTimer) clearInterval(this.refreshTimer);
    if (this.sweepTimer) clearInterval(this.sweepTimer);
    if (this.gossipTimer) clearInterval(this.gossipTimer);

    for (const rpc of this.pendingRPCs.values()) {
      clearTimeout(rpc.timer);
      rpc.reject(new Error('Node stopped'));
    }
    this.pendingRPCs.clear();

    await this.transportManager.stopAll();
    this.emit('stopped');
  }

  // ── BOOTSTRAP ─────────────────────────────

  private async bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.config.bootstrapPeers.length === 0) return;

    const pingPromises = this.config.bootstrapPeers.map(addr => this.pingAddress(addr));
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(pingPromises);
    const contacted = results.filter(r => r.status === 'fulfilled').length;
    this.emit('bootstrap:complete', { contacted, total: this.config.bootstrapPeers.length });

    // Self-lookup to populate routing table
    if (contacted > 0) {
      await this.nodeLookup(this.identity.nodeId);
    }
  }

  private async pingAddress(addr: TransportAddress): Promise<void> {
    const ping = createMessage('PING', this.identity, Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
      addresses: this.transportManager.allLocalAddresses(),
      capabilities: this.identity.getCapabilities(),
    })), undefined, 1);

    const data = this.serializeMessage(ping);
    const result = await this.transportManager.send(addr, data);
    if (!result.success) throw new Error(`Ping failed: ${result.error}`);
  }

  // ── KADEMLIA NODE LOOKUP ───────────────────

  async nodeLookup(targetId: NodeId): Promise<PeerRecord[]> {
    this.stats.routeLookups++;

    const visited = new Set<string>();
    const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(targetId, KADEMLIA_K);
    const queue: PeerRecord[] = [...closest];

    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const batch = queue.splice(0, KADEMLIA_ALPHA);
      const unvisited = batch.filter(p => !visited.has(p.nodeId.toString('hex')));
      if (unvisited.length === 0) break;

      const queries = unvisited.map(async peer => {
        visited.add(peer.nodeId.toString('hex'));
        const resp = await this.sendRPC(peer, 'FIND_NODE',
          Buffer.from(targetId.toString('hex')));
        if (!resp) return [];

        try {
          const found: PeerRecord[] = JSON.parse(resp.payload.toString('utf8'));
          return found;
        } catch {
          return [];
        }
      });

      const responses = await Promise.allSettled(queries);
      for (const resp of responses) {
        if (resp.status === 'fulfilled') {
          for (const peer of resp.value) {
            if (!visited.has(peer.nodeId.toString('hex'))) {
              this.kademlia.insert(peer);
              queue.push(peer);
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return this.kademlia.findClosest(targetId, KADEMLIA_K);
  }

  // ── MESSAGE SENDING ────────────────────────

  async send(recipientId: string, type: MessageType, payload: Buffer): Promise<boolean> {
    const msg = createMessage(type, this.identity, payload, recipientId);

    // Check if we have a direct route
    const route = this.routeTable.get(recipientId);
    if (route) {
      const peer = this.peerManager.get(Buffer.from(route.nextHop, 'hex'));
      if (peer) {
        const result = await this.transportManager.sendBestEffort(peer, this.serializeMessage(msg));
        if (result.success) {
          this.stats.messagesSent++;
          this.stats.bytesSent += payload.length;
          return true;
        }
      }
    }

    // Find closest peers and route through them
    const targetId = Buffer.from(recipientId, 'hex');
    const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(targetId, KADEMLIA_ALPHA);

    for (const peer of closest) {
      if (peer.nodeId.toString('hex') === recipientId) {
        // Direct delivery
        const result = await this.transportManager.sendBestEffort(peer, this.serializeMessage(msg));
        if (result.success) {
          this.stats.messagesSent++;
          return true;
        }
      }
    }

    // Target not reachable — store for delivery when they come online
    this.storeForward.enqueue({
      message: msg,
      receivedAt: Date.now(),
      receivedFrom: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      transport: 'tcp',
    });
    this.stats.storeForwardEnqueued++;
    return false;
  }

  async broadcast(msg: MeshMessage): Promise<number> {
    const peers = this.peerManager.reachable();
    const targets = this.gossip.selectForwardPeers(peers);
    let delivered = 0;

    const sends = targets.map(async peer => {
      const result = await this.transportManager.sendBestEffort(peer, this.serializeMessage(msg));
      if (result.success) delivered++;
    });

    await Promise.allSettled(sends);
    this.stats.gossipBroadcasts++;
    return delivered;
  }

  // ── RPC ────────────────────────────────────

  private async sendRPC(
    peer: PeerRecord,
    type: MessageType,
    payload: Buffer,
    timeoutMs: number = 5_000,
  ): Promise<MeshMessage | null> {
    const msg = createMessage(type, this.identity, payload, peer.nodeId.toString('hex'), 1);
    const data = this.serializeMessage(msg);
    const result = await this.transportManager.sendBestEffort(peer, data);
    if (!result.success) return null;

    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.pendingRPCs.delete(msg.id);
        resolve(null);
      }, timeoutMs);

      this.pendingRPCs.set(msg.id, {
        resolve: (resp) => {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          this.pendingRPCs.delete(msg.id);
          resolve(resp);
        },
        reject: () => {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          this.pendingRPCs.delete(msg.id);
          resolve(null);
        },
        timer,
      });
    });
  }

  // ── INCOMING MESSAGE HANDLING ──────────────

  private handleRawData(data: Buffer, source: TransportAddress, transport: TransportType): void {
    this.stats.bytesReceived += data.length;
    try {
      const msg = this.deserializeMessage(data);
      if (!msg) return;
      this.handleMessage(msg, source, transport);
    } catch (err) {
      this.emit('error', { type: 'parse_error', source, err });
    }
  }

  private handleMessage(msg: MeshMessage, source: TransportAddress, transport: TransportType): void {
    // Network magic check
    if (msg.version !== PROTOCOL_VERSION) return;

    // Deduplication
    if (this.deduplicator.isDuplicate(msg.id)) {
      this.stats.messagesDuplicate++;
      return;
    }

    this.stats.messagesReceived++;

    // Signature verification
    if (!verifyMessage(msg)) {
      this.emit('security:invalid_signature', { msgId: msg.id, senderId: msg.senderId });
      return;
    }

    const envelope: MessageEnvelope = {
      message: msg,
      receivedAt: Date.now(),
      receivedFrom: msg.senderId,
      transport,
    };

    // Update peer record
    this.updatePeerFromMessage(msg, source, transport);

    // Handle RPC responses
    if (msg.recipientId === this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex')) {
      const rpcId = this.extractCorrelationId(msg);
      if (rpcId && this.pendingRPCs.has(rpcId)) {
        this.pendingRPCs.get(rpcId)!.resolve(msg);
        return;
      }
    }

    // Dispatch by type
    switch (msg.type) {
      case 'PING': this.handlePing(msg, source, transport); break;
      case 'PONG': this.handlePong(msg); break;
      case 'FIND_NODE': this.handleFindNode(msg, source, transport); break;
      case 'FOUND_NODE': break; // Handled by RPC callback
      case 'GOSSIP': this.handleGossip(msg, envelope); break;
      case 'CAPABILITY_ADV': this.handleCapabilityAdv(msg); break;
      case 'CAPABILITY_QUERY': this.handleCapabilityQuery(msg, source, transport); break;
      case 'DATA': this.handleData(msg, envelope); break;
      case 'NODE_BYE': this.handleNodeBye(msg); break;
      default:
        // Forward to application layer
        this.emit('message', envelope);
    }

    // Forward if TTL > 1 and not destined for us
    if (msg.ttl > 1 && msg.recipientId !== this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex')) {
      this.forwardMessage(msg, msg.senderId);
    }
  }

  private handlePing(msg: MeshMessage, source: TransportAddress, transport: TransportType): void {
    const pong = createMessage('PONG', this.identity, Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
      correlationId: msg.id,
      addresses: this.transportManager.allLocalAddresses(),
      capabilities: this.identity.getCapabilities(),
    })), msg.senderId, 1);
    this.transportManager.send(source, this.serializeMessage(pong));

    // Deliver any stored messages for this peer
    const stored = this.storeForward.drain(msg.senderId);
    if (stored.length > 0) {
      this.emit('store_forward:delivering', { recipientId: msg.senderId, count: stored.length });
      this.stats.storeForwardDelivered += stored.length;
      for (const sm of stored) {
        this.transportManager.send(source, this.serializeMessage(sm.envelope.message));
      }
    }
  }

  private handlePong(msg: MeshMessage): void {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(msg.payload.toString('utf8'));
      const corrId = data.correlationId;
      if (corrId && this.pendingRPCs.has(corrId)) {
        this.pendingRPCs.get(corrId)!.resolve(msg);
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  private handleFindNode(msg: MeshMessage, source: TransportAddress, transport: TransportType): void {
    try {
      const targetHex = msg.payload.toString('utf8');
      const targetId = Buffer.from(targetHex, 'hex');
      const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(targetId, KADEMLIA_K);
      const resp = createMessage('FOUND_NODE', this.identity,
        Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(closest)), msg.senderId, 1);
      // Embed correlation
      (resp as any)._correlationId = msg.id;
      this.transportManager.send(source, this.serializeMessage(resp));
    } catch {}
  }

  private handleGossip(msg: MeshMessage, envelope: MessageEnvelope): void {
    if (!this.gossip.shouldForward(msg.id)) return;

    // Deliver to application
    this.emit('gossip', envelope);

    // Forward to fanout peers (exclude sender)
    if (msg.ttl > 1) {
      const forwardMsg: MeshMessage = { ...msg, hopCount: msg.hopCount + 1, ttl: msg.ttl - 1 };
      const targets = this.gossip.selectForwardPeers(this.peerManager.reachable(), msg.senderId);
      for (const peer of targets) {
        this.transportManager.sendBestEffort(peer, this.serializeMessage(forwardMsg));
        this.stats.messagesForwarded++;
      }
    }
  }

  private handleCapabilityAdv(msg: MeshMessage): void {
    try {
      const adv: CapabilityAdvertisement = JSON.parse(msg.payload.toString('utf8'));
      this.capabilityRegistry.publish(adv);
      this.emit('capability:discovered', adv);
    } catch {}
  }

  private handleCapabilityQuery(msg: MeshMessage, source: TransportAddress, transport: TransportType): void {
    try {
      const { capability } = JSON.parse(msg.payload.toString('utf8'));
      const results = this.capabilityRegistry.query(capability as NodeCapability);
      const resp = createMessage('CAPABILITY_RESP', this.identity,
        Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(results)), msg.senderId, 1);
      this.transportManager.send(source, this.serializeMessage(resp));
    } catch {}
  }

  private handleData(msg: MeshMessage, envelope: MessageEnvelope): void {
    if (msg.recipientId === this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex')) {
      this.emit('data', envelope);
    } else if (msg.ttl > 1) {
      this.forwardMessage(msg, msg.senderId);
    } else {
      this.stats.messagesDroppedTTL++;
    }
  }

  private handleNodeBye(msg: MeshMessage): void {
    const nodeId = Buffer.from(msg.senderId, 'hex');
    this.kademlia.remove(nodeId);
    this.peerManager.remove(nodeId);
    this.routeTable.remove(msg.senderId);
    this.stats.peersLost++;
    this.emit('peer:left', { nodeId: msg.senderId });
  }

  private forwardMessage(msg: MeshMessage, excludeId: string): void {
    if (msg.ttl <= 1) {
      this.stats.messagesDroppedTTL++;
      return;
    }

    const forward: MeshMessage = { ...msg, ttl: msg.ttl - 1, hopCount: msg.hopCount + 1 };

    if (msg.recipientId) {
      // Unicast: route towards destination
      const targetId = Buffer.from(msg.recipientId, 'hex');
      const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(targetId, 1);
      if (closest.length > 0 && closest[0].nodeId.toString('hex') !== excludeId) {
        this.transportManager.sendBestEffort(closest[0], this.serializeMessage(forward));
        this.stats.messagesForwarded++;
      }
    } else {
      // Broadcast: gossip forward
      const targets = this.gossip.selectForwardPeers(this.peerManager.reachable(), excludeId);
      for (const peer of targets) {
        this.transportManager.sendBestEffort(peer, this.serializeMessage(forward));
        this.stats.messagesForwarded++;
      }
    }
  }

  // ── BACKGROUND TASKS ───────────────────────

  private async heartbeat(): Promise<void> {
    const peers = this.peerManager.reachable();
    const now = Date.now();

    const checks = peers.map(async peer => {
      if (now - peer.lastSeen < HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS / 2) return;

      const ping = createMessage('PING', this.identity, Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
        addresses: this.transportManager.allLocalAddresses(),
        capabilities: this.identity.getCapabilities(),
      })), peer.nodeId.toString('hex'), 1);

      const result = await this.transportManager.sendBestEffort(peer, this.serializeMessage(ping));
      if (!result.success) {
        peer.failureCount++;
        if (peer.failureCount >= 3) {
          peer.reachable = false;
          this.emit('peer:unreachable', { nodeId: peer.nodeId.toString('hex') });
        }
      }
    });

    await Promise.allSettled(checks);
    this.stats.uptimeMs = Date.now() - this.stats.startedAt;
  }

  private async refreshKademlia(): Promise<void> {
    const staleBuckets = this.kademlia.staleBuckets();
    for (const bucketIdx of staleBuckets) {
      const refreshId = this.kademlia.refreshTarget(bucketIdx);
      await this.nodeLookup(refreshId);
    }
  }

  private sweep(): void {
    this.routeTable.sweep();
    this.capabilityRegistry.sweep();
    const removed = this.storeForward.sweep();
    if (removed > 0) {
      this.emit('store_forward:swept', { removed });
    }
  }

  private gossipTick(): void {
    // Anti-entropy: periodically gossip our state digest
    const peers = this.peerManager.allPeers().map(p => p.nodeId.toString('hex'));
    const digest = this.gossip.computeStateDigest(peers);
    const adv = createMessage('GOSSIP', this.identity, digest);
    this.broadcast(adv);
  }

  private publishCapabilities(): void {
    const adv: CapabilityAdvertisement = {
      nodeId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      capabilities: this.identity.getCapabilities(),
      metadata: {
        addresses: this.transportManager.allLocalAddresses(),
        version: PROTOCOL_VERSION,
      },
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      ttl: 3600,
      signature: this.identity.sign(
        Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ nodeId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'), capabilities: this.identity.getCapabilities() }))
      ).toString('hex'),
    };
    this.capabilityRegistry.publish(adv);
    const msg = createMessage('CAPABILITY_ADV', this.identity, Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(adv)));
    this.broadcast(msg);
  }

  // ── PEER MANAGEMENT ────────────────────────

  private updatePeerFromMessage(msg: MeshMessage, source: TransportAddress, transport: TransportType): void {
    try {
      const nodeId = Buffer.from(msg.senderId, 'hex');
      const existing = this.peerManager.get(nodeId);

      let addresses: TransportAddress[] = [source];
      let capabilities: NodeCapability[] = [];

      if (msg.type === 'PING' || msg.type === 'PONG') {
        const data = JSON.parse(msg.payload.toString('utf8'));
        if (Array.isArray(data.addresses)) addresses = data.addresses;
        if (Array.isArray(data.capabilities)) capabilities = data.capabilities;
      }

      const record: PeerRecord = {
        nodeId,
        publicKey: Buffer.from(msg.senderPublicKey, 'hex'),
        did: `did:ube:${msg.senderId}`,
        addresses: existing ? [...existing.addresses, ...addresses] : addresses,
        capabilities,
        lastSeen: Date.now(),
        latencyMs: 0,
        rttSamples: [],
        failureCount: 0,
        verified: true,
        reachable: true,
      };

      const wasNew = !this.peerManager.has(nodeId);
      this.peerManager.upsert(record);
      this.kademlia.insert(record);

      if (wasNew) {
        this.stats.peersDiscovered++;
        this.emit('peer:discovered', { nodeId: msg.senderId, capabilities, transport });
      }

      // Update route table
      this.routeTable.set(msg.senderId, {
        destination: msg.senderId,
        nextHop: msg.senderId,
        hopCount: 1,
        latencyMs: 0,
        transport,
        expiresAt: Date.now() + ROUTE_CACHE_TTL_MS,
      });
    } catch {}
  }

  // ── SERIALIZATION ──────────────────────────

  private serializeMessage(msg: MeshMessage): Buffer {
    const json = JSON.stringify({
      ...msg,
      payload: msg.payload.toString('base64'),
    });
    return Buffer.from(json, 'utf8');
  }

  private deserializeMessage(data: Buffer): MeshMessage | null {
    try {
      const obj = JSON.parse(data.toString('utf8'));
      return {
        ...obj,
        payload: Buffer.from(obj.payload, 'base64'),
      } as MeshMessage;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private extractCorrelationId(msg: MeshMessage): string | null {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(msg.payload.toString('utf8'));
      return data.correlationId ?? null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // ── SETUP ──────────────────────────────────

  private setupTransports(): void {
    this.transportManager.register(new TCPTransport(this.config.tcpPort));
    this.transportManager.register(new UDPTransport(this.config.udpPort));
  }

  // ── PUBLIC API ─────────────────────────────

  async queryCapability(capability: NodeCapability): Promise<CapabilityAdvertisement[]> {
    // First check local registry
    const local = this.capabilityRegistry.query(capability);
    if (local.length > 0) return local;

    // Broadcast query across mesh
    const queryMsg = createMessage('CAPABILITY_QUERY', this.identity,
      Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ capability })));
    await this.broadcast(queryMsg);

    // Wait briefly for responses
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 2_000));
    return this.capabilityRegistry.query(capability);
  }

  async findGateway(): Promise<PeerRecord | null> {
    const gateways = this.peerManager.byCapability('GATEWAY');
    if (gateways.length > 0) {
      // Return gateway with lowest latency
      return gateways.sort((a, b) => a.latencyMs - b.latencyMs)[0];
    }
    return null;
  }

  async findRelayNodes(count: number = 3): Promise<PeerRecord[]> {
    return this.peerManager.byCapability('RELAY').slice(0, count);
  }

  status(): NodeStatus {
    return {
      nodeId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      did: this.identity.did,
      addresses: this.transportManager.allLocalAddresses(),
      peerCount: this.peerManager.reachable().length,
      kademliaSize: this.kademlia.size(),
      storeForwardQueue: this.storeForward.totalStored(),
      capabilityRegistry: this.capabilityRegistry.size(),
      routes: this.routeTable.size(),
      stats: { ...this.stats, uptimeMs: this.running ? Date.now() - this.stats.startedAt : this.stats.uptimeMs },
      uptime: Date.now() - this.stats.startedAt,
    };
  }

  isRunning(): boolean {
    return this.running;
  }

  getPeers(): PeerRecord[] {
    return this.peerManager.reachable();
  }

  getIdentity(): NodeIdentityDoc {
    return this.identity.exportPublicDoc();
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// MESH NODE FACTORY
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class MeshNodeFactory {
  static createGatewayNode(tcpPort: number = 7770, udpPort: number = 7771): MeshNode {
    const identity = NodeIdentity.generate(['GATEWAY', 'RELAY', 'CONSENSUS', 'STORAGE']);
    return new MeshNode({ identity, tcpPort, udpPort });
  }

  static createRelayNode(tcpPort: number = 7772, udpPort: number = 7773): MeshNode {
    const identity = NodeIdentity.generate(['RELAY', 'STORAGE']);
    return new MeshNode({ identity, tcpPort, udpPort });
  }

  static createComputeNode(tcpPort: number = 7774, udpPort: number = 7775): MeshNode {
    const identity = NodeIdentity.generate(['COMPUTE', 'AI']);
    return new MeshNode({ identity, tcpPort, udpPort });
  }

  static createEdgeNode(tcpPort: number = 7776, udpPort: number = 7777, bootstraps: TransportAddress[] = []): MeshNode {
    const identity = NodeIdentity.generate([]);
    return new MeshNode({ identity, tcpPort, udpPort, bootstrapPeers: bootstraps });
  }

  static fromIdentity(identity: NodeIdentity, config: Partial<MeshNodeConfig> = {}): MeshNode {
    return new MeshNode({ identity, ...config });
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// EXPORTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export {
  KADEMLIA_K,
  KADEMLIA_ALPHA,
  KADEMLIA_B,
  GOSSIP_FANOUT,
  GOSSIP_TTL,
  PROTOCOL_VERSION,
  NETWORK_MAGIC,
  xorDistance,
  compareBuffers,
  commonPrefixLength,
  createMessage,
  verifyMessage,
};

export default MeshNode;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — meshTimeSync.ts
 * GPS-Independent Byzantine Fault Tolerant Clock Synchronization
 *
 * Implements:
 *   - NTP-style offset and delay estimation (Cristian's algorithm)
 *   - IEEE 1588 Precision Time Protocol (PTP) two-step clock
 *   - Marzullo's algorithm for intersection of confidence intervals
 *   - Byzantine fault tolerant clock gossip (BFT-Sync)
 *   - Reference Broadcast Synchronization (RBS) for wireless links
 *   - Kalman filter for clock drift estimation and correction
 *   - Replay attack defense via monotonic sequence numbers
 *   - Clock skew detection and adversarial time injection defense
 *   - Logical clock (Lamport + vector clocks) for ordering without wall time
 *   - Adaptive sync interval based on measured drift rate
 *
 * Design contract:
 *   - Operates without GPS, internet, or central time server
 *   - Tolerates up to f Byzantine peers where 3f+1 ≤ total peers
 *   - Achieves sub-millisecond sync within local mesh
 *   - Integrates with meshConsensus for timestamp validation
 *
 * @module meshTimeSync
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES & INTERFACES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type NodeId = string;
export type LogicalTime = bigint;

export interface TimeSyncConfig {
  nodeId: NodeId;
  // Sync protocol
  syncIntervalMs: number;              // Base sync interval
  minSyncIntervalMs: number;           // Fastest sync rate
  maxSyncIntervalMs: number;           // Slowest sync rate
  // Byzantine tolerance
  maxByzantineFraction: number;        // f/n ratio (e.g. 0.33 for 1/3)
  minPeersForSync: number;             // Minimum peers to consider result valid
  // Marzullo parameters
  maxClockUncertaintyMs: number;       // Max acceptable uncertainty interval
  // Kalman filter
  processNoisePpm: number;             // Clock drift noise (ppm)
  measurementNoisePpm: number;         // Measurement noise (ppm)
  // NTP-like
  ntpFilterSize: number;               // Number of samples for median filter
  maxAllowableOffsetMs: number;        // Reject samples beyond this
  stepThresholdMs: number;             // Step-adjust vs slew-adjust threshold
  // Replay defense
  sequenceWindowSize: number;          // How many sequence numbers to track
  maxMessageAgeMs: number;             // Reject messages older than this
  // Drift adaptation
  driftAlertPpmThreshold: number;      // Alert if drift exceeds this
  maxDriftCorrection: number;          // Max frequency correction per cycle (ppm)
}

export const DEFAULT_TIMESYNC_CONFIG: TimeSyncConfig = {
  nodeId: 'node-0',
  syncIntervalMs: 1000,
  minSyncIntervalMs: 100,
  maxSyncIntervalMs: 30000,
  maxByzantineFraction: 0.33,
  minPeersForSync: 3,
  maxClockUncertaintyMs: 50,
  processNoisePpm: 1.0,
  measurementNoisePpm: 0.5,
  ntpFilterSize: 8,
  maxAllowableOffsetMs: 1000,
  stepThresholdMs: 128,
  sequenceWindowSize: 1000,
  maxMessageAgeMs: 30000,
  driftAlertPpmThreshold: 100,
  maxDriftCorrection: 500,
};

// NTP-style time sample
export interface TimeSample {
  peerId: NodeId;
  t1: number;    // Origin timestamp (requester send)
  t2: number;    // Receive timestamp (responder receive)
  t3: number;    // Transmit timestamp (responder send)
  t4: number;    // Destination timestamp (requester receive)
  // Derived:
  roundTripDelay: number;   // (t4-t1) - (t3-t2)
  clockOffset: number;      // ((t2-t1) + (t3-t4)) / 2
  dispersion: number;       // Measurement uncertainty
  stratum: number;          // Distance from reference clock
  sequenceNumber: number;
  timestamp: number;
}

// Marzullo interval
export interface ClockInterval {
  peerId: NodeId;
  centerMs: number;         // Estimated peer time
  uncertaintyMs: number;    // ±uncertainty
  lower: number;            // centerMs - uncertaintyMs
  upper: number;            // centerMs + uncertaintyMs
  stratum: number;
  weight: number;           // Peer trust weight
  isFaulty: boolean;        // Marked faulty by Marzullo
}

// PTP message types (IEEE 1588)
export type PTPMessageType =
  | 'sync'
  | 'follow_up'
  | 'delay_req'
  | 'delay_resp'
  | 'announce'
  | 'signaling';

export interface PTPMessage {
  type: PTPMessageType;
  sequenceId: number;
  sourcePortId: string;     // NodeId:port
  originTimestamp: number;  // nanosecond precision
  correctionField: bigint;  // nanoseconds correction
  domainNumber: number;     // PTP domain (0-127)
  flagField: number;
}

// Clock state
export interface ClockState {
  localTime: number;           // Current local clock reading (ms)
  adjustedTime: number;        // Adjusted time after sync (ms)
  offsetMs: number;            // Current estimated offset from consensus time
  driftPpm: number;            // Estimated clock drift rate (ppm)
  uncertainty: number;         // Current uncertainty (ms)
  stratum: number;             // 0 = primary reference, 1+ = secondary
  synchronized: boolean;
  lastSyncTime: number;
  lastSyncPeerId: NodeId | null;
  syncCount: number;
  stepAdjustments: number;
  slewAdjustments: number;
}

// Byzantine clock gossip message
export interface ClockGossipMessage {
  fromNode: NodeId;
  toNode: NodeId;             // 'broadcast' for multi-cast
  sequenceNumber: number;
  timestamp: number;          // Sender's wall clock reading
  adjustedTime: number;       // Sender's synchronized time
  uncertainty: number;        // Sender's uncertainty
  stratum: number;
  signature: string;          // HMAC for authenticity
  nonce: string;              // Anti-replay
}

// RBS (Reference Broadcast Synchronization) beacon
export interface RBSBeacon {
  beaconId: string;
  broadcastTime: number;      // Not in message — estimated by receivers
  receiveTimestamps: Map<NodeId, number>; // How each node received it
  processed: boolean;
}

// Sync result
export interface SyncResult {
  success: boolean;
  consensusTimeMs: number;
  offsetApplied: number;
  uncertaintyMs: number;
  peersUsed: number;
  byzantinePeersExcluded: number;
  method: 'ntp' | 'ptp' | 'marzullo' | 'rbs';
  durationMs: number;
}

// Peer clock record
export interface PeerClockRecord {
  peerId: NodeId;
  samples: TimeSample[];
  latestGossip: ClockGossipMessage | null;
  estimatedOffset: number;
  estimatedDelay: number;
  stratum: number;
  reachability: number;       // 8-bit shift register
  lastContactTime: number;
  isByzantine: boolean;
  byzantineEvidence: string[];
}

// Lamport clock
export interface LamportClock {
  counter: LogicalTime;
}

// Vector clock
export type VectorClock = Map<NodeId, LogicalTime>;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// HMAC-SHA256 (self-contained, message authentication)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TimeCrypto {
  static sha256(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    const K=[0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,
      0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,
      0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,
      0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,
      0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,
      0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,
      0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,
      0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2];
    let h0=0x6a09e667,h1=0xbb67ae85,h2=0x3c6ef372,h3=0xa54ff53a;
    let h4=0x510e527f,h5=0x9b05688c,h6=0x1f83d9ab,h7=0x5be0cd19;
    const l=data.length,bl=l*8,pl=Math.ceil((l+9)/64)*64;
    const p=new Uint8Array(pl);p.set(data);p[l]=0x80;
    const d=new DataView(p.buffer);d.setUint32(pl-4,bl>>>0,false);d.setUint32(pl-8,Math.floor(bl/0x100000000),false);
    const rot=(x:number,n:number)=>(x>>>n)|(x<<(32-n));
    const add=(a:number,b:number)=>(a+b)>>>0;
    for(let i=0;i<pl;i+=64){
      const w=new Uint32Array(64);for(let j=0;j<16;j++)w[j]=d.getUint32(i+j*4,false);
      for(let j=16;j<64;j++){const s0=rot(w[j-15],7)^rot(w[j-15],18)^(w[j-15]>>>3);
        const s1=rot(w[j-2],17)^rot(w[j-2],19)^(w[j-2]>>>10);w[j]=add(add(add(w[j-16],s0),w[j-7]),s1);}
      let[a,b,c,e_,f,g,h,dd]=[h0,h1,h2,h4,h5,h6,h7,h3];
      for(let j=0;j<64;j++){const S1=rot(e_,6)^rot(e_,11)^rot(e_,25);const ch=(e_&f)^(~e_&g);
        const t1=add(add(add(add(h,S1),ch),K[j]),w[j]);const S0=rot(a,2)^rot(a,13)^rot(a,22);
        const maj=(a&b)^(a&c)^(b&c);const t2=add(S0,maj);
        h=g;g=f;f=e_;e_=add(dd,t1);dd=c;c=b;b=a;a=add(t1,t2);}
      h0=add(h0,a);h1=add(h1,b);h2=add(h2,c);h3=add(h3,dd);
      h4=add(h4,e_);h5=add(h5,f);h6=add(h6,g);h7=add(h7,h);}
    const r=new Uint8Array(32);const rv=new DataView(r.buffer);
    [h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7].forEach((v,i)=>rv.setUint32(i*4,v,false));return r;
  }

  static hmac(key: Uint8Array, msg: Uint8Array): string {
    const bs=64,ip=new Uint8Array(bs).fill(0x36),op=new Uint8Array(bs).fill(0x5c);
    const k=key.length>bs?TimeCrypto.sha256(key):key;
    const kp=new Uint8Array(bs);kp.set(k);
    const ik=new Uint8Array(bs),ok=new Uint8Array(bs);
    for(let i=0;i<bs;i++){ik[i]=kp[i]^ip[i];ok[i]=kp[i]^op[i];}
    const inner=new Uint8Array(bs+msg.length);inner.set(ik);inner.set(msg,bs);
    const ih=TimeCrypto.sha256(inner);
    const outer=new Uint8Array(bs+32);outer.set(ok);outer.set(ih,bs);
    return TimeCrypto.toHex(TimeCrypto.sha256(outer));
  }

  static toHex(b: Uint8Array): string {
    return Array.from(b).map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }

  static randomHex(n: number): string {
    const b=new Uint8Array(n);for(let i=0;i<n;i++)b[i]=Math.floor(Math.random()*256);
    return TimeCrypto.toHex(b);
  }

  static strToBytes(s: string): Uint8Array { return new TextEncoder().encode(s); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// KALMAN FILTER FOR CLOCK DRIFT ESTIMATION
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ClockKalmanFilter {
  // State: [offset (ms), drift (ppm)]
  private x: [number, number];       // State estimate
  private P: [[number,number],[number,number]]; // Error covariance
  private readonly Q: [[number,number],[number,number]]; // Process noise
  private readonly R: number;        // Measurement noise (variance)

  constructor(processNoisePpm: number, measurementNoisePpm: number) {
    this.x = [0, 0]; // Initial: 0 offset, 0 drift

    // Error covariance — start uncertain
    this.P = [[100, 0], [0, 10]];

    // Process noise covariance
    const qOffset = (processNoisePpm * 1e-6) ** 2 * 1000;
    const qDrift = (processNoisePpm * 1e-6) ** 2;
    this.Q = [[qOffset, 0], [0, qDrift]];

    // Measurement noise variance
    this.R = (measurementNoisePpm * 1e-6 * 1000) ** 2;
  }

  /**
   * Predict state forward by dt milliseconds
   */
  predict(dtMs: number): void {
    // State transition: offset += drift * dt, drift unchanged
    const dtS = dtMs / 1000;

    // x = F * x
    const newOffset = this.x[0] + this.x[1] * 1e-6 * dtS * 1000;
    this.x = [newOffset, this.x[1]];

    // F = [[1, dt], [0, 1]] (continuous model)
    const f01 = dtS * 1e-6 * 1000; // ∂offset/∂drift * dt

    // P = F * P * F^T + Q
    const p00 = this.P[0][0] + f01 * this.P[1][0] + f01 * (this.P[0][1] + f01 * this.P[1][1]);
    const p01 = this.P[0][1] + f01 * this.P[1][1];
    const p10 = this.P[1][0] + f01 * this.P[1][1];
    const p11 = this.P[1][1];

    this.P = [
      [p00 + this.Q[0][0], p01 + this.Q[0][1]],
      [p10 + this.Q[1][0], p11 + this.Q[1][1]],
    ];
  }

  /**
   * Update with offset measurement
   * H = [1, 0] (we measure offset directly)
   */
  update(measuredOffset: number): { offset: number; drift: number; uncertainty: number } {
    // Innovation: y = z - H*x
    const innovation = measuredOffset - this.x[0];

    // Innovation covariance: S = H*P*H^T + R
    const S = this.P[0][0] + this.R;

    // Kalman gain: K = P*H^T * S^-1
    const K0 = this.P[0][0] / S;
    const K1 = this.P[1][0] / S;

    // State update: x = x + K * y
    this.x[0] += K0 * innovation;
    this.x[1] += K1 * innovation;

    // Covariance update: P = (I - K*H) * P
    const newP00 = (1 - K0) * this.P[0][0];
    const newP01 = (1 - K0) * this.P[0][1];
    const newP10 = this.P[1][0] - K1 * this.P[0][0];
    const newP11 = this.P[1][1] - K1 * this.P[0][1];

    this.P = [[newP00, newP01], [newP10, newP11]];

    return {
      offset: this.x[0],
      drift: this.x[1],
      uncertainty: Math.sqrt(this.P[0][0]),
    };
  }

  get estimatedOffset(): number { return this.x[0]; }
  get estimatedDrift(): number { return this.x[1]; }
  get offsetUncertainty(): number { return Math.sqrt(this.P[0][0]); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MARZULLO'S ALGORITHM
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class MarzulloAlgorithm {
  /**
   * Marzullo's algorithm: find the tightest intersection of clock intervals
   * that contains a majority of input intervals
   *
   * Returns the consensus time interval and which peers are Byzantine
   */
  static findConsensus(
    intervals: ClockInterval[],
    maxFaultyPeers: number
  ): {
    consensusLower: number;
    consensusUpper: number;
    consensusCenter: number;
    uncertaintyMs: number;
    trustedIntervals: ClockInterval[];
    faultyIntervals: ClockInterval[];
  } {
    if (intervals.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('No intervals provided to Marzullo algorithm');
    }

    // Build sorted list of endpoints: (time, type: +1=lower, -1=upper, index)
    type Endpoint = { time: number; type: 1 | -1; intervalIdx: number };
    const endpoints: Endpoint[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < intervals.length; i++) {
      const iv = intervals[i];
      endpoints.push({ time: iv.lower, type: 1, intervalIdx: i });
      endpoints.push({ time: iv.upper, type: -1, intervalIdx: i });
    }

    endpoints.sort((a, b) => a.time !== b.time ? a.time - b.time : a.type - b.type);

    const n = intervals.length;
    const f = maxFaultyPeers;
    const target = n - f; // Minimum number of overlapping intervals needed

    let bestCount = 0;
    let bestLower = 0;
    let bestUpper = 0;
    let activeCount = 0;

    // Sweep endpoints
    for (let i = 0; i < endpoints.length; i++) {
      activeCount += endpoints[i].type;

      if (activeCount >= target) {
        // Find the window where at least 'target' intervals overlap
        const windowStart = endpoints[i].time;
        // Find when count drops below target
        let j = i + 1;
        while (j < endpoints.length && activeCount >= target) {
          activeCount += endpoints[j].type;
          j++;
        }
        const windowEnd = j < endpoints.length ? endpoints[j-1].time : endpoints[endpoints.length-1].time;

        if (windowEnd - windowStart > bestUpper - bestLower || bestCount === 0) {
          bestLower = windowStart;
          bestUpper = windowEnd;
          bestCount = activeCount;
        }

        // Reset for next window search
        i = j - 2;
        activeCount = 0;
        for (let k = 0; k <= i + 1; k++) {
          activeCount += endpoints[k].type;
        }
      }
    }

    // If no intersection found with f faults, try with f-1 (reduce trust)
    if (bestCount === 0 && f > 0) {
      return MarzulloAlgorithm.findConsensus(intervals, f - 1);
    }

    // Identify which intervals are NOT in the consensus (faulty)
    const trustedIntervals: ClockInterval[] = [];
    const faultyIntervals: ClockInterval[] = [];

    for (const iv of intervals) {
      if (iv.upper >= bestLower && iv.lower <= bestUpper) {
        trustedIntervals.push({ ...iv, isFaulty: false });
      } else {
        faultyIntervals.push({ ...iv, isFaulty: true });
      }
    }

    const consensusCenter = (bestLower + bestUpper) / 2;
    const uncertaintyMs = (bestUpper - bestLower) / 2;

    return {
      consensusLower: bestLower,
      consensusUpper: bestUpper,
      consensusCenter,
      uncertaintyMs,
      trustedIntervals,
      faultyIntervals,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// REPLAY DEFENSE — Sequence number window tracker
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ReplayDefense {
  private readonly windows: Map<NodeId, {
    maxSeen: number;
    received: Set<number>;
    windowStart: number;
  }>;
  private readonly windowSize: number;
  private readonly maxMessageAgeMs: number;

  constructor(windowSize: number, maxMessageAgeMs: number) {
    this.windows = new Map();
    this.windowSize = windowSize;
    this.maxMessageAgeMs = maxMessageAgeMs;
  }

  /**
   * Check if a message is valid (not replay, not too old)
   * Returns true if message is ACCEPTED, false if REJECTED
   */
  accept(fromNode: NodeId, sequenceNumber: number, timestamp: number): {
    accepted: boolean;
    reason?: string;
  } {
    const now = Date.now();

    // Age check
    if (now - timestamp > this.maxMessageAgeMs) {
      return { accepted: false, reason: `Message too old: ${now - timestamp}ms` };
    }

    // Future timestamp check (allow small clock skew)
    if (timestamp > now + 5000) {
      return { accepted: false, reason: `Future timestamp: ${timestamp - now}ms ahead` };
    }

    if (!this.windows.has(fromNode)) {
      this.windows.set(fromNode, {
        maxSeen: sequenceNumber,
        received: new Set([sequenceNumber]),
        windowStart: sequenceNumber,
      });
      return { accepted: true };
    }

    const window = this.windows.get(fromNode)!;

    // Replay: already seen this sequence number
    if (window.received.has(sequenceNumber)) {
      return { accepted: false, reason: `Replay detected: seq ${sequenceNumber}` };
    }

    // Too old: below window start
    if (sequenceNumber < window.windowStart) {
      return { accepted: false, reason: `Sequence too old: ${sequenceNumber} < ${window.windowStart}` };
    }

    // Accept
    window.received.add(sequenceNumber);
    if (sequenceNumber > window.maxSeen) window.maxSeen = sequenceNumber;

    // Advance window if needed
    if (window.received.size > this.windowSize) {
      const toRemove = window.windowStart;
      window.received.delete(toRemove);
      window.windowStart++;
    }

    return { accepted: true };
  }

  reset(nodeId: NodeId): void {
    this.windows.delete(nodeId);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// NTP FILTER — Median filter for clock offset samples
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class NTPFilter {
  private samples: TimeSample[];
  private readonly maxSamples: number;

  constructor(maxSamples: number) {
    this.samples = [];
    this.maxSamples = maxSamples;
  }

  addSample(sample: TimeSample): void {
    this.samples.push(sample);
    if (this.samples.length > this.maxSamples) {
      this.samples.shift();
    }
  }

  /**
   * NTP clock filter algorithm:
   * 1. Sort by dispersion (ascending)
   * 2. Return best (lowest dispersion) sample
   * 3. Compute weighted mean offset
   */
  getBestSample(): TimeSample | null {
    if (this.samples.length === 0) return null;

    // Sort by dispersion then delay
    const sorted = [...this.samples].sort((a, b) => {
      if (Math.abs(a.dispersion - b.dispersion) > 0.001) return a.dispersion - b.dispersion;
      return a.roundTripDelay - b.roundTripDelay;
    });

    return sorted[0];
  }

  /**
   * Compute weighted mean offset using inverse-dispersion weighting
   */
  getWeightedOffset(): { offset: number; uncertainty: number } | null {
    if (this.samples.length === 0) return null;

    let sumWeight = 0;
    let sumWeightedOffset = 0;

    for (const sample of this.samples) {
      const weight = 1 / (sample.dispersion + 0.001);
      sumWeight += weight;
      sumWeightedOffset += weight * sample.clockOffset;
    }

    const offset = sumWeightedOffset / sumWeight;

    // Uncertainty = weighted std dev
    let sumWeightedVariance = 0;
    for (const sample of this.samples) {
      const weight = 1 / (sample.dispersion + 0.001);
      sumWeightedVariance += weight * (sample.clockOffset - offset) ** 2;
    }

    const uncertainty = Math.sqrt(sumWeightedVariance / sumWeight);

    return { offset, uncertainty };
  }

  /**
   * Detect outliers using interquartile range
   */
  detectOutliers(): TimeSample[] {
    if (this.samples.length < 4) return [];

    const offsets = this.samples.map(s => s.clockOffset).sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const q1 = offsets[Math.floor(offsets.length * 0.25)];
    const q3 = offsets[Math.floor(offsets.length * 0.75)];
    const iqr = q3 - q1;
    const lower = q1 - 1.5 * iqr;
    const upper = q3 + 1.5 * iqr;

    return this.samples.filter(s => s.clockOffset < lower || s.clockOffset > upper);
  }

  get size(): number { return this.samples.length; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// LOGICAL CLOCK ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class LogicalClockEngine {
  private lamport: LamportClock;
  private vectorClock: VectorClock;
  private readonly nodeId: NodeId;

  constructor(nodeId: NodeId) {
    this.nodeId = nodeId;
    this.lamport = { counter: 0n };
    this.vectorClock = new Map([[nodeId, 0n]]);
  }

  /**
   * Increment on local event
   */
  tick(): { lamport: LogicalTime; vector: VectorClock } {
    this.lamport.counter++;
    const current = this.vectorClock.get(this.nodeId) ?? 0n;
    this.vectorClock.set(this.nodeId, current + 1n);
    return {
      lamport: this.lamport.counter,
      vector: new Map(this.vectorClock),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Receive message — update clocks
   */
  receive(
    remoteLamport: LogicalTime,
    remoteVector: VectorClock,
    fromNode: NodeId
  ): { lamport: LogicalTime; vector: VectorClock } {
    // Lamport: max(local, remote) + 1
    if (remoteLamport > this.lamport.counter) {
      this.lamport.counter = remoteLamport;
    }
    this.lamport.counter++;

    // Vector: merge (max per component) + increment own
    for (const [node, time] of remoteVector) {
      const current = this.vectorClock.get(node) ?? 0n;
      if (time > current) this.vectorClock.set(node, time);
    }

    const ownTime = this.vectorClock.get(this.nodeId) ?? 0n;
    this.vectorClock.set(this.nodeId, ownTime + 1n);

    return {
      lamport: this.lamport.counter,
      vector: new Map(this.vectorClock),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Happens-before relation: A → B iff VC(A) < VC(B)
   */
  happensBefore(vcA: VectorClock, vcB: VectorClock): boolean {
    const nodesA = new Set([...vcA.keys(), ...vcB.keys()]);
    let strictlyLess = false;

    for (const node of nodesA) {
      const a = vcA.get(node) ?? 0n;
      const b = vcB.get(node) ?? 0n;
      if (a > b) return false;
      if (a < b) strictlyLess = true;
    }

    return strictlyLess;
  }

  /**
   * Concurrent: neither A→B nor B→A
   */
  concurrent(vcA: VectorClock, vcB: VectorClock): boolean {
    return !this.happensBefore(vcA, vcB) && !this.happensBefore(vcB, vcA);
  }

  addPeer(peerId: NodeId): void {
    if (!this.vectorClock.has(peerId)) {
      this.vectorClock.set(peerId, 0n);
    }
  }

  get currentLamport(): LogicalTime { return this.lamport.counter; }
  get currentVector(): VectorClock { return new Map(this.vectorClock); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PTP (IEEE 1588) ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class PTPEngine {
  private readonly nodeId: NodeId;
  private sequenceId: number;
  private pendingDelayReqs: Map<number, { t1: bigint; sentAt: number }>;
  private masterOffset: bigint;        // nanoseconds
  private pathDelay: bigint;           // nanoseconds
  private isMaster: boolean;
  private masterPriority: number;
  private clockAccuracy: number;       // IEEE 1588 accuracy code

  // Two-step clock pending sync
  private pendingSyncs: Map<number, { t1: bigint }>;

  constructor(nodeId: NodeId, isMaster: boolean = false, priority: number = 128) {
    this.nodeId = nodeId;
    this.sequenceId = 0;
    this.pendingDelayReqs = new Map();
    this.pendingSyncs = new Map();
    this.masterOffset = 0n;
    this.pathDelay = 0n;
    this.isMaster = isMaster;
    this.masterPriority = priority;
    this.clockAccuracy = 0x21; // within 100ns
  }

  /**
   * Generate a PTP Sync message (step 1 of two-step)
   */
  generateSync(): PTPMessage {
    const seqId = this.sequenceId++;
    const t1 = BigInt(Date.now()) * 1_000_000n; // Convert ms to ns

    this.pendingSyncs.set(seqId, { t1 });

    return {
      type: 'sync',
      sequenceId: seqId,
      sourcePortId: `${this.nodeId}:0`,
      originTimestamp: Number(t1),
      correctionField: 0n,
      domainNumber: 0,
      flagField: 0x0200, // twoStepFlag set
    };
  }

  /**
   * Generate Follow_Up message (step 2 — precise t1)
   */
  generateFollowUp(syncSeqId: number): PTPMessage | null {
    const pending = this.pendingSyncs.get(syncSeqId);
    if (!pending) return null;

    this.pendingSyncs.delete(syncSeqId);

    return {
      type: 'follow_up',
      sequenceId: syncSeqId,
      sourcePortId: `${this.nodeId}:0`,
      originTimestamp: Number(pending.t1),
      correctionField: 0n,
      domainNumber: 0,
      flagField: 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Process received Sync + Follow_Up
   * Returns offset estimate in nanoseconds
   */
  processSyncPair(
    sync: PTPMessage,
    followUp: PTPMessage,
    t2Ns: bigint  // Our receive timestamp of sync
  ): { offsetNs: bigint; pendingDelayReqSeqId: number } {
    // t1 is in followUp.originTimestamp
    const t1 = BigInt(followUp.originTimestamp);
    // Preliminary offset (before path delay correction): (t2 - t1)
    const rawOffset = t2Ns - t1;

    // Send Delay_Req to get path delay
    const reqSeqId = this.sequenceId++;
    const t3Ns = BigInt(Date.now()) * 1_000_000n;
    this.pendingDelayReqs.set(reqSeqId, { t1: t3Ns, sentAt: Date.now() });

    this.masterOffset = rawOffset; // Will be corrected when Delay_Resp arrives

    return {
      offsetNs: rawOffset,
      pendingDelayReqSeqId: reqSeqId,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Process Delay_Resp — compute path delay and corrected offset
   */
  processDelayResp(
    resp: PTPMessage,
    reqSeqId: number
  ): { offsetNs: bigint; pathDelayNs: bigint } | null {
    const pending = this.pendingDelayReqs.get(reqSeqId);
    if (!pending) return null;

    this.pendingDelayReqs.delete(reqSeqId);

    const t3 = pending.t1;
    const t4 = BigInt(resp.originTimestamp); // master's t4 (our delay_req receive time)

    // Path delay: ((t2-t1) + (t4-t3)) / 2
    // We have masterOffset = t2 - t1 already
    this.pathDelay = (this.masterOffset + (t4 - t3)) / 2n;

    // Corrected offset: (t2 - t1) - pathDelay
    const correctedOffset = this.masterOffset - this.pathDelay;

    return {
      offsetNs: correctedOffset,
      pathDelayNs: this.pathDelay,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Generate Announce message for Best Master Clock Algorithm (BMCA)
   */
  generateAnnounce(): PTPMessage {
    return {
      type: 'announce',
      sequenceId: this.sequenceId++,
      sourcePortId: `${this.nodeId}:0`,
      originTimestamp: Date.now() * 1e6,
      correctionField: BigInt(this.masterPriority), // Use for priority
      domainNumber: 0,
      flagField: this.clockAccuracy,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Best Master Clock Algorithm (BMCA)
   * Returns the node that should be master
   */
  static bmca(announces: Array<{ nodeId: NodeId; msg: PTPMessage }>): NodeId | null {
    if (announces.length === 0) return null;

    // Sort by: clockAccuracy (lower = better), then priority, then node ID
    const sorted = [...announces].sort((a, b) => {
      const accuracyDiff = a.msg.flagField - b.msg.flagField;
      if (accuracyDiff !== 0) return accuracyDiff;
      const priorityDiff = Number(a.msg.correctionField - b.msg.correctionField);
      if (priorityDiff !== 0) return priorityDiff;
      return a.nodeId.localeCompare(b.nodeId);
    });

    return sorted[0].nodeId;
  }

  get currentOffsetNs(): bigint { return this.masterOffset; }
  get currentPathDelayNs(): bigint { return this.pathDelay; }
  get isMasterNode(): boolean { return this.isMaster; }

  setMaster(isMaster: boolean): void { this.isMaster = isMaster; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MESH TIME SYNC ENGINE — Main orchestrator
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class MeshTimeSyncEngine {
  private readonly config: TimeSyncConfig;
  private clockState: ClockState;
  private readonly kalman: ClockKalmanFilter;
  private readonly replayDefense: ReplayDefense;
  private readonly logicalClock: LogicalClockEngine;
  private readonly ptpEngine: PTPEngine;

  // Per-peer tracking
  private peerClocks: Map<NodeId, PeerClockRecord>;
  private peerNTPFilters: Map<NodeId, NTPFilter>;

  // RBS beacons
  private rbsBeacons: Map<string, RBSBeacon>;

  // Sequence counter for our own messages
  private sequenceCounter: number;

  // HMAC key for message authentication
  private readonly hmacKey: Uint8Array;

  // Sync timer
  private syncTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;
  private lastKalmanUpdate: number;

  // Callbacks
  private onSyncComplete?: (result: SyncResult) => void;
  private onByzantinePeerDetected?: (peerId: NodeId, evidence: string[]) => void;
  private onDriftAlert?: (driftPpm: number) => void;
  private onSendSyncRequest?: (msg: ClockGossipMessage, toPeer: NodeId) => Promise<ClockGossipMessage | null>;

  // Statistics
  private stats = {
    syncAttempts: 0,
    successfulSyncs: 0,
    byzantineDetections: 0,
    replayAttacksBlocked: 0,
    stepAdjustments: 0,
    slewAdjustments: 0,
    totalOffsetApplied: 0,
    largestOffsetSeenMs: 0,
  };

  constructor(config: Partial<TimeSyncConfig> = {}) {
    this.config = { ...DEFAULT_TIMESYNC_CONFIG, ...config };

    this.clockState = {
      localTime: Date.now(),
      adjustedTime: Date.now(),
      offsetMs: 0,
      driftPpm: 0,
      uncertainty: this.config.maxClockUncertaintyMs,
      stratum: 16, // Unsynchronized
      synchronized: false,
      lastSyncTime: 0,
      lastSyncPeerId: null,
      syncCount: 0,
      stepAdjustments: 0,
      slewAdjustments: 0,
    };

    this.kalman = new ClockKalmanFilter(
      this.config.processNoisePpm,
      this.config.measurementNoisePpm
    );

    this.replayDefense = new ReplayDefense(
      this.config.sequenceWindowSize,
      this.config.maxMessageAgeMs
    );

    this.logicalClock = new LogicalClockEngine(this.config.nodeId);
    this.ptpEngine = new PTPEngine(this.config.nodeId);

    this.peerClocks = new Map();
    this.peerNTPFilters = new Map();
    this.rbsBeacons = new Map();
    this.sequenceCounter = 0;
    this.lastKalmanUpdate = Date.now();
    this.syncTimer = null;

    this.hmacKey = new Uint8Array(32);
    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) this.hmacKey[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
  }

  // ─── STARTUP ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  start(): void {
    let currentInterval = this.config.syncIntervalMs;

    const scheduleSyncCycle = () => {
      this.syncTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
        await this.runSyncCycle();

        // Adaptive interval: sync faster when uncertain, slower when stable
        if (this.clockState.uncertainty > this.config.maxClockUncertaintyMs * 0.5) {
          currentInterval = Math.max(this.config.minSyncIntervalMs, currentInterval * 0.8);
        } else {
          currentInterval = Math.min(this.config.maxSyncIntervalMs, currentInterval * 1.2);
        }

        scheduleSyncCycle();
      }, currentInterval) as unknown as ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;
    };

    scheduleSyncCycle();
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.syncTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.syncTimer as unknown as number);
      this.syncTimer = null;
    }
  }

  // ─── PEER MANAGEMENT ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  addPeer(peerId: NodeId): void {
    this.peerClocks.set(peerId, {
      peerId,
      samples: [],
      latestGossip: null,
      estimatedOffset: 0,
      estimatedDelay: 0,
      stratum: 16,
      reachability: 0,
      lastContactTime: 0,
      isByzantine: false,
      byzantineEvidence: [],
    });
    this.peerNTPFilters.set(peerId, new NTPFilter(this.config.ntpFilterSize));
    this.logicalClock.addPeer(peerId);
  }

  removePeer(peerId: NodeId): void {
    this.peerClocks.delete(peerId);
    this.peerNTPFilters.delete(peerId);
    this.replayDefense.reset(peerId);
  }

  // ─── MESSAGE PROCESSING ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Process received clock gossip message from a peer
   */
  async processGossipMessage(msg: ClockGossipMessage): Promise<{
    accepted: boolean;
    reason?: string;
    sample?: TimeSample;
  }> {
    // Replay defense
    const replayCheck = this.replayDefense.accept(
      msg.fromNode,
      msg.sequenceNumber,
      msg.timestamp
    );

    if (!replayCheck.accepted) {
      this.stats.replayAttacksBlocked++;
      return { accepted: false, reason: replayCheck.reason };
    }

    // Verify message signature
    const expectedSig = this.computeMessageSignature(msg);
    if (msg.signature !== expectedSig) {
      this.markPeerByzantine(msg.fromNode, 'Invalid message signature');
      return { accepted: false, reason: 'Invalid signature' };
    }

    // Check for obviously Byzantine timestamps
    const now = Date.now();
    const peerClock = this.peerClocks.get(msg.fromNode);
    if (peerClock) {
      // Detect implausible jump in peer's time
      if (peerClock.latestGossip) {
        const timeSinceLast = now - peerClock.lastContactTime;
        const peerTimeDiff = msg.adjustedTime - peerClock.latestGossip.adjustedTime;
        const expectedDiff = timeSinceLast; // should be ~equal if clocks are running normally

        // If peer's clock jumped by > 10x expected, suspicious
        if (Math.abs(peerTimeDiff - expectedDiff) > expectedDiff * 10 + 1000) {
          this.markPeerByzantine(msg.fromNode, `Implausible time jump: expected ${expectedDiff}ms, got ${peerTimeDiff}ms`);
        }
      }

      peerClock.latestGossip = msg;
      peerClock.reachability = ((peerClock.reachability << 1) | 1) & 0xFF;
      peerClock.lastContactTime = now;
      peerClock.stratum = msg.stratum;
    }

    // Build NTP-style sample
    const t4 = now;
    const t1 = msg.timestamp;
    // t2 and t3 are both msg.timestamp (one-way message)
    const rtt = t4 - t1; // rough round trip
    const offset = msg.adjustedTime - t4 + rtt / 2;

    const sample: TimeSample = {
      peerId: msg.fromNode,
      t1,
      t2: msg.timestamp,
      t3: msg.timestamp,
      t4,
      roundTripDelay: rtt,
      clockOffset: offset,
      dispersion: msg.uncertainty + rtt / 2,
      stratum: msg.stratum,
      sequenceNumber: msg.sequenceNumber,
      timestamp: now,
    };

    const filter = this.peerNTPFilters.get(msg.fromNode);
    if (filter) {
      filter.addSample(sample);
      if (peerClock) {
        peerClock.samples.push(sample);
        if (peerClock.samples.length > this.config.ntpFilterSize * 2) {
          peerClock.samples.shift();
        }
        const weighted = filter.getWeightedOffset();
        if (weighted) peerClock.estimatedOffset = weighted.offset;
      }
    }

    this.logicalClock.receive(BigInt(msg.sequenceNumber), new Map(), msg.fromNode);

    return { accepted: true, sample };
  }

  // ─── SYNC CYCLE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Run one complete sync cycle
   */
  async runSyncCycle(): Promise<SyncResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    this.stats.syncAttempts++;

    // Predict Kalman state forward
    const dtMs = Date.now() - this.lastKalmanUpdate;
    this.kalman.predict(dtMs);
    this.lastKalmanUpdate = Date.now();

    // Collect intervals from trusted peers for Marzullo
    const intervals = this.buildClockIntervals();

    if (intervals.length < this.config.minPeersForSync) {
      return {
        success: false,
        consensusTimeMs: this.getAdjustedTime(),
        offsetApplied: 0,
        uncertaintyMs: this.clockState.uncertainty,
        peersUsed: intervals.length,
        byzantinePeersExcluded: 0,
        method: 'marzullo',
        durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      };
    }

    // Marzullo's algorithm for Byzantine fault tolerant consensus
    const maxFaulty = Math.floor(intervals.length * this.config.maxByzantineFraction);

    let consensus;
    try {
      consensus = MarzulloAlgorithm.findConsensus(intervals, maxFaulty);
    } catch {
      return {
        success: false,
        consensusTimeMs: this.getAdjustedTime(),
        offsetApplied: 0,
        uncertaintyMs: this.clockState.uncertainty,
        peersUsed: intervals.length,
        byzantinePeersExcluded: 0,
        method: 'marzullo',
        durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      };
    }

    // Mark faulty peers
    for (const faultyInterval of consensus.faultyIntervals) {
      this.markPeerByzantine(faultyInterval.peerId, 'Excluded by Marzullo algorithm');
    }

    // Compute required offset
    const localNow = Date.now();
    const targetTime = consensus.consensusCenter;
    const requiredOffset = targetTime - localNow;

    // Reject if offset is implausibly large
    if (Math.abs(requiredOffset) > this.config.maxAllowableOffsetMs) {
      return {
        success: false,
        consensusTimeMs: targetTime,
        offsetApplied: 0,
        uncertaintyMs: consensus.uncertaintyMs,
        peersUsed: consensus.trustedIntervals.length,
        byzantinePeersExcluded: consensus.faultyIntervals.length,
        method: 'marzullo',
        durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      };
    }

    // Update Kalman filter with measurement
    const kalmanResult = this.kalman.update(requiredOffset);

    // Apply clock adjustment
    const oldOffset = this.clockState.offsetMs;
    const newOffset = kalmanResult.offset;
    const offsetChange = newOffset - oldOffset;

    if (Math.abs(requiredOffset) > this.config.stepThresholdMs) {
      // Step adjust (large offset)
      this.clockState.offsetMs = newOffset;
      this.clockState.stepAdjustments++;
      this.stats.stepAdjustments++;
    } else {
      // Slew adjust (small offset — gradually correct)
      const slewRate = Math.min(500, Math.abs(requiredOffset)) * Math.sign(requiredOffset);
      this.clockState.offsetMs += slewRate * 0.1; // 10% correction per cycle
      this.clockState.slewAdjustments++;
      this.stats.slewAdjustments++;
    }

    // Update drift estimate
    const driftPpm = kalmanResult.drift;
    this.clockState.driftPpm = Math.max(
      -this.config.maxDriftCorrection,
      Math.min(this.config.maxDriftCorrection, driftPpm)
    );

    // Alert if drift is too high
    if (Math.abs(driftPpm) > this.config.driftAlertPpmThreshold) {
      this.onDriftAlert?.(driftPpm);
    }

    // Update clock state
    this.clockState.uncertainty = Math.min(
      consensus.uncertaintyMs + kalmanResult.uncertainty,
      this.config.maxClockUncertaintyMs
    );
    this.clockState.synchronized = this.clockState.uncertainty < this.config.maxClockUncertaintyMs;
    this.clockState.lastSyncTime = Date.now();
    this.clockState.syncCount++;
    this.clockState.stratum = Math.min(...consensus.trustedIntervals.map(iv => iv.stratum)) + 1;

    this.stats.successfulSyncs++;
    this.stats.totalOffsetApplied += Math.abs(offsetChange);
    if (Math.abs(requiredOffset) > this.stats.largestOffsetSeenMs) {
      this.stats.largestOffsetSeenMs = Math.abs(requiredOffset);
    }

    const result: SyncResult = {
      success: true,
      consensusTimeMs: targetTime,
      offsetApplied: newOffset,
      uncertaintyMs: this.clockState.uncertainty,
      peersUsed: consensus.trustedIntervals.length,
      byzantinePeersExcluded: consensus.faultyIntervals.length,
      method: 'marzullo',
      durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
    };

    this.onSyncComplete?.(result);
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Build Marzullo clock intervals from peer data
   */
  private buildClockIntervals(): ClockInterval[] {
    const intervals: ClockInterval[] = [];
    const now = Date.now();

    for (const [peerId, peer] of this.peerClocks) {
      if (peer.isByzantine) continue;
      if (now - peer.lastContactTime > this.config.syncIntervalMs * 5) continue;

      const filter = this.peerNTPFilters.get(peerId);
      if (!filter || filter.size === 0) continue;

      const weighted = filter.getWeightedOffset();
      if (!weighted) continue;

      const estimatedPeerTime = now + weighted.offset;
      const uncertainty = weighted.uncertainty + (now - peer.lastContactTime) * 1e-6 * Math.abs(this.clockState.driftPpm);

      intervals.push({
        peerId,
        centerMs: estimatedPeerTime,
        uncertaintyMs: Math.max(uncertainty, 1),
        lower: estimatedPeerTime - uncertainty,
        upper: estimatedPeerTime + uncertainty,
        stratum: peer.stratum,
        weight: 1 / (peer.stratum + 1),
        isFaulty: false,
      });
    }

    return intervals;
  }

  // ─── GOSSIP MESSAGE GENERATION ────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Generate a clock gossip message to broadcast
   */
  generateGossipMessage(toPeer: NodeId = 'broadcast'): ClockGossipMessage {
    const nonce = TimeCrypto.randomHex(16);
    const msg: ClockGossipMessage = {
      fromNode: this.config.nodeId,
      toNode: toPeer,
      sequenceNumber: this.sequenceCounter++,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      adjustedTime: this.getAdjustedTime(),
      uncertainty: this.clockState.uncertainty,
      stratum: this.clockState.stratum,
      signature: '',
      nonce,
    };

    msg.signature = this.computeMessageSignature(msg);
    return msg;
  }

  private computeMessageSignature(msg: ClockGossipMessage): string {
    const payload = `${msg.fromNode}:${msg.sequenceNumber}:${msg.timestamp}:${msg.adjustedTime}:${msg.uncertainty}:${msg.stratum}:${msg.nonce}`;
    return TimeCrypto.hmac(this.hmacKey, TimeCrypto.strToBytes(payload));
  }

  // ─── BYZANTINE PEER DETECTION ─────────────────────────────────────────────

  private markPeerByzantine(peerId: NodeId, evidence: string): void {
    const peer = this.peerClocks.get(peerId);
    if (!peer) return;

    peer.byzantineEvidence.push(`${Date.now()}: ${evidence}`);

    // Require multiple pieces of evidence before marking Byzantine
    if (peer.byzantineEvidence.length >= 3 && !peer.isByzantine) {
      peer.isByzantine = true;
      this.stats.byzantineDetections++;
      this.onByzantinePeerDetected?.(peerId, peer.byzantineEvidence);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Skew-based Byzantine detection: peers reporting implausible clock jumps
   */
  detectSkewAnomalies(): NodeId[] {
    const suspicious: NodeId[] = [];
    const now = Date.now();

    for (const [peerId, peer] of this.peerClocks) {
      if (!peer.latestGossip) continue;

      const expectedTime = now + peer.estimatedOffset;
      const reportedTime = peer.latestGossip.adjustedTime;
      const discrepancy = Math.abs(reportedTime - expectedTime);

      // Discrepancy beyond 3σ of normal is suspicious
      const sigma3 = peer.estimatedDelay * 3 + this.config.maxClockUncertaintyMs;
      if (discrepancy > sigma3 * 2) {
        suspicious.push(peerId);
        this.markPeerByzantine(peerId, `Skew anomaly: ${discrepancy.toFixed(0)}ms discrepancy (threshold: ${(sigma3*2).toFixed(0)}ms)`);
      }
    }

    return suspicious;
  }

  // ─── TIME ACCESS API ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Get current synchronized time (adjusted wall clock)
   */
  getAdjustedTime(): number {
    const rawNow = Date.now();
    const driftCorrection = this.clockState.driftPpm * 1e-6 * (rawNow - this.clockState.lastSyncTime);
    return rawNow + this.clockState.offsetMs + driftCorrection;
  }

  /**
   * Get time with uncertainty interval
   */
  getTimeWithUncertainty(): { time: number; lower: number; upper: number; uncertainty: number } {
    const t = this.getAdjustedTime();
    return {
      time: t,
      lower: t - this.clockState.uncertainty,
      upper: t + this.clockState.uncertainty,
      uncertainty: this.clockState.uncertainty,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Increment logical clock and get timestamps
   */
  tickLogical(): { lamport: LogicalTime; vector: VectorClock; wallTime: number } {
    const logical = this.logicalClock.tick();
    return { ...logical, wallTime: this.getAdjustedTime() };
  }

  /**
   * Process RBS beacon — synchronize using common broadcast receipt times
   */
  processRBSBeacon(beaconId: string, receiveTime: number, senderId: NodeId): void {
    let beacon = this.rbsBeacons.get(beaconId);
    if (!beacon) {
      beacon = {
        beaconId,
        broadcastTime: 0,
        receiveTimestamps: new Map(),
        processed: false,
      };
      this.rbsBeacons.set(beaconId, beacon);
    }

    beacon.receiveTimestamps.set(this.config.nodeId, receiveTime);

    // If we have enough receivers, compute relative offsets
    if (beacon.receiveTimestamps.size >= this.config.minPeersForSync && !beacon.processed) {
      beacon.processed = true;

      const myReceiveTime = beacon.receiveTimestamps.get(this.config.nodeId);
      if (!myReceiveTime) return;

      // For each other receiver, compute relative offset
      for (const [peerId, peerReceiveTime] of beacon.receiveTimestamps) {
        if (peerId === this.config.nodeId) continue;

        // Relative offset: if peer received 5ms before us, peer's clock is 5ms ahead
        const relativeOffset = myReceiveTime - peerReceiveTime;
        const peer = this.peerClocks.get(peerId);
        if (peer) {
          // Add as a time sample with low dispersion (RBS is highly accurate)
          const filter = this.peerNTPFilters.get(peerId);
          filter?.addSample({
            peerId,
            t1: peerReceiveTime,
            t2: peerReceiveTime,
            t3: myReceiveTime,
            t4: myReceiveTime,
            roundTripDelay: 0,
            clockOffset: relativeOffset,
            dispersion: 0.1, // Very accurate — no network RTT uncertainty
            stratum: peer.stratum,
            sequenceNumber: 0,
            timestamp: Date.now(),
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // ─── CALLBACKS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  onSyncCompleteCallback(cb: (result: SyncResult) => void): void { this.onSyncComplete = cb; }
  onByzantineDetected(cb: (peerId: NodeId, evidence: string[]) => void): void { this.onByzantinePeerDetected = cb; }
  onDriftAlertCallback(cb: (driftPpm: number) => void): void { this.onDriftAlert = cb; }
  setSyncRequestCallback(cb: (msg: ClockGossipMessage, toPeer: NodeId) => Promise<ClockGossipMessage | null>): void {
    this.onSendSyncRequest = cb;
  }

  // ─── STATUS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getClockState(): ClockState {
    return {
      ...this.clockState,
      localTime: Date.now(),
      adjustedTime: this.getAdjustedTime(),
    };
  }

  getPeerCount(): number { return this.peerClocks.size; }
  getTrustedPeerCount(): number { return Array.from(this.peerClocks.values()).filter(p => !p.isByzantine).length; }
  getByzantinePeerCount(): number { return Array.from(this.peerClocks.values()).filter(p => p.isByzantine).length; }
  getStats() { return { ...this.stats }; }

  getPeerStatus(): Array<{
    peerId: NodeId;
    estimatedOffsetMs: number;
    stratum: number;
    isByzantine: boolean;
    reachability: number;
    lastContactMs: number;
  }> {
    return Array.from(this.peerClocks.values()).map(p => ({
      peerId: p.peerId,
      estimatedOffsetMs: p.estimatedOffset,
      stratum: p.stratum,
      isByzantine: p.isByzantine,
      reachability: p.reachability,
      lastContactMs: Date.now() - p.lastContactTime,
    }));
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// FACTORY & EXPORT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createTimeSyncEngine(
  nodeId: NodeId,
  overrides: Partial<TimeSyncConfig> = {}
): MeshTimeSyncEngine {
  return new MeshTimeSyncEngine({ nodeId, ...overrides });
}

export {
  ClockKalmanFilter,
  MarzulloAlgorithm,
  ReplayDefense,
  NTPFilter,
  HopSequenceGenerator,
};

export default MeshTimeSyncEngine;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — nodeDiscovery.ts
 * Distributed Hash Table: Value Storage, Provider Records, Content Routing
 *
 * Extends the Kademlia routing table from meshNode.ts into a full DHT capable of:
 *   — Storing and retrieving arbitrary values by key
 *   — Publishing and discovering provider records (who has a given resource)
 *   — Content routing: find which nodes hold a specific piece of data
 *   — Automatic republishing before TTL expiry
 *   — Caching along lookup paths for hot-data acceleration
 *   — Namespace isolation between different data classes
 *   — Signed records: data cannot be forged or replaced without the original signer's key
 *
 * Protocol: Kademlia-derived with IPFS/libp2p Kad-DHT conventions extended
 * for sovereign operation (no external dependencies, no DHT bootstrap servers).
 *
 * Record Types:
 *   ValueRecord     — Arbitrary key→value with TTL and optional signature
 *   ProviderRecord  — Announce that this node provides a given CID/key
 *   RoutingRecord   — Announce a service endpoint at a logical address
 *   NameRecord      — Map a human-readable name to a node DID + address set
 */

import * as crypto from 'crypto';
import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import {
  NodeId,
  NodeIdentity,
  PeerRecord,
  KademliaTable,
  MeshNode,
  MeshMessage,
  createMessage,
  xorDistance,
  compareBuffers,
  KADEMLIA_K,
  KADEMLIA_ALPHA,
} from './meshNode';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

const DHT_VALUE_TTL_DEFAULT_MS = 86_400_000;    // 24 hours
const DHT_PROVIDER_TTL_MS = 43_200_000;         // 12 hours
const DHT_ROUTING_TTL_MS = 3_600_000;           // 1 hour
const DHT_NAME_TTL_MS = 604_800_000;            // 7 days
const DHT_REPLICATION_FACTOR = 20;              // Store on K closest nodes
const DHT_REPUBLISH_INTERVAL_MS = 3_600_000;    // Republish every hour
const DHT_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES = 10_000;
const DHT_MAX_VALUE_SIZE = 65_536;              // 64KB max per value
const DHT_LOOKUP_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
const DHT_CONCURRENT_LOOKUPS = 3;
const DHT_PROVIDER_FANOUT = 20;
const DHT_NAMESPACE_SEPARATOR = '/';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// KEY DERIVATION
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export type DHTKey = Buffer;  // 20-byte SHA-1 of content identifier

export enum DHTNamespace {
  VALUE = 'v',          // General key-value
  PROVIDER = 'p',       // Provider announcements
  ROUTING = 'r',        // Service routing
  NAME = 'n',           // Name resolution
  CAPABILITY = 'c',     // Capability discovery
  PUBSUB = 's',         // Pub/sub topic routing
}

export function deriveKey(namespace: DHTNamespace, identifier: string | Buffer): DHTKey {
  const ns = Buffer.from(namespace);
  const id = Buffer.isBuffer(identifier) ? identifier : Buffer.from(identifier, 'utf8');
  return crypto.createHash('sha1').update(ns).update(id).digest();
}

export function deriveContentKey(content: Buffer): DHTKey {
  return crypto.createHash('sha1').update(content).digest();
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// RECORD TYPES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface SignedRecord {
  publicKey: string;      // hex Ed25519 public key
  sequence: number;       // Monotonic counter, prevents replay of older records
  signature: string;      // hex signature over canonical record body
}

export interface ValueRecord {
  key: string;            // hex DHTKey
  namespace: DHTNamespace;
  value: Buffer;
  publishedBy: string;    // hex nodeId
  publishedAt: number;    // Unix ms
  expiresAt: number;      // Unix ms
  ttl: number;            // ms
  signed?: SignedRecord;  // Optional: cryptographic ownership proof
  version: number;        // For mutable records: increment to supersede
  immutable: boolean;     // If true, value = hash(value) — cannot be changed
}

export interface ProviderRecord {
  key: string;            // hex DHTKey of the resource being provided
  providerId: string;     // hex nodeId of the provider
  providerPublicKey: string;
  addresses: Array<{ type: string; host: string; port: number }>;
  capabilities: string[];
  publishedAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;
  metadata: Record<string, unknown>;
  signature: string;      // Provider's signature over record body
}

export interface RoutingRecord {
  serviceId: string;      // Logical service identifier
  endpoints: Array<{
    nodeId: string;
    addresses: Array<{ type: string; host: string; port: number }>;
    protocol: string;
    priority: number;
    weight: number;       // For load balancing
  }>;
  publishedAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;
  version: number;
}

export interface NameRecord {
  name: string;           // Human-readable name
  nodeId: string;         // Resolved node DID
  publicKey: string;
  addresses: Array<{ type: string; host: string; port: number }>;
  publishedAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;
  sequence: number;
  signature: string;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LOCAL STORAGE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class DHTLocalStore {
  private values: Map<string, ValueRecord> = new Map();
  private providers: Map<string, ProviderRecord[]> = new Map();
  private routes: Map<string, RoutingRecord> = new Map();
  private names: Map<string, NameRecord> = new Map();
  private readonly maxEntries: number;

  constructor(maxEntries: number = DHT_CACHE_MAX_ENTRIES) {
    this.maxEntries = maxEntries;
  }

  // ── VALUE STORE ────────────────────────────

  putValue(record: ValueRecord): 'stored' | 'superseded' | 'rejected' {
