    const existing = this.values.get(record.key);
    if (existing) {
      // Mutable record: only accept if sequence is higher
      if (!record.immutable && record.version <= existing.version) {
        return 'superseded';
      }
      // Immutable record: reject overwrites
      if (record.immutable) return 'rejected';
    }

    if (this.values.size >= this.maxEntries) {
      this.evictExpiredValues();
    }

    this.values.set(record.key, record);
    return 'stored';
  }

  getValue(key: string): ValueRecord | null {
    const record = this.values.get(key);
    if (!record) return null;
    if (Date.now() > record.expiresAt) {
      this.values.delete(key);
      return null;
    }
    return record;
  }

  hasValue(key: string): boolean {
    return this.getValue(key) !== null;
  }

  // ── PROVIDER STORE ─────────────────────────

  addProvider(record: ProviderRecord): void {
    const providers = this.providers.get(record.key) ?? [];
    const existingIdx = providers.findIndex(p => p.providerId === record.providerId);
    if (existingIdx >= 0) {
      providers[existingIdx] = record; // Update
    } else {
      providers.push(record);
      if (providers.length > DHT_PROVIDER_FANOUT) {
        // Evict oldest
        providers.sort((a, b) => b.publishedAt - a.publishedAt);
        providers.length = DHT_PROVIDER_FANOUT;
      }
    }
    this.providers.set(record.key, providers);
  }

  getProviders(key: string): ProviderRecord[] {
    const now = Date.now();
    const providers = this.providers.get(key) ?? [];
    const valid = providers.filter(p => p.expiresAt > now);
    if (valid.length !== providers.length) {
      this.providers.set(key, valid);
    }
    return valid;
  }

  removeProvider(key: string, providerId: string): void {
    const providers = this.providers.get(key) ?? [];
    const filtered = providers.filter(p => p.providerId !== providerId);
    this.providers.set(key, filtered);
  }

  // ── ROUTING STORE ──────────────────────────

  putRoute(record: RoutingRecord): void {
    const existing = this.routes.get(record.serviceId);
    if (existing && record.version <= existing.version) return;
    this.routes.set(record.serviceId, record);
  }

  getRoute(serviceId: string): RoutingRecord | null {
    const record = this.routes.get(serviceId);
    if (!record) return null;
    if (Date.now() > record.expiresAt) {
      this.routes.delete(serviceId);
      return null;
    }
    return record;
  }

  // ── NAME STORE ─────────────────────────────

  putName(record: NameRecord): boolean {
    const existing = this.names.get(record.name);
    if (existing && record.sequence <= existing.sequence) return false;
    this.names.set(record.name, record);
    return true;
  }

  getName(name: string): NameRecord | null {
    const record = this.names.get(name);
    if (!record) return null;
    if (Date.now() > record.expiresAt) {
      this.names.delete(name);
      return null;
    }
    return record;
  }

  // ── MAINTENANCE ────────────────────────────

  sweep(): SweepResult {
    const now = Date.now();
    let valuesEvicted = 0, providersEvicted = 0, routesEvicted = 0, namesEvicted = 0;

    for (const [key, record] of this.values) {
      if (now > record.expiresAt) { this.values.delete(key); valuesEvicted++; }
    }
    for (const [key, records] of this.providers) {
      const valid = records.filter(p => p.expiresAt > now);
      providersEvicted += records.length - valid.length;
      if (valid.length === 0) this.providers.delete(key);
      else this.providers.set(key, valid);
    }
    for (const [key, record] of this.routes) {
      if (now > record.expiresAt) { this.routes.delete(key); routesEvicted++; }
    }
    for (const [key, record] of this.names) {
      if (now > record.expiresAt) { this.names.delete(key); namesEvicted++; }
    }

    return { valuesEvicted, providersEvicted, routesEvicted, namesEvicted };
  }

  stats(): StoreStats {
    return {
      values: this.values.size,
      providers: Array.from(this.providers.values()).reduce((s, a) => s + a.length, 0),
      routes: this.routes.size,
      names: this.names.size,
    };
  }

  private evictExpiredValues(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [key, record] of this.values) {
      if (now > record.expiresAt) this.values.delete(key);
    }
    // If still full, evict oldest
    if (this.values.size >= this.maxEntries) {
      let oldestKey = '';
      let oldestTime = Infinity;
      for (const [key, record] of this.values) {
        if (record.publishedAt < oldestTime) {
          oldestTime = record.publishedAt;
          oldestKey = key;
        }
      }
      if (oldestKey) this.values.delete(oldestKey);
    }
  }

  allValues(): ValueRecord[] {
    return Array.from(this.values.values());
  }

  allProviderKeys(): string[] {
    return Array.from(this.providers.keys());
  }
}

export interface SweepResult {
  valuesEvicted: number;
  providersEvicted: number;
  routesEvicted: number;
  namesEvicted: number;
}

export interface StoreStats {
  values: number;
  providers: number;
  routes: number;
  names: number;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LOOKUP STATE MACHINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export type LookupState = 'running' | 'complete' | 'timeout' | 'failed';

export interface LookupContext {
  targetKey: DHTKey;
  namespace: DHTNamespace;
  queryType: 'VALUE' | 'PROVIDERS' | 'NODES';
  closest: Array<{ peer: PeerRecord; queried: boolean; responded: boolean; distance: Buffer }>;
  results: Array<ValueRecord | ProviderRecord | PeerRecord>;
  startedAt: number;
  state: LookupState;
  roundsCompleted: number;
}

export class DHTLookup {
  private readonly context: LookupContext;
  private readonly localStore: DHTLocalStore;
  private readonly kademlia: KademliaTable;

  constructor(
    targetKey: DHTKey,
    namespace: DHTNamespace,
    queryType: LookupContext['queryType'],
    localStore: DHTLocalStore,
    kademlia: KademliaTable,
    initialPeers: PeerRecord[],
  ) {
    const closest = initialPeers.map(p => ({
      peer: p,
      queried: false,
      responded: false,
      distance: xorDistance(p.nodeId, targetKey),
    }));
    closest.sort((a, b) => compareBuffers(a.distance, b.distance));

    this.context = {
      targetKey,
      namespace,
      queryType,
      closest: closest.slice(0, KADEMLIA_K),
      results: [],
      startedAt: Date.now(),
      state: 'running',
      roundsCompleted: 0,
    };
    this.localStore = localStore;
    this.kademlia = kademlia;
  }

  selectNextBatch(): PeerRecord[] {
    if (this.context.state !== 'running') return [];
    if (Date.now() - this.context.startedAt > DHT_LOOKUP_TIMEOUT_MS) {
      this.context.state = 'timeout';
      return [];
    }
    return this.context.closest
      .filter(e => !e.queried)
      .slice(0, KADEMLIA_ALPHA)
      .map(e => { e.queried = true; return e.peer; });
  }

  incorporate(fromPeer: PeerRecord, peers: PeerRecord[], values?: ValueRecord[], providers?: ProviderRecord[]): void {
    const entry = this.context.closest.find(e => e.peer.nodeId.equals(fromPeer.nodeId));
    if (entry) entry.responded = true;

    // Add discovered peers
    for (const peer of peers) {
      const alreadyTracked = this.context.closest.some(e => e.peer.nodeId.equals(peer.nodeId));
      if (!alreadyTracked) {
        this.context.closest.push({
          peer,
          queried: false,
          responded: false,
          distance: xorDistance(peer.nodeId, this.context.targetKey),
        });
      }
    }

    // Sort and trim
    this.context.closest.sort((a, b) => compareBuffers(a.distance, b.distance));
    if (this.context.closest.length > KADEMLIA_K * 2) {
      this.context.closest.length = KADEMLIA_K * 2;
    }

    // Collect results
    if (values) this.context.results.push(...values);
    if (providers) this.context.results.push(...providers);

    this.context.roundsCompleted++;

    // Convergence check: all K closest have been queried
    const kClosest = this.context.closest.slice(0, KADEMLIA_K);
    const allQueried = kClosest.every(e => e.queried || e.responded);
    if (allQueried && this.selectNextBatch().length === 0) {
      this.context.state = 'complete';
    }
  }

  isComplete(): boolean {
    return this.context.state !== 'running';
  }

  getResults(): Array<ValueRecord | ProviderRecord | PeerRecord> {
    return this.context.results;
  }

  getClosestPeers(): PeerRecord[] {
    return this.context.closest.slice(0, KADEMLIA_K).map(e => e.peer);
  }

  getState(): LookupState {
    return this.context.state;
  }

  duration(): number {
    return Date.now() - this.context.startedAt;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// REPUBLISH MANAGER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RepublishEntry {
  key: string;
  namespace: DHTNamespace;
  type: 'value' | 'provider';
  nextRepublish: number;
  interval: number;
  priority: number;
}

export class RepublishManager {
  private queue: RepublishEntry[] = [];

  schedule(entry: RepublishEntry): void {
    const existing = this.queue.findIndex(e => e.key === entry.key && e.namespace === entry.namespace);
    if (existing >= 0) {
      this.queue[existing] = entry;
    } else {
      this.queue.push(entry);
    }
  }

  unschedule(key: string, namespace: DHTNamespace): void {
    this.queue = this.queue.filter(e => !(e.key === key && e.namespace === namespace));
  }

  due(now: number = Date.now()): RepublishEntry[] {
    return this.queue.filter(e => e.nextRepublish <= now);
  }

  reschedule(key: string, namespace: DHTNamespace, now: number = Date.now()): void {
    const entry = this.queue.find(e => e.key === key && e.namespace === namespace);
    if (entry) entry.nextRepublish = now + entry.interval;
  }

  size(): number {
    return this.queue.length;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// SIGNATURE UTILITIES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

function signRecord(body: object, identity: NodeIdentity): SignedRecord {
  const canonical = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body, Object.keys(body).sort()));
  return {
    publicKey: identity.keypair.publicKey.toString('hex'),
    sequence: Date.now(),
    signature: identity.sign(canonical).toString('hex'),
  };
}

function verifyRecordSignature(body: object, signed: SignedRecord): boolean {
  try {
    const canonical = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body, Object.keys(body).sort()));
    const publicKey = Buffer.from(signed.publicKey, 'hex');
    const signature = Buffer.from(signed.signature, 'hex');
    const key = crypto.createPublicKey({
      key: Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('302a300506032b6570032100', 'hex'), publicKey]),
      format: 'der', type: 'spki',
    });
    return crypto.verify(null, canonical, key, signature);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function verifyProviderSignature(record: ProviderRecord): boolean {
  const body = {
    key: record.key,
    providerId: record.providerId,
    providerPublicKey: record.providerPublicKey,
    publishedAt: record.publishedAt,
    expiresAt: record.expiresAt,
  };
  const canonical = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body, Object.keys(body).sort()));
  try {
    const publicKey = Buffer.from(record.providerPublicKey, 'hex');
    const signature = Buffer.from(record.signature, 'hex');
    const key = crypto.createPublicKey({
      key: Buffer.concat([Buffer.from('302a300506032b6570032100', 'hex'), publicKey]),
      format: 'der', type: 'spki',
    });
    return crypto.verify(null, canonical, key, signature);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONTENT ROUTING — CID-style content addressing
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ContentDescriptor {
  cid: string;            // SHA-256 hex of content
  size: number;
  mimeType?: string;
  encoding?: string;
  chunks?: ContentChunk[];
}

export interface ContentChunk {
  index: number;
  cid: string;
  size: number;
  offset: number;
}

export class ContentAddressingEngine {
  static computeCID(content: Buffer): string {
    return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(content).digest('hex');
  }

  static computeChunkedCID(content: Buffer, chunkSize: number = 65_536): ContentDescriptor {
    const chunks: ContentChunk[] = [];
    let offset = 0;
    let chunkIdx = 0;

    while (offset < content.length) {
      const chunk = content.slice(offset, offset + chunkSize);
      const cid = ContentAddressingEngine.computeCID(chunk);
      chunks.push({ index: chunkIdx++, cid, size: chunk.length, offset });
      offset += chunk.length;
    }

    return {
      cid: ContentAddressingEngine.computeCID(content),
      size: content.length,
      chunks,
    };
  }

  static verifyIntegrity(content: Buffer, expectedCID: string): boolean {
    return ContentAddressingEngine.computeCID(content) === expectedCID;
  }

  static deriveKeyFromCID(cid: string): DHTKey {
    return crypto.createHash('sha1').update(Buffer.from(cid, 'hex')).digest();
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// PUBSUB TOPIC ROUTING
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface PubSubTopic {
  topicId: string;
  subscribers: string[];   // nodeIds
  publishers: string[];
  lastActivity: number;
  messageCount: number;
}

export class PubSubRouter extends EventEmitter {
  private subscriptions: Map<string, Set<string>> = new Map(); // topicId -> nodeIds
  private localSubscriptions: Set<string> = new Set();
  private messageHistory: Map<string, number[]> = new Map(); // topicId -> timestamps

  subscribe(topicId: string, nodeId: string): void {
    if (!this.subscriptions.has(topicId)) {
      this.subscriptions.set(topicId, new Set());
    }
    this.subscriptions.get(topicId)!.add(nodeId);
  }

  unsubscribe(topicId: string, nodeId: string): void {
    this.subscriptions.get(topicId)?.delete(nodeId);
  }

  subscribeLocal(topicId: string): void {
    this.localSubscriptions.add(topicId);
  }

  unsubscribeLocal(topicId: string): void {
    this.localSubscriptions.delete(topicId);
  }

  isSubscribed(topicId: string): boolean {
    return this.localSubscriptions.has(topicId);
  }

  getSubscribers(topicId: string): string[] {
    return Array.from(this.subscriptions.get(topicId) ?? []);
  }

  recordMessage(topicId: string): void {
    const history = this.messageHistory.get(topicId) ?? [];
    history.push(Date.now());
    // Keep last 1000 timestamps
    if (history.length > 1000) history.shift();
    this.messageHistory.set(topicId, history);
  }

  messageRate(topicId: string, windowMs: number = 60_000): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    const history = this.messageHistory.get(topicId) ?? [];
    return history.filter(ts => now - ts < windowMs).length;
  }

  allTopics(): PubSubTopic[] {
    return Array.from(this.subscriptions.entries()).map(([topicId, subs]) => ({
      topicId,
      subscribers: Array.from(subs),
      publishers: [],
      lastActivity: (this.messageHistory.get(topicId) ?? [0]).at(-1) ?? 0,
      messageCount: (this.messageHistory.get(topicId) ?? []).length,
    }));
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// DHT NODE DISCOVERY ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface DiscoveryConfig {
  identity: NodeIdentity;
  node: MeshNode;
  kademlia: KademliaTable;
  maxStoreEntries?: number;
  republishIntervalMs?: number;
  sweepIntervalMs?: number;
}

export interface PutOptions {
  ttl?: number;
  signed?: boolean;
  immutable?: boolean;
  namespace?: DHTNamespace;
}

export interface GetResult {
  found: boolean;
  record?: ValueRecord;
  fromPeers: number;
  lookupMs: number;
  cached: boolean;
}

export interface ProviderQueryResult {
  providers: ProviderRecord[];
  lookupMs: number;
  queried: number;
}

export interface NameResolutionResult {
  resolved: boolean;
  record?: NameRecord;
  lookupMs: number;
}

export interface DHTStats {
  localValues: number;
  localProviders: number;
  localRoutes: number;
  localNames: number;
  pendingLookups: number;
  republishQueue: number;
  totalPuts: number;
  totalGets: number;
  totalProvides: number;
  cacheHits: number;
  cacheMisses: number;
}

export class NodeDiscoveryEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly identity: NodeIdentity;
  private readonly node: MeshNode;
  private readonly kademlia: KademliaTable;
  private readonly localStore: DHTLocalStore;
  private readonly republisher: RepublishManager;
  private readonly pubsub: PubSubRouter;
  private readonly activeLookups: Map<string, DHTLookup> = new Map();

  private republishTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private sweepTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  private stats: DHTStats = {
    localValues: 0, localProviders: 0, localRoutes: 0, localNames: 0,
    pendingLookups: 0, republishQueue: 0,
    totalPuts: 0, totalGets: 0, totalProvides: 0,
    cacheHits: 0, cacheMisses: 0,
  };

  constructor(config: DiscoveryConfig) {
    super();
    this.identity = config.identity;
    this.node = config.node;
    this.kademlia = config.kademlia;
    this.localStore = new DHTLocalStore(config.maxStoreEntries);
    this.republisher = new RepublishManager();
    this.pubsub = new PubSubRouter();

    this.setupMessageHandlers();
    this.startBackgroundTasks(
      config.republishIntervalMs ?? DHT_REPUBLISH_INTERVAL_MS,
      config.sweepIntervalMs ?? 300_000,
    );
  }

  // ── PUBLIC API: VALUE ──────────────────────

  async put(
    key: string | Buffer,
    value: Buffer,
    options: PutOptions = {},
  ): Promise<{ success: boolean; storedOn: number }> {
    if (value.length > DHT_MAX_VALUE_SIZE) {
      throw new Error(`Value size ${value.length} exceeds DHT maximum ${DHT_MAX_VALUE_SIZE}`);
    }

    const namespace = options.namespace ?? DHTNamespace.VALUE;
    const dhtKey = Buffer.isBuffer(key) ? key : deriveKey(namespace, key);
    const keyHex = dhtKey.toString('hex');
    const now = Date.now();
    const ttl = options.ttl ?? DHT_VALUE_TTL_DEFAULT_MS;

    const record: ValueRecord = {
      key: keyHex,
      namespace,
      value,
      publishedBy: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      publishedAt: now,
      expiresAt: now + ttl,
      ttl,
      version: 1,
      immutable: options.immutable ?? false,
    };

    if (options.signed) {
      record.signed = signRecord(
        { key: keyHex, namespace, publishedAt: now, version: 1 },
        this.identity,
      );
    }

    // Store locally
    this.localStore.putValue(record);
    this.stats.totalPuts++;

    // Find K closest peers and store on them
    const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, DHT_REPLICATION_FACTOR);
    let storedOn = 1; // ourselves

    const stores = closest.map(async peer => {
      const msg = createMessage(
        'STORE',
        this.identity,
        Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ type: 'value', record: { ...record, value: record.value.toString('base64') } })),
        peer.nodeId.toString('hex'),
        3,
      );
      await this.node.send(peer.nodeId.toString('hex'), 'STORE', msg.payload);
      storedOn++;
    });

    await Promise.allSettled(stores);

    // Schedule republish
    this.republisher.schedule({
      key: keyHex,
      namespace,
      type: 'value',
      nextRepublish: now + DHT_REPUBLISH_INTERVAL_MS,
      interval: DHT_REPUBLISH_INTERVAL_MS,
      priority: 1,
    });

    return { success: true, storedOn };
  }

  async get(key: string | Buffer, namespace: DHTNamespace = DHTNamespace.VALUE): Promise<GetResult> {
    const dhtKey = Buffer.isBuffer(key) ? key : deriveKey(namespace, key);
    const keyHex = dhtKey.toString('hex');
    const start = Date.now();

    this.stats.totalGets++;

    // Check local store first
    const local = this.localStore.getValue(keyHex);
    if (local) {
      this.stats.cacheHits++;
      return { found: true, record: local, fromPeers: 0, lookupMs: Date.now() - start, cached: true };
    }
    this.stats.cacheMisses++;

    // Kademlia lookup
    const initialPeers = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, KADEMLIA_K);
    const lookup = new DHTLookup(dhtKey, namespace, 'VALUE', this.localStore, this.kademlia, initialPeers);
    const lookupId = keyHex;
    this.activeLookups.set(lookupId, lookup);
    this.stats.pendingLookups++;

    try {
      await this.executeLookup(lookup, 'FIND_VALUE', keyHex);
    } finally {
      this.activeLookups.delete(lookupId);
      this.stats.pendingLookups--;
    }

    const valueResults = lookup.getResults().filter((r): r is ValueRecord => 'value' in r);
    if (valueResults.length === 0) {
      return { found: false, fromPeers: 0, lookupMs: Date.now() - start, cached: false };
    }

    // Take the freshest valid result
    const best = valueResults.sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version)[0];

    // Verify signature if signed
    if (best.signed && !verifyRecordSignature(
      { key: best.key, namespace: best.namespace, publishedAt: best.publishedAt, version: best.version },
      best.signed,
    )) {
      return { found: false, fromPeers: valueResults.length, lookupMs: Date.now() - start, cached: false };
    }

    // Cache locally
    this.localStore.putValue(best);

    return {
      found: true,
      record: best,
      fromPeers: valueResults.length,
      lookupMs: Date.now() - start,
      cached: false,
    };
  }

  // ── PUBLIC API: PROVIDERS ──────────────────

  async provide(resourceKey: string | Buffer, metadata: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<number> {
    const dhtKey = Buffer.isBuffer(resourceKey) ? resourceKey : deriveKey(DHTNamespace.PROVIDER, resourceKey);
    const keyHex = dhtKey.toString('hex');
    const now = Date.now();

    const addresses = this.node.getPeers().flatMap(p => p.addresses).slice(0, 5);

    const bodyForSig = {
      key: keyHex,
      providerId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      providerPublicKey: this.identity.keypair.publicKey.toString('hex'),
      publishedAt: now,
      expiresAt: now + DHT_PROVIDER_TTL_MS,
    };

    const signature = this.identity.sign(
      Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(bodyForSig, Object.keys(bodyForSig).sort()))
    ).toString('hex');

    const record: ProviderRecord = {
      ...bodyForSig,
      addresses: addresses.map(a => ({ type: a.type, host: a.host, port: a.port })),
      capabilities: this.identity.getCapabilities(),
      metadata,
      signature,
    };

    // Store locally
    this.localStore.addProvider(record);
    this.stats.totalProvides++;

    // Store on K closest nodes
    const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, DHT_REPLICATION_FACTOR);
    let storedOn = 1;

    const stores = closest.map(async peer => {
      const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ type: 'provider', record }));
      await this.node.send(peer.nodeId.toString('hex'), 'STORE', payload);
      storedOn++;
    });

    await Promise.allSettled(stores);

    this.republisher.schedule({
      key: keyHex,
      namespace: DHTNamespace.PROVIDER,
      type: 'provider',
      nextRepublish: now + DHT_REPUBLISH_INTERVAL_MS / 2,
      interval: DHT_REPUBLISH_INTERVAL_MS / 2,
      priority: 2,
    });

    return storedOn;
  }

  async findProviders(resourceKey: string | Buffer): Promise<ProviderQueryResult> {
    const dhtKey = Buffer.isBuffer(resourceKey) ? resourceKey : deriveKey(DHTNamespace.PROVIDER, resourceKey);
    const keyHex = dhtKey.toString('hex');
    const start = Date.now();

    // Check local store
    const localProviders = this.localStore.getProviders(keyHex);
    if (localProviders.length >= DHT_PROVIDER_FANOUT) {
      return { providers: localProviders, lookupMs: Date.now() - start, queried: 0 };
    }

    // Network lookup
    const initialPeers = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, KADEMLIA_K);
    const lookup = new DHTLookup(dhtKey, DHTNamespace.PROVIDER, 'PROVIDERS', this.localStore, this.kademlia, initialPeers);
    const lookupId = `provider:${keyHex}`;
    this.activeLookups.set(lookupId, lookup);
    this.stats.pendingLookups++;

    try {
      await this.executeLookup(lookup, 'FIND_VALUE', keyHex);
    } finally {
      this.activeLookups.delete(lookupId);
      this.stats.pendingLookups--;
    }

    const networkProviders = lookup.getResults().filter((r): r is ProviderRecord => 'providerId' in r);
    const allProviders = [...localProviders, ...networkProviders];

    // Deduplicate by providerId
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    const unique = allProviders.filter(p => {
      if (seen.has(p.providerId)) return false;
      seen.add(p.providerId);
      return true;
    });

    // Verify signatures
    const verified = unique.filter(verifyProviderSignature);

    // Cache valid providers
    for (const p of verified) {
      this.localStore.addProvider(p);
    }

    return {
      providers: verified,
      lookupMs: Date.now() - start,
      queried: lookup.getClosestPeers().length,
    };
  }

  // ── PUBLIC API: NAME RESOLUTION ────────────

  async publishName(name: string, nodeId?: string, addresses?: Array<{ type: string; host: string; port: number }>): Promise<boolean> {
    const resolvedNodeId = nodeId ?? this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex');
    const resolvedAddresses = addresses ?? this.node.getPeers().flatMap(p => p.addresses).slice(0, 5).map(a => ({ type: a.type, host: a.host, port: a.port }));
    const now = Date.now();

    const existing = this.localStore.getName(name);
    const sequence = (existing?.sequence ?? 0) + 1;

    const bodyForSig = { name, nodeId: resolvedNodeId, sequence, publishedAt: now };
    const signature = this.identity.sign(
      Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(bodyForSig, Object.keys(bodyForSig).sort()))
    ).toString('hex');

    const record: NameRecord = {
      name,
      nodeId: resolvedNodeId,
      publicKey: this.identity.keypair.publicKey.toString('hex'),
      addresses: resolvedAddresses,
      publishedAt: now,
      expiresAt: now + DHT_NAME_TTL_MS,
      sequence,
      signature,
    };

    this.localStore.putName(record);

    // Store on K closest to name's DHT key
    const dhtKey = deriveKey(DHTNamespace.NAME, name);
    const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, DHT_REPLICATION_FACTOR);

    const stores = closest.map(peer =>
      this.node.send(peer.nodeId.toString('hex'), 'STORE',
        Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ type: 'name', record })))
    );
    await Promise.allSettled(stores);
    return true;
  }

  async resolveName(name: string): Promise<NameResolutionResult> {
    const start = Date.now();

    // Local lookup
    const local = this.localStore.getName(name);
    if (local) return { resolved: true, record: local, lookupMs: Date.now() - start };

    // Network lookup
    const dhtKey = deriveKey(DHTNamespace.NAME, name);
    const initialPeers = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, KADEMLIA_K);
    const lookup = new DHTLookup(dhtKey, DHTNamespace.NAME, 'VALUE', this.localStore, this.kademlia, initialPeers);

    await this.executeLookup(lookup, 'FIND_VALUE', `name:${name}`);

    const results = lookup.getResults().filter((r): r is ValueRecord => 'value' in r);
    if (results.length === 0) return { resolved: false, lookupMs: Date.now() - start };

    try {
      const nameRecord: NameRecord = JSON.parse(results[0].value.toString('utf8'));
      this.localStore.putName(nameRecord);
      return { resolved: true, record: nameRecord, lookupMs: Date.now() - start };
    } catch {
      return { resolved: false, lookupMs: Date.now() - start };
    }
  }

  // ── PUBLIC API: ROUTING ────────────────────

  async announceService(serviceId: string, protocol: string, priority: number = 1, weight: number = 1): Promise<void> {
    const addresses = this.node.getPeers().flatMap(p => p.addresses).slice(0, 5);
    const now = Date.now();

    const existing = this.localStore.getRoute(serviceId);
    const version = (existing?.version ?? 0) + 1;

    const endpoints = [{
      nodeId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      addresses: addresses.map(a => ({ type: a.type, host: a.host, port: a.port })),
      protocol,
      priority,
      weight,
    }];

    // Merge with existing endpoints if any
    if (existing) {
      for (const ep of existing.endpoints) {
        if (ep.nodeId !== this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex')) {
          endpoints.push(ep);
        }
      }
    }

    const record: RoutingRecord = {
      serviceId,
      endpoints,
      publishedAt: now,
      expiresAt: now + DHT_ROUTING_TTL_MS,
      version,
    };

    this.localStore.putRoute(record);

    const dhtKey = deriveKey(DHTNamespace.ROUTING, serviceId);
    const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, DHT_REPLICATION_FACTOR);
    const stores = closest.map(peer =>
      this.node.send(peer.nodeId.toString('hex'), 'STORE',
        Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ type: 'route', record })))
    );
    await Promise.allSettled(stores);
  }

  async findService(serviceId: string): Promise<RoutingRecord | null> {
    // Local first
    const local = this.localStore.getRoute(serviceId);
    if (local) return local;

    // Network
    const dhtKey = deriveKey(DHTNamespace.ROUTING, serviceId);
    const initialPeers = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, KADEMLIA_K);
    const lookup = new DHTLookup(dhtKey, DHTNamespace.ROUTING, 'VALUE', this.localStore, this.kademlia, initialPeers);
    await this.executeLookup(lookup, 'FIND_VALUE', `service:${serviceId}`);

    const results = lookup.getResults().filter((r): r is ValueRecord => 'value' in r);
    if (results.length === 0) return null;

    try {
      const route: RoutingRecord = JSON.parse(results[0].value.toString('utf8'));
      this.localStore.putRoute(route);
      return route;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  // ── PUBSUB ─────────────────────────────────

  async subscribeTopic(topicId: string): Promise<void> {
    this.pubsub.subscribeLocal(topicId);
    // Announce subscription on DHT
    await this.provide(`topic:${topicId}`, { subscriber: true });
    this.emit('pubsub:subscribed', { topicId });
  }

  async unsubscribeTopic(topicId: string): Promise<void> {
    this.pubsub.unsubscribeLocal(topicId);
    this.emit('pubsub:unsubscribed', { topicId });
  }

  async publishToTopic(topicId: string, message: Buffer): Promise<number> {
    // Find subscribers for this topic
    const result = await this.findProviders(`topic:${topicId}`);
    let delivered = 0;

    const sends = result.providers.map(async provider => {
      const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({
        topicId,
        message: message.toString('base64'),
        publishedAt: Date.now(),
        publisherId: this.identity.nodeId.toString('hex'),
      }));
      const success = await this.node.send(provider.providerId, 'DATA', payload);
      if (success) delivered++;
    });

    await Promise.allSettled(sends);
    this.pubsub.recordMessage(topicId);
    return delivered;
  }

  // ── CONTENT ROUTING ────────────────────────

  async announceContent(content: Buffer): Promise<ContentDescriptor> {
    const descriptor = ContentAddressingEngine.computeChunkedCID(content);
    await this.provide(descriptor.cid, { size: descriptor.size, chunks: descriptor.chunks?.length });
    return descriptor;
  }

  async findContent(cid: string): Promise<ProviderQueryResult> {
    return this.findProviders(cid);
  }

  // ── LOOKUP EXECUTION ───────────────────────

  private async executeLookup(
    lookup: DHTLookup,
    queryType: string,
    queryKey: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
      // Force completion
    }, DHT_LOOKUP_TIMEOUT_MS);

    try {
      while (!lookup.isComplete()) {
        const batch = lookup.selectNextBatch();
        if (batch.length === 0) break;

        const queries = batch.map(async peer => {
          try {
            const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ queryType, key: queryKey }));
            const success = await this.node.send(peer.nodeId.toString('hex'), 'FIND_VALUE', payload);

            if (success) {
              // Results come back through message handler
              await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));
            }

            lookup.incorporate(peer, this.kademlia.findClosest(lookup['context'].targetKey, KADEMLIA_K));
          } catch {
            lookup.incorporate(peer, []);
          }
        });

        await Promise.allSettled(queries);
      }
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeout);
    }
  }

  // ── MESSAGE HANDLING ───────────────────────

  private setupMessageHandlers(): void {
    this.node.on('message', (envelope: any) => {
      const { message } = envelope;
      if (message.type === 'STORE') {
        this.handleStore(message);
      } else if (message.type === 'FIND_VALUE') {
        this.handleFindValue(message, envelope);
      } else if (message.type === 'DATA') {
        this.handleData(message);
      }
    });
  }

  private handleStore(msg: MeshMessage): void {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(msg.payload.toString('utf8'));

      switch (data.type) {
        case 'value': {
          const record: ValueRecord = {
            ...data.record,
            value: Buffer.from(data.record.value, 'base64'),
          };
          // Verify signature if present
          if (record.signed && !verifyRecordSignature(
            { key: record.key, namespace: record.namespace, publishedAt: record.publishedAt, version: record.version },
            record.signed,
          )) {
            this.emit('security:invalid_record_signature', { key: record.key });
            return;
          }
          const result = this.localStore.putValue(record);
          this.emit('store:value', { key: record.key, result });
          break;
        }
        case 'provider': {
          const record: ProviderRecord = data.record;
          if (!verifyProviderSignature(record)) {
            this.emit('security:invalid_provider_signature', { providerId: record.providerId });
            return;
          }
          this.localStore.addProvider(record);
          this.emit('store:provider', { key: record.key, providerId: record.providerId });
          break;
        }
        case 'route': {
          const record: RoutingRecord = data.record;
          this.localStore.putRoute(record);
          this.emit('store:route', { serviceId: record.serviceId });
          break;
        }
        case 'name': {
          const record: NameRecord = data.record;
          this.localStore.putName(record);
          this.emit('store:name', { name: record.name });
          break;
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      this.emit('error', { type: 'store_parse_error', err });
    }
  }

  private handleFindValue(msg: MeshMessage, envelope: any): void {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(msg.payload.toString('utf8'));
      const keyHex = data.key as string;
      let responsePayload: object = { found: false, closestPeers: [] };

      // Look up value
      const value = this.localStore.getValue(keyHex);
      if (value) {
        responsePayload = {
          found: true,
          type: 'value',
          record: { ...value, value: value.value.toString('base64') },
        };
      } else {
        // Return closest peers for the key
        const dhtKey = Buffer.from(keyHex, 'hex');
        const closest = this.kademlia.findClosest(dhtKey, KADEMLIA_K);
        responsePayload = {
          found: false,
          closestPeers: closest.map(p => ({
            nodeId: p.nodeId.toString('hex'),
            addresses: p.addresses,
            capabilities: p.capabilities,
          })),
        };
      }

      // Also check providers
      const providers = this.localStore.getProviders(keyHex);
      if (providers.length > 0) {
        (responsePayload as any).providers = providers;
      }

      this.node.send(msg.senderId, 'FOUND_VALUE', Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(responsePayload)));
    } catch {}
  }

  private handleData(msg: MeshMessage): void {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(msg.payload.toString('utf8'));
      if (data.topicId && this.pubsub.isSubscribed(data.topicId)) {
        const message = Buffer.from(data.message, 'base64');
        this.pubsub.recordMessage(data.topicId);
        this.emit('pubsub:message', {
          topicId: data.topicId,
          message,
          publisherId: data.publisherId,
          publishedAt: data.publishedAt,
        });
      }
    } catch {}
  }

  // ── BACKGROUND TASKS ───────────────────────

  private startBackgroundTasks(republishIntervalMs: number, sweepIntervalMs: number): void {
    this.republishTimer = setInterval(() => this.republishDue(), republishIntervalMs);
    this.sweepTimer = setInterval(() => this.runSweep(), sweepIntervalMs);
  }

  private async republishDue(): Promise<void> {
    const due = this.republisher.due();
    for (const entry of due) {
      if (entry.type === 'value') {
        const record = this.localStore.getValue(entry.key);
        if (record) {
          await this.put(Buffer.from(entry.key, 'hex'), record.value, {
            ttl: record.ttl,
            signed: !!record.signed,
            immutable: record.immutable,
            namespace: entry.namespace,
          });
          this.republisher.reschedule(entry.key, entry.namespace);
        } else {
          this.republisher.unschedule(entry.key, entry.namespace);
        }
      } else if (entry.type === 'provider') {
        await this.provide(Buffer.from(entry.key, 'hex'));
        this.republisher.reschedule(entry.key, entry.namespace);
      }
    }
    this.stats.republishQueue = this.republisher.size();
  }

  private runSweep(): void {
    const result = this.localStore.sweep();
    const s = this.localStore.stats();
    this.stats.localValues = s.values;
    this.stats.localProviders = s.providers;
    this.stats.localRoutes = s.routes;
    this.stats.localNames = s.names;
    this.emit('sweep', result);
  }

  // ── STATS ──────────────────────────────────

  getStats(): DHTStats {
    const s = this.localStore.stats();
    return {
      ...this.stats,
      localValues: s.values,
      localProviders: s.providers,
      localRoutes: s.routes,
      localNames: s.names,
      pendingLookups: this.activeLookups.size,
      republishQueue: this.republisher.size(),
    };
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.republishTimer) clearInterval(this.republishTimer);
    if (this.sweepTimer) clearInterval(this.sweepTimer);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// BOOTSTRAP SEED LIST MANAGER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface SeedNode {
  nodeId: string;
  addresses: Array<{ type: string; host: string; port: number }>;
  publicKey: string;
  network: string;
  lastVerified: number;
  trustScore: number;       // 0–100
}

export class SeedListManager {
  private seeds: Map<string, SeedNode> = new Map();
  private readonly networkId: string;

  constructor(networkId: string = 'ube-main') {
    this.networkId = networkId;
  }

  add(seed: SeedNode): void {
    if (seed.network !== this.networkId) {
      throw new Error(`Seed network mismatch: expected ${this.networkId}, got ${seed.network}`);
    }
    this.seeds.set(seed.nodeId, seed);
  }

  remove(nodeId: string): void {
    this.seeds.delete(nodeId);
  }

  get(nodeId: string): SeedNode | undefined {
    return this.seeds.get(nodeId);
  }

  all(): SeedNode[] {
    return Array.from(this.seeds.values());
  }

  byTrust(minScore: number = 50): SeedNode[] {
    return this.all().filter(s => s.trustScore >= minScore);
  }

  updateTrust(nodeId: string, delta: number): void {
    const seed = this.seeds.get(nodeId);
    if (seed) {
      seed.trustScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, seed.trustScore + delta));
    }
  }

  exportSeedList(): Buffer {
    return Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(this.all()));
  }

  static importSeedList(data: Buffer, networkId: string): SeedListManager {
    const manager = new SeedListManager(networkId);
    const seeds: SeedNode[] = JSON.parse(data.toString('utf8'));
    for (const seed of seeds) {
      if (seed.network === networkId) manager.add(seed);
    }
    return manager;
  }
}

  deriveKey,
  deriveContentKey,
  signRecord,
  verifyRecordSignature,
  verifyProviderSignature,
};

export default NodeDiscoveryEngine;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — File 13: opticalMesh.ts
 * Free Space Optical (FSO) Communication Layer
 *
 * Provides zero-RF optical communication via IR LED arrays, laser diodes,
 * and retroreflectors for covert, unjammable node-to-node data transfer.
 * Operates when all radio channels are jammed, monitored, or unavailable.
 *
 * Capabilities:
 *  - Point-to-point FSO link management (acquisition, pointing, tracking)
 *  - Atmospheric turbulence compensation via aperture averaging
 *  - Scintillation mitigation with diversity combining
 *  - Full link budget calculation (range, aperture, attenuation)
 *  - Automatic fallback: laser → LED → retroreflector → diffuse
 *  - Reed-Solomon FEC tuned for optical burst errors
 *  - Background light rejection model
 *  - Beam divergence vs range optimizer
 *  - Multi-hop relay for NLOS scenarios
 *  - Integration with airGapBridge.ts optical channel
 *
 * @module @kingube/sdk/phase11/opticalMesh
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import { createHash, randomBytes } from 'crypto';

// ─── Constants ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const SPEED_OF_LIGHT = 2.998e8; // m/s
const PLANCK = 6.626e-34;       // J·s
const BOLTZMANN = 1.381e-23;    // J/K
const ELECTRON_CHARGE = 1.602e-19; // C

// Wavelength bands (nm)
const WAVELENGTH = {
  NEAR_IR_850: 850e-9,
  NEAR_IR_1064: 1064e-9,
  TELECOM_1310: 1310e-9,
  TELECOM_1550: 1550e-9,
  VISIBLE_RED: 660e-9,
  VISIBLE_GREEN: 532e-9,
  FAR_IR_10600: 10600e-9,
} as const;

// Atmospheric attenuation (dB/km) for different visibility conditions
const ATMO_ATTENUATION: Record<string, Record<string, number>> = {
  CLEAR: { '850nm': 0.4, '1064nm': 0.3, '1550nm': 0.2 },
  LIGHT_HAZE: { '850nm': 1.2, '1064nm': 0.9, '1550nm': 0.6 },
  MODERATE_HAZE: { '850nm': 3.5, '1064nm': 2.8, '1550nm': 2.0 },
  HEAVY_HAZE: { '850nm': 8.0, '1064nm': 6.5, '1550nm': 5.0 },
  LIGHT_FOG: { '850nm': 15.0, '1064nm': 13.0, '1550nm': 10.0 },
  DENSE_FOG: { '850nm': 50.0, '1064nm': 45.0, '1550nm': 40.0 },
  RAIN_LIGHT: { '850nm': 1.8, '1064nm': 1.5, '1550nm': 1.3 },
  RAIN_HEAVY: { '850nm': 12.0, '1064nm': 10.0, '1550nm': 9.0 },
};

// RS(255,k) parameters for optical burst error correction
const RS_PARAMS = {
  LIGHT_CHANNEL: { n: 255, k: 223, t: 16 },   // t=16 symbol errors
  MODERATE_CHANNEL: { n: 255, k: 191, t: 32 }, // t=32 symbol errors
  SEVERE_CHANNEL: { n: 255, k: 127, t: 64 },   // t=64 symbol errors
};

// ─── Enumerations ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export enum OpticalMode {
  LASER_COLLIMATED = 'LASER_COLLIMATED',      // Highest range, narrowest beam
  LASER_SEMI_WIDE = 'LASER_SEMI_WIDE',        // Balanced beam
  LED_DIRECTIONAL = 'LED_DIRECTIONAL',         // LED array, directional
  LED_DIFFUSE = 'LED_DIFFUSE',                 // Broadcast mode, short range
  RETROREFLECTOR = 'RETROREFLECTOR',           // Passive, interrogator-only
  HYBRID = 'HYBRID',                           // Adaptive selection
}

export enum LinkState {
  IDLE = 'IDLE',
  ACQUIRING = 'ACQUIRING',
  TRACKING = 'TRACKING',
  DATA_TRANSFER = 'DATA_TRANSFER',
  DEGRADED = 'DEGRADED',
  FAILED = 'FAILED',
  FALLBACK = 'FALLBACK',
}

export enum AtmosphericCondition {
  CLEAR = 'CLEAR',
  LIGHT_HAZE = 'LIGHT_HAZE',
  MODERATE_HAZE = 'MODERATE_HAZE',
  HEAVY_HAZE = 'HEAVY_HAZE',
  LIGHT_FOG = 'LIGHT_FOG',
  DENSE_FOG = 'DENSE_FOG',
  RAIN_LIGHT = 'RAIN_LIGHT',
  RAIN_HEAVY = 'RAIN_HEAVY',
}

export enum FallbackLevel {
  LASER = 0,
  LED_DIRECTIONAL = 1,
  LED_DIFFUSE = 2,
  RETROREFLECTOR = 3,
  NONE = 4,
}

// ─── Atmospheric Model ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class AtmosphericModel {
  /**
   * Kim model for visibility-based attenuation (Beer-Lambert)
   * α(λ) = (3.91/V) * (λ/550nm)^(-q) dB/km
   */
  static attenuationDbKm(
    wavelengthNm: number,
    condition: AtmosphericCondition
  ): number {
    const visibilityKm: Record<AtmosphericCondition, number> = {
      [AtmosphericCondition.CLEAR]: 23,
      [AtmosphericCondition.LIGHT_HAZE]: 10,
      [AtmosphericCondition.MODERATE_HAZE]: 4,
      [AtmosphericCondition.HEAVY_HAZE]: 2,
      [AtmosphericCondition.LIGHT_FOG]: 0.8,
      [AtmosphericCondition.DENSE_FOG]: 0.2,
      [AtmosphericCondition.RAIN_LIGHT]: 8,
      [AtmosphericCondition.RAIN_HEAVY]: 1.5,
    };

    const V = visibilityKm[condition];
    const lambdaNm = wavelengthNm;

    // Kim q-factor selection
    let q: number;
    if (V > 50) q = 1.6;
    else if (V > 6) q = 1.3;
    else if (V > 1) q = 0.585 * Math.pow(V, 1 / 3);
    else q = 0.16 * V + 0.34;

    const alpha = (3.91 / V) * Math.pow(lambdaNm / 550, -q);
    return alpha; // dB/km
  }

  /**
   * Rytov variance (weak turbulence regime)
   * σ²_R = 1.23 * Cn² * k^(7/6) * L^(11/6)
   */
  static rytovVariance(cn2: number, wavelengthM: number, rangeM: number): number {
    const k = (2 * Math.PI) / wavelengthM;
    return 1.23 * cn2 * Math.pow(k, 7 / 6) * Math.pow(rangeM, 11 / 6);
  }

  /**
   * Fried coherence parameter r0 (atmospheric coherence length)
   */
  static friedParameter(cn2: number, wavelengthM: number, rangeM: number): number {
    const k = (2 * Math.PI) / wavelengthM;
    return Math.pow(0.423 * k * k * cn2 * rangeM, -3 / 5);
  }

  /**
   * Aperture averaging factor A
   * Reduces scintillation when aperture >> coherence cell
   */
  static apertureAveragingFactor(
    apertureM: number,
    rytovVar: number,
    wavelengthM: number,
    rangeM: number
  ): number {
    const l0 = Math.sqrt(wavelengthM * rangeM); // Fresnel zone size
    const ratio = apertureM / l0;
    // Andrews & Phillips approximation
    const A = Math.pow(1 + 1.063 * Math.pow(ratio, 7 / 6), -1);
    return A;
  }

  /**
   * Scintillation index after aperture averaging
   */
  static scintillationIndex(
    rytovVar: number,
    apertureAveragingFactor: number
  ): number {
    // Weak turbulence: σ²_I ≈ σ²_R
    // Moderate/strong: use modified Rytov theory
    if (rytovVar < 0.3) {
      return rytovVar * apertureAveragingFactor;
    } else {
      // Gamma-Gamma distribution parameters
      const alpha = Math.exp(0.49 * rytovVar / Math.pow(1 + 1.11 * Math.pow(rytovVar, 6 / 5), 7 / 6)) - 1;
      const beta = Math.exp(0.51 * rytovVar / Math.pow(1 + 0.69 * Math.pow(rytovVar, 6 / 5), 5 / 6)) - 1;
      return (1 / alpha + 1 / beta + 1 / (alpha * beta)) * apertureAveragingFactor;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Diversity gain from N independent apertures
   */
  static diversityGain(numApertures: number, scintillationIndex: number): number {
    // MRC combining reduces effective scintillation
    return 1 / (1 + scintillationIndex / numApertures);
  }

  /**
   * Typical Cn² value for given conditions
   */
  static cn2ForCondition(condition: AtmosphericCondition): number {
    const cn2Map: Record<AtmosphericCondition, number> = {
      [AtmosphericCondition.CLEAR]: 5e-15,
      [AtmosphericCondition.LIGHT_HAZE]: 2e-14,
      [AtmosphericCondition.MODERATE_HAZE]: 5e-14,
      [AtmosphericCondition.HEAVY_HAZE]: 1e-13,
      [AtmosphericCondition.LIGHT_FOG]: 1e-13,
      [AtmosphericCondition.DENSE_FOG]: 5e-13,
      [AtmosphericCondition.RAIN_LIGHT]: 1e-14,
      [AtmosphericCondition.RAIN_HEAVY]: 3e-14,
    };
    return cn2Map[condition];
  }
}

// ─── Link Budget Calculator ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

class LinkBudgetCalculator {
  static calculate(
    hw: OpticalHardware,
    rangeM: number,
    condition: AtmosphericCondition,
    pointingErrorUrad: number = 0
  ): LinkBudget {
    const wavelengthM = hw.wavelengthNm * 1e-9;
    const txApertureM = hw.txApertureMm * 1e-3;
    const rxApertureM = hw.rxApertureMm * 1e-3;
    const divergenceRad = hw.divergenceMrad * 1e-3;

    // TX power in dBm
    const txPowerDbm = 10 * Math.log10(hw.txPowerMw);

    // Beam divergence loss: loss = -20*log10( D_rx / (θ * L) ) when beam > aperture
    const beamDiamAtRange = divergenceRad * rangeM;
    let beamDivergenceLossDb: number;
    if (beamDiamAtRange > rxApertureM) {
      beamDivergenceLossDb = -20 * Math.log10(rxApertureM / beamDiamAtRange);
    } else {
      beamDivergenceLossDb = 0; // Aperture captures full beam
    }

    // Atmospheric loss
    const attenuationDbKm = AtmosphericModel.attenuationDbKm(hw.wavelengthNm, condition);
    const atmosphericLossDb = attenuationDbKm * (rangeM / 1000);

    // Geometric (free-space path) loss
    const geometricLossDb = 20 * Math.log10((4 * Math.PI * rangeM) / wavelengthM);

    // Pointing loss (Gaussian beam approximation)
    const pointingLossDb = pointingErrorUrad > 0
      ? 4.343 * Math.pow((pointingErrorUrad * 1e-6) / (divergenceRad / 2), 2)
      : 0;

    // RX aperture gain
    const rxApertureGainDb = 10 * Math.log10(Math.PI * Math.pow(rxApertureM / 2, 2) / Math.pow(wavelengthM / (4 * Math.PI), 1));

    // Simplified: received power = TX - beam_loss - atmo_loss - pointing_loss
    const rxedPowerDbm = txPowerDbm - beamDivergenceLossDb - atmosphericLossDb - pointingLossDb;

    // Receiver sensitivity based on shot noise
    const photonEnergyJ = (PLANCK * SPEED_OF_LIGHT) / wavelengthM;
    const bwHz = hw.bandwidthMhz * 1e6;
    const photocurrentA = (hw.detectorResponsivity * Math.pow(10, (rxedPowerDbm - 30) / 10));
    const shotNoiseCurrent = Math.sqrt(2 * ELECTRON_CHARGE * photocurrentA * bwHz);
    const darkNoiseCurrent = Math.sqrt(2 * ELECTRON_CHARGE * hw.darkcurrentNa * 1e-9 * bwHz);
    const totalNoiseCurrent = Math.sqrt(Math.pow(shotNoiseCurrent, 2) + Math.pow(darkNoiseCurrent, 2));
    const snrLinear = photocurrentA / totalNoiseCurrent;
    const snrDb = 20 * Math.log10(Math.max(snrLinear, 1e-10));

    // BER for OOK: Q(sqrt(2*SNR))
    const qFactor = Math.sqrt(2 * snrLinear);
    const ber = 0.5 * Math.erfc(qFactor / Math.sqrt(2));

    // Receiver sensitivity (min power for BER = 1e-9)
    const targetQ = 6.0; // Q for BER ≈ 1e-9
    const minPhotocurrent = targetQ * totalNoiseCurrent;
    const minPowerW = minPhotocurrent / hw.detectorResponsivity;
    const receiverSensitivityDbm = 10 * Math.log10(minPowerW * 1000);

    const linkMarginDb = rxedPowerDbm - receiverSensitivityDbm;
    const viable = linkMarginDb > 0;

    // Maximum range where link margin = 0
    const totalLossPerKm = attenuationDbKm + (20 * Math.log10(divergenceRad * 1000 / rxApertureM) / 1); // approx
    const maxRangeM = viable
      ? rangeM * Math.pow(10, linkMarginDb / (totalLossPerKm * 2 || 20))
      : rangeM * Math.pow(10, -linkMarginDb / 20);

    // Data rate (Shannon bound approximation)
    const dataRateMbps = viable
      ? Math.min(hw.bandwidthMhz * Math.log2(1 + snrLinear), hw.bandwidthMhz * 10)
      : 0;

    return {
      txPowerDbm,
      beamDivergenceLossDb,
      atmosphericLossDb,
      geometricLossDb,
      pointingLossDb,
      rxApertureGainDb,
      rxedPowerDbm,
      receiverSensitivityDbm,
      linkMarginDb,
      maxRangeM: Math.max(maxRangeM, 0),
      dataRateMbps: Math.max(dataRateMbps, 0),
      ber: Math.max(ber, 0),
      viable,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Find optimal beam divergence for given range and aperture
   */
  static optimizeDivergence(
    hw: OpticalHardware,
    rangeM: number,
    condition: AtmosphericCondition
  ): BeamOptimizationResult {
    const rxApertureM = hw.rxApertureMm * 1e-3;
    let bestDivergence = hw.divergenceMrad;
    let bestMargin = -Infinity;
    let bestBudget: LinkBudget | null = null;

    // Sweep divergence from 0.1 mrad to 100 mrad
    for (let div = 0.1; div <= 100; div += 0.1) {
      const testHw = { ...hw, divergenceMrad: div };
      const budget = LinkBudgetCalculator.calculate(testHw, rangeM, condition);
      if (budget.linkMarginDb > bestMargin) {
        bestMargin = budget.linkMarginDb;
        bestDivergence = div;
        bestBudget = budget;
      }
    }

    const budget = bestBudget!;
    let recommendedMode: OpticalMode;
    if (bestDivergence < 1) recommendedMode = OpticalMode.LASER_COLLIMATED;
    else if (bestDivergence < 10) recommendedMode = OpticalMode.LASER_SEMI_WIDE;
    else if (bestDivergence < 30) recommendedMode = OpticalMode.LED_DIRECTIONAL;
    else recommendedMode = OpticalMode.LED_DIFFUSE;

    return {
      optimalDivergenceMrad: bestDivergence,
      optimalApertureRatioTx: bestDivergence * rangeM / (rxApertureM * 1000),
      expectedThroughputMbps: budget.dataRateMbps,
      linkMarginDb: budget.linkMarginDb,
      recommendedMode,
      rangeCapabilityM: budget.maxRangeM,
    };
  }
}

// ─── Reed-Solomon Codec ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ReedSolomonCodec {
  private readonly n: number;
  private readonly k: number;
  private readonly t: number;
  private readonly gfPoly: number;

  constructor(params: typeof RS_PARAMS[keyof typeof RS_PARAMS]) {
    this.n = params.n;
    this.k = params.k;
    this.t = params.t;
    this.gfPoly = 0x11d; // GF(2^8) primitive polynomial x^8+x^4+x^3+x^2+1
  }

  private gfMul(a: number, b: number): number {
    let result = 0;
    let aa = a;
    let bb = b;
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      if (bb & 1) result ^= aa;
      const highBit = aa & 0x80;
      aa = (aa << 1) & 0xff;
      if (highBit) aa ^= (this.gfPoly & 0xff);
      bb >>= 1;
    }
    return result;
  }

  private gfPow(base: number, exp: number): number {
    let result = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < exp; i++) result = this.gfMul(result, base);
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Generate RS generator polynomial coefficients
   */
  private generatorPoly(): number[] {
    const poly = [1];
    for (let i = 0; i < 2 * this.t; i++) {
      const term = [1, this.gfPow(2, i)];
      const newPoly = new Array(poly.length + 1).fill(0);
      for (let j = 0; j < poly.length; j++) {
        newPoly[j] ^= this.gfMul(poly[j], term[0]);
        newPoly[j + 1] ^= this.gfMul(poly[j], term[1]);
      }
      poly.length = 0;
      poly.push(...newPoly);
    }
    return poly;
  }

  /**
   * Encode data block into RS codeword
   */
  encode(data: Buffer): Buffer {
    const padded = Buffer.alloc(this.k);
    data.copy(padded, 0, 0, Math.min(data.length, this.k));

    const genPoly = this.generatorPoly();
    const remainder = new Array(2 * this.t).fill(0);

    for (let i = 0; i < this.k; i++) {
      const feedback = padded[i] ^ remainder[0];
      for (let j = 0; j < 2 * this.t - 1; j++) {
        remainder[j] = remainder[j + 1] ^ this.gfMul(genPoly[j + 1], feedback);
      }
      remainder[2 * this.t - 1] = this.gfMul(genPoly[2 * this.t], feedback);
    }

    const codeword = Buffer.alloc(this.n);
    padded.copy(codeword, 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < 2 * this.t; i++) {
      codeword[this.k + i] = remainder[i];
    }
    return codeword;
  }

  /**
   * Decode RS codeword with error correction
   */
  decode(codeword: Buffer): { data: Buffer; errorsFixed: number; valid: boolean } {
    const received = Buffer.from(codeword.slice(0, this.n));

    // Compute syndromes
    const syndromes = new Array(2 * this.t).fill(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < 2 * this.t; i++) {
      for (let j = 0; j < this.n; j++) {
        syndromes[i] = this.gfMul(syndromes[i], this.gfPow(2, i)) ^ received[j];
      }
    }

    const allZero = syndromes.every(s => s === 0);
    if (allZero) {
      return { data: received.slice(0, this.k), errorsFixed: 0, valid: true };
    }

    // Berlekamp-Massey for error locator polynomial
    let sigma = [1];
    let prevSigma = [1];
    let L = 0;
    let m = 1;
    let b = 1;

    for (let n = 0; n < 2 * this.t; n++) {
      let d = syndromes[n];
      for (let i = 1; i <= L; i++) {
        if (i < sigma.length) {
          d ^= this.gfMul(sigma[i], syndromes[n - i]);
        }
      }

      if (d === 0) {
        m++;
      } else if (2 * L <= n) {
        const temp = [...sigma];
        const scale = this.gfMul(d, this.gfPow(b, 255 - 1));
        const shifted = new Array(m).fill(0).concat(prevSigma.map(c => this.gfMul(c, scale)));
        while (shifted.length < sigma.length) shifted.push(0);
        while (sigma.length < shifted.length) sigma.push(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < sigma.length; i++) sigma[i] ^= shifted[i] || 0;
        L = n + 1 - L;
        prevSigma = temp;
        b = d;
        m = 1;
      } else {
        const scale = this.gfMul(d, this.gfPow(b, 255 - 1));
        const shifted = new Array(m).fill(0).concat(prevSigma.map(c => this.gfMul(c, scale)));
        while (shifted.length < sigma.length) shifted.push(0);
        while (sigma.length < shifted.length) sigma.push(0);
        for (let i = 0; i < sigma.length; i++) sigma[i] ^= shifted[i] || 0;
        m++;
      }
    }

    // Chien search: find error positions
    const errorPositions: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < this.n; i++) {
      let val = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < sigma.length; j++) {
        val ^= this.gfMul(sigma[j], this.gfPow(this.gfPow(2, i), j));
      }
      if (val === 0) errorPositions.push(this.n - 1 - i);
    }

    if (errorPositions.length !== L || L > this.t) {
      return { data: received.slice(0, this.k), errorsFixed: 0, valid: false };
    }

    // Forney algorithm: compute error magnitudes
    const corrected = Buffer.from(received);
    for (let i = 0; i < errorPositions.length; i++) {
      const pos = errorPositions[i];
      let omega = 0;
      for (let j = 0; j < 2 * this.t; j++) {
        omega ^= this.gfMul(syndromes[j], sigma[sigma.length - 1 - j] || 0);
      }

      let sigmaPrime = 0;
      for (let j = 1; j < sigma.length; j += 2) {
        sigmaPrime ^= this.gfMul(sigma[j], this.gfPow(this.gfPow(2, i), j - 1));
      }

      const magnitude = this.gfMul(omega, this.gfPow(sigmaPrime, 255 - 1));
      corrected[pos] ^= magnitude;
    }

    return {
      data: corrected.slice(0, this.k),
      errorsFixed: errorPositions.length,
      valid: true,
    };
  }

  get symbolsPerBlock(): number { return this.k; }
  get blockSize(): number { return this.n; }
  get errorCapability(): number { return this.t; }
}

// ─── APT Controller (Acquisition, Pointing, Tracking) ────────────────────────

class APTController extends EventEmitter {
  private azimuthDeg: number;
  private elevationDeg: number;
  private targetAzimuth: number;
  private targetElevation: number;
  private readonly precisionUrad: number;
  private trackingActive: boolean = false;
  private trackingTimer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
  private signalStrength: number = 0;

  constructor(precisionUrad: number) {
    super();
    this.precisionUrad = precisionUrad;
    this.azimuthDeg = 0;
    this.elevationDeg = 0;
    this.targetAzimuth = 0;
    this.targetElevation = 0;
  }

  /**
   * Spiral scan acquisition pattern
   */
  async acquire(
    estimatedAzimuth: number,
    estimatedElevation: number,
    searchRadiusDeg: number,
    timeoutMs: number
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    this.emit('apt:acquiring', { estimatedAzimuth, estimatedElevation });

    const startTime = Date.now();
    const stepDeg = this.precisionUrad * 1e-6 * (180 / Math.PI) * 10; // 10x precision per step
    let radius = 0;
    let angle = 0;
    const spiralStep = stepDeg;

    while (Date.now() - startTime < timeoutMs) {
      // Archimedean spiral scan
      const x = radius * Math.cos(angle);
      const y = radius * Math.sin(angle);
      const testAz = estimatedAzimuth + x;
      const testEl = estimatedElevation + y;

      this.azimuthDeg = testAz;
      this.elevationDeg = testEl;

      // Simulate signal detection
      const angularError = Math.sqrt(
        Math.pow(testAz - this.targetAzimuth, 2) +
        Math.pow(testEl - this.targetElevation, 2)
      );

      const detectedPower = Math.exp(-angularError * angularError / (2 * 0.01 * 0.01));
      if (detectedPower > 0.5) {
        this.signalStrength = detectedPower;
        this.emit('apt:acquired', { azimuth: testAz, elevation: testEl, power: detectedPower });
        await this.startTracking(updateRateHz: 10);
        return true;
      }

      angle += 0.3;
      radius = spiralStep * angle / (2 * Math.PI);
      if (radius > searchRadiusDeg) break;

      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 10));
    }

    this.emit('apt:acquisition_failed', { timeout: timeoutMs });
    return false;
  }

  private async startTracking(updateRateHz: number): Promise<void> {
    this.trackingActive = true;
    const intervalMs = 1000 / updateRateHz;

    this.trackingTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (!this.trackingActive) return;

      // Hill-climbing tracker: gradient ascent on received power
      const perturbAz = (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.precisionUrad * 1e-6 * (180 / Math.PI);
      const perturbEl = (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.precisionUrad * 1e-6 * (180 / Math.PI);

      const testAz = this.azimuthDeg + perturbAz;
      const testEl = this.elevationDeg + perturbEl;

      const angularError = Math.sqrt(
        Math.pow(testAz - this.targetAzimuth, 2) +
        Math.pow(testEl - this.targetElevation, 2)
      );
      const newPower = Math.exp(-angularError * angularError / (2 * 0.01 * 0.01));

      if (newPower > this.signalStrength) {
        this.azimuthDeg = testAz;
        this.elevationDeg = testEl;
        this.signalStrength = newPower;
      }

      this.emit('apt:tracking', {
        azimuth: this.azimuthDeg,
        elevation: this.elevationDeg,
        signalStrength: this.signalStrength,
      });

      if (this.signalStrength < 0.1) {
        this.emit('apt:tracking_lost');
      }
    }, intervalMs);
  }

  stopTracking(): void {
    this.trackingActive = false;
    if (this.trackingTimer) {
      clearInterval(this.trackingTimer);
      this.trackingTimer = null;
    }
  }

  setTarget(azimuth: number, elevation: number): void {
    this.targetAzimuth = azimuth;
    this.targetElevation = elevation;
  }

  getPointingError(): number {
    const errDeg = Math.sqrt(
      Math.pow(this.azimuthDeg - this.targetAzimuth, 2) +
      Math.pow(this.elevationDeg - this.targetElevation, 2)
    );
    return errDeg * (Math.PI / 180) * 1e6; // Convert to μrad
  }

  get currentSignalStrength(): number { return this.signalStrength; }
}

// ─── Background Light Rejection ───────────────────────────────────────────────

class BackgroundRejector {
  private readonly filter: SpectrumFilter;

  constructor(filter: SpectrumFilter) {
    this.filter = filter;
  }

  /**
   * Compute background-induced noise current
   * Iback = Rλ * Hb * π * (D/2)² * Δλ * FOV²/4
   */
  backgroundNoiseCurrent(
    irradianceWm2nm: number,   // Background irradiance per nm
    apertureM: number,
    responsivityAW: number
  ): number {
    const apertureAreaM2 = Math.PI * Math.pow(apertureM / 2, 2);
    const fovSr = Math.PI * Math.pow(this.filter.angularFov * Math.PI / 180 / 2, 2);
    const filteredIrradiance = irradianceWm2nm * this.filter.bandwidthNm * this.filter.peakTransmission;
    const backgroundPower = filteredIrradiance * apertureAreaM2 * fovSr / Math.PI;
    return responsivityAW * backgroundPower;
  }

  /**
   * SNR degradation from background light
   */
  snrDegradationDb(backgroundCurrentA: number, signalCurrentA: number): number {
    if (backgroundCurrentA <= 0) return 0;
    const snrWithout = Math.pow(signalCurrentA, 2);
    const noiseFloor = 2 * ELECTRON_CHARGE * backgroundCurrentA * 1e7; // shot noise
    const snrWith = Math.pow(signalCurrentA, 2) / (Math.pow(signalCurrentA, 2) / snrWithout + noiseFloor);
    return 10 * Math.log10(snrWithout / Math.max(snrWith, 1e-30));
  }

  /**
   * Daytime vs nighttime compensation mode
   */
  getOperatingMode(ambientLuxLevel: number): 'DAYTIME' | 'TWILIGHT' | 'NIGHTTIME' {
    if (ambientLuxLevel > 10000) return 'DAYTIME';
    if (ambientLuxLevel > 100) return 'TWILIGHT';
    return 'NIGHTTIME';
  }

  get rejectionDb(): number { return this.filter.backgroundRejectionDb; }
}

// ─── Multi-Hop Optical Router ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

class MultiHopRouter {
  private readonly nodes: Map<string, RelayNode> = new Map();
  private readonly adjacency: Map<string, Map<string, number>> = new Map(); // nodeId -> {neighbor -> quality}

  addNode(node: RelayNode): void {
    this.nodes.set(node.nodeId, node);
    if (!this.adjacency.has(node.nodeId)) {
      this.adjacency.set(node.nodeId, new Map());
    }
  }

  addLink(nodeA: string, nodeB: string, quality: number, latencyMs: number): void {
    if (!this.adjacency.has(nodeA)) this.adjacency.set(nodeA, new Map());
    if (!this.adjacency.has(nodeB)) this.adjacency.set(nodeB, new Map());
    this.adjacency.get(nodeA)!.set(nodeB, quality);
    this.adjacency.get(nodeB)!.set(nodeA, quality);
  }

  /**
   * Dijkstra shortest path using link quality as cost (maximize min quality)
   */
  findBestRoute(srcId: string, dstId: string, maxHops: number): MultiHopRoute {
    const quality = new Map<string, number>();
    const prev = new Map<string, string | null>();
    const visited = new Set<string>();
    const hops = new Map<string, number>();

    for (const nodeId of this.nodes.keys()) {
      quality.set(nodeId, 0);
      prev.set(nodeId, null);
      hops.set(nodeId, 0);
    }
    quality.set(srcId, 1.0);

    const pq: Array<[number, string]> = [[1.0, srcId]];

    while (pq.length > 0) {
      pq.sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0]);
      const [q, u] = pq.shift()!;

      if (visited.has(u)) continue;
      visited.add(u);
      if (u === dstId) break;

      const currentHops = hops.get(u) || 0;
      if (currentHops >= maxHops) continue;

      const neighbors = this.adjacency.get(u);
      if (!neighbors) continue;

      for (const [v, linkQuality] of neighbors.entries()) {
        if (visited.has(v)) continue;
        const newQ = Math.min(q, linkQuality);
        if (newQ > (quality.get(v) || 0)) {
          quality.set(v, newQ);
          prev.set(v, u);
          hops.set(v, currentHops + 1);
          pq.push([newQ, v]);
        }
      }
    }

    // Reconstruct path
    const path: string[] = [];
    let current: string | null = dstId;
    while (current !== null) {
      path.unshift(current);
      current = prev.get(current) || null;
    }

    const hopNodes: RelayNode[] = path.map(id => this.nodes.get(id) || {
      nodeId: id, positionM: { x: 0, y: 0, z: 0 }, hasLOS: false, linkQuality: 0, latencyMs: 0,
    });

    let totalLatency = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < path.length - 1; i++) {
      const n = this.nodes.get(path[i]);
      if (n) totalLatency += n.latencyMs;
    }

    const viable = path[0] === srcId && path[path.length - 1] === dstId && path.length <= maxHops + 1;
    const minQ = quality.get(dstId) || 0;

    let totalRange = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < hopNodes.length - 1; i++) {
      const a = hopNodes[i].positionM;
      const b = hopNodes[i + 1].positionM;
      totalRange += Math.sqrt(
        Math.pow(b.x - a.x, 2) + Math.pow(b.y - a.y, 2) + Math.pow(b.z - a.z, 2)
      );
    }

    return {
      srcNodeId: srcId,
      dstNodeId: dstId,
      hops: hopNodes,
      totalRangeM: totalRange,
      estimatedLatencyMs: totalLatency,
      minimumLinkQuality: minQ,
      viable,
    };
  }
}

// ─── Packet Framer ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class OpticalPacketFramer {
  private sequence: number = 0;

  frame(
    payload: Buffer,
    srcNodeId: string,
    dstNodeId: string,
    linkId: string,
    maxHops: number,
    codec: ReedSolomonCodec
  ): OpticalPacket[] {
    const blockSize = codec.symbolsPerBlock;
    const packets: OpticalPacket[] = [];

    // Fragment payload into RS blocks
    for (let offset = 0; offset < payload.length; offset += blockSize) {
      const chunk = payload.slice(offset, Math.min(offset + blockSize, payload.length));
      const rsEncoded = codec.encode(chunk);
      const id = randomBytes(8).toString('hex');
      const checksum = createHash('sha256').update(chunk).digest('hex').slice(0, 16);

      packets.push({
        id,
        linkId,
        sequence: this.sequence++,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        payloadLength: chunk.length,
        payload: chunk,
        rsEncoded,
        checksum,
        hopCount: 0,
        maxHops,
        destNodeId: dstNodeId,
        srcNodeId,
      });
    }
    return packets;
  }

  deframe(
    packets: OpticalPacket[],
    codec: ReedSolomonCodec
  ): { payload: Buffer; errorsFixed: number; valid: boolean } {
    const sorted = [...packets].sort((a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence);
    const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
    let totalErrors = 0;
    let allValid = true;

    for (const pkt of sorted) {
      const result = codec.decode(pkt.rsEncoded);
      chunks.push(result.data.slice(0, pkt.payloadLength));
      totalErrors += result.errorsFixed;
      if (!result.valid) allValid = false;

      const checksum = createHash('sha256').update(result.data.slice(0, pkt.payloadLength)).digest('hex').slice(0, 16);
      if (checksum !== pkt.checksum) allValid = false;
    }

    return {
      payload: Buffer.concat(chunks),
      errorsFixed: totalErrors,
      valid: allValid,
    };
  }
}

// ─── Optical Mesh Engine ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class OpticalMesh extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly config: OpticalMeshConfig;
  private readonly links: Map<string, FSOLink> = new Map();
  private readonly apt: APTController;
  private readonly bgRejector: BackgroundRejector;
  private readonly router: MultiHopRouter;
  private readonly framer: OpticalPacketFramer;
  private readonly codec: ReedSolomonCodec;
  private fallbackLevel: FallbackLevel = FallbackLevel.LASER;
  private currentHardware: OpticalHardware;

  constructor(config: OpticalMeshConfig) {
    super();
    this.config = config;
    this.currentHardware = { ...config.hardware };
    this.apt = new APTController(config.hardware.aptPrecisionUrad);
    this.bgRejector = new BackgroundRejector(config.backgroundFilter);
    this.router = new MultiHopRouter();
    this.framer = new OpticalPacketFramer();
    this.codec = new ReedSolomonCodec(RS_PARAMS[config.fecLevel]);

    this.apt.on('apt:tracking_lost', () => this.onTrackingLost());
    this.apt.on('apt:acquired', (info) => this.emit('optical:acquired', info));
    this.apt.on('apt:tracking', (info) => this.emit('optical:tracking', info));
  }

  // ── Link Establishment ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async establishLink(
    remoteNodeId: string,
    rangeM: number,
    azimuthDeg: number,
    elevationDeg: number,
    mode: OpticalMode = this.config.defaultMode
  ): Promise<FSOLink | null> {
    if (this.links.size >= this.config.maxLinks) {
      this.emit('optical:error', { code: 'MAX_LINKS', message: 'Maximum simultaneous links reached' });
      return null;
    }

    const linkId = `fso_${this.config.nodeId}_${remoteNodeId}_${Date.now()}`;
    const hw = this.getModeHardware(mode);

    // Calculate link budget
    const budget = LinkBudgetCalculator.calculate(
      hw,
      rangeM,
      this.config.atmosphericCondition,
      hw.hasApt ? 0 : hw.aptPrecisionUrad
    );

    if (!budget.viable || budget.linkMarginDb < this.config.minLinkMarginDb) {
      this.emit('optical:link_infeasible', {
        remoteNodeId, rangeM, linkMarginDb: budget.linkMarginDb, budget,
      });

      if (this.config.fallbackEnabled) {
        return this.tryFallback(remoteNodeId, rangeM, azimuthDeg, elevationDeg);
      }
      return null;
    }

    // Turbulence analysis
    const cn2 = AtmosphericModel.cn2ForCondition(this.config.atmosphericCondition);
    const wavelengthM = hw.wavelengthNm * 1e-9;
    const rytovVar = AtmosphericModel.rytovVariance(cn2, wavelengthM, rangeM);
    const friedParam = AtmosphericModel.friedParameter(cn2, wavelengthM, rangeM);
    const rxApertureM = hw.rxApertureMm * 1e-3;

    const apertureAveragingFactor = this.config.enableApertureAveraging
      ? AtmosphericModel.apertureAveragingFactor(rxApertureM, rytovVar, wavelengthM, rangeM)
      : 1.0;

    const scintIdx = AtmosphericModel.scintillationIndex(rytovVar, apertureAveragingFactor);
    const diversityGain = this.config.enableDiversity
      ? AtmosphericModel.diversityGain(3, scintIdx) // assume 3 apertures
      : 1.0;

    const turbulence: TurbulenceParams = {
      rytovVariance: rytovVar,
      coherenceRadius: friedParam * 100, // m to cm
      scintillationIndex: scintIdx,
      apertureAveragingFactor,
      diversityGain,
    };

    // APT acquisition
    if (hw.hasApt && this.config.hardware.hasApt) {
      this.apt.setTarget(azimuthDeg, elevationDeg);
      const acquired = await this.apt.acquire(
        azimuthDeg,
        elevationDeg,
        hw.divergenceMrad * 0.1,
        this.config.acquisitionTimeoutMs
      );

      if (!acquired) {
        this.emit('optical:acquisition_failed', { remoteNodeId, rangeM });
        return null;
      }
    }

    const link: FSOLink = {
      id: linkId,
      localNodeId: this.config.nodeId,
      remoteNodeId,
      mode,
      state: LinkState.DATA_TRANSFER,
      rangeM,
      azimuthDeg,
      elevationDeg,
      budget,
      turbulence,
      rsParams: RS_PARAMS[this.config.fecLevel],
      establishedAt: Date.now(),
      packetsRx: 0,
      packetsTx: 0,
      errorRate: 0,
      throughputMbps: budget.dataRateMbps,
    };

    this.links.set(linkId, link);
    this.router.addNode({
      nodeId: remoteNodeId,
      positionM: this.rangeAngleToPosition(rangeM, azimuthDeg, elevationDeg),
      hasLOS: true,
      linkQuality: budget.linkMarginDb / 20,
      latencyMs: (rangeM / SPEED_OF_LIGHT) * 1000,
    });

    this.emit('optical:link_established', { linkId, remoteNodeId, budget, turbulence });
    return link;
  }

  private getModeHardware(mode: OpticalMode): OpticalHardware {
    const base = { ...this.config.hardware };
    switch (mode) {
      case OpticalMode.LASER_COLLIMATED:
        return { ...base, divergenceMrad: 0.2, txPowerMw: base.txPowerMw * 0.8 };
      case OpticalMode.LASER_SEMI_WIDE:
        return { ...base, divergenceMrad: 2.0, txPowerMw: base.txPowerMw };
      case OpticalMode.LED_DIRECTIONAL:
        return { ...base, divergenceMrad: 15.0, txPowerMw: base.txPowerMw * 1.5, hasApt: false };
      case OpticalMode.LED_DIFFUSE:
        return { ...base, divergenceMrad: 60.0, txPowerMw: base.txPowerMw * 2.0, hasApt: false };
      case OpticalMode.RETROREFLECTOR:
        return { ...base, divergenceMrad: 1.0, txPowerMw: 0.1 }; // Passive RX, minimal TX
      default:
        return base;
    }
  }

  private async tryFallback(
    remoteNodeId: string,
    rangeM: number,
    azimuthDeg: number,
    elevationDeg: number
  ): Promise<FSOLink | null> {
    const fallbackOrder: OpticalMode[] = [
      OpticalMode.LASER_SEMI_WIDE,
      OpticalMode.LED_DIRECTIONAL,
      OpticalMode.LED_DIFFUSE,
    ];

    for (const mode of fallbackOrder) {
      this.emit('optical:fallback_attempt', { mode, remoteNodeId });
      const link = await this.establishLink(remoteNodeId, rangeM, azimuthDeg, elevationDeg, mode);
      if (link) {
        this.emit('optical:fallback_success', { mode, linkId: link.id });
        return link;
      }
    }

    this.emit('optical:all_fallbacks_failed', { remoteNodeId, rangeM });
    return null;
  }

  // ── Data Transfer ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async transmit(linkId: string, payload: Buffer): Promise<boolean> {
    const link = this.links.get(linkId);
    if (!link || link.state !== LinkState.DATA_TRANSFER) return false;

    const packets = this.framer.frame(
      payload,
      this.config.nodeId,
      link.remoteNodeId,
      linkId,
      this.config.maxHops,
      this.codec
    );

    link.packetsTx += packets.length;
    link.lastDataAt = Date.now();

    this.emit('optical:transmit', {
      linkId,
      packets: packets.length,
      bytes: payload.length,
      fecOverhead: packets.reduce((acc, p) => acc + p.rsEncoded.length - p.payloadLength, 0),
    });

    return true;
  }

  receivePackets(linkId: string, packets: OpticalPacket[]): Buffer | null {
    const link = this.links.get(linkId);
    if (!link) return null;

    const result = this.framer.deframe(packets, this.codec);
    link.packetsRx += packets.length;
    link.errorRate = result.errorsFixed / (packets.length * this.codec.errorCapability);

    if (!result.valid) {
      this.emit('optical:rx_error', { linkId, errorsFixed: result.errorsFixed });
      return null;
    }

    this.emit('optical:received', {
      linkId,
      bytes: result.payload.length,
      errorsFixed: result.errorsFixed,
    });

    return result.payload;
  }

  // ── Multi-Hop Routing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async routeMultiHop(
    dstNodeId: string,
    payload: Buffer,
    intermediates: RelayNode[]
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!this.config.enableMultiHop) return false;

    for (const relay of intermediates) {
      this.router.addNode(relay);
    }

    const route = this.router.findBestRoute(
      this.config.nodeId,
      dstNodeId,
      this.config.maxHops
    );

    if (!route.viable) {
      this.emit('optical:no_route', { dstNodeId });
      return false;
    }

    this.emit('optical:route_found', {
      dstNodeId,
      hops: route.hops.length,
      latencyMs: route.estimatedLatencyMs,
      minQuality: route.minimumLinkQuality,
    });

    // Transmit along route (first hop)
    if (route.hops.length > 1) {
      const firstHop = route.hops[1];
      const link = this.getLinkToNode(firstHop.nodeId);
      if (link) {
        return this.transmit(link.id, payload);
      }
    }

    return false;
  }

  // ── Beam Optimization ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  optimizeBeam(rangeM: number): BeamOptimizationResult {
    return LinkBudgetCalculator.optimizeDivergence(
      this.currentHardware,
      rangeM,
      this.config.atmosphericCondition
    );
  }

  // ── Link Management ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  teardownLink(linkId: string): void {
    const link = this.links.get(linkId);
    if (!link) return;

    link.state = LinkState.IDLE;
    this.apt.stopTracking();
    this.links.delete(linkId);
    this.emit('optical:link_torn_down', { linkId });
  }

  getLinkStatus(linkId: string): FSOLink | null {
    return this.links.get(linkId) || null;
  }

  getAllLinks(): FSOLink[] {
    return Array.from(this.links.values());
  }

  getLinkToNode(nodeId: string): FSOLink | null {
    for (const link of this.links.values()) {
      if (link.remoteNodeId === nodeId && link.state === LinkState.DATA_TRANSFER) {
        return link;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  computeLinkBudget(
    rangeM: number,
    mode: OpticalMode = this.config.defaultMode,
    condition: AtmosphericCondition = this.config.atmosphericCondition
  ): LinkBudget {
    const hw = this.getModeHardware(mode);
    return LinkBudgetCalculator.calculate(hw, rangeM, condition);
  }

  // ── Internal Handlers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private onTrackingLost(): void {
    this.emit('optical:tracking_lost');
    // Mark all links as degraded
    for (const link of this.links.values()) {
      if (link.state === LinkState.DATA_TRANSFER) {
        link.state = LinkState.DEGRADED;
        this.emit('optical:link_degraded', { linkId: link.id });
      }
    }
  }

  private rangeAngleToPosition(
    rangeM: number,
    azimuthDeg: number,
    elevationDeg: number
