  ): { x: number; y: number; z: number } {
    const az = azimuthDeg * Math.PI / 180;
    const el = elevationDeg * Math.PI / 180;
    return {
      x: rangeM * Math.cos(el) * Math.sin(az),
      y: rangeM * Math.cos(el) * Math.cos(az),
      z: rangeM * Math.sin(el),
    };
  }

  // ── Diagnostics ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  diagnostics(): Record<string, unknown> {
    const links = this.getAllLinks();
    return {
      nodeId: this.config.nodeId,
      activeLinks: links.length,
      fallbackLevel: FallbackLevel[this.fallbackLevel],
      hardware: {
        wavelengthNm: this.currentHardware.wavelengthNm,
        txPowerMw: this.currentHardware.txPowerMw,
        txApertureMm: this.currentHardware.txApertureMm,
        rxApertureMm: this.currentHardware.rxApertureMm,
        divergenceMrad: this.currentHardware.divergenceMrad,
        hasApt: this.currentHardware.hasApt,
        aptPrecisionUrad: this.currentHardware.aptPrecisionUrad,
      },
      fec: {
        level: this.config.fecLevel,
        n: this.codec.blockSize,
        k: this.codec.symbolsPerBlock,
        t: this.codec.errorCapability,
      },
      links: links.map(l => ({
        id: l.id,
        remoteNode: l.remoteNodeId,
        mode: l.mode,
        state: l.state,
        rangeM: l.rangeM,
        linkMarginDb: l.budget.linkMarginDb,
        throughputMbps: l.throughputMbps,
        errorRate: l.errorRate,
        packetsRx: l.packetsRx,
        packetsTx: l.packetsTx,
        uptimeMs: l.establishedAt ? Date.now() - l.establishedAt : 0,
      })),
      atmosphericCondition: this.config.atmosphericCondition,
      turbulenceInfo: links[0]?.turbulence ?? null,
    };
  }
}

// ─── Factory ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createOpticalMesh(overrides?: Partial<OpticalMeshConfig>): OpticalMesh {
  const defaults: OpticalMeshConfig = {
    nodeId: `optical_${randomBytes(4).toString('hex')}`,
    hardware: {
      txPowerMw: 100,
      txApertureMm: 25,
      rxApertureMm: 75,
      wavelengthNm: 1550,
      divergenceMrad: 1.0,
      detectorResponsivity: 0.9,
      darkcurrentNa: 1.0,
      bandwidthMhz: 1000,
      noiseEquivPowerDbm: -60,
      hasApt: true,
      aptPrecisionUrad: 5,
    },
    defaultMode: OpticalMode.LASER_SEMI_WIDE,
    atmosphericCondition: AtmosphericCondition.CLEAR,
    maxLinks: 8,
    maxHops: 5,
    acquisitionTimeoutMs: 30000,
    trackingUpdateRateHz: 10,
    fecLevel: 'MODERATE',
    backgroundFilter: {
      centerWavelengthNm: 1550,
      bandwidthNm: 1,
      peakTransmission: 0.95,
      backgroundRejectionDb: 40,
      angularFov: 0.5,
    },
    enableMultiHop: true,
    enableApertureAveraging: true,
    enableDiversity: true,
    fallbackEnabled: true,
    minLinkMarginDb: 3,
  };

  return new OpticalMesh({ ...defaults, ...overrides });
}

// ─── Exports ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export {
  AtmosphericModel,
  LinkBudgetCalculator,
  ReedSolomonCodec,
  APTController,
  BackgroundRejector,
  MultiHopRouter,
  OpticalPacketFramer,
  WAVELENGTH,
  ATMO_ATTENUATION,
  RS_PARAMS,
};

export default OpticalMesh;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — physicalResilience.ts
 * Physical Hardware Resilience Engine
 *
 * Implements:
 *   - Hardware watchdog daemon with heartbeat enforcement and reboot triggers
 *   - RAID-1 software mirroring for node storage with bit-rot detection
 *   - Thermal monitoring with CPU throttling and emergency shutdown
 *   - Automatic node recovery after power failure (journal replay)
 *   - Corrupt state detection via Merkle integrity tree
 *   - State rollback to last known good checkpoint
 *   - Disk health monitoring (SMART-equivalent metrics)
 *   - Memory integrity verification (ECC simulation for software)
 *   - Power failure detection and graceful shutdown sequencing
 *   - Multi-sensor fusion for environmental health scoring
 *
 * Design contract:
 *   - Zero human intervention required for recovery
 *   - All state transitions logged to tamper-evident journal
 *   - Recovery completes within defined RTO (Recovery Time Objective)
 *   - Compatible with powerSovereignty.ts for battery-aware decisions
 *
 * @module physicalResilience
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES & INTERFACES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type HealthStatus = 'healthy' | 'degraded' | 'critical' | 'failed';
export type RecoveryAction = 'none' | 'throttle' | 'soft_restart' | 'hard_restart' | 'emergency_shutdown' | 'rollback';
export type DiskStatus = 'ok' | 'warning' | 'failing' | 'failed';
export type MirrorStatus = 'synced' | 'degraded' | 'rebuilding' | 'failed';
export type JournalEntryType =
  | 'checkpoint'
  | 'state_write'
  | 'state_delete'
  | 'power_event'
  | 'thermal_event'
  | 'watchdog_kick'
  | 'recovery_start'
  | 'recovery_complete'
  | 'rollback'
  | 'mirror_sync'
  | 'integrity_check';

export interface ResilienceConfig {
  nodeId: string;
  watchdogIntervalMs: number;          // How often to check for heartbeat
  watchdogTimeoutMs: number;           // Max time without heartbeat before reboot
  maxThermalCelsius: number;           // Emergency shutdown threshold
  throttleThermalCelsius: number;      // Begin throttling at this temperature
  maxCpuLoadPercent: number;           // Trigger load shedding above this
  raidEnabled: boolean;
  raidRedundancy: number;              // Number of mirror disks (1 = RAID-1)
  checkpointIntervalMs: number;        // How often to write state checkpoints
  maxCheckpoints: number;              // How many checkpoints to retain
  bitRotCheckIntervalMs: number;       // How often to scan for silent corruption
  recoveryTimeObjectiveMs: number;     // Max acceptable recovery time
  merkleDepth: number;                 // Integrity tree depth
  powerFailureGracePeriodMs: number;   // Time to flush on power failure detection
  memoryIntegrityCheckIntervalMs: number;
  smartPollingIntervalMs: number;
}

export const DEFAULT_RESILIENCE_CONFIG: ResilienceConfig = {
  nodeId: 'node-0',
  watchdogIntervalMs: 5000,
  watchdogTimeoutMs: 30000,
  maxThermalCelsius: 85,
  throttleThermalCelsius: 70,
  maxCpuLoadPercent: 95,
  raidEnabled: true,
  raidRedundancy: 1,
  checkpointIntervalMs: 60000,
  maxCheckpoints: 10,
  bitRotCheckIntervalMs: 3600000,     // 1 hour
  recoveryTimeObjectiveMs: 30000,     // 30 seconds
  merkleDepth: 16,
  powerFailureGracePeriodMs: 2000,
  memoryIntegrityCheckIntervalMs: 300000,
  smartPollingIntervalMs: 60000,
};

// Journal entry — append-only, tamper-evident
export interface JournalEntry {
  entryId: string;
  timestamp: number;
  type: JournalEntryType;
  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
  previousEntryHash: string;
  hash: string;                        // SHA-256(timestamp+type+payload+prevHash)
}

// State checkpoint
export interface StateCheckpoint {
  checkpointId: string;
  timestamp: number;
  sequenceNumber: number;
  stateHash: string;                   // Merkle root of all state keys
  journalPosition: number;            // Journal entry index at time of checkpoint
  keyCount: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  compressionRatio: number;
}

// RAID mirror state
export interface MirrorDisk {
  diskId: string;
  path: string;                        // abstract path reference
  status: DiskStatus;
  mirrorStatus: MirrorStatus;
  totalBytes: number;
  usedBytes: number;
  lastSyncTime: number;
  lastBitRotScan: number;
  errorCount: number;
  checksumMap: Map<string, string>;    // block address → SHA-256 checksum
  pendingWrites: number;
}

// Thermal reading
export interface ThermalReading {
  timestamp: number;
  cpuTempCelsius: number;
  gpuTempCelsius: number;
  ambientTempCelsius: number;
  storageTemp: Record<string, number>;
  fanSpeedRpm: Record<string, number>;
  thermalThrottleActive: boolean;
  emergencyShutdownPending: boolean;
}

// SMART disk health metrics
export interface SmartMetrics {
  diskId: string;
  timestamp: number;
  reallocatedSectors: number;
  pendingSectors: number;
  uncorrectableErrors: number;
  powerOnHours: number;
  spinRetryCount: number;
  temperature: number;
  healthScore: number;                 // 0-100
  predictedFailureDays: number | null; // null = no prediction
}

// Watchdog state
export interface WatchdogState {
  lastHeartbeat: number;
  heartbeatCount: number;
  missedHeartbeats: number;
  consecutiveMisses: number;
  triggerCount: number;
  lastTriggerTime: number;
  armed: boolean;
}

// Memory block integrity
export interface MemoryBlock {
  blockId: string;
  address: number;
  size: number;
  checksum: string;
  lastVerified: number;
  errorCount: number;
}

// Recovery plan
export interface RecoveryPlan {
  planId: string;
  createdAt: number;
  triggerReason: string;
  steps: RecoveryStep[];
  estimatedDurationMs: number;
  targetCheckpointId: string | null;
}

export interface RecoveryStep {
  stepId: string;
  description: string;
  action: RecoveryAction;
  prerequisites: string[];
  timeoutMs: number;
}

// Node health snapshot
export interface NodeHealthSnapshot {
  timestamp: number;
  overallStatus: HealthStatus;
  overallScore: number;               // 0-100
  thermal: ThermalReading;
  cpuLoad: number;
  memoryUsagePercent: number;
  disks: SmartMetrics[];
  mirrorStatus: MirrorStatus;
  watchdogArmed: boolean;
  missedHeartbeats: number;
  lastCheckpointAge: number;          // ms since last checkpoint
  journalSize: number;
  recoveryReady: boolean;
  activeAlerts: string[];
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PURE CRYPTO (self-contained SHA-256)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ResilienceCrypto {
  static sha256(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    const K=[0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,
      0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,
      0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,
      0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,
      0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,
      0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,
      0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,
      0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2];
    let h0=0x6a09e667,h1=0xbb67ae85,h2=0x3c6ef372,h3=0xa54ff53a;
    let h4=0x510e527f,h5=0x9b05688c,h6=0x1f83d9ab,h7=0x5be0cd19;
    const msgLen=data.length,bitLen=msgLen*8;
    const paddedLen=Math.ceil((msgLen+9)/64)*64;
    const padded=new Uint8Array(paddedLen);
    padded.set(data);padded[msgLen]=0x80;
    const dv=new DataView(padded.buffer);
    dv.setUint32(paddedLen-4,bitLen>>>0,false);
    dv.setUint32(paddedLen-8,Math.floor(bitLen/0x100000000),false);
    const rot=(x:number,n:number)=>(x>>>n)|(x<<(32-n));
    const add=(a:number,b:number)=>(a+b)>>>0;
    for(let i=0;i<paddedLen;i+=64){
      const w=new Uint32Array(64);
      for(let j=0;j<16;j++)w[j]=dv.getUint32(i+j*4,false);
      for(let j=16;j<64;j++){const s0=rot(w[j-15],7)^rot(w[j-15],18)^(w[j-15]>>>3);
        const s1=rot(w[j-2],17)^rot(w[j-2],19)^(w[j-2]>>>10);
        w[j]=add(add(add(w[j-16],s0),w[j-7]),s1);}
      let[a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h]=[h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7];
      for(let j=0;j<64;j++){const S1=rot(e,6)^rot(e,11)^rot(e,25);
        const ch=(e&f)^(~e&g);const t1=add(add(add(add(h,S1),ch),K[j]),w[j]);
        const S0=rot(a,2)^rot(a,13)^rot(a,22);const maj=(a&b)^(a&c)^(b&c);
        const t2=add(S0,maj);h=g;g=f;f=e;e=add(d,t1);d=c;c=b;b=a;a=add(t1,t2);}
      h0=add(h0,a);h1=add(h1,b);h2=add(h2,c);h3=add(h3,d);
      h4=add(h4,e);h5=add(h5,f);h6=add(h6,g);h7=add(h7,h);}
    const r=new Uint8Array(32);const rv=new DataView(r.buffer);
    [h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7].forEach((v,i)=>rv.setUint32(i*4,v,false));
    return r;
  }

  static hash(str: string): string {
    const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(str);
    return ResilienceCrypto.toHex(ResilienceCrypto.sha256(bytes));
  }

  static toHex(b: Uint8Array): string {
    return Array.from(b).map(x=>x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }

  static randomId(): string {
    const b = new Uint8Array(16);
    for (let i=0;i<16;i++) b[i]=Math.floor(Math.random()*256);
    return ResilienceCrypto.toHex(b);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// TAMPER-EVIDENT JOURNAL
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class TamperEvidentJournal {
  private entries: JournalEntry[];
  private headHash: string;

  constructor() {
    this.entries = [];
    this.headHash = '0'.repeat(64);
  }

  append(type: JournalEntryType, payload: Record<string, unknown>): JournalEntry {
    const timestamp = Date.now();
    const entryId = ResilienceCrypto.randomId();
    const hashInput = `${timestamp}:${type}:${JSON.stringify(payload)}:${this.headHash}`;
    const hash = ResilienceCrypto.hash(hashInput);

    const entry: JournalEntry = {
      entryId,
      timestamp,
      type,
      payload,
      previousEntryHash: this.headHash,
      hash,
    };

    this.entries.push(entry);
    this.headHash = hash;
    return entry;
  }

  /**
   * Verify entire journal chain integrity
   */
  verify(): { valid: boolean; firstCorruptedIndex: number | null } {
    let prevHash = '0'.repeat(64);

    for (let i = 0; i < this.entries.length; i++) {
      const entry = this.entries[i];
      if (entry.previousEntryHash !== prevHash) {
        return { valid: false, firstCorruptedIndex: i };
      }
      const hashInput = `${entry.timestamp}:${entry.type}:${JSON.stringify(entry.payload)}:${entry.previousEntryHash}`;
      const expectedHash = ResilienceCrypto.hash(hashInput);
      if (expectedHash !== entry.hash) {
        return { valid: false, firstCorruptedIndex: i };
      }
      prevHash = entry.hash;
    }

    return { valid: true, firstCorruptedIndex: null };
  }

  /**
   * Replay journal from a given position to reconstruct state
   */
  replayFrom(position: number): JournalEntry[] {
    return this.entries.slice(position);
  }

  get size(): number { return this.entries.length; }
  get head(): string { return this.headHash; }
  get lastEntry(): JournalEntry | null { return this.entries[this.entries.length - 1] ?? null; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MERKLE INTEGRITY TREE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class MerkleIntegrityTree {
  private leaves: Map<string, string>; // key → hash(value)
  private root: string;
  private dirty: boolean;

  constructor() {
    this.leaves = new Map();
    this.root = '0'.repeat(64);
    this.dirty = false;
  }

  /**
   * Insert or update a key-value pair
   */
  update(key: string, value: Uint8Array): void {
    const valueHash = ResilienceCrypto.hash(key + ':' + ResilienceCrypto.toHex(
      ResilienceCrypto.sha256(value)
    ));
    this.leaves.set(key, valueHash);
    this.dirty = true;
  }

  delete(key: string): void {
    this.leaves.delete(key);
    this.dirty = true;
  }

  /**
   * Compute Merkle root over all key-value pairs
   */
  computeRoot(): string {
    if (!this.dirty) return this.root;

    const sortedKeys = Array.from(this.leaves.keys()).sort();
    if (sortedKeys.length === 0) {
      this.root = '0'.repeat(64);
      this.dirty = false;
      return this.root;
    }

    let layer: string[] = sortedKeys.map(k => this.leaves.get(k)!);

    while (layer.length > 1) {
      const next: string[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < layer.length; i += 2) {
        const left = layer[i];
        const right = layer[i + 1] ?? left; // Duplicate last node for odd count
        next.push(ResilienceCrypto.hash(left + right));
      }
      layer = next;
    }

    this.root = layer[0];
    this.dirty = false;
    return this.root;
  }

  /**
   * Generate inclusion proof for a specific key
   */
  generateProof(key: string): { proof: string[]; index: number } | null {
    const sortedKeys = Array.from(this.leaves.keys()).sort();
    const index = sortedKeys.indexOf(key);
    if (index === -1) return null;

    const proof: string[] = [];
    let layer: string[] = sortedKeys.map(k => this.leaves.get(k)!);
    let currentIndex = index;

    while (layer.length > 1) {
      const siblingIndex = currentIndex % 2 === 0 ? currentIndex + 1 : currentIndex - 1;
      if (siblingIndex < layer.length) {
        proof.push(layer[siblingIndex]);
      }
      currentIndex = Math.floor(currentIndex / 2);
      const next: string[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < layer.length; i += 2) {
        const l = layer[i], r = layer[i+1] ?? l;
        next.push(ResilienceCrypto.hash(l + r));
      }
      layer = next;
    }

    return { proof, index };
  }

  /**
   * Verify a key is in the tree with given root
   */
  verifyProof(key: string, valueHash: string, proof: string[], index: number, expectedRoot: string): boolean {
    let current = valueHash;
    let currentIndex = index;

    for (const sibling of proof) {
      if (currentIndex % 2 === 0) {
        current = ResilienceCrypto.hash(current + sibling);
      } else {
        current = ResilienceCrypto.hash(sibling + current);
      }
      currentIndex = Math.floor(currentIndex / 2);
    }

    return current === expectedRoot;
  }

  /**
   * Detect corrupted keys by comparing individual hashes
   */
  findCorruptedKeys(expectedLeaves: Map<string, string>): string[] {
    const corrupted: string[] = [];
    for (const [key, expectedHash] of expectedLeaves) {
      const actualHash = this.leaves.get(key);
      if (actualHash !== expectedHash) {
        corrupted.push(key);
      }
    }
    return corrupted;
  }

  get currentRoot(): string { return this.computeRoot(); }
  get keyCount(): number { return this.leaves.size; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// WATCHDOG DAEMON
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class WatchdogDaemon {
  private readonly config: ResilienceConfig;
  private state: WatchdogState;
  private checkInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;
  private onTrigger?: (state: WatchdogState) => Promise<void>;
  private readonly journal: TamperEvidentJournal;

  constructor(config: ResilienceConfig, journal: TamperEvidentJournal) {
    this.config = config;
    this.journal = journal;
    this.state = {
      lastHeartbeat: Date.now(),
      heartbeatCount: 0,
      missedHeartbeats: 0,
      consecutiveMisses: 0,
      triggerCount: 0,
      lastTriggerTime: 0,
      armed: false,
    };
    this.checkInterval = null;
  }

  /**
   * Arm the watchdog — starts monitoring for heartbeats
   */
  arm(onTrigger: (state: WatchdogState) => Promise<void>): void {
    this.onTrigger = onTrigger;
    this.state.armed = true;

    this.checkInterval = setInterval(async () => {
      await this.check();
    }, this.config.watchdogIntervalMs);

    this.journal.append('watchdog_kick', { action: 'armed', timestamp: Date.now() });
  }

  /**
   * Disarm the watchdog
   */
  disarm(): void {
    if (this.checkInterval) {
      clearInterval(this.checkInterval);
      this.checkInterval = null;
    }
    this.state.armed = false;
    this.journal.append('watchdog_kick', { action: 'disarmed', timestamp: Date.now() });
  }

  /**
   * Heartbeat — called by monitored process to prevent watchdog trigger
   */
  kick(): void {
    this.state.lastHeartbeat = Date.now();
    this.state.heartbeatCount++;
    this.state.consecutiveMisses = 0;
    this.journal.append('watchdog_kick', {
      action: 'kick',
      count: this.state.heartbeatCount,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    });
  }

  private async check(): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.state.armed) return;

    const now = Date.now();
    const timeSinceHeartbeat = now - this.state.lastHeartbeat;

    if (timeSinceHeartbeat > this.config.watchdogIntervalMs * 1.5) {
      this.state.missedHeartbeats++;
      this.state.consecutiveMisses++;
    }

    if (timeSinceHeartbeat > this.config.watchdogTimeoutMs) {
      this.state.triggerCount++;
      this.state.lastTriggerTime = now;

      this.journal.append('watchdog_kick', {
        action: 'triggered',
        timeSinceHeartbeat,
        consecutiveMisses: this.state.consecutiveMisses,
        triggerCount: this.state.triggerCount,
      });

      if (this.onTrigger) {
        await this.onTrigger({ ...this.state });
      }

      // Reset heartbeat to prevent immediate re-trigger
      this.state.lastHeartbeat = now;
      this.state.consecutiveMisses = 0;
    }
  }

  getState(): WatchdogState { return { ...this.state }; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// RAID-1 MIRROR MANAGER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class RAIDMirrorManager {
  private readonly config: ResilienceConfig;
  private disks: Map<string, MirrorDisk>;
  private readonly journal: TamperEvidentJournal;
  private readonly BLOCK_SIZE = 4096; // 4KB blocks
  private rebuildProgress: Map<string, number>; // diskId → 0-100%

  constructor(config: ResilienceConfig, journal: TamperEvidentJournal) {
    this.config = config;
    this.journal = journal;
    this.disks = new Map();
    this.rebuildProgress = new Map();
  }

  /**
   * Register a disk into the RAID array
   */
  addDisk(diskId: string, path: string, totalBytes: number): MirrorDisk {
    const disk: MirrorDisk = {
      diskId,
      path,
      status: 'ok',
      mirrorStatus: this.disks.size === 0 ? 'synced' : 'rebuilding',
      totalBytes,
      usedBytes: 0,
      lastSyncTime: Date.now(),
      lastBitRotScan: Date.now(),
      errorCount: 0,
      checksumMap: new Map(),
      pendingWrites: 0,
    };

    this.disks.set(diskId, disk);

    if (this.disks.size > 1) {
      this.initiateRebuild(diskId);
    }

    this.journal.append('mirror_sync', {
      action: 'disk_added',
      diskId,
      path,
      totalBytes,
    });

    return disk;
  }

  /**
   * Write data to all mirror disks (RAID-1 write)
   * Write is considered successful only if majority of disks accept it
   */
  write(address: number, data: Uint8Array): { success: boolean; writtenTo: string[]; failed: string[] } {
    const checksum = ResilienceCrypto.toHex(ResilienceCrypto.sha256(data));
    const addressKey = address.toString(16);
    const writtenTo: string[] = [];
    const failed: string[] = [];

    for (const [diskId, disk] of this.disks) {
      if (disk.status === 'failed') { failed.push(diskId); continue; }

      // Simulate write
      try {
        disk.checksumMap.set(addressKey, checksum);
        disk.usedBytes = Math.max(disk.usedBytes, address + data.length);
        disk.pendingWrites = Math.max(0, disk.pendingWrites - 1);
        writtenTo.push(diskId);
      } catch {
        disk.errorCount++;
        failed.push(diskId);
        if (disk.errorCount > 100) {
          disk.status = 'failing';
          this.journal.append('mirror_sync', {
            action: 'disk_degraded',
            diskId,
            errorCount: disk.errorCount,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    const success = writtenTo.length > this.disks.size / 2;
    if (!success) {
      this.journal.append('mirror_sync', {
        action: 'write_failed',
        address,
        writtenTo,
        failed,
      });
    }

    return { success, writtenTo, failed };
  }

  /**
   * Read from mirror array with error recovery
   * Tries each disk until successful read, uses Majority vote for corruption
   */
  read(address: number, length: number): {
    data: Uint8Array | null;
    source: string | null;
    wasRepaired: boolean;
  } {
    const addressKey = address.toString(16);
    const results: Array<{ diskId: string; checksum: string }> = [];

    for (const [diskId, disk] of this.disks) {
      if (disk.status === 'failed') continue;
      const checksum = disk.checksumMap.get(addressKey);
      if (checksum) results.push({ diskId, checksum });
    }

    if (results.length === 0) return { data: null, source: null, wasRepaired: false };

    // Majority vote — find most common checksum
    const votes = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const r of results) {
      votes.set(r.checksum, (votes.get(r.checksum) ?? 0) + 1);
    }

    let bestChecksum = '';
    let bestCount = 0;
    for (const [cs, count] of votes) {
      if (count > bestCount) { bestChecksum = cs; bestCount = count; }
    }

    const wasRepaired = results.some(r => r.checksum !== bestChecksum);
    if (wasRepaired) {
      // Repair corrupt disks (write correct data back)
      for (const r of results) {
        if (r.checksum !== bestChecksum) {
          const disk = this.disks.get(r.diskId)!;
          disk.checksumMap.set(addressKey, bestChecksum);
          disk.errorCount++;
          this.journal.append('mirror_sync', {
            action: 'bit_rot_repaired',
            diskId: r.diskId,
            address,
            corruptChecksum: r.checksum,
            correctChecksum: bestChecksum,
          });
        }
      }
    }

    const source = results.find(r => r.checksum === bestChecksum)?.diskId ?? null;
    // Return placeholder data — in production: actual disk read
    const data = new Uint8Array(length);

    return { data, source, wasRepaired };
  }

  /**
   * Full bit-rot scan of a disk
   * Reads every block and verifies checksum against mirror majority
   */
  async scanForBitRot(diskId: string): Promise<{
    scannedBlocks: number;
    corruptBlocks: number;
    repairedBlocks: number;
  }> {
    const disk = this.disks.get(diskId);
    if (!disk) return { scannedBlocks: 0, corruptBlocks: 0, repairedBlocks: 0 };

    let scannedBlocks = 0;
    let corruptBlocks = 0;
    let repairedBlocks = 0;

    const numBlocks = Math.ceil(disk.usedBytes / this.BLOCK_SIZE);

    for (let blockIdx = 0; blockIdx < numBlocks; blockIdx++) {
      const address = blockIdx * this.BLOCK_SIZE;
      const result = this.read(address, this.BLOCK_SIZE);
      scannedBlocks++;

      if (result.wasRepaired) {
        corruptBlocks++;
        repairedBlocks++;
      }
    }

    disk.lastBitRotScan = Date.now();

    this.journal.append('integrity_check', {
      action: 'bit_rot_scan_complete',
      diskId,
      scannedBlocks,
      corruptBlocks,
      repairedBlocks,
    });

    return { scannedBlocks, corruptBlocks, repairedBlocks };
  }

  /**
   * Handle disk failure — begin recovery from mirror
   */
  async handleDiskFailure(diskId: string): Promise<void> {
    const disk = this.disks.get(diskId);
    if (!disk) return;

    disk.status = 'failed';
    disk.mirrorStatus = 'failed';

    this.journal.append('mirror_sync', {
      action: 'disk_failed',
      diskId,
      errorCount: disk.errorCount,
    });

    // Find healthy mirror to rebuild from
    const healthyDisk = Array.from(this.disks.values())
      .find(d => d.diskId !== diskId && d.status === 'ok');

    if (healthyDisk) {
      // Signal that a replacement disk is needed
      this.journal.append('mirror_sync', {
        action: 'rebuild_required',
        failedDisk: diskId,
        sourceDisk: healthyDisk.diskId,
      });
    }
  }

  private initiateRebuild(newDiskId: string): void {
    this.rebuildProgress.set(newDiskId, 0);

    // Simulate rebuild progress (in production: actual block copying)
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const progress = this.rebuildProgress.get(newDiskId) ?? 0;
      const newProgress = Math.min(100, progress + 5);
      this.rebuildProgress.set(newDiskId, newProgress);

      if (newProgress >= 100) {
        const disk = this.disks.get(newDiskId);
        if (disk) {
          disk.mirrorStatus = 'synced';
          disk.lastSyncTime = Date.now();
        }
        this.rebuildProgress.delete(newDiskId);
        clearInterval(interval);

        this.journal.append('mirror_sync', {
          action: 'rebuild_complete',
          diskId: newDiskId,
        });
      }
    }, 1000);
  }

  getArrayStatus(): MirrorStatus {
    const disks = Array.from(this.disks.values());
    if (disks.every(d => d.status === 'failed')) return 'failed';
    if (disks.some(d => d.mirrorStatus === 'rebuilding')) return 'rebuilding';
    if (disks.some(d => d.status !== 'ok')) return 'degraded';
    return 'synced';
  }

  getDisks(): MirrorDisk[] { return Array.from(this.disks.values()); }
  getRebuildProgress(diskId: string): number { return this.rebuildProgress.get(diskId) ?? 0; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// THERMAL MONITOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class ThermalMonitor {
  private readonly config: ResilienceConfig;
  private readings: ThermalReading[];
  private readonly maxReadings = 100;
  private throttleActive: boolean;
  private emergencyPending: boolean;
  private onThrottle?: (reading: ThermalReading) => void;
  private onEmergency?: (reading: ThermalReading) => Promise<void>;
  private readonly journal: TamperEvidentJournal;

  // Kalman filter state for temperature estimation
  private kalmanState: {
    cpu: { estimate: number; errorCovariance: number };
    gpu: { estimate: number; errorCovariance: number };
  };

  constructor(config: ResilienceConfig, journal: TamperEvidentJournal) {
    this.config = config;
    this.journal = journal;
    this.readings = [];
    this.throttleActive = false;
    this.emergencyPending = false;
    this.kalmanState = {
      cpu: { estimate: 40, errorCovariance: 1 },
      gpu: { estimate: 35, errorCovariance: 1 },
    };
  }

  /**
   * Process a new thermal reading with Kalman filter smoothing
   */
  async processReading(raw: {
    cpuTemp: number;
    gpuTemp: number;
    ambientTemp: number;
    storageTemps?: Record<string, number>;
    fanSpeeds?: Record<string, number>;
  }): Promise<ThermalReading> {
    // Kalman filter for CPU temperature
    const processNoise = 0.1;
    const measurementNoise = 2.0;

    const cpuFiltered = this.kalmanUpdate(
      this.kalmanState.cpu,
      raw.cpuTemp,
      processNoise,
      measurementNoise
    );
    const gpuFiltered = this.kalmanUpdate(
      this.kalmanState.gpu,
      raw.gpuTemp,
      processNoise,
      measurementNoise
    );

    this.kalmanState.cpu = cpuFiltered;
    this.kalmanState.gpu = gpuFiltered;

    const reading: ThermalReading = {
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      cpuTempCelsius: cpuFiltered.estimate,
      gpuTempCelsius: gpuFiltered.estimate,
      ambientTempCelsius: raw.ambientTemp,
      storageTemp: raw.storageTemps ?? {},
      fanSpeedRpm: raw.fanSpeeds ?? {},
      thermalThrottleActive: false,
      emergencyShutdownPending: false,
    };

    // Throttle check
    if (cpuFiltered.estimate > this.config.throttleThermalCelsius) {
      if (!this.throttleActive) {
        this.throttleActive = true;
        reading.thermalThrottleActive = true;

        this.journal.append('thermal_event', {
          type: 'throttle_activated',
          cpuTemp: cpuFiltered.estimate,
          threshold: this.config.throttleThermalCelsius,
        });

        this.onThrottle?.(reading);
      }
    } else if (this.throttleActive && cpuFiltered.estimate < this.config.throttleThermalCelsius - 5) {
      this.throttleActive = false;

      this.journal.append('thermal_event', {
        type: 'throttle_deactivated',
        cpuTemp: cpuFiltered.estimate,
      });
    }

    // Emergency check
    if (cpuFiltered.estimate > this.config.maxThermalCelsius) {
      if (!this.emergencyPending) {
        this.emergencyPending = true;
        reading.emergencyShutdownPending = true;

        this.journal.append('thermal_event', {
          type: 'emergency_shutdown_triggered',
          cpuTemp: cpuFiltered.estimate,
          threshold: this.config.maxThermalCelsius,
        });

        await this.onEmergency?.(reading);
      }
    }

    this.readings.push(reading);
    if (this.readings.length > this.maxReadings) this.readings.shift();

    return reading;
  }

  /**
   * Kalman filter update step
   */
  private kalmanUpdate(
    state: { estimate: number; errorCovariance: number },
    measurement: number,
    processNoise: number,
    measurementNoise: number
  ): { estimate: number; errorCovariance: number } {
    // Predict
    const predictedCovariance = state.errorCovariance + processNoise;

    // Update
    const kalmanGain = predictedCovariance / (predictedCovariance + measurementNoise);
    const updatedEstimate = state.estimate + kalmanGain * (measurement - state.estimate);
    const updatedCovariance = (1 - kalmanGain) * predictedCovariance;

    return { estimate: updatedEstimate, errorCovariance: updatedCovariance };
  }

  /**
   * Compute recommended CPU frequency scale based on temperature
   * Returns 0.0 - 1.0 (1.0 = full speed)
   */
  computeThrottleScale(): number {
    const cpuTemp = this.kalmanState.cpu.estimate;
    if (cpuTemp <= this.config.throttleThermalCelsius) return 1.0;
    if (cpuTemp >= this.config.maxThermalCelsius) return 0.1;

    // Linear interpolation between throttle and max
    const range = this.config.maxThermalCelsius - this.config.throttleThermalCelsius;
    const excess = cpuTemp - this.config.throttleThermalCelsius;
    return Math.max(0.1, 1.0 - (excess / range) * 0.9);
  }

  /**
   * Predict time until thermal emergency based on trend
   */
  predictTimeToOverheat(): number | null {
    if (this.readings.length < 10) return null;
    const recent = this.readings.slice(-10);

    // Linear regression on CPU temperature
    const n = recent.length;
    const xMean = recent.reduce((s, r, i) => s + i, 0) / n;
    const yMean = recent.reduce((s, r) => s + r.cpuTempCelsius, 0) / n;

    let numerator = 0, denominator = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      numerator += (i - xMean) * (recent[i].cpuTempCelsius - yMean);
      denominator += (i - xMean) ** 2;
    }

    if (denominator === 0) return null;
    const slope = numerator / denominator; // degrees per reading

    if (slope <= 0) return null; // Temperature stable or decreasing

    const currentTemp = this.kalmanState.cpu.estimate;
    const degreesToCritical = this.config.maxThermalCelsius - currentTemp;
    const readingsUntilCritical = degreesToCritical / slope;
    const intervalMs = 5000; // assuming 5s polling

    return readingsUntilCritical * intervalMs;
  }

  onThrottleCallback(cb: (r: ThermalReading) => void): void { this.onThrottle = cb; }
  onEmergencyCallback(cb: (r: ThermalReading) => Promise<void>): void { this.onEmergency = cb; }

  getLatestReading(): ThermalReading | null { return this.readings[this.readings.length - 1] ?? null; }
  getCurrentCPUTemp(): number { return this.kalmanState.cpu.estimate; }
  isThrottleActive(): boolean { return this.throttleActive; }
  isEmergencyPending(): boolean { return this.emergencyPending; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// CHECKPOINT MANAGER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class CheckpointManager {
  private readonly config: ResilienceConfig;
  private checkpoints: StateCheckpoint[];
  private readonly merkleTree: MerkleIntegrityTree;
  private readonly journal: TamperEvidentJournal;
  private sequenceCounter: number;

  constructor(
    config: ResilienceConfig,
    merkleTree: MerkleIntegrityTree,
    journal: TamperEvidentJournal
  ) {
    this.config = config;
    this.merkleTree = merkleTree;
    this.journal = journal;
    this.checkpoints = [];
    this.sequenceCounter = 0;
  }

  /**
   * Write a checkpoint of the current state
   */
  async writeCheckpoint(
    stateSnapshot: Map<string, Uint8Array>
  ): Promise<StateCheckpoint> {
    // Update Merkle tree with current state
    for (const [key, value] of stateSnapshot) {
      this.merkleTree.update(key, value);
    }

    const stateHash = this.merkleTree.computeRoot();
    const totalBytes = Array.from(stateSnapshot.values())
      .reduce((s, v) => s + v.length, 0);

    const checkpoint: StateCheckpoint = {
      checkpointId: ResilienceCrypto.randomId(),
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      sequenceNumber: this.sequenceCounter++,
      stateHash,
      journalPosition: this.journal.size,
      keyCount: stateSnapshot.size,
      totalBytes,
      compressionRatio: 1.0, // in production: actual compression ratio
    };

    this.checkpoints.push(checkpoint);

    // Enforce max checkpoint limit (rotate old ones)
    if (this.checkpoints.length > this.config.maxCheckpoints) {
      this.checkpoints.shift();
    }

    this.journal.append('checkpoint', {
      checkpointId: checkpoint.checkpointId,
      sequenceNumber: checkpoint.sequenceNumber,
      stateHash,
      keyCount: stateSnapshot.size,
      totalBytes,
    });

    return checkpoint;
  }

  /**
   * Get the most recent valid checkpoint
   */
  getLastGoodCheckpoint(): StateCheckpoint | null {
    return this.checkpoints[this.checkpoints.length - 1] ?? null;
  }

  /**
   * Rollback to a specific checkpoint
   */
  async rollbackTo(checkpointId: string): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    checkpoint: StateCheckpoint | null;
    journalEntriesReplayed: number;
  }> {
    const checkpoint = this.checkpoints.find(c => c.checkpointId === checkpointId);
    if (!checkpoint) {
      return { success: false, checkpoint: null, journalEntriesReplayed: 0 };
    }

    this.journal.append('rollback', {
      action: 'rollback_start',
      targetCheckpoint: checkpointId,
      targetSequence: checkpoint.sequenceNumber,
    });

    // Replay journal entries from checkpoint position
    const replayEntries = this.journal.replayFrom(checkpoint.journalPosition);

    this.journal.append('rollback', {
      action: 'rollback_complete',
      targetCheckpoint: checkpointId,
      journalEntriesReplayed: replayEntries.length,
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      checkpoint,
      journalEntriesReplayed: replayEntries.length,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Verify checkpoint integrity
   */
  verifyCheckpoint(checkpointId: string, currentState: Map<string, Uint8Array>): {
    valid: boolean;
    mismatchedKeys: string[];
  } {
    const checkpoint = this.checkpoints.find(c => c.checkpointId === checkpointId);
    if (!checkpoint) return { valid: false, mismatchedKeys: [] };

    // Rebuild Merkle tree from current state and compare root
    const verifyTree = new MerkleIntegrityTree();
    for (const [key, value] of currentState) {
      verifyTree.update(key, value);
    }

    const currentRoot = verifyTree.computeRoot();
    const valid = currentRoot === checkpoint.stateHash;

    const mismatchedKeys: string[] = [];
    if (!valid) {
      // Find which keys differ
      for (const [key, value] of currentState) {
        const keyHash = ResilienceCrypto.hash(key + ':' + ResilienceCrypto.toHex(
          ResilienceCrypto.sha256(value)
        ));
        const storedHash = this.merkleTree['leaves'].get(key);
        if (storedHash !== keyHash) mismatchedKeys.push(key);
      }
    }

    return { valid, mismatchedKeys };
  }

  getCheckpoints(): StateCheckpoint[] { return [...this.checkpoints]; }
  getCheckpointCount(): number { return this.checkpoints.length; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// MEMORY INTEGRITY MONITOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class MemoryIntegrityMonitor {
  private blocks: Map<string, MemoryBlock>;
  private readonly journal: TamperEvidentJournal;
  private errorCount: number;

  constructor(journal: TamperEvidentJournal) {
    this.blocks = new Map();
    this.journal = journal;
    this.errorCount = 0;
  }

  /**
   * Register a memory region for integrity monitoring
   */
  registerBlock(address: number, data: Uint8Array): MemoryBlock {
    const blockId = ResilienceCrypto.randomId();
    const checksum = ResilienceCrypto.toHex(ResilienceCrypto.sha256(data));

    const block: MemoryBlock = {
      blockId,
      address,
      size: data.length,
      checksum,
      lastVerified: Date.now(),
      errorCount: 0,
    };

    this.blocks.set(address.toString(16), block);
    return block;
  }

  /**
   * Verify all registered memory blocks
   * Returns list of corrupted block addresses
   */
  verifyAll(readMemory: (address: number, size: number) => Uint8Array): {
    verified: number;
    corrupted: number;
    corruptedAddresses: number[];
  } {
    let verified = 0;
    let corrupted = 0;
    const corruptedAddresses: number[] = [];

    for (const [addrHex, block] of this.blocks) {
      const currentData = readMemory(block.address, block.size);
      const currentChecksum = ResilienceCrypto.toHex(ResilienceCrypto.sha256(currentData));

      if (currentChecksum !== block.checksum) {
        corrupted++;
        corruptedAddresses.push(block.address);
        block.errorCount++;
        this.errorCount++;

        this.journal.append('integrity_check', {
          type: 'memory_corruption',
          address: block.address,
          expectedChecksum: block.checksum,
          actualChecksum: currentChecksum,
          blockId: block.blockId,
        });
      } else {
        block.lastVerified = Date.now();
        verified++;
      }
    }

    return { verified, corrupted, corruptedAddresses };
  }

  /**
   * Update checksum after intentional write
   */
  updateChecksum(address: number, newData: Uint8Array): void {
    const block = this.blocks.get(address.toString(16));
    if (!block) return;
    block.checksum = ResilienceCrypto.toHex(ResilienceCrypto.sha256(newData));
    block.lastVerified = Date.now();
  }

  getTotalErrors(): number { return this.errorCount; }
  getBlockCount(): number { return this.blocks.size; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// SMART DISK HEALTH MONITOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class SMARTMonitor {
  private metrics: Map<string, SmartMetrics[]>; // diskId → history
  private readonly maxHistory = 50;
  private readonly journal: TamperEvidentJournal;

  constructor(journal: TamperEvidentJournal) {
    this.metrics = new Map();
    this.journal = journal;
  }

  /**
   * Record SMART metrics for a disk
   */
  recordMetrics(raw: Omit<SmartMetrics, 'healthScore' | 'predictedFailureDays'>): SmartMetrics {
    const healthScore = this.computeHealthScore(raw);
    const predictedFailureDays = this.predictFailure(raw.diskId, raw);

    const metrics: SmartMetrics = {
      ...raw,
      healthScore,
      predictedFailureDays,
    };

    if (!this.metrics.has(raw.diskId)) {
      this.metrics.set(raw.diskId, []);
    }

    const history = this.metrics.get(raw.diskId)!;
    history.push(metrics);
    if (history.length > this.maxHistory) history.shift();

    if (healthScore < 50) {
      this.journal.append('integrity_check', {
        type: 'smart_warning',
        diskId: raw.diskId,
        healthScore,
        predictedFailureDays,
        reallocatedSectors: raw.reallocatedSectors,
        uncorrectableErrors: raw.uncorrectableErrors,
      });
    }

    return metrics;
  }

  /**
   * Compute disk health score (0-100) from SMART attributes
   * Weighted combination of key failure indicators
   */
  private computeHealthScore(raw: Omit<SmartMetrics, 'healthScore' | 'predictedFailureDays'>): number {
    let score = 100;

    // Reallocated sectors: severe indicator
    if (raw.reallocatedSectors > 0) score -= Math.min(30, raw.reallocatedSectors * 3);
    // Pending sectors: moderate indicator
    if (raw.pendingSectors > 0) score -= Math.min(20, raw.pendingSectors * 2);
    // Uncorrectable errors: critical indicator
    if (raw.uncorrectableErrors > 0) score -= Math.min(40, raw.uncorrectableErrors * 10);
    // Spin retry: minor indicator
    if (raw.spinRetryCount > 0) score -= Math.min(10, raw.spinRetryCount);
    // Temperature deviation
    if (raw.temperature > 60) score -= Math.min(20, (raw.temperature - 60) * 2);
    // Age factor
    const yearsOld = raw.powerOnHours / 8760;
    if (yearsOld > 3) score -= Math.min(20, (yearsOld - 3) * 5);

    return Math.max(0, score);
  }

  /**
   * Predict time to failure using rate-of-change analysis
   */
  private predictFailure(diskId: string, current: Omit<SmartMetrics, 'healthScore' | 'predictedFailureDays'>): number | null {
    const history = this.metrics.get(diskId) ?? [];
    if (history.length < 5) return null;

    const recent = history.slice(-5);
    const scores = recent.map(m => m.healthScore);

    // Linear regression on health score
    const n = scores.length;
    const xMean = (n - 1) / 2;
    const yMean = scores.reduce((s, x) => s + x, 0) / n;

    let num = 0, den = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      num += (i - xMean) * (scores[i] - yMean);
      den += (i - xMean) ** 2;
    }

    if (den === 0) return null;
    const slope = num / den; // score change per reading

    if (slope >= 0) return null; // Improving or stable

    const currentScore = this.computeHealthScore(current);
    const readingsUntilFailure = currentScore / Math.abs(slope);
    const pollingIntervalDays = this.maxHistory > 0 ? 1 : 1; // assume daily polling
    return readingsUntilFailure * pollingIntervalDays;
  }

  getLatestMetrics(diskId: string): SmartMetrics | null {
    const history = this.metrics.get(diskId) ?? [];
    return history[history.length - 1] ?? null;
  }

  getAllDisksStatus(): Map<string, SmartMetrics | null> {
    const result = new Map<string, SmartMetrics | null>();
    for (const diskId of this.metrics.keys()) {
      result.set(diskId, this.getLatestMetrics(diskId));
    }
    return result;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PHYSICAL RESILIENCE ENGINE — Main orchestrator
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class PhysicalResilienceEngine {
  private readonly config: ResilienceConfig;
  private readonly journal: TamperEvidentJournal;
  private readonly merkleTree: MerkleIntegrityTree;
  readonly watchdog: WatchdogDaemon;
  readonly raid: RAIDMirrorManager;
  readonly thermal: ThermalMonitor;
  readonly checkpoints: CheckpointManager;
  readonly memory: MemoryIntegrityMonitor;
  readonly smart: SMARTMonitor;

  private overallHealth: HealthStatus;
  private recoveryInProgress: boolean;
  private activeAlerts: Set<string>;

  // Callbacks
  private onHealthChange?: (health: HealthStatus, snapshot: NodeHealthSnapshot) => void;
  private onRecoveryNeeded?: (plan: RecoveryPlan) => Promise<void>;
  private onPowerFailure?: () => Promise<void>;

  // Periodic timers
  private checkpointTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;
  private bitRotTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;
  private memoryCheckTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;
  private smartTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;

  constructor(config: Partial<ResilienceConfig> = {}) {
    this.config = { ...DEFAULT_RESILIENCE_CONFIG, ...config };
    this.journal = new TamperEvidentJournal();
    this.merkleTree = new MerkleIntegrityTree();
    this.watchdog = new WatchdogDaemon(this.config, this.journal);
    this.raid = new RAIDMirrorManager(this.config, this.journal);
    this.thermal = new ThermalMonitor(this.config, this.journal);
    this.checkpoints = new CheckpointManager(this.config, this.merkleTree, this.journal);
    this.memory = new MemoryIntegrityMonitor(this.journal);
    this.smart = new SMARTMonitor(this.journal);

    this.overallHealth = 'healthy';
    this.recoveryInProgress = false;
    this.activeAlerts = new Set();

    this.checkpointTimer = null;
    this.bitRotTimer = null;
    this.memoryCheckTimer = null;
    this.smartTimer = null;

    this.setupThermalCallbacks();
  }

  private setupThermalCallbacks(): void {
    this.thermal.onThrottleCallback(reading => {
      this.activeAlerts.add(`THERMAL_THROTTLE: CPU ${reading.cpuTempCelsius.toFixed(1)}°C`);
      this.evaluateHealth();
    });

    this.thermal.onEmergencyCallback(async reading => {
      this.activeAlerts.add(`THERMAL_EMERGENCY: CPU ${reading.cpuTempCelsius.toFixed(1)}°C — EMERGENCY SHUTDOWN`);
      this.overallHealth = 'critical';
      await this.initiateEmergencyShutdown('thermal_emergency');
    });
  }

  /**
   * Start all resilience subsystems
   */
  start(): void {
    // Arm watchdog
    this.watchdog.arm(async (state) => {
      await this.handleWatchdogTrigger(state);
    });

    // Checkpoint timer
    this.checkpointTimer = setInterval(async () => {
      // In production: read actual state from application
      await this.checkpoints.writeCheckpoint(new Map());
    }, this.config.checkpointIntervalMs);

    // Bit-rot scan timer
    this.bitRotTimer = setInterval(async () => {
      for (const disk of this.raid.getDisks()) {
        await this.raid.scanForBitRot(disk.diskId);
      }
    }, this.config.bitRotCheckIntervalMs);

    // Memory integrity timer
    this.memoryCheckTimer = setInterval(() => {
      this.memory.verifyAll((_address, size) => new Uint8Array(size)); // stub read
    }, this.config.memoryIntegrityCheckIntervalMs);

    // SMART polling timer
    this.smartTimer = setInterval(() => {
      this.pollSMARTMetrics();
    }, this.config.smartPollingIntervalMs);

    this.journal.append('recovery_start', {
      action: 'engine_started',
      nodeId: this.config.nodeId,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    });
  }

  /**
   * Stop all subsystems gracefully
   */
  stop(): void {
    this.watchdog.disarm();
    if (this.checkpointTimer) clearInterval(this.checkpointTimer);
    if (this.bitRotTimer) clearInterval(this.bitRotTimer);
    if (this.memoryCheckTimer) clearInterval(this.memoryCheckTimer);
    if (this.smartTimer) clearInterval(this.smartTimer);

    this.journal.append('recovery_complete', {
      action: 'engine_stopped',
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    });
  }

  /**
   * Called by node process on every operational cycle — keeps watchdog fed
   */
  heartbeat(): void {
    this.watchdog.kick();
    this.evaluateHealth();
  }

  /**
   * Handle detected power failure — execute graceful shutdown within grace period
   */
  async handlePowerFailure(): Promise<void> {
    this.journal.append('power_event', {
      type: 'power_failure_detected',
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      gracePeriodMs: this.config.powerFailureGracePeriodMs,
    });

    this.activeAlerts.add('POWER_FAILURE: Emergency flush initiated');

    // Flush all pending state within grace period
    const flushDeadline = Date.now() + this.config.powerFailureGracePeriodMs;

    // Write emergency checkpoint
    await this.checkpoints.writeCheckpoint(new Map());

    // Verify journal integrity
    const journalOk = this.journal.verify();
    if (!journalOk.valid) {
      this.journal.append('integrity_check', {
        type: 'journal_corruption_on_shutdown',
        firstCorrupted: journalOk.firstCorruptedIndex,
      });
    }

    // Sync RAID mirrors
    const mirrorStatus = this.raid.getArrayStatus();
    this.journal.append('power_event', {
      type: 'power_failure_shutdown_complete',
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      mirrorStatus,
      timeUsedMs: Date.now() - (flushDeadline - this.config.powerFailureGracePeriodMs),
    });

    await this.onPowerFailure?.();
  }

  /**
   * Recover from power failure — replay journal from last checkpoint
   */
  async recoverFromPowerFailure(): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    checkpoint: StateCheckpoint | null;
    durationMs: number;
  }> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    this.recoveryInProgress = true;

    this.journal.append('recovery_start', {
      type: 'power_failure_recovery_start',
      timestamp: startTime,
    });

    // Verify journal chain first
    const journalVerification = this.journal.verify();
    if (!journalVerification.valid) {
      // Journal corrupted — find last valid checkpoint
      const checkpoint = this.checkpoints.getLastGoodCheckpoint();
      if (checkpoint) {
        const result = await this.checkpoints.rollbackTo(checkpoint.checkpointId);
        const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;

        this.journal.append('recovery_complete', {
          type: 'power_failure_recovery_complete',
          success: result.success,
          usedCheckpoint: checkpoint.checkpointId,
          durationMs,
        });

        this.recoveryInProgress = false;
        return { success: result.success, checkpoint, durationMs };
      }

      this.recoveryInProgress = false;
      return { success: false, checkpoint: null, durationMs: Date.now() - startTime };
    }

    // Journal valid — replay from last checkpoint
    const checkpoint = this.checkpoints.getLastGoodCheckpoint();
    if (checkpoint) {
      const entriesToReplay = this.journal.replayFrom(checkpoint.journalPosition);

      // Apply journal entries to reconstruct state
      // In production: each entry type has a corresponding apply function
      this.journal.append('recovery_complete', {
        type: 'journal_replay_complete',
        entriesReplayed: entriesToReplay.length,
        fromCheckpoint: checkpoint.checkpointId,
      });
    }

    const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    this.recoveryInProgress = false;

    return {
      success: true,
      checkpoint: checkpoint,
      durationMs,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Generate and execute recovery plan when node is unhealthy
   */
  private async handleWatchdogTrigger(state: WatchdogState): Promise<void> {
    const plan = this.createRecoveryPlan('watchdog_timeout', state);
    await this.onRecoveryNeeded?.(plan);

    // Execute recovery steps
    for (const step of plan.steps) {
      await this.executeRecoveryStep(step);
    }
  }

  private createRecoveryPlan(reason: string, context: unknown): RecoveryPlan {
    const plan: RecoveryPlan = {
      planId: ResilienceCrypto.randomId(),
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      triggerReason: reason,
      estimatedDurationMs: 0,
      targetCheckpointId: this.checkpoints.getLastGoodCheckpoint()?.checkpointId ?? null,
      steps: [],
    };

    switch (reason) {
      case 'watchdog_timeout':
        plan.steps = [
          {
            stepId: '1',
            description: 'Write emergency checkpoint',
            action: 'none',
            prerequisites: [],
            timeoutMs: 5000,
          },
          {
            stepId: '2',
            description: 'Attempt soft restart of monitored process',
            action: 'soft_restart',
            prerequisites: ['1'],
            timeoutMs: 15000,
          },
          {
            stepId: '3',
            description: 'Hard restart if soft restart failed',
            action: 'hard_restart',
            prerequisites: ['2'],
            timeoutMs: 10000,
          },
        ];
        plan.estimatedDurationMs = 30000;
        break;

      case 'thermal_emergency':
        plan.steps = [
          {
            stepId: '1',
            description: 'Emergency checkpoint flush',
            action: 'none',
            prerequisites: [],
            timeoutMs: 2000,
          },
          {
            stepId: '2',
            description: 'Emergency shutdown',
            action: 'emergency_shutdown',
            prerequisites: ['1'],
            timeoutMs: 1000,
          },
        ];
        plan.estimatedDurationMs = 3000;
        break;

      case 'state_corruption':
        plan.steps = [
          {
            stepId: '1',
            description: 'Identify corruption extent',
            action: 'none',
            prerequisites: [],
            timeoutMs: 5000,
          },
          {
            stepId: '2',
            description: 'Roll back to last good checkpoint',
            action: 'rollback',
            prerequisites: ['1'],
            timeoutMs: 20000,
          },
        ];
        plan.estimatedDurationMs = 25000;
        break;
    }

    this.journal.append('recovery_start', {
      planId: plan.planId,
      reason,
      steps: plan.steps.length,
      estimatedDurationMs: plan.estimatedDurationMs,
    });

    return plan;
  }

  private async executeRecoveryStep(step: RecoveryStep): Promise<void> {
    this.journal.append('recovery_start', {
      action: 'executing_step',
      stepId: step.stepId,
      description: step.description,
      recoveryAction: step.action,
    });

    switch (step.action) {
      case 'rollback':
        const checkpoint = this.checkpoints.getLastGoodCheckpoint();
        if (checkpoint) await this.checkpoints.rollbackTo(checkpoint.checkpointId);
        break;

      case 'emergency_shutdown':
        await this.initiateEmergencyShutdown('recovery_plan');
        break;

      case 'soft_restart':
      case 'hard_restart':
        // Signal to OS/process manager
        this.journal.append('recovery_start', {
          action: step.action,
          stepId: step.stepId,
        });
        break;

      case 'throttle':
      case 'none':
        break;
    }
  }

  private async initiateEmergencyShutdown(reason: string): Promise<void> {
    this.journal.append('power_event', {
      type: 'emergency_shutdown',
      reason,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
    });

    // Last-ditch checkpoint
    await this.checkpoints.writeCheckpoint(new Map());

    // In production: signal OS to halt
  }

  private evaluateHealth(): void {
    const mirrorStatus = this.raid.getArrayStatus();
    const cpuTemp = this.thermal.getCurrentCPUTemp();
    const watchdogState = this.watchdog.getState();
    const memErrors = this.memory.getTotalErrors();

    let score = 100;
    const alerts: string[] = [];

    if (mirrorStatus === 'degraded') { score -= 20; alerts.push('RAID degraded'); }
    if (mirrorStatus === 'failed')   { score -= 50; alerts.push('RAID failed'); }
    if (cpuTemp > this.config.throttleThermalCelsius) {
      score -= 15;
      alerts.push(`CPU thermal throttle: ${cpuTemp.toFixed(1)}°C`);
    }
    if (watchdogState.consecutiveMisses > 2) {
      score -= 25;
      alerts.push(`Missed heartbeats: ${watchdogState.consecutiveMisses}`);
    }
    if (memErrors > 0) {
      score -= Math.min(30, memErrors * 5);
      alerts.push(`Memory errors: ${memErrors}`);
    }

    const prevHealth = this.overallHealth;

    if (score >= 80) this.overallHealth = 'healthy';
    else if (score >= 50) this.overallHealth = 'degraded';
    else if (score >= 20) this.overallHealth = 'critical';
    else this.overallHealth = 'failed';

    this.activeAlerts = new Set(alerts);

    if (prevHealth !== this.overallHealth && this.onHealthChange) {
      this.onHealthChange(this.overallHealth, this.getSnapshot(score, alerts));
    }
  }

  private pollSMARTMetrics(): void {
    for (const disk of this.raid.getDisks()) {
      // In production: read actual SMART data via OS interface
      this.smart.recordMetrics({
        diskId: disk.diskId,
        timestamp: Date.now(),
        reallocatedSectors: disk.errorCount,
        pendingSectors: 0,
        uncorrectableErrors: 0,
        powerOnHours: (Date.now() - disk.lastSyncTime) / 3600000,
        spinRetryCount: 0,
        temperature: 40,
      });
    }
  }

  private getSnapshot(score: number, alerts: string[]): NodeHealthSnapshot {
    const latestThermal = this.thermal.getLatestReading() ?? {
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      cpuTempCelsius: this.thermal.getCurrentCPUTemp(),
      gpuTempCelsius: 0,
      ambientTempCelsius: 0,
      storageTemp: {},
      fanSpeedRpm: {},
      thermalThrottleActive: this.thermal.isThrottleActive(),
      emergencyShutdownPending: this.thermal.isEmergencyPending(),
    };

    const lastCheckpoint = this.checkpoints.getLastGoodCheckpoint();

    return {
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      overallStatus: this.overallHealth,
      overallScore: score,
      thermal: latestThermal,
      cpuLoad: 0,
      memoryUsagePercent: 0,
      disks: [],
      mirrorStatus: this.raid.getArrayStatus(),
      watchdogArmed: this.watchdog.getState().armed,
      missedHeartbeats: this.watchdog.getState().missedHeartbeats,
      lastCheckpointAge: lastCheckpoint ? Date.now() - lastCheckpoint.timestamp : Infinity,
      journalSize: this.journal.size,
      recoveryReady: this.checkpoints.getCheckpointCount() > 0,
      activeAlerts: alerts,
    };
  }

  // ─── CALLBACKS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  onHealthChanged(cb: (health: HealthStatus, snapshot: NodeHealthSnapshot) => void): void {
    this.onHealthChange = cb;
  }

  onRecoveryRequired(cb: (plan: RecoveryPlan) => Promise<void>): void {
    this.onRecoveryNeeded = cb;
  }

  onPowerFailureDetected(cb: () => Promise<void>): void {
    this.onPowerFailure = cb;
  }

  // ─── STATUS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getHealthSnapshot(): NodeHealthSnapshot {
    const score = this.overallHealth === 'healthy' ? 95 :
                  this.overallHealth === 'degraded' ? 65 :
                  this.overallHealth === 'critical' ? 35 : 10;
    return this.getSnapshot(score, Array.from(this.activeAlerts));
  }

  verifyJournalIntegrity(): { valid: boolean; firstCorruptedIndex: number | null } {
    return this.journal.verify();
  }

  isRecoveryInProgress(): boolean { return this.recoveryInProgress; }
  getOverallHealth(): HealthStatus { return this.overallHealth; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// FACTORY & EXPORT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createResilienceEngine(
  nodeId: string,
  overrides: Partial<ResilienceConfig> = {}
): PhysicalResilienceEngine {
  return new PhysicalResilienceEngine({ nodeId, ...overrides });
}

export {
  ResilienceCrypto,
  TamperEvidentJournal,
  MerkleIntegrityTree,
};

export default PhysicalResilienceEngine;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — powerSovereignty.ts
 * Energy Sovereignty: Solar MPPT, Battery SOC, Load Shedding, RF Harvesting
 *
 * A node that runs out of power ceases to exist. Power sovereignty means the
 * node controls its own energy: harvests from solar/RF/thermal, stores in
 * batteries, budgets consumption by operation priority, and degrades gracefully
 * rather than dying suddenly. The mesh stays alive even when grid power is gone.
 *
 * Components:
 *   MPPTController       — Maximum Power Point Tracking for solar panels (P&O algorithm)
 *   BatteryMonitor       — State of Charge via Coulomb counting + Kalman filter
 *   LoadSheddingEngine   — Priority-based load shedding under power constraints
 *   RFEnergyHarvester    — Ambient RF energy harvesting from WiFi/cellular/broadcast
 *   ThermalHarvester     — Peltier/thermoelectric generation from temperature gradients
 *   PowerBudgetManager   — Per-operation energy accounting
 *   UPSController        — Seamless switchover between power sources
 *   PowerSovereigntyNode — Orchestrator: keeps node alive under any power condition
 */

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import * as crypto from 'crypto';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

const MPPT_PERTURBATION_STEP_V = 0.05;   // Voltage perturbation step (V)
const MPPT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_MS = 1_000;   // MPPT update interval
const BATTERY_CAPACITY_WH_DEFAULT = 100; // Wh (LiFePO4 100Wh default)
const BATTERY_CELL_NOMINAL_V = 3.2;      // LiFePO4 nominal voltage
const BATTERY_FULL_V = 3.65;             // LiFePO4 full charge voltage
const BATTERY_EMPTY_V = 2.80;            // LiFePO4 cutoff voltage
const COULOMB_EFFICIENCY = 0.98;         // Coulombic efficiency of LiFePO4
const KALMAN_PROCESS_NOISE = 0.001;      // SOC process noise variance
const KALMAN_MEASUREMENT_NOISE = 0.01;   // Voltage measurement noise variance
const THERMAL_THROTTLE_TEMP_C = 70;      // CPU throttle temperature
const THERMAL_SHUTDOWN_TEMP_C = 85;      // Emergency shutdown temperature
const RF_HARVEST_EFFICIENCY = 0.15;      // Typical RF-to-DC conversion efficiency
const LOAD_SHED_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 5_000;
const POWER_LOG_INTERVAL_MS = 60_000;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export type PowerSource = 'solar' | 'battery' | 'grid' | 'rf_harvest' | 'thermal' | 'wind' | 'usb';
export type BatteryChemistry = 'lifepo4' | 'li_ion' | 'lead_acid' | 'nimh' | 'supercap';
export type LoadClass = 'critical' | 'high' | 'normal' | 'low' | 'background' | 'idle';
export type PowerState = 'full' | 'normal' | 'conservation' | 'emergency' | 'critical' | 'shutdown';

export interface SolarPanel {
  id: string;
  peakWatts: number;          // Rated peak power (W)
  voc: number;                // Open circuit voltage (V)
  isc: number;                // Short circuit current (A)
  vmp: number;                // Max power point voltage (V)
  imp: number;                // Max power point current (A)
  tempCoeffPmax: number;      // Temperature coefficient of Pmax (%/°C)
  currentVoltage: number;     // Current operating voltage
  currentCurrent: number;     // Current operating current
  temperature: number;        // Panel temperature (°C)
}

export interface Battery {
  id: string;
  chemistry: BatteryChemistry;
  capacityWh: number;
  capacityAh: number;
  nominalVoltage: number;
  voltage: number;
  current: number;            // Positive = charging, negative = discharging
  temperature: number;
  cycleCount: number;
  stateOfCharge: number;      // 0.0–1.0
  stateOfHealth: number;      // 0.0–1.0 (1.0 = new)
  internalResistanceOhm: number;
}

export interface PowerLoad {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  class: LoadClass;
  nominalWatts: number;
  currentWatts: number;
  canShed: boolean;
  shedOrder: number;          // Lower = shed first
  enabled: boolean;
  minOnTimeMs: number;        // Must stay on at least this long
  lastToggle: number;
}

export interface PowerSnapshot {
  timestamp: number;
  source: PowerSource;
  solarWatts: number;
  batteryWatts: number;       // Positive = discharging
  loadWatts: number;
  rfHarvestWatts: number;
  thermalWatts: number;
  gridWatts: number;
  netWatts: number;           // Positive = charging
  batterySOC: number;
  batteryVoltage: number;
  batteryTemperature: number;
  powerState: PowerState;
  loadsActive: number;
  loadsShed: number;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// MPPT CONTROLLER — Perturb & Observe Algorithm
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class MPPTController extends EventEmitter {
  private panels: Map<string, SolarPanel> = new Map();
  private voltageSetpoints: Map<string, number> = new Map();
  private prevPower: Map<string, number> = new Map();
  private prevVoltage: Map<string, number> = new Map();
  private perturbDirection: Map<string, number> = new Map(); // +1 or -1
  private trackingTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private totalOutputWatts: number = 0;

  addPanel(panel: SolarPanel): void {
    this.panels.set(panel.id, panel);
    this.voltageSetpoints.set(panel.id, panel.vmp);
    this.prevPower.set(panel.id, 0);
    this.prevVoltage.set(panel.id, panel.vmp);
    this.perturbDirection.set(panel.id, 1);
  }

  startTracking(): void {
    this.trackingTimer = setInterval(() => this.updateAllPanels(), MPPT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_MS);
  }

  stopTracking(): void {
    if (this.trackingTimer) clearInterval(this.trackingTimer);
  }

  // P&O MPPT update for one panel
  updatePanel(panelId: string, measuredVoltage: number, measuredCurrent: number): void {
    const panel = this.panels.get(panelId);
    if (!panel) return;

    panel.currentVoltage = measuredVoltage;
    panel.currentCurrent = measuredCurrent;

    const currentPower = measuredVoltage * measuredCurrent;
    const prevP = this.prevPower.get(panelId) ?? 0;
    const prevV = this.prevVoltage.get(panelId) ?? panel.vmp;
    const direction = this.perturbDirection.get(panelId) ?? 1;

    const dP = currentPower - prevP;
    const dV = measuredVoltage - prevV;

    let newDirection = direction;
    if (Math.abs(dP) > 0.001) {
      // If power increased, continue same direction; if decreased, reverse
      newDirection = dP > 0 ? direction : -direction;
    }

    const newSetpoint = this.voltageSetpoints.get(panelId)! + newDirection * MPPT_PERTURBATION_STEP_V;
    const clampedSetpoint = Math.max(panel.vmp * 0.7, Math.min(panel.voc * 0.95, newSetpoint));

    this.voltageSetpoints.set(panelId, clampedSetpoint);
    this.prevPower.set(panelId, currentPower);
    this.prevVoltage.set(panelId, measuredVoltage);
    this.perturbDirection.set(panelId, newDirection);

    this.totalOutputWatts = this.computeTotalOutput();
    this.emit('mppt:update', { panelId, power: currentPower, voltage: measuredVoltage, setpoint: clampedSetpoint });
  }

  // Temperature correction for panel efficiency
  temperatureCorrect(panelId: string, temperature: number): void {
    const panel = this.panels.get(panelId);
    if (!panel) return;
    panel.temperature = temperature;
    // Standard test condition is 25°C
    const deltaT = temperature - 25;
    const correctedVmp = panel.vmp * (1 + panel.tempCoeffPmax / 100 * deltaT);
    // Adjust setpoint to compensate
    this.voltageSetpoints.set(panelId, correctedVmp);
  }

  getSetpoint(panelId: string): number {
    return this.voltageSetpoints.get(panelId) ?? 0;
  }

  getTotalOutputWatts(): number {
    return this.totalOutputWatts;
  }

  getEfficiency(panelId: string): number {
    const panel = this.panels.get(panelId);
    if (!panel) return 0;
    const actual = panel.currentVoltage * panel.currentCurrent;
    const theoretical = panel.peakWatts;
    return theoretical > 0 ? actual / theoretical : 0;
  }

  getIrradiance(panelId: string): number {
    // Estimate irradiance from current power vs rated power
    const panel = this.panels.get(panelId);
    if (!panel) return 0;
    const power = panel.currentVoltage * panel.currentCurrent;
    return (power / panel.peakWatts) * 1000; // W/m²
  }

  private updateAllPanels(): void {
    for (const panel of this.panels.values()) {
      // In production: read ADC for voltage and current
      // Simulation: slight random variation around MPP
      const irradiance = 800 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 200;
      const simVoltage = panel.vmp * (0.95 + Math.random() * 0.05);
      const simCurrent = panel.imp * (irradiance / 1000) * (0.9 + Math.random() * 0.1);
      this.updatePanel(panel.id, simVoltage, simCurrent);
    }
  }

  private computeTotalOutput(): number {
    let total = 0;
    for (const panel of this.panels.values()) {
      total += panel.currentVoltage * panel.currentCurrent;
    }
    return total;
  }

  panelCount(): number { return this.panels.size; }
  allPanels(): SolarPanel[] { return Array.from(this.panels.values()); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// BATTERY MONITOR — Coulomb Counting + Kalman Filter SOC
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface KalmanState {
  soc: number;           // State of charge estimate
  variance: number;      // Estimation uncertainty
}

export class BatteryMonitor extends EventEmitter {
  private batteries: Map<string, Battery> = new Map();
  private kalmanStates: Map<string, KalmanState> = new Map();
  private lastSampleTime: Map<string, number> = new Map();
  private monitorTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  addBattery(battery: Battery): void {
    this.batteries.set(battery.id, battery);
    this.kalmanStates.set(battery.id, { soc: battery.stateOfCharge, variance: 0.1 });
    this.lastSampleTime.set(battery.id, Date.now());
  }

  update(batteryId: string, voltage: number, current: number, temperature: number): void {
    const battery = this.batteries.get(batteryId);
    if (!battery) return;

    const now = Date.now();
    const dtHours = (now - (this.lastSampleTime.get(batteryId) ?? now)) / 3_600_000;
    this.lastSampleTime.set(batteryId, now);

    battery.voltage = voltage;
    battery.current = current;
    battery.temperature = temperature;

    // Coulomb counting: integrate current over time
    const coulombDelta = (current * dtHours * COULOMB_EFFICIENCY) / battery.capacityAh;
    const coulombSOC = battery.stateOfCharge + coulombDelta;

    // OCV-based SOC estimate (Open Circuit Voltage method)
    const ocvSOC = this.ocvToSOC(battery.chemistry, voltage + current * battery.internalResistanceOhm);

    // Kalman filter fusion
    const kalman = this.kalmanStates.get(batteryId)!;
    const predicted = kalman.soc + coulombDelta;
    const predictedVariance = kalman.variance + KALMAN_PROCESS_NOISE;

    // Kalman gain
    const K = predictedVariance / (predictedVariance + KALMAN_MEASUREMENT_NOISE);
    const updated = predicted + K * (ocvSOC - predicted);
    const updatedVariance = (1 - K) * predictedVariance;

    this.kalmanStates.set(batteryId, { soc: updated, variance: updatedVariance });
    battery.stateOfCharge = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, updated));

    // Health estimation: capacity fade from cycle count
    battery.stateOfHealth = Math.max(0.5, 1 - battery.cycleCount * 0.0001);

    // Internal resistance estimation via voltage sag
    if (Math.abs(current) > 0.1) {
      const sagV = Math.abs(voltage - this.ocvFromSOC(battery.chemistry, battery.stateOfCharge));
      battery.internalResistanceOhm = sagV / Math.abs(current);
    }

    this.emit('battery:update', {
      id: batteryId,
      soc: battery.stateOfCharge,
      voltage,
      current,
      temperature,
      stateOfHealth: battery.stateOfHealth,
    });

    // Thermal protection
    if (temperature > THERMAL_THROTTLE_TEMP_C) {
      this.emit('battery:thermal_warning', { id: batteryId, temperature });
    }
    if (temperature > THERMAL_SHUTDOWN_TEMP_C) {
      this.emit('battery:thermal_critical', { id: batteryId, temperature });
    }
  }

  estimateRemainingHours(batteryId: string, loadWatts: number): number {
    const battery = this.batteries.get(batteryId);
    if (!battery || loadWatts <= 0) return Infinity;
    const availableWh = battery.stateOfCharge * battery.capacityWh * battery.stateOfHealth;
    return availableWh / loadWatts;
  }

  getSOC(batteryId: string): number {
    return this.batteries.get(batteryId)?.stateOfCharge ?? 0;
  }

  getTotalSOC(): number {
    const batteries = Array.from(this.batteries.values());
    if (batteries.length === 0) return 0;
    const totalWh = batteries.reduce((s, b) => s + b.capacityWh, 0);
    const availWh = batteries.reduce((s, b) => s + b.stateOfCharge * b.capacityWh, 0);
    return totalWh > 0 ? availWh / totalWh : 0;
  }

  getAggregatedCapacityWh(): number {
    return Array.from(this.batteries.values()).reduce((s, b) => s + b.capacityWh * b.stateOfHealth, 0);
  }

  startMonitoring(): void {
    this.monitorTimer = setInterval(() => {
      for (const battery of this.batteries.values()) {
        // In production: read from ADC/BMS via I2C/SPI
        this.emit('battery:poll', { id: battery.id });
      }
    }, 5_000);
  }

  stopMonitoring(): void {
    if (this.monitorTimer) clearInterval(this.monitorTimer);
  }

  // OCV → SOC lookup table (LiFePO4 characteristic curve)
  private ocvToSOC(chemistry: BatteryChemistry, voltage: number): number {
    const curves: Record<BatteryChemistry, Array<[number, number]>> = {
      lifepo4: [
        [2.80, 0.00], [3.00, 0.05], [3.10, 0.10], [3.20, 0.20],
        [3.25, 0.40], [3.30, 0.60], [3.35, 0.80], [3.45, 0.95], [3.65, 1.00],
      ],
      li_ion: [
        [3.00, 0.00], [3.30, 0.10], [3.60, 0.30], [3.80, 0.60],
        [4.00, 0.80], [4.10, 0.90], [4.20, 1.00],
      ],
      lead_acid: [
        [11.8, 0.00], [12.0, 0.20], [12.2, 0.40], [12.4, 0.60],
        [12.6, 0.80], [12.7, 0.90], [12.8, 1.00],
      ],
      nimh: [[1.0, 0.0], [1.2, 0.5], [1.4, 1.0]],
      supercap: [[0.0, 0.0], [2.7, 1.0]],
    };

    const curve = curves[chemistry] ?? curves.lifepo4;
    if (voltage <= curve[0][0]) return 0;
    if (voltage >= curve[curve.length - 1][0]) return 1;

    for (let i = 0; i < curve.length - 1; i++) {
      if (voltage >= curve[i][0] && voltage < curve[i + 1][0]) {
        const t = (voltage - curve[i][0]) / (curve[i + 1][0] - curve[i][0]);
        return curve[i][1] + t * (curve[i + 1][1] - curve[i][1]);
      }
    }
    return 0.5;
  }

  private ocvFromSOC(chemistry: BatteryChemistry, soc: number): number {
    const lifepo4: Array<[number, number]> = [
      [0.00, 2.80], [0.05, 3.00], [0.10, 3.10], [0.20, 3.20],
      [0.40, 3.25], [0.60, 3.30], [0.80, 3.35], [0.95, 3.45], [1.00, 3.65],
    ];
    const curve = chemistry === 'lifepo4' ? lifepo4 : lifepo4;
    for (let i = 0; i < curve.length - 1; i++) {
      if (soc >= curve[i][0] && soc < curve[i + 1][0]) {
        const t = (soc - curve[i][0]) / (curve[i + 1][0] - curve[i][0]);
        return curve[i][1] + t * (curve[i + 1][1] - curve[i][1]);
      }
    }
    return 3.2;
  }

  allBatteries(): Battery[] { return Array.from(this.batteries.values()); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// RF ENERGY HARVESTER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface RFSource {
  frequency: number;    // Hz
  powerDensity: number; // μW/cm²
  type: 'wifi' | 'cellular' | 'broadcast_tv' | 'broadcast_fm' | 'gsm' | 'lte' | '5g';
  antennaSizeM2: number;
}

export interface HarvestResult {
  totalPowerUW: number;   // Microwatts
  totalPowerMW: number;   // Milliwatts
  sources: Array<{ type: string; powerUW: number }>;
  efficiency: number;
  storedEnergy: boolean;
}

export class RFEnergyHarvester extends EventEmitter {
  private sources: RFSource[] = [];
  private harvestedTodayUJ: number = 0;  // Microjoules
  private rectifierEfficiency: number = RF_HARVEST_EFFICIENCY;
  private storageSuperCapacitorUF: number = 1_000; // 1mF storage capacitor
  private storagVoltage: number = 0;
  private readonly storedMaxV: number = 5.0;

  addSource(source: RFSource): void { this.sources.push(source); }

  harvest(): HarvestResult {
    const sourceResults: Array<{ type: string; powerUW: number }> = [];
    let totalPowerUW = 0;

    for (const source of this.sources) {
      // Friis transmission: P_rx = P_tx * G_tx * G_rx * (λ/(4πd))²
      // Simplified: use measured power density directly
      const capturedPowerUW = source.powerDensity * source.antennaSizeM2 * 10_000; // Convert cm² to m²
      const harvestedUW = capturedPowerUW * this.rectifierEfficiency;
      sourceResults.push({ type: source.type, powerUW: harvestedUW });
      totalPowerUW += harvestedUW;
    }

    // Store in supercapacitor: E = ½CV²
    const harvestedJoules = (totalPowerUW * 1e-6) * (MPPT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_MS / 1000);
    const currentEnergy = 0.5 * (this.storageSuperCapacitorUF * 1e-6) * this.storedVoltage ** 2;
    const newEnergy = currentEnergy + harvestedJoules;
    this.storedVoltage = Math.min(
      this.storedMaxV,
      Math.sqrt((2 * newEnergy) / (this.storageSuperCapacitorUF * 1e-6)),
    );

    this.harvestedTodayUJ += totalPowerUW * (MPPT_SAMPLE_INTERVAL_MS / 1000);
    const stored = this.storedVoltage >= 3.0; // Enough to power MCU

    this.emit('harvest', { totalPowerUW, storedVoltage: this.storedVoltage });
    return {
