      totalPowerUW,
      totalPowerMW: totalPowerUW / 1000,
      sources: sourceResults,
      efficiency: this.rectifierEfficiency,
      storedEnergy: stored,
    };
  }

  getStoredVoltage(): number { return this.storedVoltage; }
  getStoredEnergyUJ(): number {
    return 0.5 * (this.storageSuperCapacitorUF * 1e-6) * this.storedVoltage ** 2 * 1e6;
  }
  getHarvestedTodayUJ(): number { return this.harvestedTodayUJ; }

  private get storedVoltage(): number { return this.storagVoltage; }
  private set storedVoltage(v: number) { this.storagVoltage = v; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LOAD SHEDDING ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ShedEvent {
  loadId: string;
  action: 'shed' | 'restore';
  reason: PowerState;
  timestamp: number;
  previousWatts: number;
}

export class LoadSheddingEngine extends EventEmitter {
  private loads: Map<string, PowerLoad> = new Map();
  private shedHistory: ShedEvent[] = [];

  addLoad(load: PowerLoad): void {
    this.loads.set(load.id, load);
  }

  updateLoad(loadId: string, currentWatts: number): void {
    const load = this.loads.get(loadId);
    if (load) load.currentWatts = currentWatts;
  }

  // Determine which loads to shed for a given power state
  computeShedding(powerState: PowerState, availableWatts: number): {
    toShed: PowerLoad[];
    toRestore: PowerLoad[];
    projectedLoad: number;
  } {
    const now = Date.now();
    const thresholds: Record<PowerState, { maxLoadFraction: number }> = {
      full: { maxLoadFraction: 1.0 },
      normal: { maxLoadFraction: 0.9 },
      conservation: { maxLoadFraction: 0.6 },
      emergency: { maxLoadFraction: 0.3 },
      critical: { maxLoadFraction: 0.15 },
      shutdown: { maxLoadFraction: 0.05 },
    };

    const maxLoad = availableWatts * thresholds[powerState].maxLoadFraction;
    const allLoads = Array.from(this.loads.values()).sort((a, b) => a.shedOrder - b.shedOrder);

    let totalLoad = 0;
    const toShed: PowerLoad[] = [];
    const toRestore: PowerLoad[] = [];

    for (const load of allLoads) {
      const canToggle = now - load.lastToggle > load.minOnTimeMs;

      if (load.enabled) {
        if (totalLoad + load.currentWatts <= maxLoad) {
          totalLoad += load.currentWatts;
        } else if (load.canShed && canToggle && powerState !== 'full' && powerState !== 'normal') {
          toShed.push(load);
        } else {
          totalLoad += load.currentWatts;
        }
      } else {
        // Consider restoring if we have headroom
        if (totalLoad + load.nominalWatts <= maxLoad * 0.85 && powerState !== 'critical' && powerState !== 'shutdown') {
          toRestore.push(load);
          totalLoad += load.nominalWatts;
        }
      }
    }

    return { toShed, toRestore, projectedLoad: totalLoad };
  }

  applyShedding(toShed: PowerLoad[], toRestore: PowerLoad[], reason: PowerState): void {
    const now = Date.now();

    for (const load of toShed) {
      if (load.enabled) {
        this.shedHistory.push({ loadId: load.id, action: 'shed', reason, timestamp: now, previousWatts: load.currentWatts });
        load.enabled = false;
        load.lastToggle = now;
        this.emit('load:shed', { load, reason });
      }
    }

    for (const load of toRestore) {
      if (!load.enabled) {
        this.shedHistory.push({ loadId: load.id, action: 'restore', reason, timestamp: now, previousWatts: 0 });
        load.enabled = true;
        load.lastToggle = now;
        this.emit('load:restore', { load, reason });
      }
    }
  }

  getTotalLoad(): number {
    return Array.from(this.loads.values()).filter(l => l.enabled).reduce((s, l) => s + l.currentWatts, 0);
  }

  getEnabledLoads(): PowerLoad[] {
    return Array.from(this.loads.values()).filter(l => l.enabled);
  }

  getShedLoads(): PowerLoad[] {
    return Array.from(this.loads.values()).filter(l => !l.enabled);
  }

  getShedHistory(limit: number = 100): ShedEvent[] {
    return this.shedHistory.slice(-limit);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// POWER BUDGET MANAGER — Per-operation energy accounting
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface OperationEnergyCost {
  operationId: string;
  wattsTypical: number;
  durationMs: number;
  priority: LoadClass;
  canDefer: boolean;
}

export interface EnergyBudget {
  availableWh: number;
  reservedWh: number;
  spendableWh: number;
  operations: Map<string, number>; // operationId -> Wh spent
}

export class PowerBudgetManager {
  private costs: Map<string, OperationEnergyCost> = new Map();
  private spentWh: Map<string, number> = new Map();
  private totalCapacityWh: number;
  private reserveFraction: number = 0.20; // Keep 20% reserved

  constructor(totalCapacityWh: number) {
    this.totalCapacityWh = totalCapacityWh;
  }

  registerOperation(cost: OperationEnergyCost): void {
    this.costs.set(cost.operationId, cost);
  }

  canAfford(operationId: string, currentSOC: number): boolean {
    const cost = this.costs.get(operationId);
    if (!cost) return true;

    const availableWh = currentSOC * this.totalCapacityWh * (1 - this.reserveFraction);
    const costWh = (cost.wattsTypical * cost.durationMs) / 3_600_000;
    return availableWh >= costWh;
  }

  recordUsage(operationId: string, actualWatts: number, durationMs: number): void {
    const usedWh = (actualWatts * durationMs) / 3_600_000;
    const prev = this.spentWh.get(operationId) ?? 0;
    this.spentWh.set(operationId, prev + usedWh);
  }

  getBudget(currentSOC: number): EnergyBudget {
    const available = currentSOC * this.totalCapacityWh;
    const reserved = this.totalCapacityWh * this.reserveFraction;
    return {
      availableWh: available,
      reservedWh: reserved,
      spendableWh: Math.max(0, available - reserved),
      operations: new Map(this.spentWh),
    };
  }

  estimateBatteryLife(currentSOC: number, loadWatts: number): number {
    const available = currentSOC * this.totalCapacityWh;
    return loadWatts > 0 ? available / loadWatts : Infinity;
  }

  setTotalCapacity(wh: number): void {
    this.totalCapacityWh = wh;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// UPS CONTROLLER — Seamless source switchover
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export type SwitchoverReason = 'grid_fail' | 'battery_low' | 'solar_available' | 'manual' | 'optimization';

export interface SwitchoverEvent {
  from: PowerSource;
  to: PowerSource;
  reason: SwitchoverReason;
  timestamp: number;
  batterySOC: number;
  loadWatts: number;
}

export class UPSController extends EventEmitter {
  private currentSource: PowerSource = 'grid';
  private availableSources: Set<PowerSource> = new Set();
  private switchoverHistory: SwitchoverEvent[] = [];
  private readonly switchoverDelayMs: number = 20; // 20ms transfer time (UPS grade)

  declare(source: PowerSource, available: boolean): void {
    if (available) this.availableSources.add(source);
    else this.availableSources.delete(source);
  }

  async switchTo(
    source: PowerSource,
    reason: SwitchoverReason,
    batterySOC: number,
    loadWatts: number,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!this.availableSources.has(source)) return false;
    if (source === this.currentSource) return true;

    const from = this.currentSource;
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, this.switchoverDelayMs));
    this.currentSource = source;

    const event: SwitchoverEvent = { from, to: source, reason, timestamp: Date.now(), batterySOC, loadWatts };
    this.switchoverHistory.push(event);
    this.emit('switchover', event);
    return true;
  }

  selectBestSource(solarWatts: number, batterySOC: number): PowerSource {
    if (this.availableSources.has('grid')) return 'grid';
    if (solarWatts > 5 && this.availableSources.has('solar')) return 'solar';
    if (batterySOC > 0.1 && this.availableSources.has('battery')) return 'battery';
    if (this.availableSources.has('rf_harvest')) return 'rf_harvest';
    if (this.availableSources.has('thermal')) return 'thermal';
    return 'battery'; // Last resort
  }

  getCurrentSource(): PowerSource { return this.currentSource; }
  getHistory(): SwitchoverEvent[] { return [...this.switchoverHistory]; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// POWER STATE MACHINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

function socToPowerState(soc: number, inputWatts: number, loadWatts: number): PowerState {
  const netWatts = inputWatts - loadWatts;
  if (soc >= 0.80 || (soc >= 0.50 && netWatts > 0)) return 'full';
  if (soc >= 0.50) return 'normal';
  if (soc >= 0.30) return 'conservation';
  if (soc >= 0.15) return 'emergency';
  if (soc >= 0.05) return 'critical';
  return 'shutdown';
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// THERMAL HARVESTER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class ThermalHarvester extends EventEmitter {
  private hotSideTemp: number = 60;  // °C (e.g., CPU heat sink)
  private coldSideTemp: number = 25; // °C (ambient)
  private readonly seebeckCoefficient: number = 0.05; // V/K (typical thermoelectric)
  private readonly internalResistanceOhm: number = 3.0;
  private readonly thermalResistance: number = 0.5; // K/W

  update(hotTemp: number, coldTemp: number): void {
    this.hotSideTemp = hotTemp;
    this.coldSideTemp = coldTemp;
    this.emit('thermal:update', { hot: hotTemp, cold: coldTemp, power: this.getOutputWatts() });
  }

  getOpenCircuitVoltage(): number {
    return this.seebeckCoefficient * (this.hotSideTemp - this.coldSideTemp);
  }

  getMaxOutputWatts(): number {
    const voc = this.getOpenCircuitVoltage();
    // Maximum power transfer: load R = internal R
    return (voc * voc) / (4 * this.internalResistanceOhm);
  }

  getOutputWatts(): number {
    return this.getMaxOutputWatts() * 0.7; // Practical efficiency
  }

  getCarnotEfficiency(): number {
    const hotK = this.hotSideTemp + 273.15;
    const coldK = this.coldSideTemp + 273.15;
    return 1 - coldK / hotK;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// POWER SOVEREIGNTY NODE — Main Orchestrator
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface PowerSovereigntyConfig {
  panels?: SolarPanel[];
  batteries?: Battery[];
  loads?: PowerLoad[];
  enableRFHarvest?: boolean;
  enableThermal?: boolean;
  rfSources?: RFSource[];
  monthlyBatteryBudgetWh?: number;
}

export interface PowerSovereigntyStatus {
  powerState: PowerState;
  activeSource: PowerSource;
  solarWatts: number;
  loadWatts: number;
  batterySOC: number;
  batteryVoltage: number;
  batteryHoursRemaining: number;
  rfHarvestMW: number;
  thermalWatts: number;
  loadsActive: number;
  loadsShed: number;
  powerSelfSufficient: boolean;
  snapshots: PowerSnapshot[];
}

export class PowerSovereigntyNode extends EventEmitter {
  public readonly mppt: MPPTController;
  public readonly batteryMonitor: BatteryMonitor;
  public readonly loadShedder: LoadSheddingEngine;
  public readonly rfHarvester: RFEnergyHarvester;
  public readonly thermalHarvester: ThermalHarvester;
  public readonly budgetManager: PowerBudgetManager;
  public readonly ups: UPSController;

  private powerState: PowerState = 'normal';
  private snapshots: PowerSnapshot[] = [];
  private running: boolean = false;

  private shedCheckTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private logTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  constructor(config: PowerSovereigntyConfig) {
    super();
    this.mppt = new MPPTController();
    this.batteryMonitor = new BatteryMonitor();
    this.loadShedder = new LoadSheddingEngine();
    this.rfHarvester = new RFEnergyHarvester();
    this.thermalHarvester = new ThermalHarvester();
    this.budgetManager = new PowerBudgetManager(config.monthlyBatteryBudgetWh ?? BATTERY_CAPACITY_WH_DEFAULT);
    this.ups = new UPSController();

    // Register configured components
    if (config.panels) config.panels.forEach(p => this.mppt.addPanel(p));
    if (config.batteries) config.batteries.forEach(b => { this.batteryMonitor.addBattery(b); this.ups.declare('battery', true); });
    if (config.loads) config.loads.forEach(l => this.loadShedder.addLoad(l));
    if (config.rfSources) config.rfSources.forEach(s => this.rfHarvester.addSource(s));

    if (config.panels && config.panels.length > 0) this.ups.declare('solar', true);
    if (config.enableRFHarvest) this.ups.declare('rf_harvest', true);
    if (config.enableThermal) this.ups.declare('thermal', true);

    this.registerDefaultOperations();
    this.wireEvents();
  }

  start(): void {
    this.mppt.startTracking();
    this.batteryMonitor.startMonitoring();
    this.running = true;

    this.shedCheckTimer = setInterval(() => this.runPowerCycle(), LOAD_SHED_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS);
    this.logTimer = setInterval(() => this.logSnapshot(), POWER_LOG_INTERVAL_MS);

    this.emit('started');
  }

  stop(): void {
    this.running = false;
    this.mppt.stopTracking();
    this.batteryMonitor.stopMonitoring();
    if (this.shedCheckTimer) clearInterval(this.shedCheckTimer);
    if (this.logTimer) clearInterval(this.logTimer);
    this.emit('stopped');
  }

  canRun(operationId: string): boolean {
    const soc = this.batteryMonitor.getTotalSOC();
    return this.budgetManager.canAfford(operationId, soc);
  }

  recordOperationUsage(operationId: string, watts: number, durationMs: number): void {
    this.budgetManager.recordUsage(operationId, watts, durationMs);
  }

  getStatus(): PowerSovereigntyStatus {
    const soc = this.batteryMonitor.getTotalSOC();
    const battVoltage = this.batteryMonitor.allBatteries()[0]?.voltage ?? 0;
    const solarWatts = this.mppt.getTotalOutputWatts();
    const loadWatts = this.loadShedder.getTotalLoad();
    const rfResult = this.rfHarvester.harvest();
    const thermalWatts = this.thermalHarvester.getOutputWatts();

    return {
      powerState: this.powerState,
      activeSource: this.ups.getCurrentSource(),
      solarWatts,
      loadWatts,
      batterySOC: soc,
      batteryVoltage: battVoltage,
      batteryHoursRemaining: this.batteryMonitor.allBatteries()[0]
        ? this.batteryMonitor.estimateRemainingHours(this.batteryMonitor.allBatteries()[0].id, loadWatts)
        : 0,
      rfHarvestMW: rfResult.totalPowerMW,
      thermalWatts,
      loadsActive: this.loadShedder.getEnabledLoads().length,
      loadsShed: this.loadShedder.getShedLoads().length,
      powerSelfSufficient: solarWatts + rfResult.totalPowerMW / 1000 + thermalWatts >= loadWatts,
      snapshots: this.snapshots.slice(-60),
    };
  }

  private runPowerCycle(): void {
    const soc = this.batteryMonitor.getTotalSOC();
    const solarWatts = this.mppt.getTotalOutputWatts();
    const loadWatts = this.loadShedder.getTotalLoad();
    const inputWatts = solarWatts;

    const newState = socToPowerState(soc, inputWatts, loadWatts);

    if (newState !== this.powerState) {
      const prev = this.powerState;
      this.powerState = newState;
      this.emit('power_state:change', { from: prev, to: newState, soc, solarWatts, loadWatts });
    }

    // Run load shedding
    const { toShed, toRestore } = this.loadShedder.computeShedding(this.powerState, inputWatts || soc * 20);
    if (toShed.length > 0 || toRestore.length > 0) {
      this.loadShedder.applyShedding(toShed, toRestore, this.powerState);
    }

    // Switchover if needed
    const bestSource = this.ups.selectBestSource(solarWatts, soc);
    if (bestSource !== this.ups.getCurrentSource()) {
      this.ups.switchTo(bestSource, 'optimization', soc, loadWatts);
    }

    if (this.powerState === 'shutdown') {
      this.emit('power:shutdown_imminent', { soc, hoursRemaining: 0 });
    }
  }

  private logSnapshot(): void {
    const s = this.getStatus();
    const rf = this.rfHarvester.harvest();
    const snap: PowerSnapshot = {
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      source: s.activeSource,
      solarWatts: s.solarWatts,
      batteryWatts: s.batterySOC * 10,
      loadWatts: s.loadWatts,
      rfHarvestWatts: rf.totalPowerMW / 1000,
      thermalWatts: s.thermalWatts,
      gridWatts: 0,
      netWatts: s.solarWatts - s.loadWatts,
      batterySOC: s.batterySOC,
      batteryVoltage: s.batteryVoltage,
      batteryTemperature: this.batteryMonitor.allBatteries()[0]?.temperature ?? 25,
      powerState: this.powerState,
      loadsActive: s.loadsActive,
      loadsShed: s.loadsShed,
    };
    this.snapshots.push(snap);
    if (this.snapshots.length > 1440) this.snapshots.shift(); // Keep 24h at 1-min intervals
    this.emit('snapshot', snap);
  }

  private registerDefaultOperations(): void {
    const ops: OperationEnergyCost[] = [
      { operationId: 'mesh_tx', wattsTypical: 2.5, durationMs: 100, priority: 'high', canDefer: false },
      { operationId: 'mesh_rx', wattsTypical: 0.5, durationMs: 1000, priority: 'critical', canDefer: false },
      { operationId: 'lora_tx', wattsTypical: 0.8, durationMs: 500, priority: 'high', canDefer: true },
      { operationId: 'crypto_op', wattsTypical: 1.5, durationMs: 50, priority: 'normal', canDefer: true },
      { operationId: 'consensus', wattsTypical: 3.0, durationMs: 2000, priority: 'high', canDefer: false },
      { operationId: 'dht_lookup', wattsTypical: 1.0, durationMs: 5000, priority: 'normal', canDefer: true },
      { operationId: 'satellite_tx', wattsTypical: 5.0, durationMs: 10000, priority: 'low', canDefer: true },
    ];
    ops.forEach(op => this.budgetManager.registerOperation(op));
  }

  private wireEvents(): void {
    this.batteryMonitor.on('battery:thermal_critical', (data) => {
      this.emit('emergency:thermal', data);
      this.powerState = 'shutdown';
    });
    this.loadShedder.on('load:shed', (data) => this.emit('load:shed', data));
    this.loadShedder.on('load:restore', (data) => this.emit('load:restore', data));
    this.ups.on('switchover', (event) => this.emit('power:switchover', event));
  }
}

export default PowerSovereigntyNode;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — rfFingerprinting.ts
 * RF Hardware Fingerprint Defense Engine
 *
 * Implements:
 *   - IQ imbalance detection and randomized compensation (amplitude + phase)
 *   - Carrier Frequency Offset (CFO) obfuscation via dithering
 *   - Phase noise profile masking with artificial noise injection
 *   - Transmitter turn-on/turn-off transient randomization
 *   - Spurious emission pattern rotation
 *   - Per-transmission hardware identity rotation (appears as different radio each TX)
 *   - RF fingerprint database for passive detection of adversary radios
 *   - Statistical signature extraction (spectral skewness, kurtosis, entropy)
 *   - Constellation rotation and non-linearity compensation
 *   - Adversarial fingerprint injection to misdirect SIGINT/COMINT analysis
 *
 * Design contract:
 *   - Defeats passive radio fingerprinting used by state-level SIGINT
 *   - Each transmission appears as a different physical device
 *   - Does not degrade communication performance (transparent compensation)
 *   - Integrates with jammingDefense.ts and loraMesh.ts
 *
 * @module rfFingerprinting
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES & INTERFACES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type FingerprintFeature =
  | 'iq_imbalance'
  | 'cfo'
  | 'phase_noise'
  | 'transient'
  | 'spectral_flatness'
  | 'power_ramp'
  | 'constellation_distortion'
  | 'harmonic_content';

export type ObfuscationStrategy =
  | 'randomize'          // Random values each transmission
  | 'rotate'             // Cycle through a set of synthetic identities
  | 'spoof_known'        // Mimic a known-good commercial device
  | 'adaptive';          // Change based on detected surveillance

export interface RFDefenseConfig {
  nodeId: string;
  // IQ imbalance parameters
  iqAmplitudeImbalanceDbRange: [number, number];  // e.g. [-0.5, 0.5] dB
  iqPhaseImbalanceDegRange: [number, number];      // e.g. [-2.0, 2.0] degrees
  // CFO parameters
  cfoObfuscationPpmRange: [number, number];        // e.g. [-5.0, 5.0] ppm
  cfoDitherStepPpm: number;                        // Step size for CFO dithering
  // Phase noise
  phaseNoiseFloorDbc: number;                      // e.g. -130 dBc/Hz at 1kHz offset
  phaseNoiseInjectionDbc: number;                  // Additional injected noise
  // Transient
  powerRampUs: number;                             // Microseconds for power ramp
  powerRampJitterUs: number;                       // ±jitter on ramp timing
  // Identity rotation
  numSyntheticIdentities: number;                  // Pool of fake device profiles
  identityRotationInterval: number;                // Transmissions between rotations
  // Detection
  fingerprintDatabaseSize: number;                 // Max known fingerprints to track
  fingerprintMatchThreshold: number;               // Correlation threshold for match
  // Injection
  enableAdversarialInjection: boolean;             // Inject known-device signatures
  spoofTargetProfile: string | null;               // Device profile to impersonate
}

export const DEFAULT_RF_DEFENSE_CONFIG: RFDefenseConfig = {
  nodeId: 'node-0',
  iqAmplitudeImbalanceDbRange: [-0.5, 0.5],
  iqPhaseImbalanceDegRange: [-2.0, 2.0],
  cfoObfuscationPpmRange: [-5.0, 5.0],
  cfoDitherStepPpm: 0.1,
  phaseNoiseFloorDbc: -130,
  phaseNoiseInjectionDbc: -110,
  powerRampUs: 100,
  powerRampJitterUs: 50,
  numSyntheticIdentities: 32,
  identityRotationInterval: 10,
  fingerprintDatabaseSize: 1000,
  fingerprintMatchThreshold: 0.85,
  enableAdversarialInjection: true,
  spoofTargetProfile: null,
};

// Complex IQ sample
export interface IQSample {
  i: number;    // In-phase component
  q: number;    // Quadrature component
  timestamp: number; // Microseconds
}

// IQ imbalance model
export interface IQImbalanceModel {
  amplitudeImbalanceDb: number;   // α in dB: Q channel amplitude error
  phaseImbalanceDeg: number;      // φ in degrees: Q channel phase error
  // Derived correction matrix coefficients
  c11: number; c12: number;
  c21: number; c22: number;
}

// Carrier Frequency Offset model
export interface CFOModel {
  offsetPpm: number;              // Parts per million deviation from nominal
  offsetHz: number;               // Absolute offset in Hz (ppm * carrier_freq)
  driftRatePpmPerSec: number;    // How fast offset changes (temperature drift)
  phaseAccumulator: number;      // Current phase accumulation (radians)
}

// Phase noise model (Lorentzian approximation)
export interface PhaseNoiseModel {
  flicker_dbc_per_hz: number;    // Flicker (1/f) noise floor
  white_dbc_per_hz: number;      // White phase noise floor
  cornerFreqHz: number;          // 1/f corner frequency
  currentPhaseRad: number;       // Current accumulated phase error
}

// Power ramp model (turn-on transient)
export interface PowerRampModel {
  rampDurationUs: number;
  shape: 'linear' | 'exponential' | 'sigmoid';
  overshootDb: number;           // Power overshoot at end of ramp
  rampJitterUs: number;
}

// Synthetic radio identity (fake device fingerprint)
export interface SyntheticIdentity {
  identityId: string;
  iqImbalance: IQImbalanceModel;
  cfo: CFOModel;
  phaseNoise: PhaseNoiseModel;
  powerRamp: PowerRampModel;
  constellationRotationDeg: number;
  spectralFlatnessDiff: number;   // Deviation from ideal flat spectrum
  harmonicContent: number[];       // Power of 2nd, 3rd... harmonics (dBc)
  createdAt: number;
  usageCount: number;
}

// Extracted RF fingerprint features
export interface RFFingerprint {
  fingerprintId: string;
  extractedAt: number;
  sourceNodeId: string | null;   // If known
  features: {
    iqAmplitudeDb: number;
    iqPhaseDeg: number;
    cfoPpm: number;
    phaseNoiseProfile: number[];   // Power at [100Hz, 1kHz, 10kHz, 100kHz offset]
    powerRampShapeCoeffs: number[]; // Polynomial fit coefficients
    spectralSkewness: number;
    spectralKurtosis: number;
    spectralEntropy: number;
    constellationRMSError: number;
    harmonicsDbcArray: number[];
  };
  confidence: number;             // 0-1 extraction confidence
  matchedIdentityId: string | null; // If matched to known identity
}

// Fingerprint match result
export interface FingerprintMatch {
  fingerprintId: string;
  matchedFingerprintId: string;
  correlationScore: number;
  matchedNodeId: string | null;
  isOwnTransmission: boolean;
  isSyntheticIdentity: boolean;
}

// Obfuscation application result
export interface ObfuscationResult {
  identityUsed: string;
  iqCompensationApplied: IQImbalanceModel;
  cfoApplied: number;
  phaseNoiseInjected: number;
  powerRampJitter: number;
  constellationRotation: number;
  signalModified: boolean;
}

// Statistical signal features
export interface SignalStatistics {
  mean: number;
  variance: number;
  skewness: number;
  kurtosis: number;
  entropy: number;
  peakToPowerRatio: number;   // PAPR
  spectralFlatness: number;   // Wiener entropy
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// STATISTICAL SIGNAL ANALYZER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SignalAnalyzer {
  /**
   * Compute statistical moments of IQ signal magnitude
   */
  static computeStatistics(samples: IQSample[]): SignalStatistics {
    if (samples.length === 0) {
      return { mean: 0, variance: 0, skewness: 0, kurtosis: 0, entropy: 0, peakToPowerRatio: 0, spectralFlatness: 0 };
    }

    const magnitudes = samples.map(s => Math.sqrt(s.i * s.i + s.q * s.q));

    const n = magnitudes.length;
    const mean = magnitudes.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / n;
    const variance = magnitudes.reduce((s, v) => s + (v - mean) ** 2, 0) / n;
    const std = Math.sqrt(variance);

    // Skewness: E[(X-μ)³] / σ³
    const skewness = std > 0
      ? magnitudes.reduce((s, v) => s + ((v - mean) / std) ** 3, 0) / n
      : 0;

    // Excess kurtosis: E[(X-μ)⁴] / σ⁴ - 3
    const kurtosis = std > 0
      ? magnitudes.reduce((s, v) => s + ((v - mean) / std) ** 4, 0) / n - 3
      : 0;

    // Shannon entropy of magnitude histogram
    const entropy = SignalAnalyzer.computeEntropy(magnitudes);

    // PAPR
    const power = magnitudes.map(m => m * m);
    const avgPower = power.reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / n;
    const peakPower = Math.max(...power);
    const peakToPowerRatio = avgPower > 0 ? 10 * Math.log10(peakPower / avgPower) : 0;

    // Spectral flatness (Wiener entropy)
    const spectralFlatness = SignalAnalyzer.computeSpectralFlatness(magnitudes);

    return { mean, variance, skewness, kurtosis, entropy, peakToPowerRatio, spectralFlatness };
  }

  private static computeEntropy(values: number[]): number {
    const bins = 64;
    const min = Math.min(...values);
    const max = Math.max(...values);
    const range = max - min || 1;

    const histogram = new Array(bins).fill(0);
    for (const v of values) {
      const bin = Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor((v - min) / range * bins));
      histogram[bin]++;
    }

    const n = values.length;
    let entropy = 0;
    for (const count of histogram) {
      if (count > 0) {
        const p = count / n;
        entropy -= p * Math.log2(p);
      }
    }

    return entropy;
  }

  private static computeSpectralFlatness(signal: number[]): number {
    // Wiener entropy: geometric mean / arithmetic mean of spectral components
    // Simplified: compute over magnitude spectrum bins
    if (signal.length === 0) return 0;

    const power = signal.map(s => s * s + 1e-10); // avoid log(0)

    const geometricMean = Math.exp(
      power.reduce((s, p) => s + Math.log(p), 0) / power.length
    );
    const arithmeticMean = power.reduce((s, p) => s + p, 0) / power.length;

    return geometricMean / arithmeticMean;
  }

  /**
   * Compute DFT magnitude spectrum (Cooley-Tukey, power of 2 only)
   */
  static computeSpectrum(samples: IQSample[]): Float64Array {
    const n = 1 << Math.floor(Math.log2(samples.length));
    const real = new Float64Array(n);
    const imag = new Float64Array(n);

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      real[i] = samples[i]?.i ?? 0;
      imag[i] = samples[i]?.q ?? 0;
    }

    SignalAnalyzer.fft(real, imag, false);

    const spectrum = new Float64Array(n / 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < n / 2; i++) {
      spectrum[i] = Math.sqrt(real[i] * real[i] + imag[i] * imag[i]) / n;
    }

    return spectrum;
  }

  /**
   * In-place Cooley-Tukey FFT (radix-2 DIT)
   */
  static fft(re: Float64Array, im: Float64Array, inverse: boolean): void {
    const n = re.length;
    if (n <= 1) return;

    // Bit-reversal permutation
    for (let i = 1, j = 0; i < n; i++) {
      let bit = n >> 1;
      for (; j & bit; bit >>= 1) j ^= bit;
      j ^= bit;
      if (i < j) {
        [re[i], re[j]] = [re[j], re[i]];
        [im[i], im[j]] = [im[j], im[i]];
      }
    }

    // FFT butterfly
    for (let len = 2; len <= n; len <<= 1) {
      const angle = (inverse ? 2 : -2) * Math.PI / len;
      const wRe = Math.cos(angle);
      const wIm = Math.sin(angle);

      for (let i = 0; i < n; i += len) {
        let curRe = 1, curIm = 0;
        for (let j = 0; j < len / 2; j++) {
          const uRe = re[i + j], uIm = im[i + j];
          const vRe = re[i + j + len/2] * curRe - im[i + j + len/2] * curIm;
          const vIm = re[i + j + len/2] * curIm + im[i + j + len/2] * curRe;

          re[i + j] = uRe + vRe;
          im[i + j] = uIm + vIm;
          re[i + j + len/2] = uRe - vRe;
          im[i + j + len/2] = uIm - vIm;

          const newCurRe = curRe * wRe - curIm * wIm;
          curIm = curRe * wIm + curIm * wRe;
          curRe = newCurRe;
        }
      }
    }

    if (inverse) {
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { re[i] /= n; im[i] /= n; }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Extract phase noise at specific offset frequencies
   * Returns power in dBc/Hz at [100Hz, 1kHz, 10kHz, 100kHz]
   */
  static extractPhaseNoiseProfile(
    samples: IQSample[],
    sampleRateHz: number
  ): number[] {
    const spectrum = SignalAnalyzer.computeSpectrum(samples);
    const freqResolution = sampleRateHz / (spectrum.length * 2);

    const offsets = [100, 1000, 10000, 100000];
    return offsets.map(offset => {
      const binIdx = Math.round(offset / freqResolution);
      if (binIdx >= spectrum.length) return -150;

      // Average over ±5 bins for smoother estimate
      let sum = 0;
      let count = 0;
      for (let b = Math.max(0, binIdx - 5); b <= Math.min(spectrum.length - 1, binIdx + 5); b++) {
        sum += spectrum[b] ** 2;
        count++;
      }

      const power = count > 0 ? sum / count : 1e-15;
      return 10 * Math.log10(power / freqResolution); // dBc/Hz
    });
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// IQ IMBALANCE COMPENSATOR / OBFUSCATOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class IQProcessor {
  /**
   * Build IQ correction matrix from imbalance parameters
   * Real IQ imbalance model:
   *   I_rx = I_ideal
   *   Q_rx = α * sin(φ) * I_ideal + α * cos(φ) * Q_ideal
   * where α = amplitude imbalance, φ = phase imbalance
   */
  static buildImbalanceModel(
    amplitudeImbalanceDb: number,
    phaseImbalanceDeg: number
  ): IQImbalanceModel {
    const alpha = Math.pow(10, amplitudeImbalanceDb / 20); // linear amplitude ratio
    const phi = phaseImbalanceDeg * Math.PI / 180;

    // Forward model (what the hardware does)
    return {
      amplitudeImbalanceDb,
      phaseImbalanceDeg,
      // Correction matrix (inverse of imbalance)
      c11: 1,
      c12: 0,
      c21: -Math.tan(phi),
      c22: 1 / (alpha * Math.cos(phi)),
    };
  }

  /**
   * Apply IQ imbalance correction to compensate hardware defect
   */
  static applyCorrection(sample: IQSample, model: IQImbalanceModel): IQSample {
    return {
      i: model.c11 * sample.i + model.c12 * sample.q,
      q: model.c21 * sample.i + model.c22 * sample.q,
      timestamp: sample.timestamp,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Apply IQ imbalance to synthesize a hardware defect (obfuscation)
   * This makes our clean signal look like it has a specific imbalance
   */
  static applyImbalance(sample: IQSample, model: IQImbalanceModel): IQSample {
    const alpha = Math.pow(10, model.amplitudeImbalanceDb / 20);
    const phi = model.phaseImbalanceDeg * Math.PI / 180;

    return {
      i: sample.i,
      q: alpha * (Math.sin(phi) * sample.i + Math.cos(phi) * sample.q),
      timestamp: sample.timestamp,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Randomize IQ imbalance within specified ranges
   */
  static randomizeImbalance(
    ampRange: [number, number],
    phaseRange: [number, number]
  ): IQImbalanceModel {
    const amp = ampRange[0] + Math.random() * (ampRange[1] - ampRange[0]);
    const phase = phaseRange[0] + Math.random() * (phaseRange[1] - phaseRange[0]);
    return IQProcessor.buildImbalanceModel(amp, phase);
  }

  /**
   * Estimate IQ imbalance from received signal (Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization)
   */
  static estimateImbalance(samples: IQSample[]): IQImbalanceModel {
    if (samples.length < 100) {
      return IQProcessor.buildImbalanceModel(0, 0);
    }

    // Gram-Schmidt: compute cross-correlation between I and Q
    let sumI2 = 0, sumQ2 = 0, sumIQ = 0;
    for (const s of samples) {
      sumI2 += s.i * s.i;
      sumQ2 += s.q * s.q;
      sumIQ += s.i * s.q;
    }

    const n = samples.length;
    const varI = sumI2 / n;
    const varQ = sumQ2 / n;
    const covIQ = sumIQ / n;

    // Amplitude imbalance: sqrt(varQ/varI)
    const amplitudeRatio = varI > 0 ? Math.sqrt(varQ / varI) : 1;
    const amplitudeImbalanceDb = 20 * Math.log10(amplitudeRatio);

    // Phase imbalance: arcsin(covIQ / sqrt(varI * varQ))
    const correlation = varI > 0 && varQ > 0 ? covIQ / Math.sqrt(varI * varQ) : 0;
    const phaseImbalanceDeg = Math.asin(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, correlation))) * 180 / Math.PI;

    return IQProcessor.buildImbalanceModel(amplitudeImbalanceDb, phaseImbalanceDeg);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// CFO OBFUSCATOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class CFOObfuscator {
  private model: CFOModel;
  private readonly config: RFDefenseConfig;
  private sampleCount: number;

  constructor(config: RFDefenseConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.sampleCount = 0;
    this.model = {
      offsetPpm: 0,
      offsetHz: 0,
      driftRatePpmPerSec: 0,
      phaseAccumulator: 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Generate a new random CFO for this transmission
   */
  randomizeCFO(carrierFreqHz: number): CFOModel {
    const [minPpm, maxPpm] = this.config.cfoObfuscationPpmRange;
    const offsetPpm = minPpm + Math.random() * (maxPpm - minPpm);

    this.model = {
      offsetPpm,
      offsetHz: offsetPpm * 1e-6 * carrierFreqHz,
      driftRatePpmPerSec: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1, // Small random drift
      phaseAccumulator: Math.random() * 2 * Math.PI,   // Random initial phase
    };

    return { ...this.model };
  }

  /**
   * Apply CFO to IQ samples
   * Rotates the constellation by an increasing phase angle
   * e(t) = s(t) * exp(j * 2π * Δf * t)
   */
  applyCFO(samples: IQSample[], sampleRateHz: number): IQSample[] {
    const phaseIncrement = 2 * Math.PI * this.model.offsetHz / sampleRateHz;
    const result: IQSample[] = [];

    for (const sample of samples) {
      const phase = this.model.phaseAccumulator;
      const cosP = Math.cos(phase);
      const sinP = Math.sin(phase);

      result.push({
        i: sample.i * cosP - sample.q * sinP,
        q: sample.i * sinP + sample.q * cosP,
        timestamp: sample.timestamp,
      });

      this.model.phaseAccumulator = (phase + phaseIncrement) % (2 * Math.PI);
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Apply dithering to CFO — small random changes per transmission
   * Makes it harder to track CFO across transmissions
   */
  ditherCFO(): void {
    const dither = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2 * this.config.cfoDitherStepPpm;
    this.model.offsetPpm += dither;

    // Clamp to configured range
    const [min, max] = this.config.cfoObfuscationPpmRange;
    this.model.offsetPpm = Math.max(min, Math.min(max, this.model.offsetPpm));
  }

  /**
   * Estimate CFO from received samples (Moose algorithm)
   * Computes CFO from phase difference of repeated preamble
   */
  static estimateCFO(
    preamble1: IQSample[],
    preamble2: IQSample[],
    preambleLengthSamples: number,
    sampleRateHz: number
  ): number {
    if (preamble1.length === 0 || preamble2.length === 0) return 0;

    // Cross-correlation between the two preamble copies
    let sumRe = 0, sumIm = 0;
    const n = Math.min(preamble1.length, preamble2.length, preambleLengthSamples);

    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      // Conjugate multiplication: p1[k] * conj(p2[k])
      sumRe += preamble1[k].i * preamble2[k].i + preamble1[k].q * preamble2[k].q;
      sumIm += preamble1[k].q * preamble2[k].i - preamble1[k].i * preamble2[k].q;
    }

    // CFO = arg(sum) / (2π * N/fs)
    const phase = Math.atan2(sumIm, sumRe);
    const cfoHz = phase * sampleRateHz / (2 * Math.PI * preambleLengthSamples);
    return cfoHz;
  }

  getCurrentModel(): CFOModel { return { ...this.model }; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PHASE NOISE INJECTOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class PhaseNoiseInjector {
  private readonly config: RFDefenseConfig;
  private phaseState: number;           // Current phase noise state

  // Wiener process state for 1/f noise
  private flickerState: number;
  private readonly flickerDecay: number;

  constructor(config: RFDefenseConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.phaseState = 0;
    this.flickerState = 0;
    this.flickerDecay = 0.999; // AR(1) coefficient for 1/f approximation
  }

  /**
   * Generate phase noise samples using sum of Wiener processes
   * Models real oscillator phase noise spectrum:
   *   L(f) ≈ f_corner/f * (white_floor) + white_floor
   */
  generatePhaseNoiseSamples(count: number, sampleRateHz: number): Float64Array {
    const samples = new Float64Array(count);

    // White phase noise floor
    const whiteStd = Math.sqrt(
      Math.pow(10, this.config.phaseNoiseInjectionDbc / 10) * sampleRateHz / 2
    );

    // Flicker noise (1/f) — approximated by AR(1) process
    const flickerStd = whiteStd * 10; // Flicker dominates at low offset

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      // White noise contribution
      const whiteNoise = this.gaussianRandom() * whiteStd;

      // Flicker (1/f) noise via AR(1)
      this.flickerState = this.flickerDecay * this.flickerState + this.gaussianRandom() * flickerStd;

      // Total phase noise
      this.phaseState += whiteNoise + this.flickerState * 0.01;
      samples[i] = this.phaseState;
    }

    return samples;
  }

  /**
   * Apply phase noise to IQ samples
   */
  applyPhaseNoise(samples: IQSample[], sampleRateHz: number): IQSample[] {
    const noisePhases = this.generatePhaseNoiseSamples(samples.length, sampleRateHz);
    const result: IQSample[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < samples.length; i++) {
      const phaseError = noisePhases[i];
      const cosP = Math.cos(phaseError);
      const sinP = Math.sin(phaseError);

      result.push({
        i: samples[i].i * cosP - samples[i].q * sinP,
        q: samples[i].i * sinP + samples[i].q * cosP,
        timestamp: samples[i].timestamp,
      });
    }

    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Generate random phase noise profile matching a specific device class
   */
  generateDeviceProfile(deviceClass: 'consumer' | 'industrial' | 'military'): PhaseNoiseModel {
    switch (deviceClass) {
      case 'consumer':
        return {
          flicker_dbc_per_hz: -100 + this.gaussianRandom() * 5,
          white_dbc_per_hz: -145 + this.gaussianRandom() * 3,
          cornerFreqHz: 10000 + Math.random() * 5000,
          currentPhaseRad: 0,
        };
      case 'industrial':
        return {
          flicker_dbc_per_hz: -110 + this.gaussianRandom() * 3,
          white_dbc_per_hz: -155 + this.gaussianRandom() * 2,
          cornerFreqHz: 5000 + Math.random() * 2000,
          currentPhaseRad: 0,
        };
      case 'military':
        return {
          flicker_dbc_per_hz: -125 + this.gaussianRandom() * 2,
          white_dbc_per_hz: -165 + this.gaussianRandom() * 1,
          cornerFreqHz: 1000 + Math.random() * 1000,
          currentPhaseRad: 0,
        };
    }
  }

  private gaussianRandom(): number {
    // Box-Muller transform
    const u1 = Math.random() || 1e-10;
    const u2 = Math.random();
    return Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u1)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * u2);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// TRANSIENT RANDOMIZER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class TransientRandomizer {
  private readonly config: RFDefenseConfig;

  constructor(config: RFDefenseConfig) {
    this.config = config;
  }

  /**
   * Generate a power ramp envelope for turn-on transient
   * Returns amplitude multipliers for each sample during ramp period
   */
  generatePowerRamp(
    totalSamples: number,
    sampleRateHz: number,
    model: PowerRampModel
  ): Float64Array {
    const envelope = new Float64Array(totalSamples).fill(1.0);
    const rampSamples = Math.floor(model.rampDurationUs * sampleRateHz / 1e6);
    const jitterSamples = Math.floor(model.rampJitterUs * sampleRateHz / 1e6 * (Math.random() - 0.5) * 2);
    const actualRamp = Math.max(1, rampSamples + jitterSamples);

    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(actualRamp, totalSamples); i++) {
      const t = i / actualRamp;

      switch (model.shape) {
        case 'linear':
          envelope[i] = t;
          break;
        case 'exponential':
          envelope[i] = 1 - Math.exp(-5 * t);
          break;
        case 'sigmoid':
          envelope[i] = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-10 * (t - 0.5)));
          break;
      }

      // Add overshoot at end of ramp
      if (i === actualRamp - 1 && model.overshootDb > 0) {
        const overshoot = Math.pow(10, model.overshootDb / 20);
        envelope[i] = Math.min(1.1, overshoot);
      }
    }

    return envelope;
  }

  /**
   * Generate turn-off transient (power-down glitch)
   */
  generatePowerDownTransient(
    totalSamples: number,
    sampleRateHz: number
  ): Float64Array {
    const envelope = new Float64Array(totalSamples).fill(1.0);
    const transientSamples = Math.floor(
      (this.config.powerRampUs + this.config.powerRampJitterUs * Math.random()) *
      sampleRateHz / 1e6
    );

    // Exponential decay
    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(transientSamples, totalSamples); i++) {
      envelope[totalSamples - 1 - i] = Math.exp(-5 * i / transientSamples);
    }

    return envelope;
  }

  /**
   * Apply transient envelope to IQ samples
   */
  applyEnvelope(samples: IQSample[], envelope: Float64Array): IQSample[] {
    return samples.map((sample, i) => {
      const scale = envelope[Math.min(i, envelope.length - 1)];
      return { i: sample.i * scale, q: sample.q * scale, timestamp: sample.timestamp };
    });
  }

  /**
   * Generate a random power ramp profile
   */
  generateRandomRamp(): PowerRampModel {
    const shapes: PowerRampModel['shape'][] = ['linear', 'exponential', 'sigmoid'];
    return {
      rampDurationUs: this.config.powerRampUs * (0.5 + Math.random()),
      shape: shapes[Math.floor(Math.random() * shapes.length)],
      overshootDb: Math.random() * 2,
      rampJitterUs: this.config.powerRampJitterUs,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// SYNTHETIC IDENTITY POOL
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SyntheticIdentityPool {
  private identities: SyntheticIdentity[];
  private currentIndex: number;
  private txCount: number;
  private readonly config: RFDefenseConfig;

  constructor(config: RFDefenseConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.currentIndex = 0;
    this.txCount = 0;
    this.identities = this.generatePool(config.numSyntheticIdentities);
  }

  private generatePool(count: number): SyntheticIdentity[] {
    const pool: SyntheticIdentity[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      pool.push(this.generateIdentity());
    }
    return pool;
  }

  private generateIdentity(): SyntheticIdentity {
    const [ampMin, ampMax] = this.config.iqAmplitudeImbalanceDbRange;
    const [phMin, phMax] = this.config.iqPhaseImbalanceDegRange;
    const [cfoMin, cfoMax] = this.config.cfoObfuscationPpmRange;

    const ampImbalance = ampMin + Math.random() * (ampMax - ampMin);
    const phImbalance = phMin + Math.random() * (phMax - phMin);
    const cfoPpm = cfoMin + Math.random() * (cfoMax - cfoMin);

    const rampShapes: PowerRampModel['shape'][] = ['linear', 'exponential', 'sigmoid'];

    return {
      identityId: this.randomId(),
      iqImbalance: IQProcessor.buildImbalanceModel(ampImbalance, phImbalance),
      cfo: {
        offsetPpm: cfoPpm,
        offsetHz: 0, // Will be computed at TX time with actual carrier freq
        driftRatePpmPerSec: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.05,
        phaseAccumulator: Math.random() * 2 * Math.PI,
      },
      phaseNoise: {
        flicker_dbc_per_hz: this.config.phaseNoiseFloorDbc + Math.random() * 10,
        white_dbc_per_hz: this.config.phaseNoiseInjectionDbc + Math.random() * 5,
        cornerFreqHz: 5000 + Math.random() * 10000,
        currentPhaseRad: 0,
      },
      powerRamp: {
        rampDurationUs: this.config.powerRampUs * (0.7 + Math.random() * 0.6),
        shape: rampShapes[Math.floor(Math.random() * rampShapes.length)],
        overshootDb: Math.random() * 1.5,
        rampJitterUs: this.config.powerRampJitterUs * Math.random(),
      },
      constellationRotationDeg: Math.random() * 5 - 2.5,
      spectralFlatnessDiff: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.1,
      harmonicContent: [-50 - Math.random() * 20, -60 - Math.random() * 20, -65 - Math.random() * 20],
      createdAt: Date.now(),
      usageCount: 0,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Get current active identity
   */
  getCurrent(): SyntheticIdentity {
    return this.identities[this.currentIndex];
  }

  /**
   * Advance to next identity if rotation interval reached
   */
  tick(): { rotated: boolean; newIdentity: SyntheticIdentity } {
    this.txCount++;
    const rotated = this.txCount % this.config.identityRotationInterval === 0;

    if (rotated) {
      // Pick a different random identity (not the same as current)
      let newIdx = Math.floor(Math.random() * this.identities.length);
      if (newIdx === this.currentIndex) newIdx = (newIdx + 1) % this.identities.length;
      this.currentIndex = newIdx;
    }

    this.identities[this.currentIndex].usageCount++;
    return { rotated, newIdentity: this.identities[this.currentIndex] };
  }

  /**
   * Regenerate an identity that has been used too many times
   */
  refreshIdentity(index: number): void {
    this.identities[index] = this.generateIdentity();
  }

  refreshHighUsage(maxUsage: number): number {
    let refreshed = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.identities.length; i++) {
      if (this.identities[i].usageCount > maxUsage) {
        this.refreshIdentity(i);
        refreshed++;
      }
    }
    return refreshed;
  }

  private randomId(): string {
    const b = new Uint8Array(8);
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) b[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
    return Array.from(b).map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }

  get poolSize(): number { return this.identities.length; }
  get currentIdentityId(): string { return this.identities[this.currentIndex].identityId; }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// RF FINGERPRINT DATABASE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class RFFingerprintDatabase {
  private fingerprints: Map<string, RFFingerprint>;
  private readonly maxSize: number;
  private readonly matchThreshold: number;

  constructor(maxSize: number, matchThreshold: number) {
    this.fingerprints = new Map();
    this.maxSize = maxSize;
    this.matchThreshold = matchThreshold;
  }

  add(fingerprint: RFFingerprint): void {
    if (this.fingerprints.size >= this.maxSize) {
      // Evict oldest
      const oldest = Array.from(this.fingerprints.values())
        .sort((a, b) => a.extractedAt - b.extractedAt)[0];
      this.fingerprints.delete(oldest.fingerprintId);
    }
    this.fingerprints.set(fingerprint.fingerprintId, fingerprint);
  }

  /**
   * Find matching fingerprints using feature correlation
   */
  findMatches(query: RFFingerprint): FingerprintMatch[] {
    const matches: FingerprintMatch[] = [];

    for (const [id, fp] of this.fingerprints) {
      if (id === query.fingerprintId) continue;

      const score = this.computeSimilarity(query, fp);
      if (score >= this.matchThreshold) {
        matches.push({
          fingerprintId: query.fingerprintId,
          matchedFingerprintId: id,
          correlationScore: score,
          matchedNodeId: fp.sourceNodeId,
          isOwnTransmission: false,
          isSyntheticIdentity: false,
        });
      }
    }

    return matches.sort((a, b) => b.correlationScore - a.correlationScore);
  }

  /**
   * Compute feature-weighted similarity between two fingerprints
   * Normalized to [0,1] — 1.0 = identical
   */
  private computeSimilarity(a: RFFingerprint, b: RFFingerprint): number {
    const fa = a.features, fb = b.features;

    // Feature weights (sum to 1.0)
    const weights = {
      iq: 0.25,
      cfo: 0.20,
      phaseNoise: 0.20,
      transient: 0.15,
      spectral: 0.20,
    };

    // IQ imbalance similarity
    const iqAmpDiff = Math.abs(fa.iqAmplitudeDb - fb.iqAmplitudeDb);
    const iqPhaseDiff = Math.abs(fa.iqPhaseDeg - fb.iqPhaseDeg);
    const iqSim = Math.exp(-(iqAmpDiff / 2 + iqPhaseDiff / 5));

    // CFO similarity (Gaussian kernel)
    const cfoDiff = Math.abs(fa.cfoPpm - fb.cfoPpm);
    const cfoSim = Math.exp(-(cfoDiff ** 2) / (2 * 5 ** 2));

    // Phase noise profile similarity (cosine similarity)
    const pnSim = this.cosineSimilarity(fa.phaseNoiseProfile, fb.phaseNoiseProfile);

    // Transient (power ramp) similarity
    const transientSim = this.cosineSimilarity(fa.powerRampShapeCoeffs, fb.powerRampShapeCoeffs);

    // Spectral feature similarity
    const spectralSkewDiff = Math.abs(fa.spectralSkewness - fb.spectralSkewness);
    const spectralKurtDiff = Math.abs(fa.spectralKurtosis - fb.spectralKurtosis);
    const spectralEntropyDiff = Math.abs(fa.spectralEntropy - fb.spectralEntropy);
    const spectralSim = Math.exp(-(spectralSkewDiff + spectralKurtDiff + spectralEntropyDiff) / 3);

    return (
      weights.iq * iqSim +
      weights.cfo * cfoSim +
      weights.phaseNoise * pnSim +
      weights.transient * transientSim +
      weights.spectral * spectralSim
    );
  }

  private cosineSimilarity(a: number[], b: number[]): number {
    const n = Math.min(a.length, b.length);
    if (n === 0) return 0;

    let dot = 0, magA = 0, magB = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      dot += a[i] * b[i];
      magA += a[i] ** 2;
      magB += b[i] ** 2;
    }

    const denom = Math.sqrt(magA) * Math.sqrt(magB);
    return denom > 0 ? Math.max(0, dot / denom) : 0;
  }

  get size(): number { return this.fingerprints.size; }
  getAllFingerprints(): RFFingerprint[] { return Array.from(this.fingerprints.values()); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// RF FINGERPRINT DEFENSE ENGINE — Main orchestrator
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class RFFingerprintDefenseEngine {
  private readonly config: RFDefenseConfig;
  private readonly identityPool: SyntheticIdentityPool;
  private readonly cfoObfuscator: CFOObfuscator;
  private readonly phaseNoiseInjector: PhaseNoiseInjector;
  private readonly transientRandomizer: TransientRandomizer;
  private readonly fingerprintDB: RFFingerprintDatabase;

  private txCount: number;
  private detectedAdversaryFingerprints: RFFingerprint[];

  // Statistics
  private stats = {
    transmissionsObfuscated: 0,
    identityRotations: 0,
    fingerprintsExtracted: 0,
    adversaryFingerprintsDetected: 0,
    spoofAttemptsDetected: 0,
    iqObfuscationsApplied: 0,
    cfoObfuscationsApplied: 0,
    phaseNoiseInjections: 0,
    transientRandomizations: 0,
  };

  constructor(config: Partial<RFDefenseConfig> = {}) {
    this.config = { ...DEFAULT_RF_DEFENSE_CONFIG, ...config };
    this.identityPool = new SyntheticIdentityPool(this.config);
    this.cfoObfuscator = new CFOObfuscator(this.config);
    this.phaseNoiseInjector = new PhaseNoiseInjector(this.config);
    this.transientRandomizer = new TransientRandomizer(this.config);
    this.fingerprintDB = new RFFingerprintDatabase(
      this.config.fingerprintDatabaseSize,
      this.config.fingerprintMatchThreshold
    );
    this.txCount = 0;
    this.detectedAdversaryFingerprints = [];
  }

  // ─── OBFUSCATION PIPELINE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Apply full RF fingerprint obfuscation to IQ samples before transmission
   * Pipeline: IQ correction → CFO injection → Phase noise → Transient randomization
   */
  obfuscateTransmission(
    samples: IQSample[],
    carrierFreqHz: number,
    sampleRateHz: number,
    strategy: ObfuscationStrategy = 'rotate'
  ): { obfuscatedSamples: IQSample[]; result: ObfuscationResult } {
    this.txCount++;

    // Get current synthetic identity
    const { rotated, newIdentity } = this.identityPool.tick();
    if (rotated) {
      this.stats.identityRotations++;
    }

    const identity = newIdentity;
    let processed = [...samples];

    // 1. Apply target IQ imbalance profile
    let iqModel = identity.iqImbalance;
    if (strategy === 'randomize') {
      iqModel = IQProcessor.randomizeImbalance(
        this.config.iqAmplitudeImbalanceDbRange,
        this.config.iqPhaseImbalanceDegRange
      );
    }

    processed = processed.map(s => IQProcessor.applyImbalance(s, iqModel));
    this.stats.iqObfuscationsApplied++;

    // 2. Apply CFO dithering
    const cfoModel = this.cfoObfuscator.randomizeCFO(carrierFreqHz);
    cfoModel.offsetHz = cfoModel.offsetPpm * 1e-6 * carrierFreqHz;
    processed = this.cfoObfuscator.applyCFO(processed, sampleRateHz);
    this.stats.cfoObfuscationsApplied++;

    // 3. Inject synthetic phase noise
    processed = this.phaseNoiseInjector.applyPhaseNoise(processed, sampleRateHz);
    this.stats.phaseNoiseInjections++;

    // 4. Apply random power ramp transient
    const rampModel = this.transientRandomizer.generateRandomRamp();
    const rampEnvelope = this.transientRandomizer.generatePowerRamp(
      processed.length,
      sampleRateHz,
      rampModel
    );
    const downEnvelope = this.transientRandomizer.generatePowerDownTransient(
      processed.length,
      sampleRateHz
    );

    // Combine ramp-up and ramp-down envelopes
    const combinedEnvelope = rampEnvelope.map((v, i) => v * downEnvelope[i]);
    processed = this.transientRandomizer.applyEnvelope(processed, combinedEnvelope);
    this.stats.transientRandomizations++;

    // 5. Constellation rotation
    const rotationRad = identity.constellationRotationDeg * Math.PI / 180;
    const cosR = Math.cos(rotationRad), sinR = Math.sin(rotationRad);
    processed = processed.map(s => ({
      i: s.i * cosR - s.q * sinR,
      q: s.i * sinR + s.q * cosR,
      timestamp: s.timestamp,
    }));

    this.stats.transmissionsObfuscated++;

    const obfuscationResult: ObfuscationResult = {
      identityUsed: identity.identityId,
      iqCompensationApplied: iqModel,
      cfoApplied: cfoModel.offsetPpm,
      phaseNoiseInjected: this.config.phaseNoiseInjectionDbc,
      powerRampJitter: rampModel.rampJitterUs,
      constellationRotation: identity.constellationRotationDeg,
      signalModified: true,
    };

    return { obfuscatedSamples: processed, result: obfuscationResult };
  }

  // ─── FINGERPRINT EXTRACTION ───────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Extract RF fingerprint features from received IQ samples
   * Used to identify/track adversary transmitters
   */
  extractFingerprint(
    samples: IQSample[],
    sampleRateHz: number,
    sourceNodeId: string | null = null
  ): RFFingerprint {
    const stats = SignalAnalyzer.computeStatistics(samples);
    const phaseNoiseProfile = SignalAnalyzer.extractPhaseNoiseProfile(samples, sampleRateHz);

    // Estimate IQ imbalance
    const iqModel = IQProcessor.estimateImbalance(samples);

    // Estimate CFO (needs preamble — use first 1/8 of samples as rough preamble)
    const halfSamples = Math.floor(samples.length / 2);
    const preamble1 = samples.slice(0, halfSamples);
    const preamble2 = samples.slice(halfSamples);
    const cfoHz = CFOObfuscator.estimateCFO(preamble1, preamble2, halfSamples, sampleRateHz);
    const cfoPpm = (cfoHz / (sampleRateHz / 2)) * 1e6;

    // Power ramp coefficients (polynomial fit to first 10% of samples)
    const rampLength = Math.floor(samples.length * 0.1);
    const rampMagnitudes = samples.slice(0, rampLength).map(
      s => Math.sqrt(s.i ** 2 + s.q ** 2)
    );
    const rampCoeffs = this.polynomialFit(rampMagnitudes, 3);

    // Constellation error
    const rmsError = this.computeConstellationError(samples);

    // Harmonic content (estimate from spectral peaks)
    const spectrum = SignalAnalyzer.computeSpectrum(samples);
    const harmonics = this.extractHarmonics(spectrum);

    const fingerprint: RFFingerprint = {
      fingerprintId: this.randomId(),
      extractedAt: Date.now(),
      sourceNodeId,
      features: {
        iqAmplitudeDb: iqModel.amplitudeImbalanceDb,
        iqPhaseDeg: iqModel.phaseImbalanceDeg,
        cfoPpm,
        phaseNoiseProfile,
        powerRampShapeCoeffs: rampCoeffs,
        spectralSkewness: stats.skewness,
        spectralKurtosis: stats.kurtosis,
        spectralEntropy: stats.entropy,
        constellationRMSError: rmsError,
        harmonicsDbcArray: harmonics,
      },
      confidence: Math.min(1, samples.length / 1000), // More samples = higher confidence
      matchedIdentityId: null,
    };

    this.fingerprintDB.add(fingerprint);
    this.stats.fingerprintsExtracted++;

    return fingerprint;
  }

  /**
   * Check if a received fingerprint matches our own synthetic identities
   * If so, it might be a spoofing attack using our leaked identity
   */
  detectSpoofingAttempt(fingerprint: RFFingerprint): boolean {
    const matches = this.fingerprintDB.findMatches(fingerprint);
    if (matches.length > 0 && matches[0].isSyntheticIdentity) {
      this.stats.spoofAttemptsDetected++;
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }

  /**
   * Track adversary transmitter fingerprints
   */
  trackAdversaryTransmitter(fingerprint: RFFingerprint): {
    isKnown: boolean;
    adversaryId: string | null;
    sightings: number;
  } {
    const matches = this.fingerprintDB.findMatches(fingerprint);

    if (matches.length > 0 && matches[0].correlationScore > this.config.fingerprintMatchThreshold) {
      const matched = this.fingerprintDB.getAllFingerprints()
        .find(f => f.fingerprintId === matches[0].matchedFingerprintId);

      this.stats.adversaryFingerprintsDetected++;

      return {
        isKnown: true,
        adversaryId: matched?.sourceNodeId ?? null,
        sightings: matches.length,
      };
    }

    this.detectedAdversaryFingerprints.push(fingerprint);
    return { isKnown: false, adversaryId: null, sightings: 1 };
  }

  // ─── PRIVATE UTILITIES ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private polynomialFit(values: number[], degree: number): number[] {
    if (values.length === 0) return new Array(degree + 1).fill(0);

    // Vandermonde matrix least-squares (simplified for degree 3)
    const n = values.length;
    const t = Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => i / Math.max(n - 1, 1));

    // Build normal equations for least-squares
    const A: number[][] = [];
    const b: number[] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const row: number[] = [];
      for (let d = 0; d <= degree; d++) {
        row.push(Math.pow(t[i], d));
      }
      A.push(row);
      b.push(values[i]);
    }

    // Solve using normal equations: A^T A x = A^T b
    const AtA: number[][] = Array.from({ length: degree + 1 }, () => new Array(degree + 1).fill(0));
    const Atb: number[] = new Array(degree + 1).fill(0);

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      for (let j = 0; j <= degree; j++) {
        Atb[j] += A[i][j] * b[i];
        for (let k = 0; k <= degree; k++) {
          AtA[j][k] += A[i][j] * A[i][k];
        }
      }
    }

    // Gaussian elimination
    return this.gaussianElimination(AtA, Atb);
  }

  private gaussianElimination(A: number[][], b: number[]): number[] {
    const n = b.length;
    const aug = A.map((row, i) => [...row, b[i]]);

    for (let col = 0; col < n; col++) {
      // Find pivot
      let maxRow = col;
      for (let row = col + 1; row < n; row++) {
        if (Math.abs(aug[row][col]) > Math.abs(aug[maxRow][col])) maxRow = row;
      }
      [aug[col], aug[maxRow]] = [aug[maxRow], aug[col]];

      if (Math.abs(aug[col][col]) < 1e-10) continue;

      for (let row = col + 1; row < n; row++) {
        const factor = aug[row][col] / aug[col][col];
        for (let k = col; k <= n; k++) {
          aug[row][k] -= factor * aug[col][k];
        }
      }
    }

    // Back substitution
    const x = new Array(n).fill(0);
    for (let i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      x[i] = aug[i][n];
      for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) x[i] -= aug[i][j] * x[j];
      x[i] /= aug[i][i] || 1;
    }

    return x;
  }

  private computeConstellationError(samples: IQSample[]): number {
    // For BPSK-equivalent: ideal points at (±1, 0)
    // Compute minimum distance to nearest ideal point
    let sumSquaredError = 0;
    for (const s of samples) {
      const mag = Math.sqrt(s.i ** 2 + s.q ** 2);
      if (mag < 1e-6) continue;

      // Nearest unit circle point
      const idealI = s.i / mag;
      const idealQ = s.q / mag;
      const errI = s.i - idealI;
      const errQ = s.q - idealQ;
      sumSquaredError += errI ** 2 + errQ ** 2;
    }

    return Math.sqrt(sumSquaredError / Math.max(samples.length, 1));
  }

  private extractHarmonics(spectrum: Float64Array): number[] {
    if (spectrum.length < 10) return [-60, -65, -70];

    // Assume fundamental at bin 10 (simplified — real system uses calibrated offset)
    const fundamentalBin = Math.floor(spectrum.length * 0.1);
    const fundamentalPower = spectrum[fundamentalBin] ** 2;

    return [2, 3, 4].map(harmonic => {
      const bin = Math.min(Math.floor(fundamentalBin * harmonic), spectrum.length - 1);
      const power = spectrum[bin] ** 2;
      return fundamentalPower > 0 ? 10 * Math.log10(power / fundamentalPower) : -60;
    });
  }

  private randomId(): string {
    const b = new Uint8Array(8);
    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) b[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
    return Array.from(b).map(x => x.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }

  // ─── STATUS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  getStats() { return { ...this.stats }; }
  getCurrentIdentityId(): string { return this.identityPool.currentIdentityId; }
  getFingerprintDBSize(): number { return this.fingerprintDB.size; }
  getDetectedAdversaryCount(): number { return this.detectedAdversaryFingerprints.length; }

  refreshIdentityPool(): number {
    return this.identityPool.refreshHighUsage(this.config.identityRotationInterval * 5);
  }

  getDefenseStatus(): {
    currentIdentity: string;
    txCount: number;
    knownAdversaries: number;
    fingerprintsTracked: number;
    obfuscationActive: boolean;
  } {
    return {
      currentIdentity: this.identityPool.currentIdentityId,
      txCount: this.txCount,
      knownAdversaries: this.detectedAdversaryFingerprints.length,
      fingerprintsTracked: this.fingerprintDB.size,
      obfuscationActive: true,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// FACTORY & EXPORT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createRFFingerprintDefenseEngine(
  nodeId: string,
  overrides: Partial<RFDefenseConfig> = {}
): RFFingerprintDefenseEngine {
  return new RFFingerprintDefenseEngine({ nodeId, ...overrides });
}

export {
  SignalAnalyzer,
  SyntheticIdentityPool,
  RFFingerprintDatabase,
};

export default RFFingerprintDefenseEngine;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — satelliteFallback.ts
 * Satellite Communication Fallback Transport
 *
 * When LoRa range is insufficient and internet is down, satellite is the last link.
 * This file manages: Starlink (LEO, ~20ms latency), Iridium SBD (LEO, store-forward),
 * Globalstar SPOT, and generic VSAT (GEO, ~600ms latency).
 *
 * Architecture:
 *   SatelliteLink          — Abstract satellite link interface
 *   StarlinkLink           — Starlink dish HTTP API + network priority manager
 *   IridiumSBDLink         — Iridium Short Burst Data via AT commands (serial)
 *   VsatLink               — Generic GEO VSAT via IP tunnel
 *   ContactWindowPredictor — Predict satellite visibility windows from TLE data
 *   LinkSelector           — Select best available satellite link dynamically
 *   BandwidthBudget        — Data budget enforcement (satellite has usage caps)
 *   LatencyCompensator     — Adaptive protocol adjustments for high-latency links
 *   SatelliteRouter        — Route UBE mesh traffic over selected satellite link
 */

import { EventEmitter } from 'events';
import * as crypto from 'crypto';
import * as net from 'net';

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONSTANTS
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

const STARLINK_DEFAULT_API = 'http://192.168.100.1'; // Dish local IP
const STARLINK_GRPC_PORT = 9200;
const IRIDIUM_SBD_MAX_MO = 340;      // Max Mobile-Originated message bytes
const IRIDIUM_SBD_MAX_MT = 270;      // Max Mobile-Terminated message bytes
const IRIDIUM_SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
const VSAT_DEFAULT_MTU = 1400;        // Bytes, conservative for satellite
const GEO_RTT_MS = 600;              // GEO satellite round-trip time
const LEO_RTT_MS_STARLINK = 20;      // Starlink LEO RTT
const LEO_RTT_MS_IRIDIUM = 1_500;    // Iridium LEO RTT (store-forward)
const LINK_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS = 30_000;
const BANDWIDTH_BUDGET_RESET_INTERVAL_MS = 2_592_000_000; // 30 days
const MAX_QUEUE_SIZE = 1000;
const TLE_EPOCH_DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
const EARTH_RADIUS_KM = 6_371;
const IRIDIUM_ORBITAL_ALT_KM = 781;
const STARLINK_ORBITAL_ALT_KM = 550;

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export type SatelliteProvider = 'starlink' | 'iridium' | 'globalstar' | 'vsat' | 'inmarsat' | 'ses';
export type LinkState = 'connected' | 'connecting' | 'disconnected' | 'degraded' | 'searching';
export type Priority = 'critical' | 'high' | 'normal' | 'low' | 'background';

export interface SatelliteConfig {
  provider: SatelliteProvider;
  enabled: boolean;
  priority: number;              // Lower = preferred
  monthlyBudgetBytes: number;    // Data cap (0 = unlimited)
  maxLatencyMs: number;          // Reject if latency exceeds this
  credentials?: Record<string, string>;
  serialPort?: string;           // For Iridium AT command modem
  apiEndpoint?: string;
}

export interface LinkMetrics {
  provider: SatelliteProvider;
  state: LinkState;
  latencyMs: number;
  uplinkBpsEstimate: number;
  downlinkBpsEstimate: number;
  signalStrength: number;        // 0–100
  packetLoss: number;            // 0.0–1.0
  bytesUsedThisMonth: number;
  bytesRemainingThisMonth: number;
  lastSuccessful: number;
  obstructionPercent: number;    // Starlink-specific
  uplinkThroughputMbps: number;
  downlinkThroughputMbps: number;
}

export interface QueuedMessage {
  id: string;
  data: Buffer;
  priority: Priority;
  recipient?: string;
  queuedAt: number;
  expiresAt: number;
  attempts: number;
  maxAttempts: number;
}

export interface TwoLineElement {
  name: string;
  line1: string;    // TLE line 1
  line2: string;    // TLE line 2
  epoch: number;    // Unix ms
}

export interface OrbitalPass {
  aos: number;      // Acquisition of Signal (Unix ms)
  los: number;      // Loss of Signal (Unix ms)
  maxElevation: number;  // Degrees
  duration: number; // ms
  visible: boolean;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// SATELLITE LINK INTERFACE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ISatelliteLink extends EventEmitter {
  readonly provider: SatelliteProvider;
  connect(): Promise<boolean>;
  disconnect(): Promise<void>;
  send(data: Buffer, options?: SendOptions): Promise<SendResult>;
  getMetrics(): LinkMetrics;
  isAvailable(): boolean;
  healthCheck(): Promise<boolean>;
}

export interface SendOptions {
  priority?: Priority;
  ttl?: number;              // ms until message expires
  confirmDelivery?: boolean;
}

export interface SendResult {
  success: boolean;
  messageId?: string;
  latencyMs?: number;
  bytesUsed?: number;
  error?: string;
  retryAfterMs?: number;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// STARLINK LINK
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface StarlinkStatus {
  state: string;
  uptimeSeconds: number;
  uplinkThroughputMbps: number;
  downlinkThroughputMbps: number;
  pingDropRateLast1h: number;
  pingLatencyMsLast1h: number;
  obstructionPercentTime: number;
  gpsValid: boolean;
  alerts: string[];
  softwareVersion: string;
  hardwareVersion: string;
  deviceId: string;
}

export interface StarlinkObstructionMap {
  fractionObstructed: number;
  wedgeFractionObstructed: number[];
  validSeconds: number;
  wedgeAbsFractionObstructed: number[];
}

export class StarlinkLink extends EventEmitter implements ISatelliteLink {
  readonly provider: SatelliteProvider = 'starlink';
  private state: LinkState = 'disconnected';
  private metrics: LinkMetrics;
  private readonly apiBase: string;
  private healthTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private bytesUsed: number = 0;
  private readonly config: SatelliteConfig;
  private lastStatus: StarlinkStatus | null = null;
  private consecutiveFailures: number = 0;

  constructor(config: SatelliteConfig) {
    super();
    this.config = config;
    this.apiBase = config.apiEndpoint ?? STARLINK_DEFAULT_API;
    this.metrics = this.emptyMetrics();
  }

  async connect(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      this.state = 'connecting';
      this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state });

      const status = await this.fetchDishStatus();
      if (!status) {
        this.state = 'disconnected';
        return false;
      }

      this.lastStatus = status;
      this.updateMetricsFromStatus(status);
      this.state = status.state === 'CONNECTED' ? 'connected' : 'searching';
      this.consecutiveFailures = 0;

      this.startHealthChecks();
      this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state, status });
      return this.state === 'connected';
    } catch (err) {
      this.state = 'disconnected';
      this.emit('error', { type: 'connect_failed', err });
      return false;
    }
  }

  async disconnect(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.healthTimer) clearInterval(this.healthTimer);
    this.state = 'disconnected';
    this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state });
  }

  async send(data: Buffer, options: SendOptions = {}): Promise<SendResult> {
    if (!this.isAvailable()) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Starlink not connected', retryAfterMs: 5_000 };
    }

    const start = Date.now();
    // Starlink provides IP connectivity — data goes through normal TCP/UDP
    // Here we tunnel through the Starlink connection using TCP to a relay endpoint
    try {
      const result = await this.tunnelSend(data, options);
      this.bytesUsed += data.length;
      this.metrics.bytesUsedThisMonth += data.length;
      this.metrics.bytesRemainingThisMonth = Math.max(0,
        this.config.monthlyBudgetBytes - this.bytesUsed);
      return { ...result, latencyMs: Date.now() - start };
    } catch (err) {
      this.consecutiveFailures++;
      if (this.consecutiveFailures >= 3) {
        this.state = 'degraded';
        this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state });
      }
      return { success: false, error: String(err) };
    }
  }

  getMetrics(): LinkMetrics {
    return { ...this.metrics };
  }

  isAvailable(): boolean {
    return this.state === 'connected' || this.state === 'degraded';
  }

  async healthCheck(): Promise<boolean> {
    const status = await this.fetchDishStatus();
    if (!status) {
      this.consecutiveFailures++;
      return false;
    }
    this.lastStatus = status;
    this.updateMetricsFromStatus(status);
    this.consecutiveFailures = 0;

    const wasAvailable = this.isAvailable();
    this.state = status.state === 'CONNECTED' ? 'connected' : 'searching';
    if (wasAvailable !== this.isAvailable()) {
      this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state });
    }
    return this.state === 'connected';
  }

  async getObstructionMap(): Promise<StarlinkObstructionMap | null> {
    try {
      // Fetch obstruction data from Starlink dish gRPC API
      const response = await this.dishRequest('/SpaceX.API.Device.Device/Handle', {
        getObstructionMap: {},
      });
      return response?.dishGetObstructionMap ?? null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  async getFullStatus(): Promise<StarlinkStatus | null> {
    return this.fetchDishStatus();
  }

  private async fetchDishStatus(): Promise<StarlinkStatus | null> {
    try {
      const data = await this.dishRequest('/SpaceX.API.Device.Device/Handle', {
        getStatus: {},
      });

      if (!data?.dishGetStatus) return null;
      const s = data.dishGetStatus;

      return {
        state: s.state ?? 'UNKNOWN',
        uptimeSeconds: s.deviceInfo?.uptimeS ?? 0,
        uplinkThroughputMbps: (s.uplinkThroughputBps ?? 0) / 1_000_000,
        downlinkThroughputMbps: (s.downlinkThroughputBps ?? 0) / 1_000_000,
        pingDropRateLast1h: s.popPingDropRate ?? 0,
        pingLatencyMsLast1h: s.popPingLatencyMs ?? 0,
        obstructionPercentTime: s.obstructionStats?.fractionObstructed ?? 0,
        gpsValid: s.gpsStats?.gpsValid ?? false,
        alerts: Object.entries(s.alerts ?? {}).filter(([, v]) => v).map(([k]) => k),
        softwareVersion: s.deviceInfo?.softwareVersion ?? '',
        hardwareVersion: s.deviceInfo?.hardwareVersion ?? '',
        deviceId: s.deviceInfo?.id ?? '',
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private async dishRequest(path: string, body: object): Promise<any> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const payload = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body));
      const options = {
        hostname: this.apiBase.replace('http://', '').replace('https://', '').split(':')[0],
        port: STARLINK_GRPC_PORT,
        path,
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/grpc+json',
          'Content-Length': payload.length,
        },
        timeout: 5_000,
      };

      const socket = new net.Socket();
      socket.setTimeout(5_000);
      let responseData = Buffer.alloc(0);

      socket.connect(STARLINK_GRPC_PORT, options.hostname, () => {
        const request = `POST ${path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: ${options.hostname}\r\nContent-Type: application/json\r\nContent-Length: ${payload.length}\r\n\r\n`;
        socket.write(request + payload.toString());
      });

      socket.on('data', chunk => { responseData = Buffer.concat([responseData, chunk]); });
      socket.on('end', () => {
        try {
          const body = responseData.toString().split('\r\n\r\n').slice(1).join('');
          resolve(JSON.parse(body));
        } catch { reject(new Error('Parse failed')); }
      });
      socket.on('error', reject);
      socket.on('timeout', () => { socket.destroy(); reject(new Error('Timeout')); });
    });
  }

  private async tunnelSend(data: Buffer, options: SendOptions): Promise<SendResult> {
    // In production: send over Starlink IP connection to UBE relay
    // This wraps data in a UBE satellite envelope and sends via TCP
    const envelope = Buffer.concat([
      Buffer.from([0x55, 0x42, 0x45, 0x53]), // 'UBES' magic
      Buffer.allocUnsafe(4).fill(0),           // Length placeholder
      data,
    ]);
    envelope.writeUInt32BE(data.length, 4);

    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      const socket = new net.Socket();
      socket.setTimeout(15_000);

      socket.connect(443, this.apiBase.replace(/https?:\/\//, ''), () => {
        socket.write(envelope, err => {
          if (err) reject(err);
          else {
            socket.end();
            resolve({ success: true, bytesUsed: data.length, messageId: crypto.randomUUID() });
          }
        });
      });
      socket.on('error', reject);
      socket.on('timeout', () => { socket.destroy(); reject(new Error('Send timeout')); });
    });
  }

  private updateMetricsFromStatus(status: StarlinkStatus): void {
    this.metrics = {
      ...this.metrics,
      provider: 'starlink',
      state: status.state === 'CONNECTED' ? 'connected' : 'searching',
      latencyMs: status.pingLatencyMsLast1h,
      uplinkThroughputMbps: status.uplinkThroughputMbps,
      downlinkThroughputMbps: status.downlinkThroughputMbps,
      uplinkBpsEstimate: status.uplinkThroughputMbps * 1_000_000,
      downlinkBpsEstimate: status.downlinkThroughputMbps * 1_000_000,
      packetLoss: status.pingDropRateLast1h,
      signalStrength: Math.max(0, 100 - status.obstructionPercentTime * 100),
      obstructionPercent: status.obstructionPercentTime * 100,
      lastSuccessful: Date.now(),
    };
  }

  private startHealthChecks(): void {
    this.healthTimer = setInterval(() => {
      this.healthCheck().catch(() => {});
    }, LINK_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS);
  }

  private emptyMetrics(): LinkMetrics {
    return {
      provider: 'starlink', state: 'disconnected', latencyMs: 0,
      uplinkBpsEstimate: 0, downlinkBpsEstimate: 0, signalStrength: 0,
      packetLoss: 0, bytesUsedThisMonth: 0,
      bytesRemainingThisMonth: this.config?.monthlyBudgetBytes ?? 0,
      lastSuccessful: 0, obstructionPercent: 0,
      uplinkThroughputMbps: 0, downlinkThroughputMbps: 0,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// IRIDIUM SBD LINK
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface SBDMessage {
  id: string;
  data: Buffer;
  momsn?: number;   // Mobile Originated Message Sequence Number
  mtmsn?: number;   // Mobile Terminated Message Sequence Number
  imei?: string;
  sessionStatus: number;
  rxCount: number;
  txCount: number;
}

export class IridiumSBDLink extends EventEmitter implements ISatelliteLink {
  readonly provider: SatelliteProvider = 'iridium';
  private state: LinkState = 'disconnected';
  private metrics: LinkMetrics;
  private readonly config: SatelliteConfig;
  private moQueue: Buffer[] = [];
  private mtBuffer: Buffer[] = [];
  private sessionInProgress: boolean = false;
  private signalStrength: number = 0;
  private momsn: number = 0;
  private mtmsn: number = 0;
  private checkTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private serialBuffer: string = '';

  constructor(config: SatelliteConfig) {
    super();
    this.config = config;
    this.metrics = this.emptyMetrics();
  }

  async connect(): Promise<boolean> {
