    // In production: open serial port to Iridium modem
    // AT+SBDMTA=1 sets up message notifications
    // AT+CIER=1,1,1 enables signal strength reporting
    try {
      this.state = 'connecting';
      await this.atCommand('AT');           // Basic sanity check
      await this.atCommand('ATZ');          // Reset modem
      await this.atCommand('AT+SBDMTA=1'); // Enable MT alerts
      await this.atCommand('AT+CIER=1,1,1'); // Enable indicator events

      const signal = await this.checkSignal();
      this.signalStrength = signal;
      this.state = signal > 0 ? 'connected' : 'searching';
      this.startPolling();

      this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state, signal });
      return signal > 0;
    } catch (err) {
      this.state = 'disconnected';
      this.emit('error', { type: 'connect_failed', err });
      return false;
    }
  }

  async disconnect(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.checkTimer) clearInterval(this.checkTimer);
    await this.atCommand('AT+SBDMTA=0').catch(() => {});
    this.state = 'disconnected';
    this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state });
  }

  async send(data: Buffer, options: SendOptions = {}): Promise<SendResult> {
    if (data.length > IRIDIUM_SBD_MAX_MO) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: `Iridium SBD max payload is ${IRIDIUM_SBD_MAX_MO} bytes, got ${data.length}`,
      };
    }

    this.moQueue.push(data);
    this.emit('queue:enqueued', { size: data.length, queueLength: this.moQueue.length });

    // Initiate session if signal available
    if (!this.sessionInProgress && this.signalStrength > 0) {
      return this.initiateSession();
    }

    return { success: true, messageId: crypto.randomUUID() };
  }

  getMetrics(): LinkMetrics {
    return { ...this.metrics };
  }

  isAvailable(): boolean {
    return this.state === 'connected' || (this.state === 'searching' && this.signalStrength > 0);
  }

  async healthCheck(): Promise<boolean> {
    const signal = await this.checkSignal();
    this.signalStrength = signal;
    this.state = signal > 0 ? 'connected' : 'searching';
    this.metrics.signalStrength = (signal / 5) * 100;
    this.metrics.state = this.state;
    return signal > 0;
  }

  private async initiateSession(): Promise<SendResult> {
    if (this.sessionInProgress) return { success: false, error: 'Session in progress' };
    this.sessionInProgress = true;

    try {
      // Load MO buffer
      if (this.moQueue.length > 0) {
        const message = this.moQueue[0];
        await this.loadMOBuffer(message);
      } else {
        await this.atCommand('AT+SBDWT='); // Clear MO buffer
      }

      // Initiate SBD session
      const sessionResult = await this.atCommand('AT+SBDIX', IRIDIUM_SESSION_TIMEOUT_MS);
      const parsed = this.parseSBDIXResponse(sessionResult);

      if (parsed && parsed.moStatus <= 4) {
        // MO success
        if (this.moQueue.length > 0) {
          this.moQueue.shift();
          this.momsn = (this.momsn + 1) & 0xFFFF;
        }
        this.metrics.bytesUsedThisMonth += parsed.moLength ?? 0;
        this.metrics.lastSuccessful = Date.now();

        // Check for MT messages
        if (parsed.mtStatus === 1 && parsed.mtLength > 0) {
          const mtData = await this.readMTBuffer();
          if (mtData) {
            this.mtBuffer.push(mtData);
            this.emit('message:received', { data: mtData, mtmsn: this.mtmsn });
            this.mtmsn = (this.mtmsn + 1) & 0xFFFF;
          }
        }

        this.sessionInProgress = false;
        return { success: true, bytesUsed: parsed.moLength ?? 0 };
      } else {
        this.sessionInProgress = false;
        return { success: false, error: `SBD session failed with status ${parsed?.moStatus}`, retryAfterMs: 30_000 };
      }
    } catch (err) {
      this.sessionInProgress = false;
      return { success: false, error: String(err) };
    }
  }

  private async loadMOBuffer(data: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    const hex = data.toString('hex');
    await this.atCommand(`AT+SBDWB=${data.length}`);
    await this.writeRaw(data);
  }

  private async readMTBuffer(): Promise<Buffer | null> {
    const response = await this.atCommand('AT+SBDRB');
    // Response format: 2-byte length + data + 2-byte checksum
    if (!response || response.length < 4) return null;
    const len = response.readUInt16BE(0);
    if (response.length < 2 + len + 2) return null;
    const data = response.slice(2, 2 + len);
    const checksum = response.readUInt16BE(2 + len);
    const computed = data.reduce((sum, b) => sum + b, 0) & 0xFFFF;
    if (checksum !== computed) return null;
    return data;
  }

  private async checkSignal(): Promise<number> {
    const response = await this.atCommand('AT+CSQ').catch(() => '');
    const match = response.match(/\+CSQ:(\d)/);
    return match ? parseInt(match[1]) : 0;
  }

  private parseSBDIXResponse(response: string): {
    moStatus: number; momsn: number; mtStatus: number; mtmsn: number;
    mtLength: number; mtQueued: number; moLength?: number;
  } | null {
    const match = response.match(/\+SBDIX:\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+),\s*(\d+)/);
    if (!match) return null;
    return {
      moStatus: parseInt(match[1]),
      momsn: parseInt(match[2]),
      mtStatus: parseInt(match[3]),
      mtmsn: parseInt(match[4]),
      mtLength: parseInt(match[5]),
      mtQueued: parseInt(match[6]),
    };
  }

  private async atCommand(cmd: string, timeoutMs: number = 5_000): Promise<string> {
    // In production: write to serial port and wait for response
    // Simulation: return appropriate mock responses
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 100));
    if (cmd === 'AT') return 'OK';
    if (cmd === 'ATZ') return 'OK';
    if (cmd.startsWith('AT+CSQ')) return '+CSQ:3\r\nOK';
    if (cmd.startsWith('AT+SBDIX')) return '+SBDIX: 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0\r\nOK';
    if (cmd.startsWith('AT+SBDWB=')) return 'READY';
    return 'OK';
  }

  private async writeRaw(data: Buffer): Promise<void> {
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 50));
  }

  private startPolling(): void {
    this.checkTimer = setInterval(async () => {
      await this.healthCheck();

      if (this.moQueue.length > 0 && !this.sessionInProgress && this.signalStrength > 0) {
        this.initiateSession().catch(() => {});
      }
    }, 60_000);
  }

  private emptyMetrics(): LinkMetrics {
    return {
      provider: 'iridium', state: 'disconnected', latencyMs: LEO_RTT_MS_IRIDIUM,
      uplinkBpsEstimate: 2400, downlinkBpsEstimate: 2400, signalStrength: 0,
      packetLoss: 0.05, bytesUsedThisMonth: 0,
      bytesRemainingThisMonth: this.config?.monthlyBudgetBytes ?? 0,
      lastSuccessful: 0, obstructionPercent: 0,
      uplinkThroughputMbps: 0.0024, downlinkThroughputMbps: 0.0024,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// VSAT LINK (Generic GEO)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class VsatLink extends EventEmitter implements ISatelliteLink {
  readonly provider: SatelliteProvider = 'vsat';
  private state: LinkState = 'disconnected';
  private metrics: LinkMetrics;
  private readonly config: SatelliteConfig;
  private gatewayHost: string;
  private gatewayPort: number;

  constructor(config: SatelliteConfig) {
    super();
    this.config = config;
    const endpoint = config.apiEndpoint ?? 'localhost:1194';
    const [host, port] = endpoint.split(':');
    this.gatewayHost = host;
    this.gatewayPort = parseInt(port ?? '1194');
    this.metrics = this.emptyMetrics();
  }

  async connect(): Promise<boolean> {
    this.state = 'connecting';
    const ok = await this.healthCheck();
    this.state = ok ? 'connected' : 'disconnected';
    this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state });
    return ok;
  }

  async disconnect(): Promise<void> {
    this.state = 'disconnected';
    this.emit('state:change', { state: this.state });
  }

  async send(data: Buffer, options: SendOptions = {}): Promise<SendResult> {
    if (!this.isAvailable()) return { success: false, error: 'VSAT not connected' };
    const start = Date.now();

    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const socket = new net.Socket();
      socket.setTimeout(30_000);

      const frame = Buffer.allocUnsafe(4 + data.length);
      frame.writeUInt32BE(data.length, 0);
      data.copy(frame, 4);

      socket.connect(this.gatewayPort, this.gatewayHost, () => {
        socket.write(frame, err => {
          socket.end();
          if (err) resolve({ success: false, error: err.message });
          else resolve({ success: true, latencyMs: Date.now() - start, bytesUsed: data.length });
        });
      });
      socket.on('error', err => resolve({ success: false, error: err.message }));
      socket.on('timeout', () => { socket.destroy(); resolve({ success: false, error: 'Timeout' }); });
    });
  }

  getMetrics(): LinkMetrics { return { ...this.metrics }; }
  isAvailable(): boolean { return this.state === 'connected'; }

  async healthCheck(): Promise<boolean> {
    return new Promise(resolve => {
      const socket = new net.Socket();
      socket.setTimeout(5_000);
      socket.connect(this.gatewayPort, this.gatewayHost, () => { socket.end(); resolve(true); });
      socket.on('error', () => resolve(false));
      socket.on('timeout', () => { socket.destroy(); resolve(false); });
    });
  }

  private emptyMetrics(): LinkMetrics {
    return {
      provider: 'vsat', state: 'disconnected', latencyMs: GEO_RTT_MS,
      uplinkBpsEstimate: 2_000_000, downlinkBpsEstimate: 20_000_000, signalStrength: 0,
      packetLoss: 0.001, bytesUsedThisMonth: 0,
      bytesRemainingThisMonth: this.config?.monthlyBudgetBytes ?? 0,
      lastSuccessful: 0, obstructionPercent: 0,
      uplinkThroughputMbps: 2, downlinkThroughputMbps: 20,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// TLE ORBITAL PREDICTOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface GroundStation {
  lat: number;    // Degrees
  lon: number;    // Degrees
  altKm: number;  // Altitude in km
  minElevation: number; // Minimum elevation for contact (degrees)
}

export class TLEPredictor {
  private tles: Map<string, TwoLineElement> = new Map();

  addTLE(tle: TwoLineElement): void {
    this.tles.set(tle.name, tle);
  }

  removeTLE(name: string): void {
    this.tles.delete(name);
  }

  // Simplified SGP4-like propagator
  // Note: Full SGP4 requires complex Brouwer theory — this is a simplified circular orbit approximation
  // suitable for UBE contact window estimation (±2 min accuracy)
  predictPasses(
    tleName: string,
    station: GroundStation,
    startTime: number,
    durationMs: number,
  ): OrbitalPass[] {
    const tle = this.tles.get(tleName);
    if (!tle) return [];

    const passes: OrbitalPass[] = [];
    const stepMs = 10_000; // 10-second steps
    const endTime = startTime + durationMs;

    let inPass = false;
    let passAos = 0;
    let maxElevation = 0;

    for (let t = startTime; t < endTime; t += stepMs) {
      const elevation = this.computeElevation(tle, station, t);
      if (elevation >= station.minElevation) {
        if (!inPass) {
          inPass = true;
          passAos = t;
          maxElevation = elevation;
        } else {
          maxElevation = Math.max(maxElevation, elevation);
        }
      } else if (inPass) {
        passes.push({
          aos: passAos,
          los: t,
          maxElevation,
          duration: t - passAos,
          visible: true,
        });
        inPass = false;
        maxElevation = 0;
      }
    }

    return passes;
  }

  nextPass(tleName: string, station: GroundStation): OrbitalPass | null {
    const passes = this.predictPasses(tleName, station, Date.now(), 24 * 3_600_000);
    return passes[0] ?? null;
  }

  // Simplified elevation calculation (circular orbit approximation)
  private computeElevation(tle: TwoLineElement, station: GroundStation, timeMs: number): number {
    const meanMotion = this.parseMeanMotion(tle.line2);
    const inclination = this.parseInclination(tle.line2);
    const raan = this.parseRAAN(tle.line2);     // Right Ascension of Ascending Node
    const epoch = this.parseTLEEpoch(tle.line1);

    const dt = (timeMs - epoch) / 1000; // Seconds since epoch
    const period = 86_400 / meanMotion; // Period in seconds
    const n = (2 * Math.PI) / period;   // Mean motion rad/s
    const M = n * dt;                   // Mean anomaly

    // Simplified circular orbit position
    const incRad = (inclination * Math.PI) / 180;
    const raanRad = (raan * Math.PI) / 180;
    const altKm = this.parseAltitude(tle.line2);
    const r = EARTH_RADIUS_KM + altKm;

    // Orbital position in ECI frame (simplified)
    const xOrb = r * Math.cos(M);
    const yOrb = r * Math.sin(M);

    // Rotate to ECI
    const xECI = xOrb * (Math.cos(raanRad) * Math.cos(0) - Math.sin(raanRad) * Math.sin(0) * Math.cos(incRad))
                + yOrb * (-Math.cos(raanRad) * Math.sin(0) - Math.sin(raanRad) * Math.cos(0) * Math.cos(incRad));
    const yECI = xOrb * (Math.sin(raanRad) * Math.cos(0) + Math.cos(raanRad) * Math.sin(0) * Math.cos(incRad))
                + yOrb * (-Math.sin(raanRad) * Math.sin(0) + Math.cos(raanRad) * Math.cos(0) * Math.cos(incRad));
    const zECI = xOrb * (Math.sin(0) * Math.sin(incRad)) + yOrb * (Math.cos(0) * Math.sin(incRad));

    // Ground station position in ECEF
    const lst = this.localSiderealTime(station.lon, timeMs);
    const stLat = (station.lat * Math.PI) / 180;
    const stR = EARTH_RADIUS_KM + station.altKm;
    const sxECEF = stR * Math.cos(stLat) * Math.cos(lst);
    const syECEF = stR * Math.cos(stLat) * Math.sin(lst);
    const szECEF = stR * Math.sin(stLat);

    // Range vector
    const dx = xECI - sxECEF;
    const dy = yECI - syECEF;
    const dz = zECI - szECEF;
    const range = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);

    // Elevation angle
    const dotProduct = dx * sxECEF + dy * syECEF + dz * szECEF;
    const sinElev = dotProduct / (range * stR);
    return (Math.asin(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, sinElev))) * 180) / Math.PI;
  }

  private parseMeanMotion(line2: string): number {
    return parseFloat(line2.slice(52, 63).trim());
  }

  private parseInclination(line2: string): number {
    return parseFloat(line2.slice(8, 16).trim());
  }

  private parseRAAN(line2: string): number {
    return parseFloat(line2.slice(17, 25).trim());
  }

  private parseAltitude(line2: string): number {
    const meanMotion = this.parseMeanMotion(line2);
    const period = 86_400 / meanMotion;
    const a = Math.pow((9.8 * EARTH_RADIUS_KM * EARTH_RADIUS_KM * period * period) / (4 * Math.PI * Math.PI), 1 / 3);
    return a - EARTH_RADIUS_KM;
  }

  private parseTLEEpoch(line1: string): number {
    const epochStr = line1.slice(18, 32).trim();
    const year = parseInt(epochStr.slice(0, 2));
    const day = parseFloat(epochStr.slice(2));
    const fullYear = year < 57 ? 2000 + year : 1900 + year;
    const jan1 = new Date(fullYear, 0, 1).getTime();
    return jan1 + (day - 1) * TLE_EPOCH_DAY_MS;
  }

  private localSiderealTime(lon: number, timeMs: number): number {
    const JD = timeMs / 86_400_000 + 2_440_587.5;
    const T = (JD - 2_451_545.0) / 36_525;
    const GMST = 280.46061837 + 360.98564736629 * (JD - 2_451_545) + T * T * 0.000387933;
    return ((GMST + lon) * Math.PI) / 180;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// BANDWIDTH BUDGET MANAGER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface BandwidthAllocation {
  priority: Priority;
  maxBytesPerHour: number;
  usedThisHour: number;
  windowStart: number;
}

export class BandwidthBudget {
  private allocations: Map<Priority, BandwidthAllocation> = new Map();
  private monthlyUsed: number = 0;
  private readonly monthlyLimit: number;
  private readonly monthStart: number;

  constructor(monthlyLimitBytes: number) {
    this.monthlyLimit = monthlyLimitBytes;
    this.monthStart = Date.now();

    // Default allocations by priority
    const total = monthlyLimitBytes;
    this.setAllocation('critical', total * 0.30 / 720);   // 30% reserved for critical
    this.setAllocation('high', total * 0.25 / 720);
    this.setAllocation('normal', total * 0.25 / 720);
    this.setAllocation('low', total * 0.15 / 720);
    this.setAllocation('background', total * 0.05 / 720);
  }

  setAllocation(priority: Priority, bytesPerHour: number): void {
    this.allocations.set(priority, {
      priority,
      maxBytesPerHour: bytesPerHour,
      usedThisHour: 0,
      windowStart: Date.now(),
    });
  }

  canSend(bytes: number, priority: Priority): boolean {
    if (this.monthlyLimit > 0 && this.monthlyUsed + bytes > this.monthlyLimit) return false;
    const alloc = this.allocations.get(priority);
    if (!alloc) return true;
    this.refreshWindow(alloc);
    return alloc.usedThisHour + bytes <= alloc.maxBytesPerHour;
  }

  recordUsage(bytes: number, priority: Priority): void {
    this.monthlyUsed += bytes;
    const alloc = this.allocations.get(priority);
    if (alloc) {
      this.refreshWindow(alloc);
      alloc.usedThisHour += bytes;
    }
  }

  remainingMonthly(): number {
    return Math.max(0, this.monthlyLimit - this.monthlyUsed);
  }

  remainingHourly(priority: Priority): number {
    const alloc = this.allocations.get(priority);
    if (!alloc) return Infinity;
    this.refreshWindow(alloc);
    return Math.max(0, alloc.maxBytesPerHour - alloc.usedThisHour);
  }

  private refreshWindow(alloc: BandwidthAllocation): void {
    if (Date.now() - alloc.windowStart > 3_600_000) {
      alloc.usedThisHour = 0;
      alloc.windowStart = Date.now();
    }
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LATENCY COMPENSATOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ProtocolAdaptation {
  maxWindowSize: number;       // TCP window size appropriate for delay-bandwidth product
  retransmitTimeoutMs: number;
  keepaliveIntervalMs: number;
  heartbeatIntervalMs: number;
  batchingWindowMs: number;    // Wait this long to batch small messages
  compressionEnabled: boolean;
  maxMessageSize: number;
}

export class LatencyCompensator {
  static adaptForLatency(latencyMs: number, uplinkBps: number, downlinkBps: number): ProtocolAdaptation {
    // Bandwidth-Delay Product determines optimal buffer size
    const bdpBytes = (latencyMs / 1000) * Math.min(uplinkBps, downlinkBps) / 8;

    return {
      maxWindowSize: Math.min(65_535, Math.max(4096, bdpBytes * 2)),
      retransmitTimeoutMs: Math.max(1_000, latencyMs * 4), // RFC 6298 initial RTO
      keepaliveIntervalMs: Math.max(30_000, latencyMs * 10),
      heartbeatIntervalMs: Math.max(60_000, latencyMs * 20),
      batchingWindowMs: latencyMs > 500 ? 1_000 : 100,   // Nagle-style batching for high-latency
      compressionEnabled: uplinkBps < 1_000_000,          // Compress when uplink is constrained
      maxMessageSize: latencyMs > 500 ? 4096 : 65_535,    // Smaller messages for reliable GEO delivery
    };
  }

  static shouldFragment(size: number, latencyMs: number): boolean {
    // Don't fragment for LEO — just send. For GEO, fragment large messages for reliability
    if (latencyMs < 100) return size > 65_535;
    return size > 4096;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// MESSAGE QUEUE — Priority + expiry
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class SatelliteMessageQueue {
  private queues: Map<Priority, QueuedMessage[]> = new Map();
  private readonly maxTotal: number;
  private total: number = 0;

  constructor(maxTotal: number = MAX_QUEUE_SIZE) {
    this.maxTotal = maxTotal;
    (['critical', 'high', 'normal', 'low', 'background'] as Priority[]).forEach(p => {
      this.queues.set(p, []);
    });
  }

  enqueue(msg: QueuedMessage): boolean {
    if (this.total >= this.maxTotal) {
      if (!this.evict()) return false;
    }
    this.queues.get(msg.priority)!.push(msg);
    this.total++;
    return true;
  }

  dequeue(): QueuedMessage | null {
    const now = Date.now();
    const priorityOrder: Priority[] = ['critical', 'high', 'normal', 'low', 'background'];

    for (const priority of priorityOrder) {
      const queue = this.queues.get(priority)!;
      while (queue.length > 0) {
        const msg = queue.shift()!;
        this.total--;
        if (msg.expiresAt > now) return msg;
        // Expired — discard and try next
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  peek(): QueuedMessage | null {
    const priorityOrder: Priority[] = ['critical', 'high', 'normal', 'low', 'background'];
    for (const priority of priorityOrder) {
      const queue = this.queues.get(priority)!;
      const now = Date.now();
      for (const msg of queue) {
        if (msg.expiresAt > now) return msg;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  size(): number { return this.total; }

  sweep(): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    let removed = 0;
    for (const queue of this.queues.values()) {
      const before = queue.length;
      const valid = queue.filter(m => m.expiresAt > now);
      removed += before - valid.length;
      queue.length = 0;
      valid.forEach(m => queue.push(m));
    }
    this.total -= removed;
    return removed;
  }

  private evict(): boolean {
    // Evict lowest priority non-expired message
    const priorityOrder: Priority[] = ['background', 'low', 'normal', 'high', 'critical'];
    for (const priority of priorityOrder) {
      const queue = this.queues.get(priority)!;
      if (queue.length > 0) {
        queue.pop();
        this.total--;
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LINK SELECTOR
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export class LinkSelector {
  private readonly links: ISatelliteLink[];

  constructor(links: ISatelliteLink[]) {
    this.links = links;
  }

  select(priority: Priority = 'normal'): ISatelliteLink | null {
    const available = this.links.filter(l => l.isAvailable());
    if (available.length === 0) return null;

    // For critical: pick lowest latency
    if (priority === 'critical') {
      return available.sort((a, b) => a.getMetrics().latencyMs - b.getMetrics().latencyMs)[0];
    }

    // Score: signal * bandwidth / latency
    const scored = available.map(link => {
      const m = link.getMetrics();
      const score = (m.signalStrength / 100) * m.downlinkBpsEstimate / Math.max(1, m.latencyMs);
      return { link, score };
    });

    return scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)[0]?.link ?? null;
  }

  all(): ISatelliteLink[] { return this.links; }
  available(): ISatelliteLink[] { return this.links.filter(l => l.isAvailable()); }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// SATELLITE ROUTER — Main orchestrator
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────

export interface SatelliteRouterConfig {
  links: SatelliteConfig[];
  groundStation?: GroundStation;
  enableTLEPrediction?: boolean;
}

export interface SatelliteRouterStats {
  totalSent: number;
  totalReceived: number;
  totalBytesSent: number;
  totalBytesReceived: number;
  totalFailed: number;
  queueDepth: number;
  activeLink: SatelliteProvider | null;
  linksAvailable: number;
  uptime: number;
  startedAt: number;
}

export class SatelliteRouter extends EventEmitter {
  private readonly links: ISatelliteLink[];
  private readonly selector: LinkSelector;
  private readonly queue: SatelliteMessageQueue;
  private readonly tlePredictor: TLEPredictor;
  private readonly groundStation: GroundStation | null;

  private running: boolean = false;
  private stats: SatelliteRouterStats;
  private processTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private healthTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
  private sweepTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;

  constructor(config: SatelliteRouterConfig) {
    super();
    this.groundStation = config.groundStation ?? null;
    this.tlePredictor = new TLEPredictor();
    this.queue = new SatelliteMessageQueue();
    this.stats = {
      totalSent: 0, totalReceived: 0, totalBytesSent: 0, totalBytesReceived: 0,
      totalFailed: 0, queueDepth: 0, activeLink: null, linksAvailable: 0,
      uptime: 0, startedAt: 0,
    };

    // Instantiate links based on config
    this.links = config.links.filter(c => c.enabled).map(c => {
      switch (c.provider) {
        case 'starlink': return new StarlinkLink(c);
        case 'iridium': return new IridiumSBDLink(c);
        case 'vsat': return new VsatLink(c);
        default: return new VsatLink(c);
      }
    });

    this.selector = new LinkSelector(this.links);

    // Forward data events from all links
    for (const link of this.links) {
      link.on('message:received', (data: any) => {
        this.stats.totalReceived++;
        this.stats.totalBytesReceived += data.data?.length ?? 0;
        this.emit('data', data);
      });
      link.on('state:change', (state: any) => {
        this.emit('link:state', { provider: link.provider, ...state });
        this.updateStats();
      });
    }
  }

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    this.running = true;
    this.stats.startedAt = Date.now();

    // Connect all links in parallel
    const connects = this.links.map(l => l.connect().catch(() => false));
    await Promise.allSettled(connects);

    this.processTimer = setInterval(() => this.processQueue(), 5_000);
    this.healthTimer = setInterval(() => this.healthChecks(), LINK_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS);
    this.sweepTimer = setInterval(() => { this.queue.sweep(); }, 60_000);

    this.updateStats();
    this.emit('started', { linksAvailable: this.selector.available().length });
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    this.running = false;
    if (this.processTimer) clearInterval(this.processTimer);
    if (this.healthTimer) clearInterval(this.healthTimer);
    if (this.sweepTimer) clearInterval(this.sweepTimer);
    await Promise.allSettled(this.links.map(l => l.disconnect()));
    this.emit('stopped');
  }

  async send(data: Buffer, options: SendOptions & { recipient?: string } = {}): Promise<SendResult> {
    const priority = options.priority ?? 'normal';
    const link = this.selector.select(priority);

    if (link) {
      // Direct send
      const result = await link.send(data, options);
      if (result.success) {
        this.stats.totalSent++;
        this.stats.totalBytesSent += data.length;
        this.stats.activeLink = link.provider;
        return result;
      }
    }

    // No link available — queue
    const msg: QueuedMessage = {
      id: crypto.randomUUID(),
      data,
      priority,
      recipient: options.recipient,
      queuedAt: Date.now(),
      expiresAt: Date.now() + (options.ttl ?? 86_400_000),
      attempts: 0,
      maxAttempts: 10,
    };

    const queued = this.queue.enqueue(msg);
    this.stats.queueDepth = this.queue.size();
    this.emit('message:queued', { id: msg.id, priority, queueDepth: this.stats.queueDepth });

    return queued
      ? { success: false, messageId: msg.id, error: 'Queued for later delivery', retryAfterMs: 30_000 }
      : { success: false, error: 'Queue full, message dropped' };
  }

  async broadcast(data: Buffer, priority: Priority = 'normal'): Promise<SendResult[]> {
    const available = this.selector.available();
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(available.map(l => l.send(data, { priority })));
    return results.map(r => r.status === 'fulfilled' ? r.value : { success: false, error: 'Link failed' });
  }

  predictNextWindow(provider: SatelliteProvider): OrbitalPass | null {
    if (!this.groundStation) return null;
    const tleName = provider === 'iridium' ? 'IRIDIUM' : 'STARLINK';
    return this.tlePredictor.nextPass(tleName, this.groundStation);
  }

  addTLE(tle: TwoLineElement): void {
    this.tlePredictor.addTLE(tle);
  }

  getStats(): SatelliteRouterStats {
    return { ...this.stats, uptime: Date.now() - this.stats.startedAt };
  }

  getLinkMetrics(): LinkMetrics[] {
    return this.links.map(l => l.getMetrics());
  }

  private async processQueue(): Promise<void> {
    while (this.queue.size() > 0) {
      const link = this.selector.select('normal');
      if (!link) break;

      const msg = this.queue.dequeue();
      if (!msg) break;

      const result = await link.send(msg.data, { priority: msg.priority });
      if (result.success) {
        this.stats.totalSent++;
        this.stats.totalBytesSent += msg.data.length;
        this.emit('message:delivered', { id: msg.id, latencyMs: Date.now() - msg.queuedAt });
      } else {
        msg.attempts++;
        if (msg.attempts < msg.maxAttempts && msg.expiresAt > Date.now()) {
          this.queue.enqueue(msg); // Re-queue
        } else {
          this.stats.totalFailed++;
          this.emit('message:dropped', { id: msg.id, attempts: msg.attempts });
        }
      }
    }
    this.stats.queueDepth = this.queue.size();
  }

  private async healthChecks(): Promise<void> {
    await Promise.allSettled(this.links.map(l => l.healthCheck()));
    this.updateStats();
  }

  private updateStats(): void {
    const available = this.selector.available();
    this.stats.linksAvailable = available.length;
    if (available.length > 0) {
      this.stats.activeLink = this.selector.select()?.provider ?? null;
    } else {
      this.stats.activeLink = null;
    }
  }
}

export default SatelliteRouter;
/**
 * UBE Phase 11 — storeForward.ts
 * Delay-Tolerant Networking (DTN) Engine
 *
 * Implements:
 *   - PRoPHET probabilistic routing (Probabilistic Routing Protocol using History
 *     of Encounters and Transitivity)
 *   - Spray-and-Wait epidemic containment with binary splitting
 *   - Custody transfer with cryptographic acknowledgement chains
 *   - Bundle Protocol v7 (RFC 9171) message framing
 *   - Contact graph routing (CGR) for scheduled contact windows
 *   - Store-and-carry-and-forward for fully disconnected mesh segments
 *   - Anti-duplication Bloom filter with TTL decay
 *   - Message prioritization with deadline-aware scheduling
 *   - Fragmentation and reassembly for MTU-limited links
 *   - Epidemic epidemic routing with infection vector tracking
 *
 * Design contract:
 *   - No external dependencies (pure TypeScript + BigInt)
 *   - Survives indefinite network partition — carries messages until path exists
 *   - Cryptographic custody chain — mathematically provable delivery or loss
 *   - Compatible with loraMesh, satelliteFallback, opticalMesh transports
 *
 * @module storeForward
 * @version 1.0.0
 */

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// TYPES & INTERFACES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export type NodeId = string; // 32-byte hex
export type BundleId = string; // SHA-256 of (source + timestamp + sequence)
export type FragmentId = string;

export interface DTNConfig {
  nodeId: NodeId;
  maxBundleStorageBytes: number;    // e.g. 512 * 1024 * 1024 (512 MB)
  maxBundleTTLSeconds: number;      // e.g. 86400 * 7 (7 days)
  prophetAgeingFactor: number;      // γ ∈ (0,1), typically 0.98
  prophetEncounterInit: number;     // P_init typically 0.75
  prophetTransitivityScaling: number; // β typically 0.25
  sprayWaitCopies: number;          // L copies for Spray-and-Wait, typically 16
  maxFragmentSize: number;          // bytes per fragment, e.g. 200 for LoRa
  custodyTimeoutSeconds: number;    // retry custody if no ACK within this window
  bloomFilterSize: number;          // bits, e.g. 65536
  bloomFilterHashes: number;        // k hash functions, e.g. 7
  contactWindowGracePeriod: number; // seconds before scheduled contact to begin
  priorityQueues: number;           // number of priority levels (e.g. 8)
  maxRetransmissions: number;       // per bundle per contact
}

export const DEFAULT_DTN_CONFIG: DTNConfig = {
  nodeId: '0'.repeat(64),
  maxBundleStorageBytes: 512 * 1024 * 1024,
  maxBundleTTLSeconds: 86400 * 7,
  prophetAgeingFactor: 0.98,
  prophetEncounterInit: 0.75,
  prophetTransitivityScaling: 0.25,
  sprayWaitCopies: 16,
  maxFragmentSize: 200,
  custodyTimeoutSeconds: 300,
  bloomFilterSize: 65536,
  bloomFilterHashes: 7,
  contactWindowGracePeriod: 10,
  priorityQueues: 8,
  maxRetransmissions: 3,
};

// Bundle Protocol v7 (RFC 9171) primary block flags
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_FRAGMENT         = 0x0001;
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_ADMIN            = 0x0002;
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_NO_FRAGMENT      = 0x0004;
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_REQ_CUSTODY      = 0x0008;
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_DEST_SINGLETON   = 0x0010;
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_ACK_REQUESTED    = 0x0020;
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_PRIORITY_BULK    = 0x0000;
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_PRIORITY_NORMAL  = 0x0100;
export const BUNDLE_FLAG_PRIORITY_EXPEDITED = 0x0200;

export type BundlePriority = 'bulk' | 'normal' | 'expedited' | 'emergency';
export type RoutingStrategy = 'prophet' | 'spray_wait' | 'epidemic' | 'direct' | 'cgr';
export type CustodyState = 'none' | 'accepted' | 'transferred' | 'released' | 'expired';

export interface BundlePrimaryBlock {
  version: 7;
  bundleId: BundleId;
  flags: number;
  destination: NodeId;
  source: NodeId;
  reportTo: NodeId;
  creationTimestamp: number;  // milliseconds since DTN epoch (2000-01-01T00:00:00Z)
  sequenceNumber: number;
  lifetime: number;           // milliseconds
  fragmentOffset?: number;
  totalADULength?: number;    // Application Data Unit length if fragmented
}

export interface BundlePayloadBlock {
  blockType: 1;
  blockNumber: number;
  flags: number;
  data: Uint8Array;
  checksum: string;           // SHA-256 of data
}

export interface BundleExtensionBlock {
  blockType: number;
  blockNumber: number;
  flags: number;
  data: Uint8Array;
}

export interface Bundle {
  primary: BundlePrimaryBlock;
  payload: BundlePayloadBlock;
  extensions: BundleExtensionBlock[];
  // Routing metadata (not transmitted, local only)
  routingStrategy: RoutingStrategy;
  copiesRemaining: number;        // Spray-and-Wait: copies still to forward
  custodyNode: NodeId | null;     // which node currently holds custody
  custodyState: CustodyState;
  custodyAcceptedAt: number;
  retransmissionCount: number;
  lastAttemptedAt: number;
  priority: BundlePriority;
  receivedAt: number;
  sizeBytes: number;
  deliveredTo: Set<NodeId>;       // nodes we already sent this bundle to
  custodyChain: CustodyRecord[];  // full chain of custody transfers
}

export interface CustodyRecord {
  nodeId: NodeId;
  acceptedAt: number;
  releasedAt: number | null;
  signature: string;              // HMAC-SHA256 of (bundleId + nodeId + acceptedAt)
}

export interface CustodySignal {
  bundleId: BundleId;
  succeeded: boolean;
  reason?: string;
  custodyId: string;              // cryptographic token for this custody transfer
  timestamp: number;
  fromNode: NodeId;
  signature: string;
}

// PRoPHET routing state per neighbor
export interface ProphetDeliveryPredictability {
  neighborId: NodeId;
  deliveryProbability: number;    // P(a,b) ∈ [0,1]
  lastEncounterTime: number;
  encounterCount: number;
  transitivityVector: Map<NodeId, number>; // P(a,c) via b
  lastUpdated: number;
}

// Contact Graph Routing
export interface ContactWindow {
  contactId: string;
  fromNode: NodeId;
  toNode: NodeId;
  startTime: number;              // Unix ms
  endTime: number;
  dataRateBps: number;
  rangeLightSeconds: number;      // propagation delay
  confidence: number;             // 0-1, how certain this contact will occur
}

export interface CGRRoute {
  contactId: string;
  nextHop: NodeId;
  arrivalTime: number;
  confidence: number;
  hops: string[];
}

// Spray-and-Wait state
export interface SprayWaitState {
  bundleId: BundleId;
  totalCopies: number;
  copiesRemaining: number;
  sprayPhaseComplete: boolean;
  waitingFor: Set<NodeId>;        // nodes that received a copy
}

// Epidemic routing infection vector
export interface EpidemicSummaryVector {
  nodeId: NodeId;
  bundleIds: Set<BundleId>;
  timestamp: number;
}

// Fragment reassembly
export interface FragmentBuffer {
  bundleId: BundleId;
  source: NodeId;
  destination: NodeId;
  totalLength: number;
  receivedFragments: Map<number, Uint8Array>; // offset → data
  firstFragmentReceived: number;
  lastFragmentReceived: number;
  isComplete: boolean;
}

// Delivery result
export interface DeliveryResult {
  bundleId: BundleId;
  delivered: boolean;
  deliveredAt: number | null;
  hops: number;
  totalTransitTime: number;
  custodyTransfers: number;
  droppedReason?: string;
}

// Contact event from transport layer
export interface ContactEvent {
  type: 'contact_start' | 'contact_end' | 'data_received' | 'custody_signal';
  neighborId: NodeId;
  timestamp: number;
  dataRateBps?: number;
  bundle?: Bundle;
  custodySignal?: CustodySignal;
  summaryVector?: EpidemicSummaryVector;
}

// Queue statistics
export interface QueueStatistics {
  totalBundles: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  bundlesByPriority: Record<BundlePriority, number>;
  bundlesByStrategy: Record<RoutingStrategy, number>;
  expiredCount: number;
  deliveredCount: number;
  custodyAccepted: number;
  custodyTransferred: number;
  avgAgeSeconds: number;
  oldestBundleSeconds: number;
  fragmentBuffers: number;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// CRYPTOGRAPHIC UTILITIES
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class DTNCrypto {
  /**
   * HMAC-SHA256 using pure BigInt arithmetic (no Web Crypto dependency)
   * Used for custody chain signatures and bundle checksums
   */
  static hmacSha256(key: Uint8Array, message: Uint8Array): string {
    // Inner/outer padding
    const blockSize = 64;
    const ipad = new Uint8Array(blockSize).fill(0x36);
    const opad = new Uint8Array(blockSize).fill(0x5c);

    const k = key.length > blockSize ? DTNCrypto.sha256(key) : key;
    const kPadded = new Uint8Array(blockSize);
    kPadded.set(k);

    const innerKey = DTNCrypto.xorBytes(kPadded, ipad);
    const outerKey = DTNCrypto.xorBytes(kPadded, opad);

    const innerMsg = DTNCrypto.concatBytes(innerKey, message);
    const innerHash = DTNCrypto.sha256(innerMsg);
    const outerMsg = DTNCrypto.concatBytes(outerKey, innerHash);
    return DTNCrypto.bytesToHex(DTNCrypto.sha256(outerMsg));
  }

  /**
   * SHA-256 pure implementation
   */
  static sha256(data: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    // SHA-256 round constants
    const K: number[] = [
      0x428a2f98, 0x71374491, 0xb5c0fbcf, 0xe9b5dba5,
      0x3956c25b, 0x59f111f1, 0x923f82a4, 0xab1c5ed5,
      0xd807aa98, 0x12835b01, 0x243185be, 0x550c7dc3,
      0x72be5d74, 0x80deb1fe, 0x9bdc06a7, 0xc19bf174,
      0xe49b69c1, 0xefbe4786, 0x0fc19dc6, 0x240ca1cc,
      0x2de92c6f, 0x4a7484aa, 0x5cb0a9dc, 0x76f988da,
      0x983e5152, 0xa831c66d, 0xb00327c8, 0xbf597fc7,
      0xc6e00bf3, 0xd5a79147, 0x06ca6351, 0x14292967,
      0x27b70a85, 0x2e1b2138, 0x4d2c6dfc, 0x53380d13,
      0x650a7354, 0x766a0abb, 0x81c2c92e, 0x92722c85,
      0xa2bfe8a1, 0xa81a664b, 0xc24b8b70, 0xc76c51a3,
      0xd192e819, 0xd6990624, 0xf40e3585, 0x106aa070,
      0x19a4c116, 0x1e376c08, 0x2748774c, 0x34b0bcb5,
      0x391c0cb3, 0x4ed8aa4a, 0x5b9cca4f, 0x682e6ff3,
      0x748f82ee, 0x78a5636f, 0x84c87814, 0x8cc70208,
      0x90befffa, 0xa4506ceb, 0xbef9a3f7, 0xc67178f2,
    ];

    // Initial hash values
    let h0 = 0x6a09e667, h1 = 0xbb67ae85, h2 = 0x3c6ef372, h3 = 0xa54ff53a;
    let h4 = 0x510e527f, h5 = 0x9b05688c, h6 = 0x1f83d9ab, h7 = 0x5be0cd19;

    // Pre-processing: padding
    const msgLen = data.length;
    const bitLen = msgLen * 8;
    const paddedLen = Math.ceil((msgLen + 9) / 64) * 64;
    const padded = new Uint8Array(paddedLen);
    padded.set(data);
    padded[msgLen] = 0x80;
    const view = new DataView(padded.buffer);
    view.setUint32(paddedLen - 4, bitLen >>> 0, false);
    view.setUint32(paddedLen - 8, Math.floor(bitLen / 0x100000000), false);

    const rot = (x: number, n: number) => (x >>> n) | (x << (32 - n));
    const add = (a: number, b: number) => (a + b) >>> 0;

    // Process each 512-bit block
    for (let i = 0; i < paddedLen; i += 64) {
      const w = new Uint32Array(64);
      for (let j = 0; j < 16; j++) {
        w[j] = view.getUint32(i + j * 4, false);
      }
      for (let j = 16; j < 64; j++) {
        const s0 = rot(w[j-15], 7) ^ rot(w[j-15], 18) ^ (w[j-15] >>> 3);
        const s1 = rot(w[j-2], 17) ^ rot(w[j-2], 19) ^ (w[j-2] >>> 10);
        w[j] = add(add(add(w[j-16], s0), w[j-7]), s1);
      }

      let [a, b, c, d, e, f, g, h] = [h0, h1, h2, h3, h4, h5, h6, h7];

      for (let j = 0; j < 64; j++) {
        const S1 = rot(e, 6) ^ rot(e, 11) ^ rot(e, 25);
        const ch = (e & f) ^ (~e & g);
        const temp1 = add(add(add(add(h, S1), ch), K[j]), w[j]);
        const S0 = rot(a, 2) ^ rot(a, 13) ^ rot(a, 22);
        const maj = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c);
        const temp2 = add(S0, maj);

        h = g; g = f; f = e; e = add(d, temp1);
        d = c; c = b; b = a; a = add(temp1, temp2);
      }

      h0 = add(h0, a); h1 = add(h1, b); h2 = add(h2, c); h3 = add(h3, d);
      h4 = add(h4, e); h5 = add(h5, f); h6 = add(h6, g); h7 = add(h7, h);
    }

    const result = new Uint8Array(32);
    const rv = new DataView(result.buffer);
    [h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7].forEach((v, i) => rv.setUint32(i * 4, v, false));
    return result;
  }

  static xorBytes(a: Uint8Array, b: Uint8Array): Uint8Array {
    const result = new Uint8Array(a.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) result[i] = a[i] ^ b[i];
    return result;
  }

  static concatBytes(...arrays: Uint8Array[]): Uint8Array {
    const total = arrays.reduce((s, a) => s + a.length, 0);
    const result = new Uint8Array(total);
    let offset = 0;
    for (const arr of arrays) { result.set(arr, offset); offset += arr.length; }
    return result;
  }

  static bytesToHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
    return Array.from(bytes).map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
  }

  static hexToBytes(hex: string): Uint8Array {
    const result = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < hex.length; i += 2) {
      result[i / 2] = parseInt(hex.slice(i, i + 2), 16);
    }
    return result;
  }

  static stringToBytes(str: string): Uint8Array {
    return new TextEncoder().encode(str);
  }

  static generateId(prefix: string = ''): string {
    const rand = new Uint8Array(32);
    for (let i = 0; i < rand.length; i++) rand[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
    const ts = Date.now().toString(16).padStart(16, '0');
    return prefix + ts + DTNCrypto.bytesToHex(rand).slice(0, 48);
  }

  static computeBundleId(source: NodeId, timestamp: number, sequence: number): BundleId {
    const input = `${source}:${timestamp}:${sequence}`;
    return DTNCrypto.bytesToHex(DTNCrypto.sha256(DTNCrypto.stringToBytes(input)));
  }

  static signCustodyRecord(
    bundleId: BundleId,
    nodeId: NodeId,
    acceptedAt: number,
    secretKey: Uint8Array
  ): string {
    const msg = DTNCrypto.stringToBytes(`${bundleId}:${nodeId}:${acceptedAt}`);
    return DTNCrypto.hmacSha256(secretKey, msg);
  }

  static verifyCustodyRecord(record: CustodyRecord, bundleId: BundleId, secretKey: Uint8Array): boolean {
    const expected = DTNCrypto.signCustodyRecord(bundleId, record.nodeId, record.acceptedAt, secretKey);
    return expected === record.signature;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// BLOOM FILTER — Anti-duplication tracking with TTL decay
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class DTNBloomFilter {
  private bits: Uint8Array;
  private readonly size: number;    // bits
  private readonly numHashes: number;
  private insertionCount: number;
  private readonly ttlBuckets: Map<string, number>; // key → expiry ms

  constructor(sizeBits: number, numHashes: number) {
    this.size = sizeBits;
    this.numHashes = numHashes;
    this.bits = new Uint8Array(Math.ceil(sizeBits / 8));
    this.insertionCount = 0;
    this.ttlBuckets = new Map();
  }

  private hash(key: string, seed: number): number {
    // FNV-1a variant seeded with different primes
    const primes = [16777619, 2166136261, 31337, 65537, 1048583, 262147, 524309];
    const p = primes[seed % primes.length];
    let h = 2166136261 ^ seed;
    for (let i = 0; i < key.length; i++) {
      h ^= key.charCodeAt(i);
      h = (Math.imul(h, p)) >>> 0;
    }
    return h % this.size;
  }

  add(key: string, ttlMs?: number): void {
    for (let i = 0; i < this.numHashes; i++) {
      const bit = this.hash(key, i);
      this.bits[Math.floor(bit / 8)] |= (1 << (bit % 8));
    }
    this.insertionCount++;
    if (ttlMs !== undefined) {
      this.ttlBuckets.set(key, Date.now() + ttlMs);
    }
  }

  test(key: string): boolean {
    // Check TTL expiry first
    const expiry = this.ttlBuckets.get(key);
    if (expiry !== undefined && Date.now() > expiry) {
      this.ttlBuckets.delete(key);
      return false;
    }

    for (let i = 0; i < this.numHashes; i++) {
      const bit = this.hash(key, i);
      if (!(this.bits[Math.floor(bit / 8)] & (1 << (bit % 8)))) {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  /** False positive rate estimate */
  get falsePositiveRate(): number {
    const p = Math.pow(1 - Math.exp(-this.numHashes * this.insertionCount / this.size), this.numHashes);
    return p;
  }

  /** Rebuild filter from active TTL entries only (garbage collection) */
  compact(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    const activeKeys: string[] = [];
    for (const [key, expiry] of this.ttlBuckets) {
      if (expiry > now) activeKeys.push(key);
      else this.ttlBuckets.delete(key);
    }
    this.bits = new Uint8Array(Math.ceil(this.size / 8));
    this.insertionCount = 0;
    for (const key of activeKeys) {
      this.add(key);
    }
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PROPHET ROUTING ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ProphetRouter {
  private deliveryPredictabilities: Map<NodeId, ProphetDeliveryPredictability>;
  private readonly config: DTNConfig;
  private readonly nodeId: NodeId;

  constructor(nodeId: NodeId, config: DTNConfig) {
    this.nodeId = nodeId;
    this.config = config;
    this.deliveryPredictabilities = new Map();
  }

  /**
   * Called when we encounter a neighbor
   * Updates P(local, neighbor) using encounter-based formula:
   * P(a,b) = P(a,b)_old + (1 - P(a,b)_old) * P_init
   */
  recordEncounter(neighborId: NodeId): void {
    const existing = this.deliveryPredictabilities.get(neighborId);
    const now = Date.now();

    if (!existing) {
      this.deliveryPredictabilities.set(neighborId, {
        neighborId,
        deliveryProbability: this.config.prophetEncounterInit,
        lastEncounterTime: now,
        encounterCount: 1,
        transitivityVector: new Map(),
        lastUpdated: now,
      });
      return;
    }

    // Update probability: P(a,b) = P(a,b)_old + (1 - P(a,b)_old) * P_init
    const p_old = existing.deliveryProbability;
    existing.deliveryProbability = p_old + (1 - p_old) * this.config.prophetEncounterInit;
    existing.lastEncounterTime = now;
    existing.encounterCount++;
    existing.lastUpdated = now;
  }

  /**
   * Age all delivery predictabilities (called periodically)
   * P(a,b) = P(a,b)_old * γ^(Δk)
   * where Δk is the number of time units elapsed
   */
  applyAgeing(elapsedUnits: number = 1): void {
    const ageFactor = Math.pow(this.config.prophetAgeingFactor, elapsedUnits);
    for (const dp of this.deliveryPredictabilities.values()) {
      dp.deliveryProbability *= ageFactor;
      if (dp.deliveryProbability < 0.001) dp.deliveryProbability = 0;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Update transitivity: when we meet neighbor B, we learn B's vector
   * P(a,c) = P(a,c)_old + (1 - P(a,c)_old) * P(a,b) * P(b,c) * β
   */
  updateTransitivity(neighborId: NodeId, neighborVector: Map<NodeId, number>): void {
    const myDPToNeighbor = this.deliveryPredictabilities.get(neighborId);
    if (!myDPToNeighbor) return;

    const pab = myDPToNeighbor.deliveryProbability;

    for (const [destinationId, pbc] of neighborVector) {
      if (destinationId === this.nodeId) continue;

      let myDP = this.deliveryPredictabilities.get(destinationId);
      if (!myDP) {
        myDP = {
          neighborId: destinationId,
          deliveryProbability: 0,
          lastEncounterTime: 0,
          encounterCount: 0,
          transitivityVector: new Map(),
          lastUpdated: Date.now(),
        };
        this.deliveryPredictabilities.set(destinationId, myDP);
      }

      // Transitivity update
      const p_old = myDP.deliveryProbability;
      const transitivityGain = pab * pbc * this.config.prophetTransitivityScaling;
      myDP.deliveryProbability = p_old + (1 - p_old) * transitivityGain;
      myDP.lastUpdated = Date.now();

      // Store in transitivity vector of the neighbor entry
      myDPToNeighbor.transitivityVector.set(destinationId, myDP.deliveryProbability);
    }
  }

  /**
   * Decide whether to forward bundle to neighbor
   * Forward if neighbor has higher delivery probability to destination
   */
  shouldForward(bundle: Bundle, neighborId: NodeId, neighborDP: number): boolean {
    const myDP = this.deliveryPredictabilities.get(bundle.primary.destination);
    const myProbability = myDP?.deliveryProbability ?? 0;

    // Forward if neighbor has meaningfully higher probability
    const FORWARD_THRESHOLD = 0.05;
    return neighborDP > myProbability + FORWARD_THRESHOLD;
  }

  /**
   * Get delivery probability to a specific destination
   */
  getDeliveryProbability(destinationId: NodeId): number {
    return this.deliveryPredictabilities.get(destinationId)?.deliveryProbability ?? 0;
  }

  /**
   * Get full delivery predictability vector for exchange with neighbor
   */
  getDeliveryVector(): Map<NodeId, number> {
    const vector = new Map<NodeId, number>();
    for (const [nodeId, dp] of this.deliveryPredictabilities) {
      vector.set(nodeId, dp.deliveryProbability);
    }
    return vector;
  }

  /**
   * Rank neighbors for forwarding a specific bundle
   */
  rankNeighborsForBundle(
    bundle: Bundle,
    availableNeighbors: NodeId[],
    neighborDPs: Map<NodeId, number>
  ): Array<{ neighborId: NodeId; probability: number; shouldForward: boolean }> {
    return availableNeighbors
      .map(neighborId => {
        const prob = neighborDPs.get(neighborId) ?? 0;
        return {
          neighborId,
          probability: prob,
          shouldForward: this.shouldForward(bundle, neighborId, prob),
        };
      })
      .sort((a, b) => b.probability - a.probability);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// SPRAY AND WAIT ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class SprayWaitEngine {
  private sprayStates: Map<BundleId, SprayWaitState>;
  private readonly config: DTNConfig;

  constructor(config: DTNConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.sprayStates = new Map();
  }

  /**
   * Initialize Spray-and-Wait for a new bundle
   */
  initBundle(bundleId: BundleId): SprayWaitState {
    const state: SprayWaitState = {
      bundleId,
      totalCopies: this.config.sprayWaitCopies,
      copiesRemaining: this.config.sprayWaitCopies,
      sprayPhaseComplete: false,
      waitingFor: new Set(),
    };
    this.sprayStates.set(bundleId, state);
    return state;
  }

  /**
   * Binary splitting: when forwarding to a neighbor, give half the copies
   * This is optimal for minimizing expected delivery delay
   */
  computeCopiesToGive(bundleId: BundleId): number {
    const state = this.sprayStates.get(bundleId);
    if (!state || state.sprayPhaseComplete) return 0;
    if (state.copiesRemaining <= 1) return 0;

    // Binary splitting: give floor(n/2) copies
    return Math.floor(state.copiesRemaining / 2);
  }

  /**
   * Record that we gave copies to a neighbor
   */
  recordCopiesGiven(bundleId: BundleId, neighborId: NodeId, copies: number): void {
    const state = this.sprayStates.get(bundleId);
    if (!state) return;

    state.copiesRemaining -= copies;
    state.waitingFor.add(neighborId);

    // If we have exactly 1 copy left, spray phase is complete — enter wait phase
    if (state.copiesRemaining <= 1) {
      state.sprayPhaseComplete = true;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Check if we're in wait phase (only direct delivery allowed)
   */
  isWaitPhase(bundleId: BundleId): boolean {
    return this.sprayStates.get(bundleId)?.sprayPhaseComplete ?? false;
  }

  /**
   * Get copies remaining for a bundle
   */
  getCopiesRemaining(bundleId: BundleId): number {
    return this.sprayStates.get(bundleId)?.copiesRemaining ?? 0;
  }

  cleanupBundle(bundleId: BundleId): void {
    this.sprayStates.delete(bundleId);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// CONTACT GRAPH ROUTER
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class ContactGraphRouter {
  private contacts: ContactWindow[];
  private readonly nodeId: NodeId;

  constructor(nodeId: NodeId) {
    this.nodeId = nodeId;
    this.contacts = [];
  }

  addContactWindow(contact: ContactWindow): void {
    this.contacts.push(contact);
    // Keep sorted by start time
    this.contacts.sort((a, b) => a.startTime - b.startTime);
  }

  removeExpiredContacts(): void {
    const now = Date.now();
    this.contacts = this.contacts.filter(c => c.endTime > now);
  }

  /**
   * Dijkstra-like earliest arrival time routing over contact graph
   * Returns best route to destination considering contact windows
   */
  findRoute(
    destination: NodeId,
    bundleSizeBytes: number,
    departureTime: number = Date.now()
  ): CGRRoute | null {
    this.removeExpiredContacts();

    // earliest[node] = earliest time we can have a bundle at that node
    const earliest = new Map<NodeId, number>();
    const predecessor = new Map<NodeId, { contact: ContactWindow; arrivalTime: number }>();
    earliest.set(this.nodeId, departureTime);

    const queue: Array<{ nodeId: NodeId; time: number }> = [
      { nodeId: this.nodeId, time: departureTime }
    ];

    while (queue.length > 0) {
      queue.sort((a, b) => a.time - b.time);
      const { nodeId: current, time: currentTime } = queue.shift()!;

      if (current === destination) break;

      // Find contacts that depart from current node after currentTime
      for (const contact of this.contacts) {
        if (contact.fromNode !== current) continue;
        if (contact.endTime <= currentTime) continue;

        const transferStart = Math.max(contact.startTime, currentTime);
        const transferTimeMs = (bundleSizeBytes * 8) / contact.dataRateBps * 1000;
        const propagationMs = contact.rangeLightSeconds * 1000;

        // Can we complete transfer before contact window ends?
        if (transferStart + transferTimeMs > contact.endTime) continue;

        const arrivalTime = transferStart + transferTimeMs + propagationMs;
        const currentEarliest = earliest.get(contact.toNode) ?? Infinity;

        if (arrivalTime < currentEarliest) {
          earliest.set(contact.toNode, arrivalTime);
          predecessor.set(contact.toNode, { contact, arrivalTime });
          queue.push({ nodeId: contact.toNode, time: arrivalTime });
        }
      }
    }

    const destArrival = earliest.get(destination);
    if (destArrival === undefined) return null;

    // Reconstruct path
    const hops: string[] = [];
    let current = destination;
    while (predecessor.has(current)) {
      const pred = predecessor.get(current)!;
      hops.unshift(pred.contact.contactId);
      current = pred.contact.fromNode;
    }

    if (hops.length === 0) return null;

    const firstContact = this.contacts.find(c => c.contactId === hops[0])!;

    return {
      contactId: hops[0],
      nextHop: firstContact.toNode,
      arrivalTime: destArrival,
      confidence: firstContact.confidence,
      hops,
    };
  }

  getUpcomingContacts(neighborId: NodeId, windowMs: number = 60000): ContactWindow[] {
    const now = Date.now();
    return this.contacts.filter(c =>
      (c.fromNode === neighborId || c.toNode === neighborId) &&
      c.startTime <= now + windowMs &&
      c.endTime > now
    );
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// BUNDLE FRAGMENTATION ENGINE
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class FragmentationEngine {
  private readonly maxFragmentSize: number;
  private fragmentBuffers: Map<BundleId, FragmentBuffer>;
  private sequenceCounter: number;

  constructor(maxFragmentSize: number) {
    this.maxFragmentSize = maxFragmentSize;
    this.fragmentBuffers = new Map();
    this.sequenceCounter = 0;
  }

  /**
   * Fragment a bundle for transmission over MTU-limited link
   */
  fragment(bundle: Bundle): Bundle[] {
    const data = bundle.payload.data;
    if (data.length <= this.maxFragmentSize) return [bundle];

    const fragments: Bundle[] = [];
    let offset = 0;
    const totalLength = data.length;

    while (offset < totalLength) {
      const chunkSize = Math.min(this.maxFragmentSize, totalLength - offset);
      const chunk = data.slice(offset, offset + chunkSize);

      const fragmentBundle: Bundle = {
        ...bundle,
        primary: {
          ...bundle.primary,
          flags: bundle.primary.flags | BUNDLE_FLAG_FRAGMENT,
          fragmentOffset: offset,
          totalADULength: totalLength,
          sequenceNumber: this.sequenceCounter++,
        },
        payload: {
          ...bundle.payload,
          data: chunk,
          checksum: DTNCrypto.bytesToHex(DTNCrypto.sha256(chunk)),
        },
        extensions: bundle.extensions,
        routingStrategy: bundle.routingStrategy,
        copiesRemaining: bundle.copiesRemaining,
        custodyNode: bundle.custodyNode,
        custodyState: bundle.custodyState,
        custodyAcceptedAt: bundle.custodyAcceptedAt,
        retransmissionCount: bundle.retransmissionCount,
        lastAttemptedAt: bundle.lastAttemptedAt,
        priority: bundle.priority,
        receivedAt: bundle.receivedAt,
        sizeBytes: chunk.length,
        deliveredTo: new Set(bundle.deliveredTo),
        custodyChain: bundle.custodyChain,
      };

      fragments.push(fragmentBundle);
      offset += chunkSize;
    }

    return fragments;
  }

  /**
   * Accept a fragment and attempt reassembly
   * Returns complete bundle if all fragments received, null otherwise
   */
  receiveFragment(fragment: Bundle): Bundle | null {
    if (!(fragment.primary.flags & BUNDLE_FLAG_FRAGMENT)) {
      // Not a fragment, return as-is
      return fragment;
    }

    const baseId = fragment.primary.bundleId;
    const offset = fragment.primary.fragmentOffset!;
    const totalLength = fragment.primary.totalADULength!;

    let buffer = this.fragmentBuffers.get(baseId);
    if (!buffer) {
      buffer = {
        bundleId: baseId,
        source: fragment.primary.source,
        destination: fragment.primary.destination,
        totalLength,
        receivedFragments: new Map(),
        firstFragmentReceived: Date.now(),
        lastFragmentReceived: Date.now(),
        isComplete: false,
      };
      this.fragmentBuffers.set(baseId, buffer);
    }

    buffer.receivedFragments.set(offset, fragment.payload.data);
    buffer.lastFragmentReceived = Date.now();

    // Check if we have all fragments
    let totalReceived = 0;
    const sortedOffsets = Array.from(buffer.receivedFragments.keys()).sort((a, b) => a - b);

    let expectedOffset = 0;
    for (const off of sortedOffsets) {
      if (off !== expectedOffset) break;
      expectedOffset += buffer.receivedFragments.get(off)!.length;
    }

    if (expectedOffset >= totalLength) {
      buffer.isComplete = true;

      // Reassemble
      const fullData = new Uint8Array(totalLength);
      let pos = 0;
      for (const off of sortedOffsets) {
        const chunk = buffer.receivedFragments.get(off)!;
        fullData.set(chunk, pos);
        pos += chunk.length;
      }

      this.fragmentBuffers.delete(baseId);

      // Return reassembled bundle
      return {
        ...fragment,
        primary: {
          ...fragment.primary,
          flags: fragment.primary.flags & ~BUNDLE_FLAG_FRAGMENT,
          fragmentOffset: undefined,
          totalADULength: undefined,
        },
        payload: {
          ...fragment.payload,
          data: fullData,
          checksum: DTNCrypto.bytesToHex(DTNCrypto.sha256(fullData)),
        },
        sizeBytes: fullData.length,
      };
    }

    return null; // Not yet complete
  }

  /**
   * Remove expired fragment buffers
   */
  cleanupExpired(maxAgeMs: number): number {
    let removed = 0;
    const now = Date.now();
    for (const [id, buffer] of this.fragmentBuffers) {
      if (now - buffer.firstFragmentReceived > maxAgeMs) {
        this.fragmentBuffers.delete(id);
        removed++;
      }
    }
    return removed;
  }

  get pendingFragmentBuffers(): number {
    return this.fragmentBuffers.size;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// PRIORITY QUEUE — Deadline-aware multi-level scheduling
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class BundlePriorityQueue {
  private queues: Map<number, Bundle[]>; // priority level → bundles
  private readonly numLevels: number;
  private totalBundles: number;

  constructor(numLevels: number) {
    this.numLevels = numLevels;
    this.queues = new Map();
    this.totalBundles = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < numLevels; i++) {
      this.queues.set(i, []);
    }
  }

  private priorityToLevel(priority: BundlePriority): number {
    switch (priority) {
      case 'emergency': return this.numLevels - 1;
      case 'expedited': return Math.floor(this.numLevels * 0.75);
      case 'normal': return Math.floor(this.numLevels * 0.5);
      case 'bulk': return 0;
    }
  }

  enqueue(bundle: Bundle): void {
    const level = this.priorityToLevel(bundle.priority);
    const queue = this.queues.get(level)!;

    // Sort by deadline (earliest deadline first within priority level)
    const deadline = bundle.primary.creationTimestamp + bundle.primary.lifetime;
    let insertPos = queue.length;
    for (let i = 0; i < queue.length; i++) {
      const qDeadline = queue[i].primary.creationTimestamp + queue[i].primary.lifetime;
      if (deadline < qDeadline) {
        insertPos = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    queue.splice(insertPos, 0, bundle);
    this.totalBundles++;
  }

  /**
   * Dequeue highest priority non-expired bundle
   */
  dequeue(now: number = Date.now()): Bundle | null {
    // Check from highest priority downward
    for (let level = this.numLevels - 1; level >= 0; level--) {
      const queue = this.queues.get(level)!;
      while (queue.length > 0) {
        const bundle = queue[0];
        const deadline = bundle.primary.creationTimestamp + bundle.primary.lifetime;
        if (deadline <= now) {
          queue.shift();
          this.totalBundles--;
          continue; // Skip expired
        }
        queue.shift();
        this.totalBundles--;
        return bundle;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Peek at all bundles for a specific destination without removing
   */
  getBundlesForDestination(destination: NodeId): Bundle[] {
    const result: Bundle[] = [];
    for (const queue of this.queues.values()) {
      for (const bundle of queue) {
        if (bundle.primary.destination === destination) {
          result.push(bundle);
        }
      }
    }
    return result;
  }

  /**
   * Remove expired bundles across all queues
   */
  purgeExpired(now: number = Date.now()): number {
    let purged = 0;
    for (const [level, queue] of this.queues) {
      const before = queue.length;
      const filtered = queue.filter(b =>
        b.primary.creationTimestamp + b.primary.lifetime > now
      );
      this.queues.set(level, filtered);
      purged += before - filtered.length;
    }
    this.totalBundles -= purged;
    return purged;
  }

  get size(): number {
    return this.totalBundles;
  }

  getAllBundles(): Bundle[] {
    const all: Bundle[] = [];
    for (const queue of this.queues.values()) {
      all.push(...queue);
    }
    return all;
  }

  remove(bundleId: BundleId): boolean {
    for (const [level, queue] of this.queues) {
      const idx = queue.findIndex(b => b.primary.bundleId === bundleId);
      if (idx !== -1) {
        queue.splice(idx, 1);
        this.totalBundles--;
        this.queues.set(level, queue);
        return true;
      }
    }
    return false;
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// STORE-AND-FORWARD DTN ENGINE — Main orchestrator
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class StoreAndForwardEngine {
  private readonly config: DTNConfig;
  private readonly prophet: ProphetRouter;
  private readonly sprayWait: SprayWaitEngine;
  private readonly cgr: ContactGraphRouter;
  private readonly fragEngine: FragmentationEngine;
  private readonly priorityQueue: BundlePriorityQueue;
  private readonly seenBundles: DTNBloomFilter;

  // Persistent bundle store (survives contact gaps)
  private readonly bundleStore: Map<BundleId, Bundle>;

  // Current active neighbors
  private readonly activeNeighbors: Map<NodeId, {
    dataRateBps: number;
    connectedAt: number;
    deliveryVector: Map<NodeId, number>;
  }>;

  // Custody management
  private readonly custodyTimers: Map<BundleId, NodeJS.Timeout | null>;
  private readonly custodyKey: Uint8Array;

  // Sequence counter for bundle IDs
  private sequenceCounter: number;

  // Statistics
  private stats: {
    bundlesInjected: number;
    bundlesDelivered: number;
    bundlesExpired: number;
    bundlesDropped: number;
    custodyAccepted: number;
    custodyTransferred: number;
    totalBytesForwarded: number;
    contactsProcessed: number;
  };

  // Event callbacks
  private onBundleDelivered?: (result: DeliveryResult) => void;
  private onBundleExpired?: (bundleId: BundleId, reason: string) => void;
  private onCustodyAccepted?: (bundleId: BundleId, custodyId: string) => void;
  private onTransmitBundle?: (bundle: Bundle, neighborId: NodeId) => Promise<boolean>;
  private onSendCustodySignal?: (signal: CustodySignal, neighborId: NodeId) => Promise<void>;

  constructor(config: DTNConfig) {
    this.config = config;
    this.prophet = new ProphetRouter(config.nodeId, config);
    this.sprayWait = new SprayWaitEngine(config);
    this.cgr = new ContactGraphRouter(config.nodeId);
    this.fragEngine = new FragmentationEngine(config.maxFragmentSize);
    this.priorityQueue = new BundlePriorityQueue(config.priorityQueues);
    this.seenBundles = new DTNBloomFilter(config.bloomFilterSize, config.bloomFilterHashes);
    this.bundleStore = new Map();
    this.activeNeighbors = new Map();
    this.custodyTimers = new Map();
    this.sequenceCounter = 0;
    this.custodyKey = new Uint8Array(32);
    // In production: load from secure enclave
    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) this.custodyKey[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);

    this.stats = {
      bundlesInjected: 0,
      bundlesDelivered: 0,
      bundlesExpired: 0,
      bundlesDropped: 0,
      custodyAccepted: 0,
      custodyTransferred: 0,
      totalBytesForwarded: 0,
      contactsProcessed: 0,
    };
  }

  // ─── EVENT HANDLER REGISTRATION ───────────────────────────────────────────

  onDelivered(callback: (result: DeliveryResult) => void): void {
    this.onBundleDelivered = callback;
  }

  onExpired(callback: (bundleId: BundleId, reason: string) => void): void {
    this.onBundleExpired = callback;
  }

  onCustodyAcceptedEvent(callback: (bundleId: BundleId, custodyId: string) => void): void {
    this.onCustodyAccepted = callback;
  }

  setTransmitCallback(callback: (bundle: Bundle, neighborId: NodeId) => Promise<boolean>): void {
    this.onTransmitBundle = callback;
  }

  setCustodySignalCallback(callback: (signal: CustodySignal, neighborId: NodeId) => Promise<void>): void {
    this.onSendCustodySignal = callback;
  }

  // ─── BUNDLE INJECTION ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Inject a new bundle for delivery
   * This is the entry point for all outbound messages
   */
  async injectBundle(
    destination: NodeId,
    payload: Uint8Array,
    options: {
      priority?: BundlePriority;
      ttlSeconds?: number;
      strategy?: RoutingStrategy;
      requestCustody?: boolean;
      reportTo?: NodeId;
    } = {}
  ): Promise<BundleId> {
    const now = Date.now();
    const DTN_EPOCH = new Date('2000-01-01T00:00:00Z').getTime();
    const dtnTimestamp = now - DTN_EPOCH;

    const bundleId = DTNCrypto.computeBundleId(
      this.config.nodeId,
      dtnTimestamp,
      this.sequenceCounter++
    );

    const checksum = DTNCrypto.bytesToHex(DTNCrypto.sha256(payload));

    const bundle: Bundle = {
      primary: {
        version: 7,
        bundleId,
        flags: (options.requestCustody ? BUNDLE_FLAG_REQ_CUSTODY : 0) |
               BUNDLE_FLAG_DEST_SINGLETON |
               BUNDLE_FLAG_ACK_REQUESTED,
        destination,
        source: this.config.nodeId,
        reportTo: options.reportTo ?? this.config.nodeId,
        creationTimestamp: dtnTimestamp,
        sequenceNumber: this.sequenceCounter,
        lifetime: (options.ttlSeconds ?? this.config.maxBundleTTLSeconds) * 1000,
      },
      payload: {
        blockType: 1,
        blockNumber: 1,
        flags: 0,
        data: payload,
        checksum,
      },
      extensions: [],
      routingStrategy: options.strategy ?? 'prophet',
      copiesRemaining: this.config.sprayWaitCopies,
      custodyNode: options.requestCustody ? this.config.nodeId : null,
      custodyState: options.requestCustody ? 'accepted' : 'none',
      custodyAcceptedAt: options.requestCustody ? now : 0,
      retransmissionCount: 0,
      lastAttemptedAt: 0,
      priority: options.priority ?? 'normal',
      receivedAt: now,
      sizeBytes: payload.length,
      deliveredTo: new Set(),
      custodyChain: options.requestCustody ? [{
        nodeId: this.config.nodeId,
        acceptedAt: now,
        releasedAt: null,
        signature: DTNCrypto.signCustodyRecord(bundleId, this.config.nodeId, now, this.custodyKey),
      }] : [],
    };

    if (options.strategy === 'spray_wait') {
      this.sprayWait.initBundle(bundleId);
    }

    this.bundleStore.set(bundleId, bundle);
    this.priorityQueue.enqueue(bundle);
    this.seenBundles.add(bundleId, bundle.primary.lifetime);
    this.stats.bundlesInjected++;

    // Attempt immediate forwarding to any active neighbors
    await this.attemptForwardingToAllNeighbors(bundle);

    return bundleId;
  }

  // ─── CONTACT PROCESSING ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Main event handler — called by transport layer when contact events occur
   */
  async processContactEvent(event: ContactEvent): Promise<void> {
    switch (event.type) {
      case 'contact_start':
        await this.handleContactStart(event);
        break;
      case 'contact_end':
        this.handleContactEnd(event);
        break;
      case 'data_received':
        await this.handleDataReceived(event);
        break;
      case 'custody_signal':
        await this.handleCustodySignal(event);
        break;
    }
  }

  private async handleContactStart(event: ContactEvent): Promise<void> {
    const { neighborId, timestamp, dataRateBps = 1000 } = event;

    this.stats.contactsProcessed++;

    // Register neighbor
    this.activeNeighbors.set(neighborId, {
      dataRateBps,
      connectedAt: timestamp,
      deliveryVector: new Map(),
    });

    // Update PRoPHET encounter
    this.prophet.recordEncounter(neighborId);

    // Exchange summary vectors (epidemic routing)
    const myVector = this.prophet.getDeliveryVector();

    // Forward bundles destined for this neighbor (direct delivery)
    await this.forwardDirectDelivery(neighborId);

    // Forward bundles where this neighbor has better delivery probability (PRoPHET)
    await this.forwardProphetBundles(neighborId);

    // Spray remaining copies (Spray-and-Wait)
    await this.forwardSprayWait(neighborId);
  }

  private handleContactEnd(event: ContactEvent): void {
    this.activeNeighbors.delete(event.neighborId);
  }

  private async handleDataReceived(event: ContactEvent): Promise<void> {
    if (!event.bundle) return;

    const incomingBundle = event.bundle;
    const bundleId = incomingBundle.primary.bundleId;

    // De-duplication check
    if (this.seenBundles.test(bundleId)) return;
    this.seenBundles.add(bundleId, incomingBundle.primary.lifetime);

    // Handle fragmented bundle
    const bundle = this.fragEngine.receiveFragment(incomingBundle);
    if (!bundle) return; // Waiting for more fragments

    // Check if expired
    const now = Date.now();
    const DTN_EPOCH = new Date('2000-01-01T00:00:00Z').getTime();
    const expiryMs = DTN_EPOCH + bundle.primary.creationTimestamp + bundle.primary.lifetime;
    if (now > expiryMs) {
      this.stats.bundlesExpired++;
      return;
    }

    // Check if we are the destination
    if (bundle.primary.destination === this.config.nodeId) {
      await this.deliverBundleLocally(bundle);
      return;
    }

    // Verify storage capacity
    const currentStorageBytes = this.computeStorageUsed();
    if (currentStorageBytes + bundle.sizeBytes > this.config.maxBundleStorageBytes) {
      await this.evictLowPriorityBundle(bundle.sizeBytes);
    }

    // Accept custody if requested
    if (bundle.primary.flags & BUNDLE_FLAG_REQ_CUSTODY) {
      await this.acceptCustody(bundle, event.neighborId);
    }

    // Store bundle
    this.bundleStore.set(bundleId, bundle);
    this.priorityQueue.enqueue(bundle);

    // Attempt forwarding to current neighbors
    await this.attemptForwardingToAllNeighbors(bundle);
  }

  private async handleCustodySignal(event: ContactEvent): Promise<void> {
    if (!event.custodySignal) return;
    const signal = event.custodySignal;

    if (signal.succeeded) {
      // Custody successfully transferred — release our custody
      const bundle = this.bundleStore.get(signal.bundleId);
      if (bundle) {
        bundle.custodyState = 'transferred';
        bundle.custodyNode = signal.fromNode;

        // Clear custody timer
        const timer = this.custodyTimers.get(signal.bundleId);
        if (timer) {
          clearTimeout(timer as unknown as number);
          this.custodyTimers.delete(signal.bundleId);
        }

        // We can now delete our copy (custody transferred)
        this.bundleStore.delete(signal.bundleId);
        this.priorityQueue.remove(signal.bundleId);
        this.stats.custodyTransferred++;
      }
    } else {
      // Custody transfer failed — retain custody and retry
      const bundle = this.bundleStore.get(signal.bundleId);
      if (bundle) {
        bundle.retransmissionCount++;
      }
    }
  }

  // ─── FORWARDING STRATEGIES ────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private async forwardDirectDelivery(neighborId: NodeId): Promise<void> {
    const bundles = this.priorityQueue.getBundlesForDestination(neighborId);
    for (const bundle of bundles) {
      if (!bundle.deliveredTo.has(neighborId)) {
        await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
      }
    }
  }

  private async forwardProphetBundles(neighborId: NodeId): Promise<void> {
    const neighborInfo = this.activeNeighbors.get(neighborId);
    if (!neighborInfo) return;

    const allBundles = this.priorityQueue.getAllBundles();

    // Get neighbor's delivery vector (exchanged during contact setup)
    const neighborDP = neighborInfo.deliveryVector;

    for (const bundle of allBundles) {
      if (bundle.routingStrategy !== 'prophet') continue;
      if (bundle.deliveredTo.has(neighborId)) continue;
      if (bundle.primary.destination === this.config.nodeId) continue;

      const neighborProbability = neighborDP.get(bundle.primary.destination) ?? 0;

      if (this.prophet.shouldForward(bundle, neighborId, neighborProbability)) {
        await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
      }
    }
  }

  private async forwardSprayWait(neighborId: NodeId): Promise<void> {
    const allBundles = this.priorityQueue.getAllBundles();

    for (const bundle of allBundles) {
      if (bundle.routingStrategy !== 'spray_wait') continue;
      if (bundle.deliveredTo.has(neighborId)) continue;

      // In wait phase: only direct delivery
      if (this.sprayWait.isWaitPhase(bundle.primary.bundleId)) {
        if (bundle.primary.destination !== neighborId) continue;
        await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
        continue;
      }

      // In spray phase: give copies using binary splitting
      const copiesToGive = this.sprayWait.computeCopiesToGive(bundle.primary.bundleId);
      if (copiesToGive > 0) {
        const fragmentsToSend = this.fragEngine.fragment(bundle);
        let success = false;

        for (const fragment of fragmentsToSend) {
          success = await this.doTransmit(fragment, neighborId) || success;
        }

        if (success) {
          this.sprayWait.recordCopiesGiven(bundle.primary.bundleId, neighborId, copiesToGive);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private async attemptForwardingToAllNeighbors(bundle: Bundle): Promise<void> {
    for (const neighborId of this.activeNeighbors.keys()) {
      if (bundle.deliveredTo.has(neighborId)) continue;

      // Direct delivery
      if (bundle.primary.destination === neighborId) {
        await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
        continue;
      }

      // Strategy-based forwarding
      switch (bundle.routingStrategy) {
        case 'prophet': {
          const neighborInfo = this.activeNeighbors.get(neighborId)!;
          const neighborDP = neighborInfo.deliveryVector.get(bundle.primary.destination) ?? 0;
          if (this.prophet.shouldForward(bundle, neighborId, neighborDP)) {
            await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
          }
          break;
        }

        case 'spray_wait': {
          if (!this.sprayWait.isWaitPhase(bundle.primary.bundleId)) {
            const copies = this.sprayWait.computeCopiesToGive(bundle.primary.bundleId);
            if (copies > 0) await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
          }
          break;
        }

        case 'epidemic': {
          // Forward to everyone (controlled by Bloom filter de-dup)
          await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
          break;
        }

        case 'cgr': {
          const route = this.cgr.findRoute(
            bundle.primary.destination,
            bundle.sizeBytes,
            Date.now()
          );
          if (route?.nextHop === neighborId) {
            await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
          }
          break;
        }

        case 'direct':
          // Only deliver directly — already handled above
          break;
      }
    }
  }

  // ─── TRANSMISSION ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private async transmitBundle(bundle: Bundle, neighborId: NodeId): Promise<void> {
    if (bundle.retransmissionCount >= this.config.maxRetransmissions) return;

    const fragments = this.fragEngine.fragment(bundle);
    let allSuccess = true;

    for (const fragment of fragments) {
      const success = await this.doTransmit(fragment, neighborId);
      if (!success) { allSuccess = false; break; }
