    }

    if (allSuccess) {
      bundle.deliveredTo.add(neighborId);
      bundle.lastAttemptedAt = Date.now();
      this.stats.totalBytesForwarded += bundle.sizeBytes;

      // Handle custody transfer
      if (bundle.primary.flags & BUNDLE_FLAG_REQ_CUSTODY && bundle.custodyState === 'accepted') {
        await this.initiateCustodyTransfer(bundle, neighborId);
      }
    } else {
      bundle.retransmissionCount++;
    }
  }

  private async doTransmit(bundle: Bundle, neighborId: NodeId): Promise<boolean> {
    if (!this.onTransmitBundle) return false;
    try {
      return await this.onTransmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  // ─── CUSTODY MANAGEMENT ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private async acceptCustody(bundle: Bundle, fromNode: NodeId): Promise<void> {
    const now = Date.now();
    const custodyId = DTNCrypto.generateId('c-');

    const record: CustodyRecord = {
      nodeId: this.config.nodeId,
      acceptedAt: now,
      releasedAt: null,
      signature: DTNCrypto.signCustodyRecord(
        bundle.primary.bundleId,
        this.config.nodeId,
        now,
        this.custodyKey
      ),
    };

    bundle.custodyState = 'accepted';
    bundle.custodyNode = this.config.nodeId;
    bundle.custodyAcceptedAt = now;
    bundle.custodyChain.push(record);
    this.stats.custodyAccepted++;

    // Send custody acceptance signal
    const signal: CustodySignal = {
      bundleId: bundle.primary.bundleId,
      succeeded: true,
      custodyId,
      timestamp: now,
      fromNode: this.config.nodeId,
      signature: DTNCrypto.hmacSha256(
        this.custodyKey,
        DTNCrypto.stringToBytes(`${bundle.primary.bundleId}:${custodyId}:${now}`)
      ),
    };

    if (this.onSendCustodySignal && fromNode) {
      await this.onSendCustodySignal(signal, fromNode);
    }

    if (this.onCustodyAccepted) {
      this.onCustodyAccepted(bundle.primary.bundleId, custodyId);
    }

    // Set custody timeout for retransmission
    this.setCustodyTimer(bundle);
  }

  private async initiateCustodyTransfer(bundle: Bundle, neighborId: NodeId): Promise<void> {
    // Custody transfer happens after acknowledgement
    // For now, optimistically mark as transferred
    // In production: wait for CUSTODY_SIGNAL from neighbor
    bundle.custodyState = 'transferred';
    this.stats.custodyTransferred++;
  }

  private setCustodyTimer(bundle: Bundle): void {
    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
      // Custody timeout — the bundle was not acknowledged
      // Retransmit to another neighbor if possible
      const stored = this.bundleStore.get(bundle.primary.bundleId);
      if (!stored || stored.custodyState === 'transferred') return;

      // Find a different neighbor to try
      for (const neighborId of this.activeNeighbors.keys()) {
        if (!bundle.deliveredTo.has(neighborId)) {
          await this.transmitBundle(bundle, neighborId);
          this.setCustodyTimer(bundle);
          return;
        }
      }
    }, this.config.custodyTimeoutSeconds * 1000) as unknown as null;

    this.custodyTimers.set(bundle.primary.bundleId, timer);
  }

  // ─── LOCAL DELIVERY ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private async deliverBundleLocally(bundle: Bundle): Promise<void> {
    const now = Date.now();
    const DTN_EPOCH = new Date('2000-01-01T00:00:00Z').getTime();
    const createdAt = DTN_EPOCH + bundle.primary.creationTimestamp;

    const result: DeliveryResult = {
      bundleId: bundle.primary.bundleId,
      delivered: true,
      deliveredAt: now,
      hops: bundle.custodyChain.length,
      totalTransitTime: now - createdAt,
      custodyTransfers: bundle.custodyChain.length,
    };

    this.stats.bundlesDelivered++;
    this.bundleStore.delete(bundle.primary.bundleId);
    this.priorityQueue.remove(bundle.primary.bundleId);

    if (this.onBundleDelivered) {
      this.onBundleDelivered(result);
    }
  }

  // ─── MAINTENANCE ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Called periodically — age PRoPHET probabilities, purge expired bundles
   */
  async runMaintenance(): Promise<void> {
    const now = Date.now();

    // Age PRoPHET delivery predictabilities
    this.prophet.applyAgeing(1);

    // Purge expired bundles
    const purged = this.priorityQueue.purgeExpired(now);
    if (purged > 0) {
      this.stats.bundlesExpired += purged;
      // Remove from persistent store too
      for (const [id, bundle] of this.bundleStore) {
        const DTN_EPOCH = new Date('2000-01-01T00:00:00Z').getTime();
        const expiryMs = DTN_EPOCH + bundle.primary.creationTimestamp + bundle.primary.lifetime;
        if (now > expiryMs) {
          this.bundleStore.delete(id);
          if (this.onBundleExpired) {
            this.onBundleExpired(id, 'TTL expired');
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Compact Bloom filter periodically
    if (this.seenBundles.falsePositiveRate > 0.1) {
      this.seenBundles.compact();
    }

    // Clean up old fragment buffers
    this.fragEngine.cleanupExpired(this.config.maxBundleTTLSeconds * 1000);
  }

  /**
   * Evict lowest priority bundle to make room
   */
  private async evictLowPriorityBundle(requiredBytes: number): Promise<void> {
    const allBundles = this.priorityQueue.getAllBundles()
      .filter(b => b.custodyState !== 'accepted') // Never evict bundles we have custody of
      .sort((a, b) => {
        const priorityOrder = { bulk: 0, normal: 1, expedited: 2, emergency: 3 };
        return priorityOrder[a.priority] - priorityOrder[b.priority];
      });

    let freed = 0;
    for (const bundle of allBundles) {
      if (freed >= requiredBytes) break;
      this.priorityQueue.remove(bundle.primary.bundleId);
      this.bundleStore.delete(bundle.primary.bundleId);
      freed += bundle.sizeBytes;
      this.stats.bundlesDropped++;

      if (this.onBundleExpired) {
        this.onBundleExpired(bundle.primary.bundleId, 'Evicted: storage pressure');
      }
    }
  }

  // ─── CONTACT GRAPH API ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  addContactWindow(contact: ContactWindow): void {
    this.cgr.addContactWindow(contact);
  }

  updateNeighborDeliveryVector(neighborId: NodeId, vector: Map<NodeId, number>): void {
    const neighborInfo = this.activeNeighbors.get(neighborId);
    if (neighborInfo) {
      neighborInfo.deliveryVector = vector;
    }
    this.prophet.updateTransitivity(neighborId, vector);
  }

  // ─── STATUS & STATISTICS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

  private computeStorageUsed(): number {
    let total = 0;
    for (const bundle of this.bundleStore.values()) {
      total += bundle.sizeBytes;
    }
    return total;
  }

  getStatistics(): QueueStatistics {
    const allBundles = this.priorityQueue.getAllBundles();
    const now = Date.now();
    const DTN_EPOCH = new Date('2000-01-01T00:00:00Z').getTime();

    const byPriority: Record<BundlePriority, number> = {
      bulk: 0, normal: 0, expedited: 0, emergency: 0
    };
    const byStrategy: Record<RoutingStrategy, number> = {
      prophet: 0, spray_wait: 0, epidemic: 0, direct: 0, cgr: 0
    };

    let totalAge = 0;
    let oldest = 0;

    for (const b of allBundles) {
      byPriority[b.priority]++;
      byStrategy[b.routingStrategy]++;
      const ageMs = now - (DTN_EPOCH + b.primary.creationTimestamp);
      totalAge += ageMs;
      if (ageMs > oldest) oldest = ageMs;
    }

    return {
      totalBundles: allBundles.length,
      totalBytes: this.computeStorageUsed(),
      bundlesByPriority: byPriority,
      bundlesByStrategy: byStrategy,
      expiredCount: this.stats.bundlesExpired,
      deliveredCount: this.stats.bundlesDelivered,
      custodyAccepted: this.stats.custodyAccepted,
      custodyTransferred: this.stats.custodyTransferred,
      avgAgeSeconds: allBundles.length > 0 ? totalAge / allBundles.length / 1000 : 0,
      oldestBundleSeconds: oldest / 1000,
      fragmentBuffers: this.fragEngine.pendingFragmentBuffers,
    };
  }

  getEngineStats() {
    return { ...this.stats };
  }

  getDeliveryProbability(destination: NodeId): number {
    return this.prophet.getDeliveryProbability(destination);
  }

  getStorageUtilization(): { usedBytes: number; maxBytes: number; percent: number } {
    const used = this.computeStorageUsed();
    return {
      usedBytes: used,
      maxBytes: this.config.maxBundleStorageBytes,
      percent: (used / this.config.maxBundleStorageBytes) * 100,
    };
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// DTN NETWORK MANAGER — Multi-engine coordination
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export class DTNNetworkManager {
  private readonly engine: StoreAndForwardEngine;
  private readonly config: DTNConfig;
  private maintenanceInterval: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null;
  private readonly secretKey: Uint8Array;

  constructor(config: Partial<DTNConfig> & { nodeId: NodeId }) {
    this.config = { ...DEFAULT_DTN_CONFIG, ...config };
    this.engine = new StoreAndForwardEngine(this.config);
    this.maintenanceInterval = null;
    this.secretKey = new Uint8Array(32);
    for (let i = 0; i < 32; i++) this.secretKey[i] = Math.floor(Math.random() * 256);
  }

  /**
   * Start the DTN engine with maintenance loop
   */
  start(maintenanceIntervalMs: number = 30000): void {
    this.maintenanceInterval = setInterval(async () => {
      await this.engine.runMaintenance();
    }, maintenanceIntervalMs);
  }

  stop(): void {
    if (this.maintenanceInterval) {
      clearInterval(this.maintenanceInterval);
      this.maintenanceInterval = null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Send a message — automatically selects best routing strategy
   */
  async send(
    destination: NodeId,
    data: Uint8Array,
    options: {
      priority?: BundlePriority;
      ttlSeconds?: number;
      forceStrategy?: RoutingStrategy;
      requestCustody?: boolean;
    } = {}
  ): Promise<BundleId> {
    // Auto-select strategy based on network state
    let strategy: RoutingStrategy = options.forceStrategy ?? 'prophet';

    if (!options.forceStrategy) {
      const dp = this.engine.getDeliveryProbability(destination);
      if (dp > 0.8) {
        strategy = 'direct';
      } else if (dp > 0.3) {
        strategy = 'prophet';
      } else {
        // Unknown destination — use Spray-and-Wait for epidemic containment
        strategy = 'spray_wait';
      }
    }

    return this.engine.injectBundle(destination, data, {
      ...options,
      strategy,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Register a scheduled contact window (for CGR routing)
   */
  scheduleContact(contact: ContactWindow): void {
    this.engine.addContactWindow(contact);
  }

  /**
   * Handle incoming contact from transport layer
   */
  async onContact(event: ContactEvent): Promise<void> {
    await this.engine.processContactEvent(event);
  }

  /**
   * Update neighbor's delivery probability vector (exchanged on contact)
   */
  updateNeighborVector(neighborId: NodeId, vector: Map<NodeId, number>): void {
    this.engine.updateNeighborDeliveryVector(neighborId, vector);
  }

  // Event handler proxies
  onBundleDelivered(cb: (result: DeliveryResult) => void): void {
    this.engine.onDelivered(cb);
  }

  onBundleExpired(cb: (bundleId: BundleId, reason: string) => void): void {
    this.engine.onExpired(cb);
  }

  setTransmitCallback(cb: (bundle: Bundle, neighborId: NodeId) => Promise<boolean>): void {
    this.engine.setTransmitCallback(cb);
  }

  setCustodySignalCallback(cb: (signal: CustodySignal, neighborId: NodeId) => Promise<void>): void {
    this.engine.setCustodySignalCallback(cb);
  }

  getStatistics(): QueueStatistics {
    return this.engine.getStatistics();
  }

  getEngineStats() {
    return this.engine.getEngineStats();
  }

  getStorageUtilization() {
    return this.engine.getStorageUtilization();
  }

  getDeliveryProbability(destination: NodeId): number {
    return this.engine.getDeliveryProbability(destination);
  }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// FACTORY & EXPORT
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

export function createDTNEngine(
  nodeId: NodeId,
  overrides: Partial<DTNConfig> = {}
): DTNNetworkManager {
  return new DTNNetworkManager({ nodeId, ...overrides });
}

export {
  DTNCrypto,
  DTNBloomFilter,
  ProphetRouter,
  SprayWaitEngine,
  ContactGraphRouter,
  FragmentationEngine,
  BundlePriorityQueue,
};

export default StoreAndForwardEngine;
